### [1] [MVWeaver: A Hierarchical Music Video Generation Agent with a Learned Song-to-Visual Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06478)
*Sifei Li,Minyan Luo,Xu Li,Guodong Qi,Xincan Wang,Hanwen Wang,Chen Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Oliver Deussen,Weiming Dong*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出MVWeaver，一种音乐视频生成智能体，通过层次化规划和学到的"歌曲到视觉"桥接机制，将歌曲理解转化为可执行的镜头计划，以提升音乐视频的长时一致性和歌曲对齐的视觉发展。系统在1,861对真实歌曲-MV数据上进行LoRA监督微调，实验证明其在歌曲驱动的视觉翻译、视觉丰富度和一致性方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动音乐视频生成系统虽能生成视觉上合理的镜头，但在长时连贯性和以歌曲为根基的视觉发展方面表现不足。需要一个能够将歌曲深层理解转化为可执行视觉计划的系统来弥补这一差距。

Method: MVWeaver架构包含三个核心组件：(1)全面的歌曲分析模块，(2)构建层次化计划的视觉规划器，以及(3)渲染计划内容的下游图像和视频生成模型。核心创新在于从真实MV中学习"歌曲到视觉"桥接知识——通过构建1,861对真实歌曲-MV配对，包含歌曲侧、MV侧及教师推断的歌曲-视觉推理标注，利用LoRA监督微调通用LLM，使其能够预测引导层次化视觉规划的桥接信息。

Result: 实验表明该方法的歌曲驱动的视觉翻译更强、视觉发展更丰富、概念及镜头间一致性更高；消融实验支持了学习桥接条件化的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过引入可学习的歌曲到视觉桥接并结合真实MV的监督信号，MVWeaver显著提升了音乐视频生成的歌曲对齐性和长时连贯性，验证了从真实MV数据中学习规划知识这一方向的有效性。

Abstract: Music videos are an important form of audiovisual expression in contemporary culture. They translate and extend the expressive content of songs through deliberate visual design. Existing automatic music video (MV) generation systems can generate visually plausible shots, yet often struggle with long-form coherence and song-grounded visual development. We present MVWeaver, a music video generation agent that integrates hierarchical planning with a learned song-to-visual bridge that translates song understanding into executable shot plans. The MVWeaver architecture comprises a comprehensive song analysis module, a visual planner that constructs hierarchical plans, and downstream image and video generation models that render the planned content. To equip a general-purpose LLM with MV-specific song-to-visual knowledge, we learn a bridge between song analysis and visual planning from real-MV-derived supervision and curate 1,861 real-world song--MV pairs with structured song-side, MV-side, and teacher-inferred song-to-visual rationale annotations. Using these annotations, we perform LoRA-based supervised fine-tuning (SFT) of a large language model to predict song-to-visual bridges that guide hierarchical visual planning. Our experiments demonstrate stronger song-grounded visual translation, richer visual development, and greater conceptual and shot-to-shot coherence, while ablations support the benefits of learned bridge conditioning.

</details>


### [2] [Vision-Guided Text Prompt Tuning for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06497)
*Xiaoran Kou,Jingyi Wu,Peng Sun,Yang Liu,Hong Chen*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出VG-TPT方法，通过视觉引导的文本提示调优（Vision-Guided Text Prompt Tuning），在冻结的BERT编码器中逐层注入视觉情感线索，实现多模态情感分析中可控、自适应且参数高效的视觉校准。该方法在CMU-MOSEI和CMU-MOSI数据集上优于纯文本基线，并以仅2.4M可训练参数取得与全模态方法相当或更好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感分析需要同时建模文本语义和非语言情感线索，但盲目的多模态融合可能引入视觉噪声并扭曲文本语义；同时，完全微调大规模视觉和文本编码器成本高昂且容易在小规模MSA基准上过拟合。因此需要一种可控、自适应且参数高效的视觉-文本融合方式。

Method: VG-TPT采用视觉引导文本提示调优策略，将视觉情感线索通过逐层自适应提示注入冻结的BERT编码器中。具体而言，该方法依赖于一个共引导路由器（co-guided router），该路由器结合演进的文本状态和视觉引导特征，从可训练的提示库中组合生成逐样本和逐层特定的提示，从而实现视觉对冻结文本表示的可控校准。

Result: 实验在CMU-MOSEI和CMU-MOSI数据集上进行，结果表明VG-TPT在文本基线之上持续提升性能，并与多种全模态微调方法相比达到竞争性或更优的结果，同时仅更新2.4M个可训练参数。

Conclusion: VG-TPT验证了视觉信息可以作为可控的校准信号引导冻结文本编码器进行有效的情感理解，避免了昂贵的全骨干网微调和简单的后期特征融合所带来的问题，为多模态情感分析提供了一种参数高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis requires effective modeling of both verbal semantics and non-verbal affective cues. A central challenge is to calibrate text-centered sentiment understanding with visual facial evidence in a controlled, adaptive, and parameter-efficient manner. Text usually serves as the semantic anchor, whereas visual cues provide complementary evidence for ambiguous or implicit expressions; however, indiscriminate fusion may introduce visual noise and distort textual semantics. Moreover, fully fine-tuning large visual and textual encoders is costly and prone to overfitting on limited and scenario-dependent MSA benchmarks. To address these issues, we propose Vision-Guided Text Prompt Tuning (VG-TPT), which formulates visual-text sentiment modeling as controllable visual calibration of frozen text representations. VG-TPT injects visual affective cues into a frozen BERT encoder through layer-wise adaptive prompts, rather than relying on late-stage feature fusion or full backbone tuning. A co-guided router composes prompts from a trainable prompt bank according to both the evolving text state and the visual guidance feature, enabling sample-specific and layer-specific modulation. Experiments on CMU-MOSEI and CMU-MOSI show that VG-TPT consistently improves over text-only baselines and achieves competitive or superior performance compared with several full-modality methods, while updating only 2.4M trainable parameters. The code is available at https://github.com/ma-tubu/VG-TPT.

</details>


### [3] [AdoDAS: A Privacy-Preserving Multimodal Challenge for Adolescent Depression, Anxiety, and Stress Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07038)
*Zhaojie Luo,Junkun Wang,Tianhua Qi,Yuxuan Wu,Xin Zhao,Tetsuya Takiguchi,Tomoko Matsui,Kun Qian,Fei Wang,Shuqiong Wu,Zhengjun Yue,Hiroshi Ishiguro,Xinyuan Qian,Haizhou Li*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文介绍AdoDAS大挑战赛，在隐私保护政策下利用去标识化的视听表征和ASR文本对青少年抑郁、焦虑和压力进行多任务筛查及DASS-21条目序数预测。6000名参与者提供24000个片段，领先方案在筛查任务达到0.5921平均F1，在条目预测任务达到0.2776平均二次加权Kappa。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 青少年抑郁、焦虑和压力问题亟需可扩展的辅助评估工具，这些工具应补充而非替代专业评估。同时需在隐私保护的前提下利用多模态数据进行大规模自动筛查。

Method: 挑战赛采用隐私保护政策，隐藏未成年人原始录音，仅分发匿名化的音视频表征和ASR转录文本。设置双赛道：多任务二分类D/A/S筛查与21个DASS-21条目序数预测。数据覆盖脚本朗读和三段开放对话共四种会话类型，共计6000名参与者、24000个片段。

Result: 191个注册团队中，最终榜单包含95个合格的筛查团队和64个条目预测团队。音视频基线在平均F1上为0.4604，平均二次加权Kappa为0.2675；领先提交分别达到0.5921和0.2776。代表性系统强调跨会话建模、时间多模态融合、心理测量结构和任务感知校准。

Conclusion: 该挑战赛验证了在严格隐私约束下利用多模态机器学习辅助青少年心理评估的可行性与潜力，领先方案的表现显著超越基线，但仍有较大提升空间，未来需进一步优化模型架构与评估精度。

Abstract: Adolescent depression, anxiety, and stress (D/A/S) call for scalable tools that complement, rather than replace, professional evaluation. Under a privacy-preserving policy, the AdoDAS Grand Challenge withholds minors' raw recordings and distributes anonymized audio-visual representations and ASR-derived text. Its 6,000 participants provide 24,000 segments across one scripted-reading and three open-response sessions. Two tracks assess multi-task binary D/A/S screening and ordinal prediction of 21 DASS-21 item responses. From 191 registrations, the final leaderboards included 95 eligible screening teams and 64 item-prediction teams. Audio-visual baselines achieved 0.4604 mean F1 and 0.2675 mean Quadratic Weighted Kappa; leading submissions reached 0.5921 and 0.2776. Representative systems emphasize cross-session modelling, temporal multimodal fusion, psychometric structure, and task-aware calibration.

</details>


### [4] [Can Agents Win the Video Browser Showdown?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07311)
*Bastian Jäckl,Zuzana Vopálková,Daniel A. Keim,Jakub Lokoč*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文研究现代视觉语言模型（VLM）和智能体方法能否自主完成交互式视频检索任务，即仅凭初始搜索意图描述，无需人工参与，通过智能体控制现有交互式视频搜索系统来达到搜索目标。结果表明，VLM智能体在多项设置中能达到与历史专家操作系统竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视频集合的搜索通常是交互式过程，用户既需要持有搜索意图，又需要在迭代搜索循环中不断操作化这一意图。作者希望探究现代VLM和智能体方法能否在给定初始搜索目标描述的前提下，全自主地完成传统上需要交互的搜索任务，从而减少或消除人工参与。

Method: 研究构建了一个由VLM驱动的智能体系统，将其与现有的交互式视频检索系统相结合。视频检索系统负责视频的索引和高效查询，而VLM智能体则分析检索到的排名靠前的候选结果，并根据分析结果自主决定下一步动作（如修改查询、浏览更多候选等），从而形成一个自动化闭环搜索流程。

Result: 实验表明，现代VLM智能体能够从初始意图描述出发，自主操作交互式视频检索系统解决许多搜索任务，在多种设置下取得的性能可与历史上由专家操作的强基线系统相竞争。

Conclusion: 研究证明了智能体驱动的自主交互式视频搜索是可行的，VLM智能体可以在不需要全程人工交互的情况下完成大量搜索任务，这为大规模视频检索的自动化提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: Searching large video collections is typically an interactive process in which users play two roles. First, they hold the search intent: the underlying goal that determines what content they seek and why. Second, users must operationalize this intent through an iterative search loop. Users translate their intent into queries, browse the retrieved candidates, and refine their queries based on the results. In this paper, we investigate the capabilities of modern Vision Language Models (VLM) and agentic approaches to reach search goals interactively and fully autonomously. Specifically, we study whether a provided initial specification of a search goal might be sufficient to solve traditionally interactive search tasks with an agentic system. Provided that the involved VLMs are not aware of the whole large video dataset in advance, the key challenge lies in the effective combination of an existing interactive video search system and a smart VLM agent controlling the system. While the search system provides indexing and efficient querying, the VLM-based agents analyze top-ranked items and make decisions about next actions. Our results show that modern agents can autonomously operate interactive video retrieval systems to solve many search tasks from an initial intent description, achieving performance competitive with strong historical expert-operated systems in several settings.

</details>


### [5] [Concept-Level Risk and Calibration for Governance in Diffusion Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08517)
*Kun Xu,Yushu Zhang,Tao Wang,Shuren Qi,Barbara Carminati,Elena Ferrari,Yuming Fang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出CLRC框架，一个面向扩散模型的概念级概率审计与报告框架，将治理相关的概念行为形式化为伯努利语义事件，并通过概念风险算子将模型-通道配置映射为结构化的风险画像，支持跨提示接口、嵌入通道、模型和记录条件的安全评估比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着扩散模型的语义控制从自然语言提示扩展到学习嵌入和干预管道，现有的基于启发式审计、对抗探测或任务特定擦除基准的方法难以统一、系统地跨模型、跨条件通道、跨部署条件进行安全与治理评估，尤其是在身份关联和隐私相关概念上。

Method: 将治理相关的概念行为定义为随机生成诱导的伯努利语义事件；定义概念风险算子，将模型-通道配置映射为结构化风险画像；采用样本级事后校准和配置级风险聚合；引入多协议池化校准器以改进保留概率的可靠性。

Result: 在SD1.5、SD2.1和SDXL上的实验显示概念族、通道、记录条件和转移协议之间存在一致但非均匀的运行风险模式。嵌入通道访问和混淆提示暴露的风险常被标准提示评估低估；多协议池化校准器改善了保留概率可靠性，但单一标准校准器不可迁移。

Conclusion: CLRC为多媒体生成系统提供了概率化和决策感知治理的统一审计模式，使跨不同提示接口、嵌入通道和部署条件下的安全评估成为可能。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become a core paradigm for multimedia generation, offering powerful concept-driven controllability for personalization, semantic editing, and selective unlearning. However, as semantic control extends beyond natural-language prompts to learned embeddings and intervention pipelines, the safety and governance of these systems become increasingly difficult to evaluate in a unified manner, especially for safety-sensitive, identity-linked, and other privacy-relevant concepts. Existing studies mainly rely on heuristic audits, adversarial probing, or task-specific erasure benchmarks, and therefore provide limited support for systematic comparison across models, conditioning channels, and deployment conditions. We present a concept-level probabilistic audit and reporting framework for diffusion models. We formalize governance-relevant concept behaviors as Bernoulli semantic events induced by stochastic generation, and define a Concept Risk Operator that maps model-channel configurations to structured risk profiles, enabling comparison across prompting interfaces, learned embedding channels, models, and recorded conditions. We apply sample-level post-hoc calibration and configuration-level risk aggregation, and show that probability error can change thresholded actions near policy boundaries. Experiments on SD1.5, SD2.1, and SDXL reveal consistent yet non-uniform operational risk patterns across concept families, channels, recorded conditions, and shifted protocols. In particular, embedding-based access and obfuscated prompts expose risks often understated by standard-prompt evaluation. A pooled multi-protocol calibrator improves held-out probability reliability, but we do not claim transfer from a standard-only calibrator. CLRC provides a common audit schema for probabilistic and decision-aware governance of multimedia generation systems.

</details>

### [6] [The 2M Multiplication Algorithm for Complex Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05419)
*Peter Caday*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了2M算法，将复数矩阵乘法从传统的4次实数GEMM（或3M算法的3次）减少到仅需2次同尺寸实数GEMM，适用于整数矩阵；对浮点矩阵则与Ozaki-II方案结合，实现了约等于真实GEMM两倍时间的高性能复数GEMM，并推导出新的SYRK/HERK对称秩k更新算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的复数矩阵乘法需要4次实数GEMM，即使用3M算法也需3次实数GEMM。作者希望进一步减少所需实数矩阵乘法的次数，以提升复数矩阵计算效率。

Method: 将3M算法减少为2M算法：对于实数部分和虚数部分均为整数的矩阵，只需2次同尺寸实数GEMM即可完成复数GEMM，辅以二次时间复杂度的预处理和后处理步骤。对于浮点矩阵，将2M算法与Ozaki-II方案（即基于浮点数拆分的精确矩阵乘法方案）自然结合，构建实用的高性能复数浮点GEMM算法。

Result: 整数矩阵复数GEMM仅需2次实数GEMM；浮点矩阵复数GEMM在大约两倍实数GEMM的时间内完成。同时作为推论，导出了内部使用全尺寸矩形GEMM的对称秩k更新（SYRK/HERK）新算法。

Conclusion: 通过将复数矩阵乘法所需的实数GEMM次数从4次（传统）或3次（3M）进一步降至2次，显著降低了计算复杂度。结合Ozaki-II方案使得浮点场景下也具备实用性，为高性能复数线性代数库提供了新的理论和方法基础。

Abstract: Complex matrix multiplication is typically computed using 4 real matrix multiplications (GEMMs) of the same size. The well-known 3M multiplication algorithm reduces this cost to 3 real GEMMs, together with quadratic time pre- and post-processing steps.
  In this paper, we reduce 3M to 2M for matrices with integer real and imaginary parts, performing complex GEMM with only 2 real GEMMs of the same size, along with quadratic time pre- and post-processing. For floating-point matrices, 2M multiplication combines naturally with the Ozaki-II scheme, yielding a practical, high-performance algorithm for computing a complex floating-point GEMM in roughly twice the time of a real GEMM of the same size. As corollaries, we derive new algorithms for symmetric rank-$k$ updates (SYRK/HERK) that internally use full rectangular GEMMs.

</details>


### [7] [Sustained 70B-Class AWQ Inference on a Single NVIDIA L20: Throughput, Stability, Energy, and Quality Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05420)
*Yin Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本技术报告评估了单张NVIDIA L20 48GB GPU能否承载70B级量化模型的推理服务。在vLLM + AWQ Marlin配置下，Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct-AWQ在24小时10并发负载测试中完成36,740个请求，零失败，维持108.84 tokens/s输出吞吐，能耗7.92 kWh（0.330输出tokens/J）。并发1-16的重复测试均成功。结论支持一个窄范围声明：精心配置的单L20可服务70B级量化端点，但不保证无损质量保持、低延迟或生产级SLA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 70B级开源语言模型的推理服务通常被认为需要80GB加速器、多GPU张量并行系统或厂商托管推理方案。作者希望探索是否存在更轻量、更经济的替代路径——即单块48GB的NVIDIA L20 GPU能否满足实际的量化服务负载需求，从而降低部署门槛和成本。

Method: 作者在单张NVIDIA L20 48GB GPU上使用vLLM 0.8.5.post1和AWQ Marlin推理引擎部署Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct-AWQ模型。设计了固定形状的工作负载（约512输入tokens、256输出tokens），执行了24小时10并发的浸泡测试，同时设置1/4/8/16不同并发的重复运行实验。通过nvidia-smi采样GPU功耗并计算能耗指标，同时评估了MMLU、CMMLU、GSM8K、MT-Bench和8K LongBench等基准的质量表现。

Result: 24小时并发10测试完成36,740/36,740个请求，零失败、零OOM或崩溃。系统达到108.84输出tokens/s，p95首个token时延6.61s，p95端到端时延23.54s。能耗约7.92 kWh，对应0.330输出tokens/J。并发1-16的12/12次重复运行全部成功，并发16下平均127.22±12.68输出tokens/s。质量基准上MMLU 0.8130、CMMLU 0.8309、GSM8K 0.8082，MT-Bench和LongBench子集测试也全部完成。

Conclusion: 证据支持一个狭窄且明确的结论：在固定形状的吞吐导向负载下，精心配置的单张L20 GPU可以有效地服务Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct-AWQ。但该工作不声称AWQ量化无损保留质量、不适用于低延迟交互式场景、不能保证广泛的生产SLA覆盖，也不能与BF16/FP16基线做等价性比较。

Abstract: Serving 70B-class open-weight language models is usually associated with 80GB accelerators, tensor-parallel multi-GPU systems, or vendor-managed inference profiles. This technical report evaluates whether a single NVIDIA L20 48GB GPU can sustain a useful 70B-class quantized serving workload. We measure Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct-AWQ served with vLLM 0.8.5.post1 and AWQ Marlin on one L20. Under a fixed workload of approximately 512 input tokens and 256 output tokens, a 24-hour concurrency-10 soak completed 36,740/36,740 requests with no request failures and no vLLM CUDA OOM, traceback, or killed-process signatures. The system sustained 108.84 output tokens/s, with p95 time-to-first-token of 6.61s and p95 end-to-end latency of 23.54s. GPU-board power sampled through nvidia-smi produced an estimated 7.92 kWh over the run, corresponding to 0.330 output tokens/J and 1.008 total tokens/J. Repeated fixed-shape runs at concurrency 1, 4, 8, and 16 completed 12/12 runs successfully; the concurrency-16 condition averaged 127.22 +/- 12.68 output tokens/s over three runs. The same AWQ endpoint also produced absolute quality scores of 0.8130 on MMLU, 0.8309 on CMMLU, and 0.8082 on GSM8K, plus 80/80 MT-Bench answer generations and a 60-item 8K LongBench subset. The evidence supports a narrow claim: a carefully configured single L20 can serve Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct-AWQ as a throughput-oriented 70B-class endpoint under the tested fixed-shape workload. It does not prove lossless AWQ quality retention, low-latency interactive serving, broad production SLA coverage, or equivalence to a BF16/FP16 baseline.

*Tong Niu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于"Golomb尺子最优性判定"问题的紧凑CNF编码（含O(n²L)个子句），并通过小型实例验证（n≤12）生成机器可核验的LRAT最优性证书。论文还给出了OGR(29, L=757)的编码规模估算，提出了一种面向多核商用硬件的cube-and-conquer并行分解方案，并构建了端到端的证书验证流水线（CaDiCaL → 结构检查 → drat-trim/cake_lpr验证）。若成功，将为已知n≤5之外的任何OGR值提供首个形式化验证的最优性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Golomb尺子最优问题（OGR）是经典组合学基准问题，目前OGR(2)至OGR(28)已通过分布式志愿计算解决，OGR(29)正在计算中（预计2026-2027年完成）。研究者希望提供一种形式化验证的方法来产生可机器核验的最优性证书，填补该领域缺乏形式化证明的空白，并推动OGR(29)的求解。

Method: 论文设计了一种紧凑的CNF编码来表示GR(n,L)判定问题（是否存在阶为n、长度恰为L的Golomb尺子），编码规模为O(n²L)个子句。利用该编码对n≤12的情况进行了小型实例验证扫描，生成LRAT格式的机器可核验最优性证书。作者还给出了OGR(29, L=757)的具体编码规模估计，并提出cube-and-conquer分解策略以支持Mallob风格的多核并行计算。完整的证书流水线包括CaDiCaL求解器（启用LRAT输出）、结构合理性检查、以及由drat-trim或cake_lpr进行的形式化验证。

Result: 论文成功对n≤12的OGR实例进行了验证扫描，输出了可由机器核验的LRAT最优性证书。给出了OGR(29)在L=757时的闭合式编码规模估计，并提出了面向商品多核硬件的cube-and-conquer并行分解方案。整条证书生成与验证流水线是端到端完整的，但尚未完成OGR(29)的实际求解与最优性验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为Golomb尺子最优性问题提供了首个系统性的SAT形式化验证框架，一旦对任何n>5的OGR值完成形式化最优性证明，将开创该领域的先河。未来工作包括利用该框架推进OGR(29)的分布式并行求解并产出正式证书。

Abstract: A Golomb ruler of order~$n$ is an integer set $\{a_0<a_1<\cdots<a_{n-1}\}$ whose $\binom{n}{2}$ pairwise differences $a_j-a_i$ ($i<j$) are all distinct. The optimal Golomb ruler problem asks for $\mathrm{OGR}(n)=\min\{a_{n-1}-a_0\}$ and is a classical combinatorial benchmark. The values $\mathrm{OGR}(2),\dots,\mathrm{OGR}(28)$ are settled through distributed volunteer search (the Distributed.net OGR project); $\mathrm{OGR}(29)$ is in active computation, with verification expected in late 2026 or early 2027. The recent upper bound $\mathrm{OGR}(29)\le 757$ of Lee, Park, and Kim (arXiv:2510.0122, October~2025) tightens the search window. This note describes a compact CNF encoding of the decision problem $\mathrm{GR}(n,L)$ (``is there a Golomb ruler of order~$n$ with length exactly~$L$?'') with $O(n^2 L)$ clauses, together with a small-case verification sweep that emits machine-checkable LRAT certificates of optimality for $\mathrm{OGR}(n)$ at $n\le 12$. We then give closed-form encoding-size estimates for $\mathrm{OGR}(29, L=757)$ and propose a cube-and-conquer decomposition aimed at a Mallob-style parallel run on commodity multi-core hardware. The certificate pipeline (CaDiCaL with --lrat=true, a structural sanity-check, then formal validation by drat-trim or cake\_lpr) is end-to-end. A formally verified optimality proof for any single $\mathrm{OGR}(n)$ value beyond the trivial $n\le 5$ would be a first in the field.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 引入STORE自供应存储架构，自动选择最优存储后端，通过将自供应逻辑提升到平台层面，实现零接触和零配置的供应方式，从而消除开发人员的手动配置负担。

Conclusion: STORE通过平台级的自动化存储供应，有效解决了无服务器计算中存储配置复杂的问题，在显著降低开发成本的同时保持了良好的性能和可扩展性。

Abstract: Serverless computing provides on-demand elasticity, pay-per-use, and simplified deployment. However, serverless functions are typically stateless and depend on external storage services such as object stores or databases to exchange data or support stateful functions. Provisioning and configuring these storage systems still requires manual setup or declarative scripts, introducing complexity, slowing development, and increasing the risk of configuration errors. To address these challenges, in this paper, we introduce \textsc{STORE}, a self-provisioning storage architecture for serverless functions. STORE automatically selects the optimal storage backend and eliminates developer effort through zero-touch and zero-configuration provisioning, achieved by moving the self-provisioning logic to the platform level. Our evaluation results show that STORE reduces implementation effort by up to 84% compared to well-established Infrastructure-as-Code frameworks such as Terraform and Pulumi while maintaining low latency and linear scalability under realistic workloads, without introducing performance overhead.

</details>

*Yuchen Zhu,Longxiang Yin,Wanyu Wang,Jieke Lin,Guoqiang Zou,Zirui Cao,Yuling Yuan,Xiaolan Fan,Lifen Chen,Hao Zheng,Qizhang He,Hongyu Zhou,Chunhai Yu*

TL;DR: 本文针对具身智能计算中感知、计算、控制三任务分离部署导致的时延高、效率低问题，提出基于飞腾FT-2000/4与寒武纪MLU370的国产异构计算平台，采用NPU片上融合部署与全流程算子协同优化策略，在MLU370上实现感知-计算-控制一体化执行。实验表明单帧时延18.7ms，较NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier加速2.89倍，MLU利用率82.6%，精度与主流平台相当。

Conclusion: 该工作为具身智能计算服务的国产化工程应用提供了实用的参考方案。

Abstract: Embodied intelligent computing integrates perception, computation and control. Traditional separate deployment of the three tasks leads to frequent data transmission, high latency and low hardware efficiency, failing to satisfy millisecond-level real-time requirements in dynamic scenarios. Besides, most operator optimization methods rely on foreign GPU platforms, while full-process collaborative optimization for domestic Phytium-Cambricon heterogeneous architectures is insufficient. This paper builds a domestic heterogeneous computing platform with Phytium FT-2000/4 processor and Cambricon MLU370 acceleration card, and proposes an NPU on-chip fusion deployment and full-process operator collaborative optimization strategy for perception, computation and control pipelines. Targeting embodied robot applications, modular optimization is conducted, including MLU hardware adaptation of motion blur correction operators for high-speed imaging, lightweight inference optimization of ViT models, and customized operator development for multi-DOF inverse kinematics solution. An on-chip data closed-loop and pipeline collaboration-based single-card solution is proposed to implement integrated execution of all perception-computation-control tasks on MLU370. Experimental results show that the proposed method achieves a full-process single-frame latency of 18.7 ms and a speedup of 2.89 compared with NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier, with 82.6% MLU utilization and comparable accuracy to mainstream platforms. This work offers a practical reference for domestic engineering applications of integrated embodied intelligent computing services.

</details>


### [11] [Measurement-Driven Diagnosis and Mitigation of Host-CPU Co-location Interference in Single-GPU LLM Serving on a Multi-GPU Server](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05425)
*Guanjie Cheng,Guowei Li,Yingying Wen,Xinkui Zhao,Zhe Liu,Shuiguang Deng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了单GPU LLM推理服务中主机CPU工作负载的共置干扰问题，提出核心路径尾部指数(CPTI)和核心尾部抑制(CTS)指标，并构建了CoTail诊断流程，通过筛选工作负载风险、剖析服务阶段尾部、选择OS级保护策略并验证解码SLO合规性，显著改善了共置场景下的推理服务质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU服务器中的主机CPU在LLM推理期间通常利用不足，共置CPU工作负载可以提高资源利用率，但会严重损害服务质量。现有工作主要改进LLM服务引擎或研究CPU-GPU边界延迟，缺乏关于外部CPU工作负载如何影响服务路径以及操作员应如何选择保护策略的指导。

Method: 作者首先观察并发现主要问题并非GPU内核变慢，而是CPU工作负载放大了GPU工作提交前CPU侧服务阶段的尾部延迟。基于此，提出CPTI和CTS两个指标，构建CoTail测量驱动的诊断流程，包括：筛选工作负载风险、剖析服务阶段尾部特征、选择OS级保护策略、验证解码SLO合规性四个步骤。

Result: 在主要实验设置中，未受保护的nginx共置使吞吐量降低78.8%，TTFT增加429.5%，TPOT增加362.4%。CoTail引导的保护策略使nginx吞吐量最高提升4.4倍，TPOT降低4.5倍。在公共基线部署SLO下，CoTail满足全部12个oracle可行保留用例（对比Always-rt为10/12，Macro-only为11/12），并将RT使用从28减少到22个用例，共租户平均减速从56.65%降至51.21%。

Conclusion: CoTail通过测量驱动的诊断方法，能够准确识别CPU共置干扰对LLM服务路径的真实影响位置（CPU侧尾部而非GPU内核），并提供有效的OS级保护策略选择，在保障推理服务SLO的同时提升了共置工作负载的性能。

Abstract: Host CPUs in GPU servers are often under-used during LLM inference. Co-locating CPU workloads can improve resource use, but it can also seriously hurt serving quality. Existing work mainly improves LLM serving engines or studies CPU-GPU boundary delays. It gives limited guidance on how external CPU workloads affect the serving path and how operators should choose protection policies. This paper studies host-CPU co-location interference in single-GPU LLM serving. We show that the main observed problem is not slower GPU kernels. Instead, CPU workloads amplify long tails in CPU-side serving stages before GPU work is submitted. To capture this effect, we introduce the Core Path Tail Index (CPTI) and Core Tail Suppression (CTS). Based on these metrics, we build CoTail, a measurement-driven diagnostic procedure that screens workload risk, profiles serving-stage tails, selects OS-level protections, and validates decode SLO compliance. In our primary setup, unprotected nginx co-location reduces throughput by 78.8%, increases TTFT by 429.5%, and increases TPOT by 362.4%. CoTail-guided protections improve nginx throughput by up to 4.4x and reduce TPOT by 4.5x. Under a common-baseline deployment SLO, CoTail satisfies all 12 oracle-feasible held-out cases, compared with 10/12 for Always-rt and 11/12 for Macro-only. It also reduces RT usage from 28 to 22 cases and lowers mean co-tenant slowdown from 56.65% to 51.21%.

</details>

*Verner Vlačić*

TL;DR: 本文提出两种新的基于共享的并行化策略（preemptive propagation和ARPOS），并构建了基于异步MPI的框架用于rIC3硬件模型检查器，实验证明相比经典lemma sharing能显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 属性导向可达性（PDR）是常用的硬件模型检查技术，但其并行化效率低下，现有方法（如lemma sharing）随着处理器数量增加可扩展性受限。

Method: 提出两种新的共享式并行化策略：preemptive propagation和ARPOS，并与经典lemma sharing进行对比；开发了基于异步MPI消息传递框架，集成到最先进的rIC3硬件模型检查器中。

Result: 在2025年硬件模型检查竞赛基准上的实验结果表明，preemptive propagation策略相比经典lemma sharing能带来显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: preemptive propagation策略在PDR并行化方面优于经典lemma sharing，为大规模并行硬件验证提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Property Directed Reachability (PDR) is a commonly used technique for automated hardware model checking, yet efficiently parallelizing it remains a significant challenge. Existing approaches, such as lemma sharing, often suffer from limited scalability as processor counts increase. In this work, we present two novel sharing-based parallelization strategies, preemptive propagation and ARPOS, and compare their performance with classical lemma sharing. To this end, we develop an asynchronous MPI-based message passing framework for the state-of-the-art rIC3 hardware model checker. Experimental results on the 2025 Hardware Model Checking competition benchmark demonstrate that our preemptive propagation strategy yields a significant performance boost over classical lemma sharing.

</details>


### [13] [Constella: A Novel Framework for Cost-Efficient Distributed AI Inference in LEO Space Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05427)
*Andrija Stanisic,Milos Gravara,Juan Luis Herrera,Stefan Nastic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Constella框架，利用DNN分割技术在LEO卫星星座中实现分布式AI推理，通过离线资源识别器确定处理器与通信卫星的最优比例，并结合在线分配算法自适应路由数据，在真实卫星数据集上将系统成本降低最多两个数量级、端到端推理延迟降低最多2.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LEO卫星星座的空间数据中心日益受到关注，但异构卫星角色的星座管理面临成本优化挑战：过度配置处理卫星会增加系统成本，配置不足则会限制系统效率，亟需一种能够确定最优星座规模和卫星角色比例的方法。

Method: Constella包含两个核心组件：一是离线资源标识器，用于确定处理器与通信卫星的最优比例；二是在线分配算法，利用星座遥测数据在星座内部及向地面站自适应地路由数据。

Result: 在真实世界卫星数据集和多种复杂场景下的评估表明，与现有方法相比，Constella将系统成本降低最多两个数量级，端到端推理延迟降低最多2.7倍，同时保持不低于81.9%的推理成功率。

Conclusion: Constella通过DNN分割与自适应资源分配，有效解决了LEO卫星星座中AI推理工作负载的成本与效率平衡问题，为实现成本高效的空间数据中心执行AI推理提供了可行的框架方案。

Abstract: Space data centers built from Low-Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite constellations are gaining increasing attention as a scalable computing infrastructure. With access to abundant solar energy and high-throughput optical inter-satellite links, such constellations can run AI workloads directly in orbit, enabling new in-space application types while optimizing existing ones such as Earth observation. However, managing satellite constellations that combine heterogeneous satellite roles introduces a cost optimization challenge. Determining the appropriate constellation size and satellite role ratio for a given workload is challenging, as over-provisioning processing satellites increases system cost, while under-provisioning limits system efficiency. To enable cost-efficient execution of AI inference workloads in such space data centers, we present Constella, a novel framework that leverages DNN splitting for distributed AI inference in LEO satellite constellations. Constella comprises an offline resource identifier that determines the optimal ratio of processor-to-communicator satellites and an online assignment algorithm. The algorithm utilizes constellation telemetry to adaptively route data within the constellation and to ground stations. We evaluate Constella on a real-world satellite dataset across scenarios of increasing complexity. Results demonstrate a reduction in system cost by up to two orders of magnitude and lower end-to-end inference latency by up to 2.7x compared to other approaches, while maintaining no less than 81.9% inference success rate.

</details>


### [14] [Grid Checkpointing: an Innovative Rollback Support for Speculative Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05428)
*Andrea Mazzucchi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"网格检查点"（Grid Checkpointing）的新技术，用于推测性模拟中的应用程序透明增量检查点。该方法将内存更新操作的拦截与内存映射管理完全分离，通过在模拟对象状态所在内存区域上叠加网格布局，无需知道哪些块实际被分配或更新即可实现增量检查点，同时完全避免了记录被访问内存区域的地址。实验结果表明该方法在性能开销上几乎可以忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在推测性模拟中，现有的增量检查点在追踪内存更新时需要记录被访问内存区域的地址，同时需要知道对象状态中哪些块被实际分配或更新，这引入了显著的运行时开销和实现复杂性。

Method: 提出网格检查点技术，核心思想是将内存更新操作的拦截与内存映射管理完全分离。在承载模拟对象状态块的内存区域上叠加网格布局，通过极少的插桩指令追踪模拟对象状态中发生的内存更新，无需知道哪些块真正被分配或更新，也无需记录被触碰内存区域的地址。

Result: 将该方案嵌入到开源软件包PARSIR（并行模拟运行器）中，并进行了实验研究，与其他现有方案进行了对比。结果表明，该方法通过内存更新追踪实现增量检查点，其运行时开销几乎可以忽略不计。

Conclusion: 网格检查点技术提供了一种高效且低开销的应用透明增量检查点方案，在推测性模拟场景中具有明显优势，能够以接近零的运行时成本实现内存更新追踪和增量检查点。

Abstract: We present a new technique for application-transparent incremental checkpointing in speculative simulation, referred to as {\em Grid Checkpointing}. With our proposal, we fully separate the interception of memory update operations from memory-map management. This enables tracing memory updates that occur in the state of a simulation object via an extremely limited set of instrumentation instructions. At the methodological level, our proposal exploits an overlaid grid layout on the memory area used to host the chunks of an object state, and manages concise data for enabling incremental checkpointing with no need for knowing which chunks are really allocated/updated into the object state. Additionally, grid checkpointing fully avoids logging the addresses of the touched memory zones, as instead takes place in other existing proposals. We embedded our solution into the PARSIR (PARallel SImulation Runner) open source package and report the results of an experimental study that compares grid checkpointing with other solutions available in the literature. The results demonstrate that our approach enables incremental checkpointing via memory update tracing with nearly negligible runtime cost.

</details>


### [15] [Accelerating Sparse Linear Solvers in OpenFOAM using RISC-V Vector Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05429)
*Gabriele Ceccolini,Federico Ficarelli,Filippo Barbari,Simone Bnà,Andrea Bartolini*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文在两种不同的RISC-V架构（长向量EPAC加速器和短向量Sophon SG2044 CPU）上，通过使用RISC-V向量指令集优化稀疏矩阵向量乘法（SpMV），并将其集成到自定义平滑器中，实现了对OpenFOAM等传统CFD代码的高效加速。实验结果显示在EPAC上获得6倍加速，在Sophon SG2044上获得1.5倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenFOAM等CFD框架主要依赖MPI进行域分解级并行，对向量/SIMD架构的支持仅局限于编译器自动向量化。此外，OpenFOAM内部矩阵数据格式不适合向量处理器所需的连续内存访问，限制了新兴向量架构上的性能表现。

Method: 作者在长向量EPAC加速器和短向量Sophon SG2044 CPU两种RISC-V平台上，使用RISC-V向量内建函数（intrinsics）优化SpMV，并在运行时将OpenFOAM内部数据转换为向量友好格式，集成到自定义平滑器中。

Result: 在EPAC测试芯片上平滑器获得6倍加速；在Monte Cimone（MCv2）集群的Sophon SG2044处理器上实现1.5倍平滑器加速。

Conclusion: 通过针对RISC-V向量架构的显式优化和数据格式转换，传统CFD代码可以在研究型和商业型新兴硬件上均获得有效加速，验证了向量化改造对遗留科学计算代码的价值。

Abstract: Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) relies heavily on the efficiency of linear solvers based on sparse linear algebra kernels. Widely used frameworks like OpenFOAM exploit parallelism primarily at the domain decomposition level via MPI. Support for vector/SIMD architectures is limited to compiler auto-vectorization. Furthermore, support for such architectures is limited by OpenFOAM's internal matrix data format, which is intrinsically ill-suited for the contiguous memory accesses required for efficient execution on vector processors. In this work, we focused on two very different RISC-V architectures: the prototype long-vector EPAC accelerator and the commercial short-vector CPU Sophon SG2044. On these platforms, we optimized the Sparse Matrix-Vector multiplication (SpMV) using RISC-V vector intrinsics and integrated it into a custom smoother, performing a runtime conversion of internal data into a vector-friendly format. Experimental results on the EPAC test chip show a 6x speedup for the smoother; benchmarks on Monte Cimone (MCv2) cluster with the Sophon SG2044 processor achieve a 1.5x smoother speedup, proving that legacy CFD codes can be effectively accelerated on both research and commercial emerging hardware.

</details>

<details>

Result: 该模型有望成为金融行业拥抱代币化的发展方向，去中心化资产元数据注册表可能成为全球CSD提供的一种新型服务。

Conclusion: 去中心化资产元数据注册表可作为传统CSD功能的延伸，为金融行业在全球范围内推进资产代币化提供可行的基础设施方向。

Abstract: The effort to tokenize non-currency assets faces several hurdles, including the lack of a scalable decentralized computing infrastructure to manage asset-related metadata. While the centralized securities depository model has served the financial industry well for several decades, the vision of tokenization at a global scale requires new infrastructure that enables distributed control while protecting the integrity of asset-related metadata, regardless of where it is stored. In this paper, we discuss the decentralized artifacts metadata registry model for tokenized assets as a possible direction for the financial industry seeking to embrace tokenization. The artifacts metadata registries extend the function of the traditional CSD, and could in fact be a new type of service offered by CSDs around the world.

</details>


### [17] [COAST: Congestion-Aware Start-Time Recommendations for Carbon-Aware HPC Jobs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05443)
*Weibin Feng,Abhishek Dasgupta,Zeynep Duygu Tekler,Sudha Ahuja,Jin Zheng,Xun Jiang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出COAST，一种拥塞感知的HPC作业开始时间推荐机制，通过博弈论建模协调碳感知作业，在不造成时间槽拥塞的前提下减少碳排放，相比碳贪婪策略可降低峰值负载23.8%并保留93%的碳减排收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碳感知的HPC作业无协调地向低碳时段转移会导致推荐开始时间集中在同一时段，造成拥塞并削弱碳减排收益。因此需要一种协调机制来平衡碳节约与作业延迟及拥塞之间的矛盾。

Method: COAST将每个决策批次建模为精确势博弈（exact potential game），通过最佳响应更新收敛到稳定的开始时间推荐，从而在碳节约与作业延迟/拥塞之间取得平衡。

Result: 基于真实HPC能源数据和电网碳强度轨迹的仿真显示：COAST可减少15.4%的碳排放，仅增加1.5小时平均启动延迟；与碳贪婪推荐相比，峰值时间槽负载降低23.8%，同时保留93%的碳减排收益。

Conclusion: 拥塞感知的启动时间协调机制能为灵活的碳感知HPC作业带来显著的碳减排与负载均衡收益，量化验证了协调策略的实际价值。

Abstract: High-performance computing (HPC) workloads consume substantial amounts of electricity, and their carbon emissions vary over time with the carbon intensity of grid electricity. However, uncoordinated shifting of carbon-aware HPC jobs toward low-carbon periods can concentrate recommended start times in the same time slots, creating congestion and eroding the resulting carbon benefits. This paper proposes COAST, a congestion-aware start-time recommendation mechanism that coordinates HPC jobs by balancing carbon savings against additional job delay and congestion in recommended start-time slots. COAST formulates each decision batch as an exact potential game, enabling best-response updates to converge to stable start-time recommendations. Under an idealized realization model that assumes each job can start at its recommended time, we conduct trace-driven simulations using public HPC energy data and historical grid carbon-intensity traces. The results show that COAST reduces estimated carbon emissions by 15.4\% with 1.5 hours of additional average start delay. Compared with carbon-greedy start-time recommendations, COAST reduces peak time-slot load by 23.8\% while preserving 93\% of the achievable carbon savings. These results quantify the potential benefits of congestion-aware start-time coordination for flexible, carbon-aware HPC jobs.

</details>


### [18] [A Three-Layer Caching Architecture for Low-Latency LLM Web Search on Commodity CPU Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05463)
*Ayushman Bhattacharya,Nihal Gazi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了OreoLook（前身为lixSearch），一个基于自动化浏览器代理和LLM推理的开源答案引擎，并提出了一种三层缓存架构来解决会话上下文丢失、重复查询和URL重复嵌入等问题。系统在单台8 vCPU服务器上实现了89.3%的Redis键空间命中率和0.1 ms读延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着OreoLook用户量增长，系统暴露出三个关键性能问题：会话丢失上下文、等价查询触发冗余LLM调用、URL在多个会话中被重复嵌入计算，导致资源浪费和用户体验下降。

Method: 提出三层缓存架构：（1）会话上下文窗口，使用Redis维护最近消息的滚动窗口，超限自动溢出到Huffman压缩的磁盘归档；（2）语义查询缓存，通过嵌入向量的余弦相似度识别改写查询，消除冗余LLM调用；（3）URL嵌入缓存，跨会话去重嵌入计算。另设后台LRU驱逐守护进程，将空闲会话从Redis迁移到磁盘并按需重新载入。

Result: 在单台8 vCPU Intel Cascade Lake服务器（2 GHz、32 GB RAM，30个Hypercorn工作进程，3个容器副本）上部署后，系统实现89.3%的Redis键空间聚合命中率、0.1 ms读延迟，仅1.38 MB内存开销。

Conclusion: 所提出的三层缓存架构能有效缓解会话上下文丢失和冗余计算问题，支持对话在数小时乃至数天后按保留策略恢复，为LLM驱动的答案引擎提供了低成本的缓存优化方案。

Abstract: AI-powered search products such as ChatGPT search, Google's AI Overviews, and Perplexity provide LLM-synthesized answers grounded in live web results. We developed OreoLook (formerly lixSearch), an open-source answer engine using automated browser agents and provider-routed LLM inference. Its local search, caching, session-management, and embedding stack runs on commodity CPU hardware; answer synthesis is performed by a remote inference provider. As usage grew, sessions lost context, equivalent queries triggered redundant work, and URLs were repeatedly embedded across sessions.
  We present a three-layer caching architecture: (1) a Session Context Window maintaining a rolling window of recent messages in Redis with automatic overflow to Huffman-compressed disk archives; (2) a Semantic Query Cache catches rephrasings via cosine similarity on embedding vectors, eliminating redundant LLM invocations; and (3) a URL Embedding Cache that deduplicates embedding computations across sessions. Deployed on a single 8-vCPU Intel Cascade Lake server (2 GHz, 32 GB RAM) running 30 Hypercorn worker processes across three containerized replicas, the evaluated system reported an 89.3% aggregate Redis keyspace hit rate with 0.1 ms read latency and just 1.38 MB of memory overhead. A background LRU eviction daemon migrates idle sessions from Redis to disk and re-hydrates them on demand, enabling conversations that can be resumed hours or days later under the configured retention policy.

</details>


### [19] [Toward Sustainable Distributed LLM Inference: A Systems Synthesis and Research Agenda for an Energy-, Carbon-, and Cache-Aware llm-d Control Plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05565)
*Twinkll Sisodia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述/综合类论文，探讨了LLM推理服务中的可持续性（能耗与碳排放）问题，将文献中关于工作负载、批处理、KV缓存复用、prefill/decode划分、硬件选择等因素的研究结果关联起来，提出为分布式推理控制平面（如llm-d）设计"可持续推理控制平面（SICP）"，将可持续性视为跨模型、阶段、缓存、硬件、区域和时间的控制问题，而非单一"绿色"方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的可持续性问题正从训练阶段转向服务（serving）阶段，生产环境中能耗和碳排放受多种因素影响（工作负载形态、批处理、KV缓存复用、prefill/decode放置、硬件选择、功率状态、区域碳强度、SLO等）。现有文献对这些因素多是分别研究，缺乏系统性的综合关联分析，尤其缺少从分布式推理控制平面工程决策视角的整合。

Method: 本文不提出新的基准结果，而是通过文献综合（synthesis）方法，将已发表的性能、能耗、碳和成本结果按重复出现的"设计模式"分组，基于这些模式为llm-d这类分布式推理控制平面勾画"可持续推理控制平面（SICP）"架构。该控制平面在路由和扩缩时会综合考虑延迟、能耗、碳排、缓存复用、服务成本和质量，同时将TTFT/TPOT SLO作为硬约束。此外提出了基于"SLO满足的goodput/焦耳"和"SLO满足的goodput/克CO2e"的评估框架及可复现实验方案。

Result: 核心发现是：可持续LLM推理不太可能来自单一的"绿色"模型或单一加速器，而是更自然地应被视为一个跨模型、阶段（prefill/decode）、缓存、硬件、副本、区域和时间的控制问题。论文给出了SICP的设计蓝图和基于SLO满足的goodput指标的评估框架。

Conclusion: 从文献综合中得出的主要观察是：要提高LLM推理的可持续性，需要将可持续性作为推理控制平面的一个核心控制目标来系统处理，替代碎片化的单点优化。未来的方向是工程化地实现SICP并利用所提出的评估框架进行系统验证。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) sustainability is increasingly a serving-systems problem, not only a training problem. In production, energy and carbon impact depend on more than model size: workload shape, batching, key-value (KV) cache reuse, prefill/decode placement, model and accelerator choice, power state, geographic carbon intensity, and service-level objectives (SLOs) all matter. Recent systems papers study many of these factors separately. This paper connects those results and asks a practical engineering question: what do they imply when the decision point is a distributed inference control plane such as llm-d?
  The contribution here is synthesis, not a new set of benchmark results. Reported performance, energy, carbon, and cost improvements remain the results of the cited papers and systems. I group the literature into recurring design patterns and use those patterns to sketch a Sustainable Inference Control Plane (SICP) for llm-d. The proposed control plane would consider latency, energy, carbon, cache reuse, serving cost, and quality when routing and scaling, while keeping TTFT/TPOT SLOs as hard constraints. I also outline an evaluation framework based on SLO-satisfied goodput per joule and per gram CO2e, together with a reproducible experimental plan. The main observation from connecting the literature is that sustainable LLM inference is unlikely to come from one "green" model or one accelerator; it is more naturally treated as a control problem across model, phase, cache, hardware, replica, region, and time.

</details>


### [20] [Sharpedo: Dual-Mode Uncertified DAG-Based Consensus Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05580)
*Zeno de Angeli*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Sharpedo，这是首个双模式无证书有向无环图（DAG）共识协议。该协议能够在部分同步网络假设下实现高性能，同时在异步环境下仍能保证进度。Sharpedo融合了Mysticeti和Mahi-Mahi的优点，并引入了一种动态调度机制以提升系统效率和性能优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的共识协议中，Mysticeti在部分同步假设下具有低延迟但缺乏异步鲁棒性，而Mahi-Mahi虽能在异步环境中保证进度但延迟更高。虽然已有双模式协议存在，但没有一个能够充分利用无证书DAG这一新型数据结构来同时获得两种模式的优势。

Method: 研究首先梳理共识协议的发展脉络，分析不同协议如何处理网络假设约束并探索现有的双模式协议。在此基础上，提出了Sharpedo——首个双模式无证书DAG共识协议，集成了新颖的动态调度机制以提升系统效率和性能。

Result: Sharpedo实现了"两全其美"：在部分同步场景下保持类Mysticeti的高性能，同时在异步设置下提供类似Mahi-Mahi的强进度保证，并通过动态调度机制进一步优化系统性能。

Conclusion: Sharpedo填补了双模式协议在无证书DAG数据结构应用上的空白，为共识协议在不同网络条件下的自适应性提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Mysticeti and Mahi-Mahi represent leading approaches to consensus protocols, leveraging a novel uncertified Directed Acyclic Graph data structure to achieve substantial performance benefits compared to prior work. Mysticeti and Mahi-Mahi differ in their underlying network assumptions, where the former makes assumptions about timing bounds for communication while the latter does not. Mysticeti trades robustness for lower levels of latency in ideal conditions, whereas Mahi-Mahi favours increased latency in order to ensure stronger progress guarantees in asynchronous settings. Although dual-mode protocols exist (i.e., protocols that can adapt do different network assumptions), none have been implemented to take full advantage of the novel data structure seen in the aforementioned protocols. Thus, our research project aims to trace the progression of consensus protocols, analyse how they address network assumptions constraints, and explore dual-mode protocols. Then, we present Sharpedo, the first dual-mode uncertified Directed Acyclic Graph consensus protocols which aims to achieve the best of both worlds: the high-performance of partially-synchronous protocols, while providing guarantees of progress even in asynchronous settings. Furthermore, Sharpedo integrates a novel dynamic scheduling mechanism resulting in improved system efficiency and performance optimization.

</details>


### [21] [DejaVu: Unifying Memory Allocations to Eliminate Redundant Copies on Unified-Memory SoCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05635)
*Yuheng Zhu,Yanbo Zhao,Jiajia Li,Man-Ki Yoon*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DejaVu是一个针对统一内存（UMA）边缘平台GPU应用的优化系统，通过安全移除CPU与GPU缓冲区之间冗余的显式内存拷贝来提升性能。它提供了两条路径：DejaVu-SR（源码级LLVM编译优化）和DejaVu-DR（面向闭源二进制的profile引导优化器），在保证程序语义正确的前提下消除不必要的拷贝开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU应用从离散GPU移植到统一内存边缘平台时，仍然继承了"CPU一个缓冲区+GPU另一个缓冲区+执行前后拷贝"的离散GPU内存抽象。但UMA硬件的CPU和GPU共享同一物理DRAM，这些拷贝并未跨越物理边界，浪费了带宽、时间和能量。尽管UMA平台日益普及，由于生产软件栈、库和示例是为跨离散GPU的可移植性而编写的，这一低效模式仍然普遍存在。简单地合并缓冲区不可行，因为程序可能依赖两个缓冲区不同或依赖拷贝来排序CPU和GPU访问。

Method: DejaVu在程序不依赖上述拷贝语义副作用时安全地移除拷贝，沿两条互补路径实现：（1）DejaVu-SR：编译时的LLVM转换，证明安全性并在原地重写被接受的配对；（2）DejaVu-DR：面向闭源部署的profile引导二进制优化器，通过profile验证稳定的分配/拷贝模式，在运行时拦截匹配的调用以合并profile匹配的缓冲区对，同时保留被移除拷贝的排序效果。

Result: 在三个NVIDIA Jetson平台上的七个基准测试中，DejaVu的收益随基线时间中拷贝占比的增加而增长。拷贝密集型负载加速最高达6.9倍，闭源端到端应用加速1.10-1.14倍。源码路径和二进制路径均能达到手动优化可实现性能的99%以上。

Conclusion: DejaVu证明，即使在语义约束下，通过智能地识别并安全移除UMA平台上冗余的CPU-GPU显式拷贝，可以显著提升性能，且不必要求所有软件都有源码可用——通过源码级分析（DejaVu-SR）和二进制级profile引导优化（DejaVu-DR）两条路径，可以覆盖更多实际部署场景，达到接近手动优化的性能水平。

Abstract: GPU applications on unified-memory (UMA) edge platforms often inherit a discrete-GPU memory abstraction in which they allocate one buffer for the CPU, another for the GPU, and copy data between them before and after GPU execution. On UMA hardware these buffers reside in the same physical DRAM, so the copies consume bandwidth, time, and energy without moving data across a physical boundary. Despite the growing adoption of UMA platforms, this pattern remains common because production software stacks, libraries, and samples were written for portability across discrete GPUs. However, removing these copies is not as simple as merging the two buffers, because the original program may rely on the two buffers being distinct, or on the copy itself ordering CPU and GPU accesses.
  DejaVu removes these copies only when the program does not depend on the effects above. It does so along two complementary paths, depending on whether source is available. DejaVu-SR is a compile-time LLVM transformation that proves safety and rewrites accepted pairs in place. DejaVu-DR is a profile-guided binary optimizer for closed-source deployments that profiles and validates stable allocation/copy patterns and, at runtime, intercepts the matching calls to coalesce profile-matched pairs while preserving the ordering effects of removed copies.
  Across seven benchmarks on three NVIDIA Jetson platforms, DejaVu's benefit grows with the fraction of baseline time spent on copies. Copy-dominated workloads speed up by up to 6.9$\times$, closed-source end-to-end applications speed up by 1.10-1.14$\times$. Both source and binary paths achieve $\ge$99% of the performance achievable by manual optimization.

</details>


### [22] [CoCoFL_Continual_Computing_for_Federated_Learning_over_Intermittent_Satellite-Ground_Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05997)
*Yun Shen,Kun Guo,Xi Yang,Yaoqi Liu,Yisheng Zhao,Wei Feng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出CoCoFL框架，在LEO卫星辅助联邦学习中，让未调度设备利用持续的本地计算更新模型并考虑模型陈旧性，通过联合优化设备调度和本地训练轮次，实现了更快的收敛速度和更高的测试精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在卫星辅助联邦学习场景中，星地链路间歇性导致每个可见窗口内只有部分设备参与全局聚合，未调度设备的本地计算和数据资源被闲置浪费；同时，部分设备参与下的数据异构性可能使全局模型产生偏差，恶化学习性能。

Method: 提出基于持续计算的联邦学习框架CoCoFL：调度设备参与全局模型聚合，未调度设备继续更新本地模型并考虑模型陈旧性；基于收敛性分析，在可见窗口相关时间约束下联合优化设备调度策略以及调度/未调度设备的本地训练轮次数量。

Result: 实验结果表明，相较于基线方法，CoCoFL实现了更快的收敛速度、更低的训练损失和更高的测试精度。

Conclusion: 通过充分利用未调度设备的计算资源并合理处理模型陈旧性，CoCoFL有效缓解了部分设备参与带来的数据异构性问题，提升了卫星辅助联邦学习的整体性能。

Abstract: Low earth orbit (LEO) satellite constellations enable geographically distributed ground devices to collaboratively train a global model via federated learning (FL) without sharing raw data, with applications in environmental monitoring and disaster prediction. However, in satellite-assisted FL scenarios, intermittent satellite-ground links allow only a subset of devices to participate in global aggregation within each visibility window, leaving unscheduled devices idle and their local computational and data resources underutilized. Under partial device participation, data heterogeneity among devices may bias the global model toward certain devices, thereby deteriorating learning performance. In this regard, we propose a continual computing based federated learning framework, referred to as CoCoFL, in which scheduled devices participate in the global model aggregation, while unscheduled devices continue updating their local models taking into account model staleness. Guided by the convergence analysis of CoCoFL and subject to visible-window-related time constraints, we jointly optimize the device scheduling and the number of local epochs for scheduled and unscheduled devices. Experimental results demonstrate that CoCoFL achieves faster convergence, lower training loss, and higher test accuracy compared with baselines.

</details>

*Xiaosong Chen,Shaoheng Nie,Zhongmin Zhao,Zizhao Mo,Jiapeng Chen,Huanle Xu,Zeren Li,Weiwei Sun,ChengZhong Xu*

TL;DR: Poseidon是一个面向异构加速器集群的大语言模型高效训练框架，通过有向无环图（DAG）构建显式训练时间模型，并采用阶段级剪枝（early stopping + 部分估计）和基于脊状分布模式的层到阶段映射两种理论支持的高效策略，在不牺牲最优训练效率的前提下大幅缩小配置搜索空间。实验表明，在异构集群上相比现有最优系统，训练吞吐量最高提升2.76倍。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出Poseidon框架，核心是基于有向无环图（DAG）的显式训练时间模型。在DAG基础上，采用两种高效且具有理论基础的策略：(1) 通过早期停止和部分估计进行阶段级剪枝；(2) 利用脊状分布模式进行层到阶段的映射。这两种策略有效缩减搜索空间，同时不牺牲最优训练效率。

Result: 在异构加速器集群上的实验表明，Poseidon相比现有最优系统，训练吞吐量最高可提升2.76倍。

Conclusion: Poseidon通过精确的训练时间建模和高效的搜索策略，有效解决了异构集群上LLM预训练的并行化优化难题，在减少搜索成本的同时实现了显著的训练吞吐量提升。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of accelerator technologies, pre-training large language models (LLMs) on heterogeneous accelerator clusters has become increasingly crucial for maximizing hardware utilization. Existing systems, however, suffer from inaccurate training time modeling, which undermines the parallelization optimizations built upon it. Moreover, for current approaches, the vast configuration search space makes exhaustive exploration infeasible, forcing a trade-off between search time and training efficiency.
  To overcome these limitations, we introduce Poseidon, an efficient and scalable LLM training framework designed with heterogeneity awareness. Its core is an explicit training time model based on a directed acyclic graph. Building on this graph, Poseidon employs two efficient, theoretically grounded strategies: stage-level pruning via early stopping with partial estimation, and layer-to-stage mapping exploiting a ridge-like distribution pattern. These strategies reduce the search space without sacrificing optimal training efficiency. Experiments on heterogeneous clusters show that Poseidon improves training throughput by up to $2.76\times$ over state-of-the-art systems.

</details>


### [24] [Diamond Agent: Agentic Control of Federated HPC Resources as a Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06181)
*Haotian Xie,Junlin Chen,Mingkai Zheng,Yifan Zhu,Minu Mathew,Max Burnette,Yadu Babuji,Volodymyr Kindratenko,Shivaram Venkataraman,Kyle Chard,Ian Foster,Zhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文设计了Diamond Agent，一个基于typed skills接口的智能代理系统，用于在异构集群间实现HPC工作流的智能执行，通过统一跨站点资源发现、数据移动、任务执行和结果检索，实现了跨多个超算中心的协作调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构集群间高效聚合和编排计算能力面临四大实际挑战：跨独立管理集群的工作流上下文保持、站点间大数据集移动、站点特定环境与调度策略推理，以及利用实时队列和资源状态进行高效任务调度。现有系统难以统一解决这些问题。

Method: Diamond Agent采用智能代理架构，以typed skills作为接口，提供代理端工作区，统一跨站点资源发现、资源规格化、数据移动、任务执行和结果检索。一个中心化Diamond Agent实例可操作多台超算而无需在每个登录节点部署。它将高层代理动作翻译为站点特定执行，通过Globus Transfer移动数据，利用实时系统能力和队列信息选择可行部署位置，并通过事件驱动续接机制解耦代理动作与长时间批处理任务。

Result: 实验基于27小时遥测数据和19轮多站点匹配提交（包含83个作业，覆盖四台生产超算），相比固定站点基线，Diamond Agent将相对于最快观测放置位置的中位额外完成时间从42秒降至4秒，实现了10.5倍的改进。

Conclusion: Diamond Agent通过智能代理架构有效解决了跨异构集群HPC工作流执行中的上下文保持、数据移动、环境推理和实时调度四大挑战，显著提升了任务调度的效率与灵活性。

Abstract: Efficiently aggregating and orchestrating computing power across heterogeneous clusters for HPC workflows faces four practical challenges: preserving workflow context across independently administered clusters, moving large datasets between sites, reasoning about site-specific environments and scheduler policies, and exploiting live queue and resource states for efficient task scheduling. To this end, we design Diamond Agent, an agentic system that enables intelligent execution of HPC workflows across heterogeneous clusters with typed skills as the interface. Diamond Agent provides an agent-facing workspace and skills that unify cross-site resource discovery, resource specification, data movement, task execution, and result retrieval. A centralized Diamond Agent instance can operate multiple supercomputers without being deployed separately on each login node. Diamond Agent translates high-level agent actions into valid site-specific executions, moves data through Globus Transfer, and uses live system capability and queue information to select feasible placements. Its event-driven continuation mechanism decouples agent actions from long-running batch jobs: persistent services monitor remote execution and resume the agent only when a result or decision-relevant event is available. We experiment with 27 hours of telemetry and 19 matched multi-site submission rounds comprising 83 jobs across four production supercomputers. Compared with a fixed-site baseline, Diamond Agent reduces the median additional completion time relative to the fastest observed placement from 42 seconds to 4 seconds, a 10.5x reduction.

</details>


### [25] [Water-network decisions share one hydraulic gradient, and it can now be computed exactly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06323)
*Tianwei Mu,Yue Wang,Mingzhe Yuan,Wenhong Wang,Qing Luo,Min Xiao,Jun Li,Hui Yang,Manhong Huang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种使水分配管网(WDN)的全局梯度算法可完全微分的方法，正向传播复现参考引擎的离散器件与状态切换，反向传播通过复用正向传播的终端分解求解隐式伴随方程，从而一次稀疏求解即可同时获得所有参数的梯度，并在GPU上跨场景批处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水分配管网的标定、泄漏定位和传感器布置本质上是连续参数的决策问题，但传统的液压引擎只返回解而不提供导数，导致实践中只能依赖无导数搜索或代理模型，且代理模型的误差会传递给最终结果。

Method: 将全局梯度算法本身构建为精确可微分：正向传播完全复现参考引擎的离散设备、状态切换和低流量线性化；反向传播通过复用正向传播的终端分解来求解隐式伴随方程，一次额外的稀疏求解即可同时得到所有参数的梯度，并支持在单个GPU上跨场景批处理。

Result: 在52个公开、合成及运营网络和8,140个仿真帧上，所有网络均达到验收标准；与EPANET 2.2的最大水头偏差为1.137e-13英尺，其中25个网络完全一致。一次伴随求解替代了905管道L-TOWN基准上有限差分粗糙度雅可比所需的906次仿真；泄漏反演训练循环在256个场景下每个优化步仅需463-470毫秒，比先前流程快191倍。梯度标定在中位595次模型调用内达到终点，而五种调参元启发式方法中最强者需要8,060次才能匹配训练损失，其中两种在20,000次内无法做到。

Conclusion: 该方法使梯度标定在大规模水分配管网上高效可行，并能逐管道审计既有传感器对粗糙度参数的约束能力及应补充的传感器位置，直接用于公用事业当前运营的模型。

Abstract: Calibration, leak localisation and sensor placement on water distribution networks (WDNs) are decisions about continuous parameters, yet the hydraulic engine that defines the physics returns a solution and no derivatives, so practice falls back on derivative-free search or on surrogates whose error the answer inherits. We make the global gradient algorithm itself exactly differentiable: the forward pass reproduces the reference engine's discrete devices, status switching and low-flow linearisation included, and the backward pass solves the implicit adjoint by reusing the forward pass's terminal factorisation, so one extra sparse solve returns every parameter's gradient at once, batched over scenarios on one graphics processor. Across 52 public, synthetic and operational networks and 8,140 simulation frames, every network meets the acceptance criterion, the largest head deviation from EPANET 2.2 is 1.137e-13 ft and 25 agree exactly. One adjoint solve replaces the 906 simulations a finite-difference roughness Jacobian costs on the 905-pipe L-TOWN benchmark, and a leak-inversion training loop runs at 463-470 ms per optimiser step for 256 scenarios, 191 times the prior pipeline. Gradient calibration reaches its endpoint within a median 595 model calls, where the strongest of five tuned metaheuristics needs 8,060 to match it on the training loss and two never do within 20,000. On a 554-link operating network, one adjoint pass audits, pipe by pipe, which roughness parameters the installed sensors can constrain and which sensors to add, on the model the utility already operates.

</details>


### [26] [Cops and Lethal Robber on Rings: A Distributed Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06388)
*Amanpreet Singh Saini,Ashish Saxena,Kaushik Mondal*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在分布式环境下引入了一种"致命强盗"模型，强盗拥有无限速度且能杀死进入其所在顶点的警察，作者在静态环上证明了n个警察不够用，并提出了一个需要n+⌊log n⌋+4个警察的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Cops and Robber游戏主要在顺序设置中研究围捕强盗的问题，但在分布式设置中强盗被赋予更强能力（可杀死警察）的场景尚未被研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，并将相关工作与Intruder Capture和Black Hole Search问题联系起来。

Method: 作者定义了"致命强盗"（lethal robber）及"警察与致命强盗"问题，在分布式设置下的静态环（大小为n）上进行研究。强盗在奇数轮移动且具有无限速度，警察在偶数轮移动，进入强盗所在顶点的警察会被杀死。作者证明了在最坏情况下n个警察不足以完成任务（即使从同一顶点出发），并设计了一个从任意初始配置出发、最坏情况需要n+⌊log n⌋+4个警察的算法。

Result: 在n大小的静态环上，证明了即使所有警察从同一顶点出发，n个警察也不足以完成对致命强盗的包围；提出了一个算法，从任意初始配置出发，最坏情况下需要n+⌊log n⌋+4个警察即可完成任务。

Conclusion: 本文首次在分布式设置中研究了具有动态性和致命性的强盗模型，给出了环上的理论下界（n个不够），并构造性地证明了n+⌊log n⌋+4个警察的上界，为后续在其他拓扑结构上的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: The Cops and Robber game is extensively studied in the sequential setting where the main goal is to capture the robber. Capturing the robber means at least one cop, and the robber will be at the same vertex together at some time. There are several variants, including variants where the goal of the cops is to surround the robber. Surrounding the robber means there is at least one cop in each of the neighboring vertices of the robber's position. In this paper, we introduce it in the distributed setting while empowering the robber by saying it can even kill cops. Specifically, in our model, the robber moves in odd rounds and has unbounded speed, cops move in even rounds, and if one or more cops move into a vertex where the robber is currently residing, all these cops get killed. We call this {\it lethal robber}. This also connects our work to the Intruder Capture and Black Hole Search problems by introducing an entity which is dynamic as well as lethal, a setting that, to the best of our knowledge, has not been studied.
  In this work, we introduce the lethal robber, define the {\it cops and lethal robber} problem in the distributed setting and study it on a static ring of size $n$. We prove $n$ cops are not enough, even if all start from the same vertex, and provide an algorithm starting from an arbitrary initial configuration that requires $n+\lfloor\log n \rfloor+4$ cops in the worst case.

</details>


### [27] [Sharing a Fabric with Collective Communication: Two Storage Penalties in Deep Learning Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06506)
*Chen Wang,Wenzhao Wu,Hyojin Kim,Jae-Sung Yeom*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了HPC系统上分布式深度学习训练中NCCL/RCCL集合通信与并行文件系统I/O共享网络织物所带来的性能问题，并提出通过DYAD节点本地NVMe暂存方案消除这两种影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在HPC系统上进行分布式深度学习训练时，集合通信和存储I/O共享同一网络织物，会导致DataLoader停顿和通信竞争两类性能损失，影响训练效率。

Method: 在Slingshot-11系统上使用真实的GNN训练负载进行实验分析，识别两类性能损失及其机制，并通过DYAD实现节点本地NVMe暂存，将存储I/O从共享网络上分离以消除这两类影响。

Result: DYAD相比直接Lustre读取实现7.4倍加速，相比VAST实现1.06倍加速；第二epoch缓存完全预热后DataLoader停顿完全消除，实现1.31倍于VAST的加速。Lustre I/O可导致all-reduce延迟高达145倍。

Conclusion: 节点本地NVMe暂存（DYAD）通过将存储I/O移出共享网络织物路径，能够同时消除DataLoader停顿和集合通信竞争两类性能损失，显著提升分布式深度学习训练效率。

Abstract: Distributed DL training on HPC systems often shares one network fabric between NCCL/RCCL collective communication and parallel-filesystem I/O. Using a real GNN training workload on a Slingshot-11 system, we show that this sharing imposes two distinct costs. The primary cost is heavy-tailed DataLoader stalls: the typical DataLoader wait is just 15 ms at steady state, yet spikes to multiple seconds in 28% of Lustre iterations and 12% of VAST iterations. The secondary cost is traffic-class contention on collective communication: Lustre I/O stalls the all-reduce by up to 145$\times$ in an isolated benchmark. The two costs arise from different mechanisms. I/O stall latency affects any storage path that traverses the shared fabric, whereas all-reduce network contention occurs only when storage and collective communication share the same traffic class. Their common root cause is that storage I/O traverses the shared fabric. This work shows that node-local NVMe staging via DYAD (Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/flux-framework/dyad) eliminates both effects by keeping storage I/O off that path. Across a full training epoch, DYAD achieves a 7.4 times speedup over direct Lustre reads and a 1.06 times speedup over VAST. By the second epoch, once the local cache is fully warmed, DataLoader stalls are eliminated entirely, allowing DYAD to reach a 1.31 times speedup over VAST.

</details>


### [28] [EStream: Fast and Memory-Efficient MoE Prefill through Expert Virtualization on Mobile NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06551)
*Junming Zhang,Zhenzhe Zheng,Fan Wu,Xiaoyao Huang,Jie Wu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: EStream是一个让混合专家（MoE）模型能在移动NPU上高效运行的系统。它通过"单一编译专家图+运行时绑定"和"专家虚拟化+硬件感知配置"两大机制，解决了MoE动态路由与NPU静态编译不匹配、以及模型专家超出手机内存容量的问题，在商用骁龙手机上实现了2.25-27.57倍纯预填充TTFT加速和最多12.29倍峰值物理内存降低，并支持高达467亿参数的MoE模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动端LLM部署（如预填充服务）需求日益增长，但现有系统主要依赖稠密模型而非能力更强的MoE模型。根本原因是：MoE预填充与移动NPU不兼容——NPU图在编译时固定，而MoE在运行时才选择专家；且一次请求几乎触发所有专家，超出手机内存容量。这导致更强大的MoE模型在移动端被严重低效利用。

Method: EStream包含三个核心技术：(1) 单一编译的专家图服务所有专家，每个专家的路由token和权重地址在调用时绑定，使MoE动态执行完全在NPU上完成，无需填充、CPU或GPU回退；(2) 专家虚拟化：将专家池存放在UFS闪存中，通过固定大小的NPU可寻址arena按组换入换出，加载与计算重叠，使内存受限于arena而非整个模型；(3) 硬件感知配置算法：自动配置UFS-NPU流水线，最大化加载-计算重叠。

Result: 在商用骁龙手机上，对3个7B-16B MoE模型和256-4096 token提示共18种对比设置下，相比各设置下最快基线，EStream实现2.25-27.57倍纯预填充TTFT加速，峰值物理内存降低1.19-12.29倍，并支持扩展到高达46.7B参数的MoE模型。

Conclusion: EStream证明了通过将NPU的固定编译属性与MoE的运行时决策进行解耦，并为专家权重引入闪存虚拟化机制，可以使大规模MoE模型在移动NPU上高效运行，大幅突破移动端AI的内存和计算瓶颈，为移动端部署更先进的MoE模型铺平了道路。

Abstract: Mobile vendors and application developers increasingly deploy LLMs on smartphones for diverse prefill-only services. Yet current systems rely mainly on dense models whose regular computation maps efficiently to mobile NPUs, leaving more capable MoEs underused. MoE prefill does not fit mobile NPUs: NPU graphs are fixed at compile time, yet MoE picks experts at runtime; and one request touches most experts, more than a phone can hold in memory. We present EStream, which resolves both by separating what the NPU must fix from what MoE decides at runtime. A single compiled expert graph serves every expert, with each expert's routed tokens and weight address bound at call time, so dynamic MoE execution runs entirely on the NPU without padding or CPU/GPU fallback. Expert virtualization keeps the expert pool in UFS flash storage and pages it through a fixed-size NPU-addressable arena, group by group, with loading hidden behind computation, so memory is bounded by the arena rather than by the model. It further introduces a hardware-aware configuration algorithm that automatically configures the UFS--NPU pipeline and maximizes loading--computation overlap. Across 18 comparative settings covering three 7B--16B MoEs and 256--4,096-token prompts, we evaluate EStream on a commercial Snapdragon smartphone. Compared to the fastest baseline at each setting, EStream achieves a 2.25--27.57X pure-prefill TTFT speedup and reduces peak physical memory by 1.19--12.29X. EStream further scales to MoE models with up to 46.7B parameters.

</details>


### [29] [Gutenberg: Taming Latency-Critical Cloud Services with Near-Data-Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06691)
*Qiushi Lin,Phillip B. Gibbons,Jovan Stojkovic,Yiwei Zhao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Gutenberg是一个面向可变、延迟敏感的云服务的CPU+近数据处理（NDP）混合系统，通过CPU端delta缓冲暂存子页更新避免写时全页同步复制，并通过在线控制器联合决策页面放置、复制、CPU/NDP执行和路由，在TailBench基准上将平均和p99延迟分别降低达80.4%和85.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延迟敏感的云服务对内存带宽和隔离性有高要求，现有NDP系统通过缓存和复制改善局部性，但写操作使副本维护成本高昂，且不均衡的计算和内存流量会使少数NDP单元过载、增加尾延迟。现有面向吞吐量的调度器无法充分应对这些共存云服务的挑战。

Method: Gutenberg采用三项核心技术：(1) CPU端delta缓冲暂存子页更新，使热写页面无需急切全页同步即可保持复制；(2) 引入CPU辅助核在NDP执行或副本维护成本高时协助请求执行；(3) 在线控制器利用访问模式、队列压力和先前决策反馈，联合决定页面放置、复制、CPU/NDP执行和路由，同时提供服务间隔离与公平资源分配。此外，对CPU-NDP协调协议进行了模型检查以验证正确性。

Result: 在ZSim配合Ramulator校准内存时序的TailBench平台上评估，跨多个服务Gutenberg相比先前系统将平均延迟和p99延迟分别降低最多80.4%和85.8%，同时改善隔离性和公平性，并能适应变化的负载行为。

Conclusion: Gutenberg通过将delta缓冲与CPU辅助核结合粗粒度的在线联合控制器，有效解决了共享NDP系统中写复制开销、计算/内存流量不均衡和尾延迟问题，展现了在延迟敏感的云服务场景下CPU+NDP协同架构的潜力。

Abstract: Latency-critical cloud services place growing pressure on memory while requiring isolation, fairness, and predictable QoS. Near-data processing (NDP) reduces data movement by executing requests close to memory, and prior systems further improve locality through caching and replication. However, writes make replica maintenance expensive, while uneven compute and memory traffic can overload a few NDP units and increase tail latency. Existing throughput-oriented schedulers do not fully address these challenges for co-located cloud services.
  We present Gutenberg, a CPU+NDP for mutable, latency-critical cloud services. Gutenberg stages subpage updates in a CPU-resident delta buffer, allowing hot writable pages to remain replicated without eager full-page synchronization. It also adopts CPU helper cores to assist request execution when NDP execution or replica maintenance becomes costly. An online controller jointly decides page placement, replication, CPU/NDP execution, and routing using access patterns, queue pressure, and feedback from prior decisions. The system further enforces isolation and fair resource allocation across services. We also model-check CPU--NDP coordination protocol for correctness.
  We evaluate on TailBench using ZSim with Ramulator-calibrated memory timing. Across evaluated services, Gutenberg outperforms prior systems, reducing average and p99 latency by up to 80.4% and 85.8%. It also improves isolation and fairness while adapting to changing workload behaviors.

</details>


### [30] [ForgeStencil: Automating Per-Case Stencil Specialization from Kernels to 100+ Real Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06694)
*Yaojian Chen,Yuxuan Li,Wubing Wan,Lin Gan,Guangwen Yang,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ForgeStencil提出了一种基于代码合成代理的"每案例特化"方法：Kernel Agent为每个具体的stencil形状、精度和应用场景合成专用的CUDA内核，App Agent则在100多个真实应用中定位热点、重写应用结构并集成验证。实验表明纯stencil替换贡献了少数加速，大部分提速来自结构性和主机侧重写；在A100上f32几何平均加速2.35x，端到端应用中位数1.41x，且所有失败案例均被如实记录而非虚报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有stencil DSL、代码生成器和自动调优器追求通用性——用单一人工方法应对所有情况，仅在微基准上验证，因为每案例特化成本过高。但最快的stencil内核因形状、精度和宿主应用而异，通用方法难以在各场景下达到最优性能。

Method: 利用代码合成代理（Code-synthesis agents）降低特化成本，采用双代理架构：1) Kernel Agent为每个配置合成CUDA内核，生成专用的算子矩阵；2) App Agent定位热点、重写应用结构、逐个变更进行验证和集成。所有结果通过测量完整性测试框架校验，防止虚报加速。

Result: 在A100上，合成的f32内核相对每案例SOTA基线达到2.35x几何平均加速（fp16为1.95x）；在100多个真实工业/科学代码上端到端应用中位数加速为1.41x。值得注意的是，大部分加速来自结构性重写而非纯stencil替换，且加速幅度与基线已调优程度负相关。

Conclusion: 每案例特化在代码合成代理的成本下降后已成为可行且高效的范式，其收益来源于针对具体场景的专门优化而非通用方法复用。同时，该工作强调了测量完整性和诚实报告的重要性——失败的案例被记录为拒绝或降级而非虚报为加速。

Abstract: On modern GPUs the fastest stencil kernel depends on the stencil's shape, precision, and host application, and a kernel tuned for one case is rarely fastest for another. Stencil DSLs, code generators, and autotuners instead pursued generality: a single human-authored method reused across cases and validated mainly on microbenchmarks, because per-case specialization was too costly to scale. ForgeStencil starts from the opposite assumption. Code-synthesis agents have reduced that cost enough to build a fresh solution for each case and deploy it end-to-end in real software. A Kernel Agent synthesizes CUDA and forges a per-configuration matrix of specialized operators that matches or exceeds the strongest publicly available state-of-the-art (SOTA) baseline for each case. An App Agent extends the principle to whole applications: it locates hotspots, rewrites application structure, and validates and integrates each change across 100+ real industrial and scientific codes. Most of the measured speedup comes from structural and host-side rewrites, with pure stencil replacement in the minority; the gain also correlates negatively with how well the baseline was already tuned, consistent with gains coming from specialization rather than generic reuse. Every result is checked by a measurement-integrity harness that turns an overstated speedup into a system-level error. The forged kernels reach a same-precision f32 geometric mean of 2.35x against the per-case SOTA baselines (fp16 gains, 1.95x, disclosed separately; A100), and the end-to-end application median is 1.41x across 100 codes, all against same-architecture GPU baselines with program-provided validation and timing. Of 116 candidates, every one that failed the correctness, measurement, or speedup criteria was recorded as rejected or downgraded instead of written up as a speedup.

</details>


### [31] [Don't Be Afraid to Die: Black Hole Search in Dynamic Graphs with Fewer Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06850)
*Kass Bileski,Avery Miller*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究动态图网络中黑洞定位问题，将所需代理数量从2δ_BH+17降低至2δ_BH+3，几乎匹配已知下界2δ_BH+1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在1-有界1-区间连通的动态图中定位黑洞是一个关键的分布式计算问题。此前已有工作给出了2δ_BH+17个代理的充分条件，而下界为2δ_BH+1，两者之间存在较大差距，作者希望缩小这一间隙。

Method: 通过改进现有算法的代理调度策略和探索协议，优化了代理在分散配置下的协作与信息共享机制，从而在保持安全性的前提下大幅减少所需代理数量。

Result: 证明了2δ_BH+3个代理即足以解决该问题，将已有结果从2δ_BH+17显著改进，逼近2δ_BH+1的理论下界。

Conclusion: 这一结果几乎匹配下界，表明在1-有界1-区间连通动态图中定位黑洞所需的代理数量本质上已被确定，差距仅为常数2。

Abstract: We consider a team of synchronous mobile agents operating in a port-labeled network. There is one node in the network, called a black hole, that permanently destroys any agent that visits the node. The team of agents must safely locate the black hole, i.e., at least one agent must survive, terminate its algorithm at a node adjacent to the black hole, and output the port number that leads to the black hole from its current position. In the setting where the network is a 1-bounded 1-interval connected dynamic graph, Kaur et al. (SSS 2025) showed that a team consisting of $2δ_{BH}+17$ agents is sufficient to solve the task from a scattered configuration, where $δ_{BH}$ denotes the degree of the black hole node. We show that $2δ_{BH}+3$ agents are sufficient, nearly matching the $2δ_{BH}+1$ lower bound provided in Kaur et al. (ICDCN 2025).

</details>


### [32] [Unified AI Gateway: A Framework for Joint Model Routing and KV Cache Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06940)
*Jiaxun Lu,Xiang Zhang,Yunfeng Shao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出"统一AI网关"（Unified AI Gateway）系统架构，作为边缘部署的AI流量枢纽，在请求时协同完成模型路由、KV缓存管理和计算放置决策，并通过后台缓存管理优化KV缓存的放置、复制、检索和生命周期，以降低LLM推理中多模型选择带来的集成成本和缓存重建成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理日益涉及多种规模、能力、价格和提供商不同的模型，给开发者带来两类成本：一是多模型间选择与切换的集成成本；二是模型不可用或不兼容时重建KV缓存的推理成本。

Method: 提出统一AI网关的系统设置，在请求时联合选择目标模型、执行位置和KV缓存动作（受任务质量、延迟、成本和资源约束），同时后台执行缓存管理动作优化KV缓存的放置、复制、检索和生命周期。综合现有关于KV缓存重用、压缩、跨模型映射、分布式存储和迁移的证据，分析将其集成到一个系统中的挑战。基于八种典型工作负载进行工作负载级分析模拟。

Result: 在八种典型工作负载下，工作负载级分析模拟显示TTFT（首令牌时间）加速1.25倍至13.28倍，输入成本效益提升1.20倍至6.16倍。

Conclusion: 统一AI网关有望通过系统级协同优化模型路由与KV缓存管理，显著降低多模型推理场景下的延迟和成本，但将各类KV缓存技术能力整合为统一系统仍面临诸多挑战。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference increasingly spans models that differ in size, capability, price, and provider. This shift creates two costs for developers. One is the integration cost of choosing among and switching between many models. The other is the inference cost of rebuilding a KV cache when it is unavailable or incompatible with the selected model. We define and analyze the Unified AI Gateway as a system setting for an edge-deployed AI traffic hub. It coordinates model routing, KV cache management, and compute placement across end devices, edge resources, and cloud model services. At request time, the gateway jointly selects a target model, an execution site, and a KV cache action under task-quality, latency, cost, and resource constraints. In parallel, background cache-management actions optimize KV cache placement, replication, retrieval, and lifecycle decisions for subsequent requests. We synthesize existing evidence on KV cache reuse, compression, cross-model mapping, distributed storage, and transfer, and discuss the remaining challenges of integrating these capabilities into one system. Across eight typical workload profiles, our workload-level analytical simulation reports TTFT speedups of 1.25$\times$--13.28$\times$ and input-cost benefits of 1.20$\times$--6.16$\times$.

</details>


### [33] [TreeRedux: Separating Concerns in Spark's Distributed Tree Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06982)
*David A. G. Harrison,Ivan Cao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出TreeRedux，一个最小化的Apache Spark扩展，通过在executor上执行终端finalize操作，将聚合状态U映射为紧凑结果V，从而避免将大量中间聚合状态物化到driver上，解决了大规模树聚合中driver内存瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Apache Spark的树聚合原语默认将树根放在driver上，使得driver需要像executor一样参与中间聚合状态的计算。对于大规模聚合任务，中间聚合状态可能远大于最终结果（如精确分位数计算和重元素识别），导致driver面临巨大的计算和内存压力。即使新版Spark可将根移至executor，完成后的聚合结果仍需返回并在driver上物化，未能根本解决问题。

Method: TreeRedux添加了一个终端finalize操作，此操作在executor上执行，将聚合状态U映射为紧凑结果V，使得V而非可能很大的U被物化在driver上。这是一个最小扩展，只增加了最终处理步骤。

Result: 在精确分位数计算中，TreeRedux应用于GK Select后消除了driver的epsilon-n内存项，使driver内存需求降至与Spark GK Sketch相同渐近阶。实验中，默认GK Select在25亿元素时遭遇driver OOM；Spark的executor侧最终聚合选项将上限扩展至约160-180亿元素但仍需driver物化最终聚合状态；Redux Select在280亿元素时仍未出现driver OOM。TreeRedux还使Space-Saving sketch支持比在driver上物化完整sketch的最大配置大32倍的容量。

Conclusion: TreeRedux通过将finalize操作移出driver，有效消除了driver在处理大规模聚合时的内存瓶颈，显著提升了对内存敏感型聚合算法的可扩展性，且实现开销极小。

Abstract: By default, Apache Spark's tree aggregation primitives place the tree root on the driver, requiring the driver to participate in the same aggregation computation over intermediate aggregation state as executor nodes. For large aggregates, this can expose the single coordinator to substantial computation and memory requirements.
  Recent Spark versions optionally move the root to an executor, but the completed aggregate must still be returned to and materialized on the driver. We demonstrate this limitation using exact quantile computation and heavy-hitter identification, where the intermediate aggregation state can be substantially larger than the desired final result.
  We propose TreeRedux, a minimal extension that adds a terminal finalize operation, executed on an executor, that maps the aggregation state U to a compact result V, so that V rather than the potentially large U is materialized on the driver.
  For exact quantile computation, applying TreeRedux to GK Select removes the driver's epsilon-n memory term, reducing driver memory requirements to the same asymptotic order as Spark's GK Sketch. In our experiments, the default GK Select implementation encountered a driver out-of-memory error at 2.5 billion elements. Spark's executor-side final aggregation option extended this limit to approximately 16-18 billion elements but still required the final aggregation state to be materialized on the driver. Redux Select completed through 28 billion elements without a driver out-of-memory error. TreeRedux allowed Space-Saving sketches with up to 32x the capacity of the largest configuration that materializes a full sketch on the driver.

</details>

*Xuetao Chen,Cong Ma,Xiangyu Meng,Du Wu,Zhengyang Bai,Tao Luo,Zhaorui Zhang,Emmanuel Jeannot,Edgar Josafat Martinez Noriega,Xun Wang,Peng Chen,Amelie Chi Zhou,Mohamed Wahib*
