TL;DR: 本文提出edgeFBP，一种面向Nvidia Jetson SoC设备的轻量级CT重建框架，通过端到端流水线设计和混合精度策略（利用半精度Tensor Cores加速反投影核），在功耗与内存受限的边缘设备上实现高效图像重建，相比RTK库在Jetson Nano和AGX上分别获得1.83倍和2.56倍加速，且在25瓦功率预算下能量效率比DGX A100高5-48倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT成像在边缘设备上执行面临计算能力、内存容量和能耗预算的严重限制，如何在资源受限的边缘设备上高效完成CT重建是一个亟待解决的关键问题。

Method: edgeFBP采用端到端流水线设计进行高效的核外图像重建，并利用混合精度策略，借助半精度Tensor Cores加速计算瓶颈——反投影（back-projection, BP）内核。

Result: 在Jetson Nano上相比RTK库实现1.83倍加速，在Jetson AGX上实现2.56倍加速；在严格25瓦功耗预算下，Jetson Nano上的能量效率比Nvidia DGX A100高出5-48倍。

Conclusion: edgeFBP证明了在资源受限的边缘设备上进行高效CT重建的可行性，使数据中心级成像任务能够在受限边缘设备上运行，为边缘智能医疗成像提供了有前景的技术路径。

Abstract: Computed Tomography (CT) is an essential 3D imaging technology widely used in medical diagnostics and scientific research. However, performing CT imaging on edge devices is challenging due to limitations in computational power, memory capacity, and energy budget. This paper presents an efficient CT reconstruction framework, called edgeFBP, designed for Nvidia Jetson System-on-Chip (SoC) devices. edgeFBP adopts an end-to-end pipeline design for efficient out-of-core image reconstruction under tight power and memory constraints. edgeFBP utilizes a mixed-precision strategy leveraging half-precision Tensor Cores (TCs) to accelerate the bottleneck back-projection (BP) kernel. edgeFBP achieves a 1.83x speedup over the widely used RTK library on Jetson Nano and a 2.56x speedup on Jetson AGX. Under a strict 25-Watt power budget, edgeFBP on Jetson Nano achieves up to 5-48x higher energy efficiency than an Nvidia DGX A100, enabling datacenter-scale imaging on constrained edge devices.

</details>


### [35] [The Maximum Mutual Visibility Set on a Cactus Graph and the Self-stabilizing Constructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07253)
*Yonghwan Kim,Yuichi Sudo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了仙人掌图（cactus graphs）中的最大互视集（MVS）问题，首次确定了该类图中最大MVS的规模，并提出两种自稳定算法来构造这些集合：一种基于单棵BFS树，在O(D)轮内稳定，平均每进程使用O(log n)比特；另一种使用并行BFS树，在O(|C_max|+|T_max|)轮内稳定，该复杂度被证明是渐近紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大MVS问题在一般图中是NP完全的，因此需要限制图类以寻找可行解法。仙人掌图是低树宽图类的基础，但该图上最大MVS问题此前仍未解决，填补这一空白具有理论意义和实际价值。

Method: 论文首先确定了仙人掌图中最大MVS的规模；随后设计了两种自稳定算法。第一种算法利用单棵BFS树构造MVS，稳定时间为O(D)轮；第二种算法利用并行BFS树，稳定时间为O(|C_max|+|T_max|)轮，两者在存储复杂度上也有所差异。

Result: 给出了仙人掌图中最大MVS的确定结果；提出了两种自稳定算法，并证明了第二种算法的稳定轮数O(|C_max|+|T_max|)是渐近最优的，即使在|C_max|+|T_max|=o(D)的图上也成立。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了仙人掌图上最大MVS问题的开放性难题，不仅给出了大小上界的确切刻画，还提供了高效的自稳定构造算法，其中并行BFS树算法的复杂度已被证明在渐近意义下不可改进。

Abstract: Given a graph $G=(V,E)$, let $S$ ($\subseteq V$) be a set of vertices. Two vertices are \emph{mutually visible} if there exists a shortest path in $G$ between them that does not contain any other vertex of $S$. A set $S$ is a \emph{Mutual Visibility Set} (\MVS) if every pair of vertices in $S$ is mutually visible.
  The concept of \MVS s in graphs has attracted significant attention since its introduction, as it provides an important structural property of graphs. However, determining a maximum \MVS\ in general graphs is computationally intractable; the decision problem of whether a graph admits an \MVS\ of size at least $k$ has been shown to be \emph{NP-complete}. Thus, prior work has focused on finding maximal \MVS s or restricting attention to specific graph classes.
  Cactus graphs form a fundamental low-treewidth class, yet the maximum \MVS\ problem for this class remains open. In this paper, we first determine the size of maximum \MVS~in cactus graphs, and introduce two self-stabilizing algorithms that construct such sets. The first algorithm uses a single BFS tree and stabilizes in $O(D)$ rounds with $O(\log n)$ bits per process on average; the second one uses parallel BFS trees and stabilizes in $O(|C_{\max}|+|T_{\max}|)$ rounds, which we show to be asymptotically tight as a function of these two parameters, even on graphs where $|C_{\max}|+|T_{\max}| = o(D)$.

</details>


### [36] [PLATOS: A Power and Latency-Aware Task-Oriented Scheduling Strategy for Healthcare IoT in Fog Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07318)
*Mohammed Alaa Ala'anzy,Zulfiqar Ahmad,Zhanar Mukash*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PLATOS的新型调度策略，通过任务导向的四阶段调度实现HIoT环境中功耗和延迟的双重优化。与现有最优方法相比，PLATOS在iFogSim2仿真中将能耗降低18.72%，延迟降低8.65%，有效提升了HIoT系统的效率和响应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HIoT技术在医疗行业的快速发展带来了实时数据采集分析的机遇，但其快速扩张也引入了雾计算环境中延迟增加和能耗升高的问题，尤其是在管理电池供电设备时更为突出，亟需一种能同时优化功耗和延迟的调度方案。

Method: PLATOS调度策略分四个阶段实施：第一阶段将HIoT任务分为优先级导向、存储导向和计算导向三类；第二阶段通过识别每个任务类别中执行延迟最低的雾计算资源来实现延迟优化；第三阶段通过选择能耗最小的资源来实现功耗优化；第四阶段在决策阶段将高性能雾资源分配给高优先级任务，其余任务则根据延迟和功耗优化阶段生成的任务映射列表进行调度。

Result: 在iFogSim2中进行的仿真实验表明，与现有最优方法相比，PLATOS将能耗降低了18.72%，延迟降低了8.65%。

Conclusion: PLATOS的改进有效提升了HIoT系统的效率和响应能力，有助于提高患者护理效果并推动主动式医疗服务交付。

Abstract: Healthcare Internet of Things (HIoT) technology is revolutionising the healthcare industry by enabling real-time data collection and analysis for personalised patient care. However, the rapid expansion of HIoT technology introduces challenges such as increased latency and higher energy consumption in fog computing environments, particularly when managing battery-operated devices. To address these issues, this work proposes a novel scheduling strategy that optimises both power consumption and latency through task-oriented scheduling for HIoT tasks. The proposed strategy, named PLATOS (Power and Latency Aware Task Oriented Scheduling), is implemented in four sequential phases. In the first phase, HIoT tasks are categorised into three groups: priority-oriented, storage-oriented, and computational-oriented. The second phase focuses on latency optimisation by identifying the fog computing resources that yield the lowest execution delay for each task category. In the third phase, power optimisation is achieved by selecting the resources that minimise energy consumption. Finally, in the decision-making phase, high-performance fog resources are allocated to high-priority tasks while the remaining tasks are scheduled based on a mapped list derived from the latency and power optimisation phases. Simulation experiments conducted in iFogSim2 demonstrate that PLATOS reduces energy consumption by 18.72% and latency by 8.65% when compared to the state-of-the-art. These improvements enhance the efficiency and responsiveness of HIoT systems and contribute to more effective patient care and proactive healthcare service delivery.

</details>


### [37] [From Bracha to Coded MBRB: Benchmarking Byzantine Reliable Broadcast Implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07521)
*Yenan Wang,Jesper Kullberg,Fabian Paglianno Persson,Elad Michael Schiller,Timothé Albouy*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在统一Go代码库中实现了三种拜占庭可靠广播协议（Bracha、AFRT、Coded MBRB），并进行了大规模实战评测，涵盖Shadow网络模拟器、原生性能分析、云平台及FABRIC测试床，结果表明Coded MBRB在大数据包场景下降低传输量并改善延迟，但将成本转移到密码学与编码计算上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拜占庭可靠广播（BRB）和消息对抗容忍的拜占庭可靠广播（MBRB）的运行为不仅受规范和渐进通信界影响，还受序列化、密码学、缓冲、编排、部署环境及故障注入语义等实现层面因素影响，现有研究缺乏实现级别的实证评估。

Method: 在共享Go代码库中实现Bracha、AFRT和Coded MBRB三种算法，配备统一编排、工具化、基于解析器的规范检查、故障注入和开源可复现构件，通过Shadow网络模拟器（最高30节点、40MB负载、92,190次运行）和多种部署环境进行评估。

Result: Coded MBRB在云环境中对较大负载降低了传输数据量并改善延迟，但增加了密码学与编码计算开销；Bracha和AFRT在小负载下CPU成本更低，但随着负载增长其全负载传播增加了处理、分配和网络开销。解析器在全部2,361,600个检查条目中未发现重复投递、冲突投递或与发送方负载不同的投递。

Conclusion: 论文提供了实现级证据和可扩展构件来基准测试BRB和MBRB作为可执行分布式系统组件，揭示了算法描述本身无法体现的瓶颈和运行权衡，为系统设计者选择与优化协议提供了实证参考。

Abstract: Byzantine Reliable Broadcast (BRB) and Message-Adversary-Tolerant Byzantine Reliable Broadcast (MBRB) are reliable-dissemination abstractions for fault-tolerant distributed systems. Yet their operational behavior is shaped not only by specifications and asymptotic communication bounds, but also by serialization, cryptography, buffering, orchestration, deployment environment, and fault-injection semantics. This paper implements and evaluates Bracha [Information and Computation, 1987], AFRT by Albouy et al. [TCS, 2023], and Coded MBRB by Albouy et al. [OPODIS, 2024]. We implement the algorithms in a shared Go codebase with common orchestration, instrumentation, parser-based specification checks, fault injection, and an open-source reproducibility artifact. The evaluation uses single-shot broadcasts in the Shadow network simulator, native profiling, a Google Cloud Platform deployment, and a distributed FABRIC testbed, covering controlled experiments up to 30 nodes, payloads up to 40 MB, 92,190 runs, and 2,361,600 parser-checked entries. The results show that Coded MBRB reduces transmitted data and improves latency in the evaluated cloud setting for larger payloads, but shifts cost to cryptographic and coding computation. Bracha and AFRT incur lower CPU costs at smaller payloads, but their full-payload dissemination increases processing, allocation, and network costs as payloads grow. Across the tested configurations, the parser found no duplicate deliveries, conflicting deliveries, or deliveries of values different from the sender's payload. The paper contributes implementation-level evidence and an extensible artifact for benchmarking BRB and MBRB as executable distributed-system components, exposing bottlenecks and operational trade-offs that are hidden by algorithmic descriptions alone.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式MoE推理中，细粒度的计算-通信重叠虽好，但计算和通信的CTA会争抢SM上有限的驻留容量。固定资源划分无法适应输入大小、路由专家负载和内核配置的变化，导致通信积压或减少专家计算并行度。

Method: 提出波量化(wave-quantized)分析模型和启动时资源管理器：利用路由tile数量、内核占用率、GPU驻留约束和分裂级就绪依赖，在每次启动前选择通信CTA数量和资源分区，无需候选执行或性能剖析或内核重编译，并集成到公开的COMET A100实现（FLUX）中。

Result: 在四块NVIDIA A100上评估三种MoE模型和多种并行策略。在15个真实p90工作负载中，分析选择器相对测量oracle的平均遗憾为3.22%，平均求解开销为0.157微秒。相对COMET，GEMM2+GatherRS算子、完整post-router MoE层和完整模型prefill分别获得2.528x、1.771x和1.185x的几何平均加速比，最大加速比分别为4.218x、2.584x和1.439x。

Conclusion: 该方法在TP=2/EP=2的所有可行序列长度（至少4096）下均优于COMET、Megatron core-TE和FastMoE TP+NCCL，表明基于分析模型的自适应资源管理方案能有效解决分布式MoE推理中计算-通信重叠的资源分配问题。

Abstract: Fine-grained computation--communication overlap in distributed Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) inference allows communication to begin as partial compute results become ready. However, cooperative thread arrays (CTAs) performing computation and communication contend for finite residency capacity on streaming multiprocessors (SMs). Because a resident CTA generally retains its allocated SM resources until completion, CTAs that cannot be co-resident must wait for resources, resulting in wave-like execution. A fixed resource partition cannot adapt to changes in input size, routed expert load, and kernel configuration, potentially causing a communication backlog or reducing expert compute parallelism.
  We present a wave-quantized analytical model and launch-time resource manager for dependency-coupled overlap pipelines. Using routed-tile counts, kernel occupancy, GPU residency constraints, and split-level readiness dependencies, it selects the communication-CTA count and resource partition before each launch without candidate execution, per-workload profiling, or kernel recompilation. We integrate the method into the public COMET A100 implementation in FLUX.
  We evaluate three MoE models on four NVIDIA A100 GPUs under several parallelism strategies at the GEMM2+GatherRS operator, complete post-router MoE layer, and complete-model prefill levels. Across 15 real-p90 workloads, the analytical selector achieves 3.22 percent mean regret relative to the measured oracle with a mean solver overhead of 0.157 microseconds. Over COMET, our method achieves geometric-mean speedups of 2.528x at the GEMM2+GatherRS operator, 1.771x at the complete post-router MoE layer, and 1.185x for complete-model prefill, with maxima of 4.218x, 2.584x, and 1.439x, respectively. At every feasible TP=2/EP=2 sequence length of at least 4,096, our implementation outperforms COMET, Megatron core-TE, and FastMoE TP+NCCL.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 将SNN建模为超图以利用硬件特性（核间多播和核内复制），提出GPU加速的映射流水线：采用围绕硬件约束设计的多级划分方案，放置阶段通过递归二分初始化，随后通过重复交换将强连接的计算核聚集在一起进行细化。

Result: 模型实验表明，与现有串行工具相比，所提方法在脉冲传输延迟上降低16%以上，能耗降低42%，并行映射速度平均提升18-280倍。

Conclusion: GPU加速的映射流水线在保持高质量解的同时大幅提升映射效率，有效解决大规模SNN在神经形态硬件上的核分配与布局难题。

Abstract: SNNs running on neuromorphic hardware use spikes to achieve sparse and energy-efficient communication over a mesh of cores. In turn, system performance heavily depends on the assignment of neurons to cores: the mapping. Since hardware features inter-core multicast and intra-core replication of spikes, we model SNNs as hypergraphs to exploit both opportunities for reducing communication traffic. Mapping thus comprises two NP-hard problems: hypergraph partitioning and placement on the lattice of cores. High-quality solutions to both are critical, yet increasingly difficult as networks scale to millions of neurons. Therefore, we propose a GPU-accelerated pipeline for SNN mapping: a multi-level partitioning scheme is devised around hardware constraints, while placement is initialized through recursive bisection, followed by refinement pulling together strongly connected cores through repeated swaps. Model-based experiments show upwards of 16% lower latency and 42% lower energy for spike movements over existing sequential tools, while our parallel mapper is on average 18-280x faster.

</details>


### [40] [Fast Multidimensional Approximate Agreement with Optimal Resilience Using Ball Validity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07599)
*Tijana Milentijević,Stefan Schmid*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究多维近似一致性问题，提出最小包围球（MEB）有效性及其乘法松弛版本c-MEB有效性。核心贡献包括：自适应MEB收缩算法（同步模型，n>(d+1)t，每轮收缩因子1/√2）、无维度膨胀定理以及利用该定理实现的同步算法（最优弹性n>3t，收缩因子√3/2，√6-MEB有效性），并扩展到异步设置。这是首个同时具备最优弹性、常数c-MEB有效性和无坐标收缩的多维近似一致算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的凸有效性导致容错阈值随维度增长，而最小包围球（MEB）有效性及其松弛版本c-MEB有效性可能在容错能力上优于传统方法。作者希望设计在多维空间中具有最优弹性的近似一致协议，同时保持常数级别的MEB有效性保证，并消除对坐标系的依赖。

Method: 论文提出两大技术贡献：(1) Adaptive MEB Contraction——一种无坐标的同步算法，在n>(d+1)t条件下每轮将正确MEB半径收缩1/√2，同时满足√2-MEB有效性；(2) 维度无关的膨胀定理——若有限族欧氏球中任意β个球有公共交点，则将所有球半径膨胀√(β/(β-1))倍可保证整体公共交集。将该定理应用于局部MEB安全区域的候选球，可推导出同步算法n>3t、异步算法（有无膨胀两种变体）的容错与收缩保证。此外还对Minimum-Diameter Averaging (MDA)算法推导了MEB有效性界值。

Result: 同步模型下：n>(d+1)t时每轮MEB半径收缩1/√2（√2-MEB有效），n>3t时实现最优弹性且收缩因子√3/2（√6-MEB有效）。异步模型下：无膨胀时n>(d+2)t，收缩因子√(2/3)，√6-MEB有效；有膨胀时n>4t，收缩因子√15/4，2√10-MEB有效。相比MDA，本文算法在弹性上严格更优，且提供更强的MEB有效性保证。还给出了分析紧确性的反例。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入MEB有效性和c-MEB有效性，突破了凸有效性下容错阈值随维度增长的限制，实现了与维度无关的最优弹性（n>3t）多维近似一致协议。膨胀定理作为核心理论工具具有独立意义，为后续MEB有效性方向的算法设计提供了通用框架。异步扩展和与MDA的对比进一步展示了该方法的普适性和优势。

Abstract: Multidimensional approximate agreement requires $n$ processes with inputs in $\mathbb{R}^d$ to output vectors close to each other, despite up to $t$ Byzantine faults. Under convex validity, outputs must lie in the convex hull of the correct inputs, which leads to resilience thresholds that grow with the dimension. We instead study Minimum Enclosing Ball (MEB) validity and its multiplicative relaxation $c$-MEB validity. Our first contribution is Adaptive MEB Contraction, a coordinate-free algorithm that, in the synchronous model with $n>(d+1)t$ contracts the correct MEB radius by $1/\sqrt2$ per round for $α=1$ while satisfying $\sqrt2$-MEB validity. We also give an example showing that this bound is tight for our analysis.
  Our main technical contribution is a dimension-free inflation theorem: if every $β$ balls in a finite family of Euclidean balls have a common point, then inflating each radius by $\sqrt{β/(β-1)}$ guarantees a common intersection. Applying the theorem to the candidate balls defining the local MEB-safe areas results in a synchronous algorithm with optimal resilience $n>3t$, contraction factor $\sqrt3/2$ and $\sqrt6$-MEB validity. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first multidimensional approximate agreement algorithm with optimal resilience, constant $c$-MEB validity and coordinate-free contraction.
  We further extend the approach to the asynchronous setting. Without inflation we obtain resilience $n>(d+2)t$, contraction factor $\sqrt{2/3}$ and $\sqrt6$-MEB validity, whereas with inflation for $n>4t$ we get the contraction factor $\sqrt{15}/4$ and $2\sqrt{10}$-MEB validity. Finally, we compare our guarantees with existing algorithms, including Minimum-Diameter Averaging (MDA), for which we derive MEB-validity guarantees. Our algorithms achieve strictly better resilience while providing substantially stronger MEB-validity guarantees than MDA.

</details>

*Guangneng Chen,David Garcia Selfa,Pablo Quesada Barriuso*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地对Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）在多节点多GPU高性能计算基础设施上的数据并行训练进行了实证可扩展性研究，从强扩展、弱扩展、通信开销和模型规模扩展四个维度进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然KAN的逼近性质已受到广泛关注，但其在分布式多GPU训练下的行为尚未得到系统性的表征，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在FinisTerrae III超级计算机上使用PyTorch Distributed Data Parallel（DDP），利用最多8块NVIDIA A100 GPU分布在4个节点上进行实验，从强扩展、弱扩展、通信开销和模型规模扩展四个维度评估KAN训练的可扩展性。

Result: KAN训练在8个GPU上达到74.7%的并行效率，获得5.97倍加速；弱扩展表现为单GPU到多GPU的初始吞吐量下降后保持稳定；通信开销呈非单调模式（1.3%-6.1%），主要由All-Reduce算法选择和节点间延迟驱动；参数与内存比率随模型规模改善但训练时间扩展不佳。

Conclusion: KAN的操作符级优化与数据并行优化是互补的，研究提供了GPU拓扑和模型规模选择的部署指南，并讨论了合成回归评估的局限性。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) replace the fixed activation functions and linear weights of Multi-Layer Perceptrons (MLPs) with learnable univariate functions on network edges, offering improved interpretability and, in some settings, competitive parameter efficiency. While the approximation properties of KANs have received considerable attention, their behavior under distributed, multi-GPU training has not been systematically characterized. This paper presents an empirical scalability study of data-parallel KAN training on multi-node, multi-GPU high-performance computing (HPC) infrastructure, evaluated along four dimensions: strong scaling, weak scaling, communication overhead, and model-size scaling.
  Experiments were conducted on the FinisTerrae III supercomputer using up to 8 NVIDIA A100 GPUs across 4 nodes with PyTorch Distributed Data Parallel (DDP). KAN training reaches 74.7% parallel efficiency at 8 GPUs with a 5.97x speedup, consistent with conventional deep learning workloads. Weak scaling shows an initial single-to-multi-GPU throughput drop followed by strong stability. Communication overhead follows a non-monotonic pattern (1.3%-6.1%), driven primarily by All-Reduce algorithm selection and inter-node latency rather than KAN's edge-wise gradient structure. The parameter-to-memory ratio improves with model size even as training time scales unfavorably.
  These results indicate that operator-level and data-parallel optimizations for KAN are complementary. We provide deployment guidelines for GPU topology and model-size selection, and discuss the limitations of a synthetic-regression evaluation.

</details>


### [42] [Interactive Debugger for Performance Portable Python HPC Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07912)
*Ivan Grigorik,Gabriel Kosmacher,George Biros,Milos Gligoric*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PKDB是首个针对Python编写的GPU和多线程底层内核的交互式调试器，支持标准调试功能（断点、单步、变量检查）以及两项高级能力：实时代码评估和内核调用点替换，无需修改源代码即可在真实设备上执行调试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python在HPC领域被广泛使用（如PyKokkos框架），但缺乏对GPU和多线程底层内核的交互式调试支持。现有开发者只能依赖print语句、框架特定断言或仅CPU执行，这些方法需要修改程序或数据，且可能掩盖设备相关的bug。

Method: PKDB利用Python和PyKokkos的动态特性实现：(i) 实时代码评估——允许开发者在暂停的内核中间执行任意Python表达式或整个内核而无需重启进程；(ii) 内核调用点替换——允许正在运行的内核被动态更新和重新加载，仅需重新编译和重新执行该内核，无需重启应用程序。

Result: 在Intel、AMD和NVIDIA CPU以及NVIDIA和AMD GPU上的性能评估显示，PKDB引入了有限的额外开销，对日常使用具有实用性。

Conclusion: PKDB为Python HPC生态系统带来了关键的调试功能，使开发者能够在保持实际设备执行的同时进行交互式调试，填补了Python HPC领域缺乏交互式调试支持的重要空白。

Abstract: We propose PKDB, the first interactive debugger for GPU and multithreaded low-level kernels written in Python. Python is widely used in high performance computing (HPC), with frameworks such as PyKokkos translating Python-embedded domain-specific languages to native code that runs across OpenMP-threaded CPUs and various GPUs. Yet interactive debugging support for such code is absent: developers resort to print statements, framework-specific assertions, or CPU-only execution, the last of which requires altering the program or its data and can mask device-specific bugs. PKDB enables standard interactive debugging like breakpoints, stepping, and variable inspection while preserving actual on-device execution without source modification. Beyond these fundamentals, PKDB introduces two advanced capabilities that exploit the dynamic nature of Python and PyKokkos: (i) Live code evaluation, which lets developers execute arbitrary Python expressions or entire kernels in the middle of a paused kernel without restarting the process; (ii) Kernel call site substitution, which allows an actively running kernel to be updated and reloaded on the fly, so only the kernel is recompiled and re-executed without restarting the application. Our performance evaluation on Intel, AMD, and NVIDIA CPUs, and NVIDIA and AMD GPUs shows that PKDB introduces limited overhead and is practical for everyday use while introducing critical debugging features to the Python HPC ecosystem.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在计算连续体中利用高级用户意图（自然语言描述）进行微服务部署决策的架构，通过生成式人工智能将意图翻译为低层SLO，并结合基础设施描述决定微服务的部署位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低层SLO增加了计算连续体用户的使用门槛，为了提高计算连续体的大规模采用，需要基于高层业务词汇的微服务部署决策方法。

Method: 提出一种架构，以自然语言描述的高级用户意图作为输入，利用生成式AI将意图翻译为低层SLO，并将SLO与基础设施描述结合来决定应用各微服务的部署位置。

Result: 实现了该架构的原型并进行了评估，证明了该方法的可行性。

Abstract: The placement of microservices in the compute continuum plays a vital role in delivering services that comply with customers' needs, such as reduced latency, storage requirements, quality of service and availability. To achieve customers' needs in the geographically dispersed architecture of the compute continuum, Service Level Objectives (SLOs) have been largely used in decision-making to place microservices. However, because low-level SLOs increase the barrier to entry for continuum users, placement decisions based on high-level business vocabulary are required if the compute continuum is to be adopted at scale. This paper proposes an architecture for microservices placement decisions in the computing continuum utilizing high-level user intents described in natural language as input. The approach utilizes Generative Artificial Intelligence to translate the intents to low-level SLOs, which are used along with the infrastructure description to decide where different microservices that compose an application must be deployed so that SLOs are met. We implement and evaluate a prototype of the architecture to demonstrate the approach's feasibility.

</details>


### [44] [SemBridge: Compiling Consumer Observations into Cross-Stack Communication Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08231)
*Genlang Chen,Junyi Zhu,Yuanshan Lin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出SemBridge，一种在分布式张量系统与集体通信系统之间建立"消费者观察"语义层的框架。它通过将图和运行时事实编译为类型化契约，描述消费者可见结果及其交付义务，并利用确定性lowerer和符号检查器在CUDA/NCCL与CANN/HCCL上验证执行计划。实验表明其在九条真实边上减少启动开销，在结果边上减少99.97%以上的流量并提升吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式张量系统与集体通信系统分别在"值存储位置"和"操作执行优化"上建模，但当不同厂商运行时之间无法共享原生通信器时，两者都无法明确描述远程消费者必须观察到的语义。这个边界鸿沟导致了可替代性、完成性、所有权、需求等关键语义的缺失。

Method: SemBridge将图与运行时事实编译为类型化契约，涵盖来源性（provenance）、可替代性、完成性、权威性、需求性及本地域位置等六大维度。通过确定性lowerer构建后端无关的通信计划，并使用符号检查器在NCCL/HCCL执行前验证计划有效性。采用独立布局规划器和字节最小化器作为对比基线。

Result: 在72种结构转换中，布局规划器仅建立54种完整义务，字节最小化器提出40个语义无效候选均被SemBridge拒绝。在九条真实边上，SemBridge生成观察感知计划，全部减少启动次数，三条结果边上减少负载字节。在双主机1GbE容量溢出部署中，源投影削减99.97%以上结果流量，吞吐量提升8.92%-80.20%。所有18个MiniMax重启对比场景均偏好源投影。Qwen3-14B MLP切片验证了位级激活分片交付和行并行部分量的HCCL完成。

Conclusion: 消费者观察（consumer observation）应作为介于放置（placement）与集体执行（collective execution）之间的独立语义层。SemBridge证明了通过类型化契约加符号验证的方式，能够安全地跨越异构后端边界，实现优化且语义正确的分布式通信。

Abstract: Distributed-tensor systems specify where values reside, while collective systems optimize how requested operations execute. At a boundary between vendor runtimes that cannot share a native communicator, neither abstraction states what a remote consumer must observe. SemBridge fills this gap by compiling graph and runtime facts into a typed contract for the consumer-visible result and its delivery obligations. The contract captures provenance, substitutability, completion, authority, demand, and native-domain locality. A deterministic lowerer constructs backend-neutral communication plans, and a symbolic checker validates each plan before execution across CUDA/NCCL and CANN/HCCL. An independent layout-only planner handles all 72 structural transitions but establishes only 54 complete obligations; a byte-only minimizer proposes 40 semantically invalid candidates, all rejected by SemBridge. On nine real edges, SemBridge produces distinct observation-aware plans that reduce startups on all nine and payload bytes on the three result edges. A live CUDA/CANN run derives and executes full-logit reconstruction, source projection, and owner-token delivery from log-probability, token-only, and owner-scoped requests. On a measured two-host 1-GbE capacity-spillover deployment, source projection cuts result traffic by more than 99.97% and increases throughput by 8.92-80.20% across Dense, MoE, and MiniMax workloads. All 18 MiniMax restart pairs at concurrency 1, 8, and 16 favor source projection. A Qwen3-14B MLP slice additionally verifies bitwise activation-shard delivery and HCCL completion of row-parallel partials. These results establish consumer observation as a semantic layer between placement and collective execution.

</details>

*Maysam Khatib,Moysis Symeonides,Demetris Trihinas,George Pallis,Marios D. Dikaiakos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对边缘连续体中的自托管LLM推理进行了受控测量研究，评估了Jetson AGX Orin和近边缘服务器（CPU-only与GPU模式）上多种开源LLM及量化变体的准确性、延迟和能耗，并与云端的GPT-4o进行对比，发现GPU服务器延迟最低、Jetson能耗较低，且通过Pareto前沿分析指出仅依据计算侧推理指标做部署决策可能导致延迟敏感交互式Web服务的次优放置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益广泛地作为智能Web服务的后端，但在边缘连续体中部署需要平衡质量、延迟、模型规模和能耗等多重目标，目前缺乏对自托管LLM推理在边缘节点上的系统性测量研究。

Method: 采用受控测量实验方法：在Jetson AGX Orin和近边缘服务器（含CPU-only与GPU两种推理模式）上部署多个开源权重LLM及其量化变体，使用固定的问答工作负载进行基准测试，以GPT-4o作为云端准确性和延迟参考，测量准确性、模型占用、逐token解码延迟、预填充延迟和整体执行能耗，并进行Pareto前沿分析研究流式传输开销对部署决策的影响。

Result: GPU服务器执行提供最低的计算端延迟；Jetson Orin展现出更低的实测能耗；CPU-only执行在该工作负载下延迟和能耗均处于劣势。参数量和权重文件大小不能可靠预测实际准确性或延迟。Pareto分析表明计算侧指标单独不足以指导延迟敏感交互式Web服务的部署决策。

Conclusion: 边缘连续体上的LLM服务部署需要在多个维度间权衡，计算侧的推理指标（如延迟与能耗）并不足以独立决定最优放置策略，考虑端到端流式传输等系统层面因素后，部署决策可能发生改变，需要更全面的视角来优化延迟敏感的Web服务。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as backends for intelligent web services, but serving them across the edge continuum requires balancing quality, latency, model footprint, and energy. This paper presents a controlled measurement study of self-hosted LLM inference across edge and near-edge deployment nodes: an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin and a near-edge server with CPU-only and GPU-enabled inference modes. We evaluate multiple open-weight LLMs and quantization variants using a fixed question-answering workload, and compare them against GPT-4o as a cloud-hosted accuracy and latency reference. Our benchmarking pipeline reports accuracy, model footprint, per-token decoding latency, prefill latency, and overall execution energy. The results show that GPU-enabled server execution provides the lowest compute-side latency, while Jetson Orin shows lower measured energy, consistent with its lower platform power under our setup. CPU-only execution is consistently dominated in latency for our workload and shows higher measured energy. We also show that parameter count and downloaded weight-file size alone do not reliably predict observed accuracy or latency. Finally, using Pareto-frontier analysis, we study how deployment decisions may change under possible streamed-token delivery overheads, highlighting that compute-side inference metrics alone can lead to suboptimal placement for latency-sensitive interactive web services.

</details>


### [46] [Sample-Guided Exact Top-K Selection for Long-Context Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08450)
*Siran Liu,Yang Xue,Theo Tang,Changxu Shao,Qian Cheng,Haimeng Ren,Donghua Jiang,Haipeng Ming,Lehua Ding,Zhonghan Lin,Shengying Wei,Wei Liu,Kai Liu,Jianchen Zhu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出HPC-Ops Top-K，一种样本引导的精确选择器，用于处理稀疏注意力得分行中变长（ragged）Top-K问题。该方法是利用固定步长采样视图提出粗略边界，通过必需的完整行遍历来验证和形成候选集，并用嵌套二级边界处理欠填充情况，确保采样只影响性能而不影响正确性。GPU实现融合了完整行认证与候选形成，支持持久化、KV拆分和直接精确执行三种模式。在Hy4-Preview索引器得分上，相比最快的验证外挂精确基线实现1.29-1.75×加速，几何平均1.55×加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的精确Top-K选择器在处理稀疏注意力时，必须对完整得分行进行一次遍历（用于认证/修复），这导致即使采用固定大小子集保留策略，仍需处理随着上下文长度增长而扩展的行数据。生产级基数选择器在能发现第一个可操作边界前必须完成整行遍历，之后还需另一轮行级遍历进行精确精化，造成不必要的开销。作者观察到定位紧凑的上尾分布所需分辨率远低于确定精确秩边界，且固定步长的部分视图能在变长场景下保持与整行秩的校准关系，这为样本引导提供了理论依据。

Method: 提出HPC-Ops Top-K方法，核心流程为：(1) 固定步长视图首先提出行局部粗糙边界；(2) 强制性的完整行遍历认证该边界的充分性、形成已接纳候选集，并初始化对未解析前沿的FP32精确精化；(3) 嵌套的二级边界和精确恢复机制处理欠填充提案，在任何输出提交前确保正确性。采样仅控制常见路径的计算量但绝不损害正确性。GPU实现融合了完整行认证与候选形成，并在图可捕获的变长分发机制后结合了持久化、KV拆分和直接精确执行三种模式。

Result: 在Hy4-Preview索引器得分上，HPC-Ops Top-K在20种算子配置中比最快的外部精确基线快1.29-1.75倍，几何平均加速1.55倍；在两个框架派生的稀疏注意力轨迹上分别获得1.36倍和1.48倍加速。实现已开源在Tencent的HPC-Ops库中。

Conclusion: 本文证明通过对变长稀疏注意力得分行进行样本引导的粗略边界提议，可以在不牺牲精确性的前提下大幅减少Top-K选择的计算开销。采样用于控制常见路径工作量而正确性由完整行遍历保证，这一设计在保持精确输出的同时实现了显著加速，并已作为开源库HPC-Ops的一部分发布。

Abstract: Sparse attention bounds downstream attention work by retaining a fixed-size subset of indexed tokens, but its standalone exact Top-$K$ stage must still process materialized score rows whose length grows with context. Production radix selectors discover their first actionable boundary only after a complete-row pass, forcing another row-scale traversal before exact refinement. We observe that locating a compact upper tail requires substantially less resolution than identifying the exact rank boundary, and that fixed-stride partial views of the current row remain calibrated to the corresponding complete-row rank across ragged lengths. We present HPC-Ops Top-K, a sample-guided exact selector for ragged sparse-attention score rows. A fixed-stride view proposes a row-local coarse boundary; the mandatory complete-row pass certifies its sufficiency, forms the admitted candidate set, and initializes exact FP32 refinement over the unresolved frontier. A nested secondary boundary and exact recovery handle underfilled proposals before any output is committed, so sampling controls common-path work but never correctness. The GPU implementation fuses complete-row certification and candidate formation, and combines persistent, KV-split, and direct-exact execution behind graph-capturable ragged-row dispatch. We evaluate HPC-Ops Top-K on indexer scores from Hy4-Preview. It outperforms the fastest verified external exact baseline by $1.29$--$1.75\times$ across 20 operator configurations, with a $1.55\times$ geometric-mean speedup. It further achieves $1.36\times$ and $1.48\times$ speedups on two framework-derived sparse-attention traces. The implementation is available in HPC-Ops, Tencent's open-source high-performance operator library for LLM inference, at https://github.com/Tencent/hpc-ops.

</details>

Abstract: The transition from traditional High Performance Computing (HPC) to the Computing Continuum emphasizes efficient resource management and sustainable practices across Multi-Scale hybrid architectures. This paper introduces a multidimensional metric framework to characterize these systems and guide deployment strategies for modern workloads. The framework combines Architectural Performance metrics (such as Throughput, Latency, Scalability), System Utilization, and key Sustainability and Accuracy indicators (such as Energy Efficiency and Power Consumption). Using a modular hybrid testbed, experiments reveal complex relationships among metrics, especially the trade-offs between accuracy and energy, and the efficiency of hybrid nodes. The guidelines help identify optimal operating points and lay the groundwork for improving orchestrators and schedulers (e.g., Kubernetes) to assign demanding applications, including AI and Quantum Computing, to suitable system modules, ensuring high performance and sustainability.

</details>

*Haozhe Fan,Wei Wang,Xingchen Liu,Man Liu,Xingjian Tian,Haoquan Long,Zedong Liu,Daran Sun,Jinwu Yang,Bo Yang,Jie Liu,Yonggang Che,Hairui Zhao,Guangming Tan,Dingwen Tao*

TL;DR: 本文提出CC-Bench，一个轻量级、可扩展、面向应用的通信压缩基准测试套件，用于在真实执行条件下评估分布式HPC和LLM工作负载中的通信压缩效果，通过声明式环境建模和函数级拦截实现跨库可移植评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式HPC和LLM工作负载中数据移动成为主要性能瓶颈，通信压缩虽可降低开销，但现有基准测试缺乏对多样化后端、现实数据集、应用特定精度指标以及重叠引起的资源争用的支持，导致压缩收益难以评估。

Method: CC-Bench通过声明式应用环境建模将分析逻辑与通信库、数据集和保真度指标解耦；结合函数级拦截与硬件计数器监控，表征每个阶段的延迟、硬件利用率、数值保真度和计算干扰；采用HPC和LLM工作负载的代表性数据集，在CPU和GPU集群上评估三个支持压缩的通信库。

Result: 在CPU和GPU集群上对三个支持压缩的通信库进行评估，揭示了精度-性能权衡和瓶颈，为实际部署和优化提供指导。

Conclusion: CC-Bench提供了一种可移植、应用导向的通信压缩评估方案，能够有效揭示压缩技术在实际场景中的精度-性能权衡与实际瓶颈，为分布式工作负载的通信优化提供了实用指南。

Abstract: Distributed HPC and LLM workloads increasingly require efficient communication for scalability, yet growing data movement has become a major performance bottleneck. Communication compression can reduce this overhead and complement execution-level optimizations, but its benefits remain difficult to assess because existing benchmarks lack support for diverse backends, realistic datasets, application-specific accuracy metrics, and overlap-induced resource contention. We present CC-Bench, a lightweight, extensible, and application-oriented benchmark suite for evaluating communication compression under realistic execution conditions. CC-Bench uses declarative application-environment modeling to decouple profiling logic from communication libraries, datasets, and fidelity metrics, enabling portable cross-library evaluation. It further combines function-level interception and hardware counter monitoring to characterize per-phase latency, hardware utilization, numerical fidelity, and computation interference. With representative datasets from HPC and LLM workloads, CC-Bench evaluates three compression-enabled communication libraries on CPU and GPU clusters, revealing accuracy-performance trade-offs and bottlenecks to guide practical deployment and optimization.

</details>

*Daniel Rosendo,Renan Souza,Kelsey Carter,John Lagergren,Frédéric Suter,Shelaine L. Curd,David Weston,Rafael Ferreira da Silva*

Method: 通过美国能源部Genesis Mission平台提供可复用服务，以Orchestrated Platform for Autonomous Laboratories的植物表型分析工作流为示例，耦合橡树岭国家实验室先进植物表型实验室与Frontier超级计算机开展跨设施实验。

Result: 在为期40天的镍处理实验中，该工作流将原本约12小时的人工分析缩短为只需数秒到数分钟即可完成的交互式查询。

Conclusion: Genesis平台通过提供共享的跨设施服务能力，有效支持了自主科学实验的开展，显著提升了跨设施科学发现的效率。

Abstract: Autonomous, cross-facility science requires capabilities that no individual project should have to build for itself: managed execution for long-lived services, versioned distribution of models to remote compute systems, governed access to large language models, a shared substrate for experimental data, and end-to-end provenance. The U.S. Department of Energy Genesis Mission platform, delivered through the American Science Cloud, provides these as reusable services. This paper reports how the Genesis platform enables cross-facility experiments and accelerates scientific discovery. We explore the plant phenotyping workflow of the Orchestrated Platform for Autonomous Laboratories as the exemplar: it couples Oak Ridge National Laboratory's Advanced Plant Phenotyping Laboratory with the Frontier supercomputer. In a 40-day nickel-treatment campaign, the resulting workflow replaced roughly twelve hours of manual analysis with interactive queries returning in seconds to minutes.

</details>


### [50] [ContinuumBench: Benchmarking Joint Autoscaling and Placement Across Evaluation Regimes in the Cloud-Edge Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08946)
*Lanpei Li,Antonino Vaccarella,Vincenzo Lomonaco,Massimo Coppola*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ContinuumBench是一个基准测试框架，用于在受控条件下评估云-边缘控制器中的服务放置与弹性伸缩策略。研究发现，在所研究的容量受限场景中，弹性容量而非放置复杂度决定了任务完成率；自动伸缩策略而非放置优化是决定延迟合规的关键因素。完成感知的计分方式与仅考虑完成任务的计分方式会产生不同的控制器排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云-边缘控制器的评估常常混淆性能增益来源：放置和伸缩被单独研究、工作负载与连通性假设不明确、基于已完成任务的指标掩盖了未完成任务。缺乏一个控制这些因素的统一评估框架。

Method: 提出ContinuumBench基准，建立在ECLYPSE模拟器之上，引入到达（arrivals）、工作者弹性（worker elasticity）、间歇传输、缓冲和故障等机制以闭环控制。采用完成感知（completion-aware）核算方法，将迟到、未完成和丢弃的任务均视为截止期违规。通过统一协议比较仅放置型（placement-only）与可伸缩型（scale-capable）控制器，在声明的场景和压力条件下进行多场景多机制评估。

Result: 在九种控制器、四种场景和两种机制下评估发现：所研究机制以容量受限为主，弹性容量而非放置复杂度推动完成率；容量充足后，自动伸缩策略决定任务按时到达的比例。无迁移时放置重规划没有可测量的效果，而免费迁移构成例外。可伸缩型控制器接近过度配置参考线，仅放置型控制器随负载增加而性能下降；放置质量仅在容量耗尽时才能区分控制器。此外，计分方式本身影响结论——按完成计数与完成感知计分可能产生不同的控制器排名。

Conclusion: 评估设计中的核算选择会实质性地改变结论，完成感知指标对于公正比较控制器至关重要。研究提示研究者应明确区分容量弹性与放置优化的贡献，避免混淆二者的性能增益来源。

Abstract: Cloud-edge controllers coordinate service placement, replica scaling, and resource pre-warming to keep end-to-end latency within application deadlines. But evaluations often obscure the source of a reported gain: placement and scaling are studied separately; workload, connectivity, and calibration assumptions remain implicit; and metrics over completed tasks hide unfinished work. We present ContinuumBench, a benchmark that controls these factors. Its completion-aware accounting treats late, unfinished, and discarded tasks as deadline misses. A common protocol compares placement-only and scale-capable controllers under declared regimes and stressors. Built on the ECLYPSE simulator, ContinuumBench adds arrivals, worker elasticity, intermittent transport, buffering, and failures to close the control loop. We evaluate nine controllers across four scenarios and two regimes. The studied regimes are capacity-bound: elastic capacity, not placement sophistication, drives completion, and once capacity suffices, the choice of autoscaling policy decides how much of that work arrives on time. Placement re-planning has no measurable effect without relocation, while cost-free migration defines the observed exception. Consequently, scale-capable controllers approach an over-provisioned reference while placement-only controllers degrade with load; and placement quality separates controllers only once capacity is exhausted. Finally, the accounting choice itself changes the reported result: completion-only and completion-aware scoring can rank controllers differently.

</details>

### [51] [AhaBench: Do Agents Learn from Prior Experience? A Benchmark for Long-Horizon Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05435)
*Zerui Cheng,Jiawei Xu,Huacan Chai,Jiayang Sun,Pramod Viswanath,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AhaBench是一个评估语言智能体在长期任务中"经验学习能力"的基准套件，由三个组件组成（Aha-Puzzle、Aha-Euler、Aha-Vending），通过三部分记分卡（初始得分、经验后得分、学习增益）来衡量模型在获得有用经验后是否能将其转化为相关条件下的行为改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数评估在单次提示后重置智能体，或仅评分一条轨迹的最终状态，无法回答"当模型获得有用经验后，在移除/改变/延迟支持的相关条件下，其后续行为是否得到改善"这一核心操作性问题。

Method: 构建包含三个组件的基准套件：Aha-Puzzle测试在解决隐藏状态谜题后能否进行无提示探索；Aha-Euler将Project-Euler式数学思想转化为教/留出任务对并带精确验证器；Aha-Vending模拟售货智能体在延迟反馈和运营事故中的盈利能力。使用三部分记分卡（初始得分、经验后得分、学习增益）进行分解评估。

Result: 在八模型面板上，Claude Opus 4.6在经验后综合得分（64.3）和学习增益（+25.8）上领先，Gemini 3.1 Pro以63.4紧随其后。分解结果显示：谜题轨迹提升有支持的得分但难以转化为无提示探索行为；Aha-Euler完整教学达78.6-100.0%，而仅答案迁移仅为0.0-73.9%；Aha-Vending区分了盈利的事故处理与破产和无订单失败。

Conclusion: AhaBench的核心实证发现是：能善用显式支持的模型、能达到高经验后得分的模型、以及在运行中提升最多的模型并不总是同一批模型，这表明现有评估指标可能掩盖了不同能力的差异。论文发布了完整的基准任务、评分标准、验证器、模拟器代码及接口。

Abstract: Modern language agents are expected to operate over long horizons: they ask follow-up questions, reuse worked examples, handle tool feedback, and adapt to delayed consequences. Most evaluations still reset the agent after a prompt or score only the final state of one trajectory. AhaBench asks a more operational question: when a fixed model receives useful experience, does its later behavior improve under a related evaluation condition where the obvious support has been removed, changed, or delayed? The suite contains three components. Aha-Puzzle tests no-hint exploration after solved hidden-state puzzles; Aha-Euler turns Project-Euler-style mathematical ideas into generated taught/held-out tasks with exact validators; and Aha-Vending, an open-source implementation inspired by Vending-Bench, tests whether a simulated vending agent remains profitable while handling delayed feedback and operational incidents. AhaBench reports a three-part scorecard: Initial Score measures starting competence, Post-Experience Score measures the later empirical outcome, and Learning Lift is their difference. This decomposition is the main empirical message: models that use visible support well, models that reach high post-experience scores, and models that improve most during a run are not always the same. On the common eight-model panel, Claude Opus 4.6 leads aggregate Post-Experience Score at 64.3 and aggregate Learning Lift at +25.8, with Gemini 3.1 Pro close behind at 63.4. The component results explain the split: puzzle traces raise supported scores but often fail to become no-hint exploration behavior; Aha-Euler full teaching reaches 78.6-100.0% while answer-only transfer ranges from 0.0 to 73.9%; and Aha-Vending separates profitable incident handling from bankruptcy and no-order failure. We release benchmark tasks, rubrics, validators, simulator code, and interfaces for evaluating new agents.

</details>


### [52] [When Do Options Help? Policy Necrosis and Redundant Coverage in Option-Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05508)
*Bingyun Liu,Yuheng Jing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过理论与实验揭示了Option-Critic算法的三个关键发现：其终止规则实际与"始终终止"等价、option内部策略存在"策略坏死"现象、多个option带来性能提升的真正原因是降低了所有option在同一状态同时失败的概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Option-Critic算法声称随着options数量增加性能会提升，但缺乏对其内在机制的解释。作者希望深入剖析该算法性能提升的真正原因，理解其内部组件各自的实际贡献。

Method: 通过理论分析证明终止规则在特定条件下的等价性，提出状态层面的"策略坏死"检测方法，并通过实验验证三个核心发现：终止规则的无贡献性、内部策略的探索不足问题，以及多option效果源于联合失败概率的下降。

Result: 研究发现终止规则实际与始终终止等价；在典型option中约五分之三（60%）的状态发生策略坏死；强制每步终止后option数量曲线不变；多个option使所有option在同一状态同时失败的概率从59%降至4%。

Conclusion: Option-Critic的性能提升并非来自其termination机制或单个option内部策略的复杂性，而是源于多个option在状态空间中的"互补性"——降低了系统性失败的风险。修复探索问题后，单个option也能完成任务。

Abstract: Option-critic learns options: sub-policies together with a learned rule for when each one hands control back. Its headline result is that performance improves as options are added. We explain that result, with theory and experiment. First, the termination rule option-critic learns by maximising return contributes nothing. When the termination test and the policy that picks options read the same values, the test fires at every step, so the learned rule is identical to always terminating. When that policy explores and the test does not, as in option-critic itself, the rule can block the exploration; there are instances where it suffers $Ω(T)$ regret while always terminating holds to $O(\log T)$. Forcing termination at every step leaves the option-count curve intact. Second, the policy inside an option barely explores at all, so a state locks onto the first action that looked good and never updates again. We name this policy necrosis, give a state-level test for it, and find three fifths of states necrotic in a typical option. Restoring exploration repairs those states, and one option then solves the task. Third, extra options improve no option; what falls is the chance that all of them fail in the same state, from $59\\%$ to $4\\%$, and performance follows that joint quantity.

</details>


### [53] [Multi-granularity Adaptive Hypergraph Representation Learning via Granular-ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05574)
*Sen Zhao,Yifan Guan,Jinyuan Ni,Gaojie Xu,Zhang Xu,Xiaoyu Lian,Yi Liu,Yi Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MGHRL框架，通过自适应粒度超图生成策略（基于粒度球的自适应分裂）和多种子网络的多粒度超图网络，高效捕获图中多粒度高阶关系，显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超图表示学习方法大多依赖预定义的规则生成超边，未能考虑图拓扑结构的多样性和超边的多粒度特性，从而难以自适应地发现高阶层关系并高效处理复杂结构信息。

Method: 提出多粒度超图表示学习框架MGHRL，包含两个核心组件：(1) 自适应粒度超图生成策略，通过粒度球的自适应分裂在多个粒度层级生成超边，依据图拓扑结构捕获高层阶关系；(2) 多粒度超图网络，包含多个子网络以捕获不同粒度超边的特征，并通过层次化可逆连接进行特征整合。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验结果显示，MGHRL的性能显著优于现有的基线模型。

Conclusion: MGHRL通过自适应、多粒度的超图生成方式和多子网络的特征集成机制，有效克服了传统方法在超边生成上的局限，为超图表示学习提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Hypergraph representation learning aims to capture high-order information in graphs by constructing hyperedges that simultaneously connect multiple nodes. These hyperedges adapt to the graph's topological features, facilitating the extraction of high-order relationships at multiple granularities. Most prior work relies on predefined definitions to generate hyperedges, overlooking the diversity in graph topological structures and the multi-granularity characteristics of hyperedges. As a result, this limits their ability to effectively and adaptively discover high-order relationships and efficiently process complex structural information. To address this limitation, we propose a novel framework called \underline{M}ulti-\underline{G}ranularity \underline{H}ypergraph \underline{R}epresentation \underline{L}earning (MGHRL). MGHRL introduces an Adaptive Granular Hypergraph Generation strategy, which generates hyperedges at multiple levels of granularity through the adaptive splitting of granular-ball, effectively capturing high-order relationships based on the graph's topological structure. Additionally, we propose a Multi-Granularity Hypergraph Network with multiple sub-networks, capturing features from hyperedges at different granularities and integrating them via hierarchical reversible connections. Experimental results show that MGHRL significantly outperforms baseline models on benchmark datasets.

</details>


### [54] [Capsule Lens: Locating and Tracking Concept Geometry in Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05575)
*Yiming Tang,Harshvardhan Saini,Samyak Jha,Huaming Chen,Xufeng Duan,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Capsule Lens框架，用封闭形式的胶囊几何形状来刻画概念在模型内部表示中的占据区域，并分别应用于静态和动态表示分析，揭示不同训练设置下的概念几何动态变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性研究中现有方法主要将表示映射到可解释空间，缺乏对概念如何在表示空间中占据区域的直接刻画。已有假设缺乏严格验证且多聚焦于静态表示，难以追踪概念几何的动态变化。

Method: Capsule Lens框架将概念占据的区域拟合为一种简单可追踪的几何形式——胶囊，由若干可解释参数定义，以封闭形式拟合概念几何并在保留样本上验证。该框架应用于静态与动态两类表示。

Result: 静态表示上成功定位多种模型的概念几何并揭示重要几何特征；动态表示上三个案例（CLIP预训练、VQA的RL后训练、数学推理的RL后训练）显示出质上不同的几何动态，从CLIP预训练的广泛网络级重构到RL后训练的局部概念特定变化。发现与既有文献一致，也有新的观察。

Conclusion: Capsule Lens作为定位、分析、追踪静态与动态表示中概念几何的有力工具，有助于深化对深度学习可解释性的理解。

Abstract: Understanding how concepts are encoded in the internal representations of machine learning models is a central problem in mechanistic interpretability, essential both for the science of deep learning and for the trustworthy deployment of increasingly capable models. Existing approaches to interpret model representations mainly map representations onto more interpretable spaces and do not directly characterize how concepts occupy representation space; various hypotheses have been proposed, but often lack of rigorous validation and largely focus on static representations. In this work, we introduce Capsule Lens, a framework that matches the region a concept occupies with a simple, trackable geometric form, a capsule, defined by several interpretable parameters, fitted in closed form to each concept's geometry and validated on held-out samples. We apply Capsule Lens in two major settings: static and dynamic representations. On static representations, we demonstrate how to locate concept geometry across various models, and how the span and norm curves uncover important geometric characteristics. On dynamic representations, we present three case studies tracking representation drifts induced by distinct training settings, CLIP pretraining, RL post-training on visual question answering, and RL post-training on mathematical reasoning. These analyses reveal qualitatively different geometric dynamics, ranging from broad network-wide restructuring in CLIP pretraining to localized and concept-specific changes in RL post-training. Our results include findings aligned with existing literature as well as novel observations. We believe Capsule Lens stands as a promising tool for locating, analyzing, and tracking concept geometry in both static and dynamic representations.

</details>


### [55] [HB-PVI: A Hierarchical Bayesian Personalization and Value-of-Information Framework for Complex Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05582)
*Hammed A. Olayinka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HB-PVI框架，一个分层贝叶斯个性化与信息价值框架，用于联合建模参与者异质性、四种个性化机制的收益与危害，以及额外标签的经济价值。在47名参与者的MUSIC-CAR复杂活动队列中评估发现，个性化增益通常很小甚至为负，基于信息价值的停止规则在所有决策状态下都选择不购买标签，相比固定的十次适配个性化可减少100%的标注成本，同时保持可忽略的性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化可以提升活动识别性能，但参与者间增益存在异质性，且每个额外校准标签都有获取成本。现有方法缺乏一个统一框架来同时权衡个性化收益、潜在危害和标签获取的经济价值，难以支持"是否值得为每个参与者收集标签"的部署决策。

Method: 构建分层贝叶斯个性化与信息价值（HB-PVI）框架：采用泄漏安全的留一参与者评估，结合顺序蒙特卡洛参与者效应更新器、Student-t分层增益模型，以及一步期望样本信息价值（EVSI）停止规则。对四种个性化机制（适配器、适配器加头部、原型残差等）进行联合建模，在经济成本-效益框架下评估每种机制。

Result: 适配器个性化产生小的正向平均F1增益（1个标签时0.00099增至10个标签时0.00198），而适配器加头部和原型残差个性化平均为负。在实际效益阈值和成本设定下，一步EVSI在所有决策状态均为零，政策决定不购买任何标签，保留群体推断。相比固定十次适配个性化减少100%标注，后验均值F1损失仅0.00217，后验概率0.9992低于0.005容差。HB-PVI在216个成本阈值设置中的199个为效用最优。

Conclusion: 当个性化增益相对于标注、计算和危害成本较小时，应优先采用群体优先部署策略。在健康传感应用中，信息价值推理而非原始预测精度应当驱动个性化决策。

Abstract: Personalization can improve activity-recognition performance, but participant-specific gains are heterogeneous, and every additional calibration label has an acquisition cost. This study presents HB-PVI, a hierarchical Bayesian personalization and value-of-information framework jointly modeling participant heterogeneity, the benefit and harm of four personalization mechanisms, and the economic value of an additional label, for the 47-participant MUSIC-CAR complex-activity cohort. A leakage-safe, leave-one-participant-out evaluation combines a sequential-Monte-Carlo participant-effect updater with a Student-$t$ hierarchical gain model and a one-step expected-value-of-sample-information (EVSI) stopping rule. Adapter personalization produced small positive mean F1 gains, growing from 0.00099 at one label to 0.00198 at ten, while adapter-plus-head and prototype-residual personalization were negative on average. Under the primary practical-benefit threshold ($Δ_{\min}=0.01$) and cost setting, one-step EVSI was zero at every decision state, so the policy purchased no labels and retained population inference for all 47 participants, matching always-stop exactly (region-of-practical-equivalence probability $=1$). Relative to fixed ten-shot adapter personalization, this reduced labeling by 100\% while keeping the posterior mean F1 loss at 0.00217 (95\% credible interval, 0.00048 to 0.00389), with posterior probability 0.9992 of remaining below the 0.005 tolerance. HB-PVI was utility-optimal in 199 of 216 cost-threshold settings and in every setting at or above the primary label cost. These results argue for a population-first deployment policy whenever personalization gains are small relative to labeling, computation, and harm costs, and show that value-of-information reasoning, not raw predictive accuracy, should drive personalization decisions in health-sensing applications.

</details>


### [56] [Endogenous Exploration in Reinforcement Learning with Intrinsic Curiosity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05650)
*Armando Vieira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由内在好奇心驱动的强化学习框架，用于非平稳、稀疏或缺失奖励的环境。该框架结合外部奖励与认知动机机制，假设中等程度的"不连贯性"下探索效果最佳，并基于液态状态机（LSM）实现，在LunarLander-v2和BipedalWalker-v3基准上与PPO和ICM等深度RL算法达到竞争性表现。实验还发现Active Inference无法复现该好奇心窗口，表明所提动力学探索了不同的探索机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习在奖励稀疏、延迟或完全缺失的环境中探索效率低下，缺乏有效的内在动机引导机制来应对非平稳环境下的结构化探索需求。

Method: 构建结合外部奖励和认知动机（epistemic motivation）的RL框架，通过调节系统的"不连贯性"水平来引导探索方向，并以液态状态机（LSM）作为实现基座进行验证。

Result: 在LunarLander-v2和BipedalWalker-v3两个基准任务上，该方法与PPO和ICM等主流深度RL算法相比取得了竞争性的性能表现；同时验证了中等不连贯性水平下探索最优的假设，并揭示该方法与Active Inference在探索机制上存在本质差异。

Conclusion: 好奇心驱动的中间态探索窗口是有效探索的关键条件，本框架为处理复杂非平稳环境中的稀疏奖励问题提供了一种新的可行路径，其探索动力学区别于现有的Active Inference方法。

Abstract: We propose a reinforcement learning framework in which exploration is driven by intrinsic curiosity, designed for scenarios where environments are non-stationary and rewards are sparse, delayed, uninformative, or absent. In our model, action selection is guided by a combination of external rewards and an epistemic motivation mechanism that biases the agent toward structured exploratory directions. The central hypothesis is that effective exploration emerges at intermediate levels of incoherence, while performance degrades under both overly rigid and overly disordered dynamics. To test this idea, we implement the framework on top of a Liquid State Machine (LSM) substrate and evaluate it on two standard benchmarks: the discrete-action LunarLanderv2 and the continuous-control BipedalWalkerv3. The proposed method achieves competitive performance on both tasks relative to established deep RL algorithms, including Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) and Intrinsic Curiosity Module (ICM). We further show that the curiosity window is not recovered in Active Inference agents under the same analysis, suggesting that the proposed dynamics capture a distinct exploration regime

</details>


### [57] [Robustness of LLM-Generated SystemVerilog Assertions to Semantics-Preserving RTL Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05658)
*FNU Aditi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受控的蜕变评估方法，用于检验LLM生成SystemVerilog断言（SVA）在语义保持的RTL变换下的稳定性。基于VERT数据集构建了40个程序的评估集，对Qwen2.5-Coder-7B和DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite两个模型在操作数重排、标识符重命名和冗余括号三种变换下进行测试。结果显示，9.7%-27.0%的原本正确的行为在变换后变得错误，表明单一的准确率指标无法充分反映LLM在断言生成中的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成SVA的评测通常只报告单一语法表示下的正确性，这种"点准确率"无法揭示当同一RTL行为以不同方式书写时模型输出的稳定性，因此无法真实反映模型的鲁棒性和可靠性。

Method: 从VERT数据集构建质量过滤的控制池和分层40程序评估集（包含295个赋值行为），对两个开源代码模型使用相同提示词和贪心解码，研究三种语义保持变换。除基线准确率外，还测量条件鲁棒性、不变性失败和任意翻转率，并使用10,000样本聚类自助法区间在RTL程序级别进行分析。

Result: 在所有六种模型-变换条件下，9.7%-27.0%原本正确的行为在语义保持变换后出错。标识符重命名下DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite准确率从53.9%提升到63.7%，但同时19.5%的原本正确行为失败。人工审查30个正确转错误的样例，识别出路径谓词丢失、分支极性错误、布尔结构损坏和输出契约违规四类问题。

Conclusion: 点准确率不足以表征LLM在断言生成中的可靠性，需要引入鲁棒性感知的评估方法用于AI辅助硬件验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being explored for automating SystemVerilog Assertion (SVA) generation, yet most evaluations report correctness on a single syntactic representation of an input. Such point accuracy does not reveal whether a model's correct output is stable when the same RTL behavior is written differently. This paper presents a controlled metamorphic evaluation of LLM-based SVA generation under semantics-preserving RTL transformations. Starting from the VERT dataset, we construct a quality-filtered conditional-control pool and a stratified 40-program evaluation set containing 295 assignment behaviors. We evaluate two open code models, Qwen2.5-Coder-7B and DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite, with an identical evaluation prompt and greedy decoding. Three transformations are studied: operand reordering, deterministic identifier renaming, and redundant parenthesization. Beyond baseline and transformed accuracy, we measure conditional robustness, invariance failure, and any-flip rate, with 10,000-sample clustered bootstrap intervals at the RTL-program level. Across all six model-transformation conditions, 9.7%-27.0% of behaviors that were correct on the original RTL become incorrect after a semantics-preserving transformation. Aggregate accuracy can therefore hide substantial instability: under identifier renaming, DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite improves from 53.9% to 63.7% accuracy while 19.5% of its originally correct behaviors fail. Manual review of 30 sampled correct-to-wrong transitions identifies dropped path predicates, branch-polarity errors, Boolean-structure corruption, and output-contract violations. The results show that point accuracy alone is insufficient for characterizing LLM reliability in assertion generation and motivate robustness-aware evaluation for AI-assisted hardware verification.

</details>


### [58] [PAC-Private Autoregressive Generation: Calibrating Noise to Ensemble Disagreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05676)
*Mina Mirzadehsarcheshmeh,Amir Keyvan Khandani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将PAC隐私从分类任务扩展到自回归生成任务。通过构造128个重叠世界并训练每世界适配器，利用世界间投票分歧决定PAC噪声量，实现了在每token极低隐私预算下保留大量微调性能，同时在成员推断攻击下优于PMixED方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在私有文本上微调后通常通过API提供服务，隐私泄露主要发生在生成输出而非模型权重。现有方法如PMixED每次发布都消耗隐私预算且长期依赖公共模型；PAC隐私虽能根据输出稳定性自适应地添加噪声，但尚未从分类任务扩展到自回归生成场景。

Method: 构造m=128个重叠世界，每条记录恰好出现在m/2个世界中，在冻结的公共模型上为每个世界训练一个适配器。真实世界即为秘密。在每个token生成时，公共模型定义候选集，各世界投票，其后验加权分歧决定PAC噪声量；若各世界一致则无需校准噪声。同时引入了耦合解码策略，在保持隐私核算的同时避免贪心解码导致的退化问题。

Result: 在WikiText-103和GPT-2-small上，以每token 2^-32的隐私预算保留了74%的微调收益；在10^6个token后成员推断成功率被限制在51.08%；后验熵估计的泄露约为实际预算的17%。与PMixED在相同成员推断约束下对比，在10^2到10^6 token范围内保留了98%的非隐私性能空间，而PMixED最多56%，且无交叉。

Conclusion: PAC隐私成功扩展至自回归生成领域，能够在极低隐私预算下大幅保留模型性能，显著优于PMixED。同时指出推理隐私不等于内容保护——即使成员推断优势为零，被记忆的canary仍可能以相同速率被生成，这为隐私保护机制的设计提供了重要边界认知。

Abstract: Language models adapted on private text are often served through APIs, so privacy leakage occurs through generated outputs rather than exposed weights. Private prediction protects these releases. Methods such as PMixED incur privacy cost at each release and increasingly rely on the public model over long horizons. PAC privacy instead calibrates noise to output variability across possible secrets, adding less noise when predictions are stable. To our knowledge, PAC-private prediction has not previously been extended from classification to autoregressive generation.
  We construct $m=128$ overlapping worlds from the private corpus, with each record appearing in exactly $m/2$ worlds, and train one adapter per world over a frozen public model. The realized world is the secret. At each token, the public model defines a candidate set, the worlds vote, and their posterior-weighted disagreement determines the PAC noise; unanimity requires no calibration noise. We prove $I(S;Y_{1:T}) \leq I(S;H_T) \leq bT$. Our contributions are extending PAC privacy to autoregressive generation, handling adaptive self-generated contexts, and introducing coupled decoding that preserves privacy accounting while avoiding greedy degeneration.
  On WikiText-103 with GPT-2-small, we retain 74% of the fine-tuning gain at a per-token budget of $2^{-32}$, while membership-inference success is bounded by 51.08% after $10^6$ tokens; posterior-entropy estimates of leakage are roughly 17% of the charged budget. Inference privacy is not content protection: even when membership advantage on a memorized canary is indistinguishable from zero, the canary is emitted at the same rate. Against PMixED under matched membership-inference bounds on the same data universe and test set, we retain 98% of non-private headroom from $10^2$ to $10^6$ tokens, versus at most 56%, with no crossover.

</details>

*Zhankun Luo,Abolfazl Hashemi*

TL;DR: 本文研究混合线性回归（MLR）中响应的方差保持扩散，分析了未知混合权重下分数匹配的统计保证与固定扩散噪声水平下的损失几何和优化信号。结果表明估计量收敛到真实参数，标度误差收敛到极大似然估计的高斯极限，并在低噪声和高噪声极限下给出了EM相关的梯度展开与行为刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合线性回归中响应扩散的方差保持特性以及未知混合权重下的分数匹配行为尚未被系统分析，需要将分数匹配的统计保证与损失几何、优化信号分离研究。

Method: 通过KL散度将扩散路径上的去噪分数匹配目标与似然及末态差异联系起来；在固定噪声水平下将分数匹配损失分解为交叉熵和EM算子，推导EM相关的低噪声梯度展开及隐变量方差修正项；在高噪声极限下分析各向同性协方差下该极限损失的梯度下降行为，并沿固定高信噪比射线考察分数匹配不平衡梯度与隐变量方差项的逐点收敛性。

Result: 在温和正则条件和末态调度下，估计量收敛到MLR的地面真实参数，标度误差收敛到MLE的高斯极限；低噪声下得到含隐变量方差修正项的EM相关梯度展开；高噪声下沿固定高信噪比射线，分数匹配不平衡梯度和隐变量方差项逐点趋于零；数值实验验证了理论发现与统计保证。

Conclusion: 方差保持扩散下的分数匹配为混合线性回归提供了理论上有保证的估计框架，其统计行为与MLE一致，且在不同噪声尺度下与EM算法结构存在深层联系。

Abstract: We study variance-preserving diffusion of the response in mixed linear regression (MLR) with unknown mixing weights. Our analysis separates the statistical guarantees of score matching from the loss geometry and optimization signal at a fixed diffusion noise level. The KL divergence links the denoising score matching objective integrated over the diffusion path with the likelihood and a terminal discrepancy. Under mild regularity conditions and terminal schedule, the resulting estimator converges up to the ground truth parameters of MLR, and its scaled error converges to the Gaussian limit of the maximum-likelihood estimator. At a fixed scale of the diffusion noise level, we derive a decomposition linking the score matching loss to cross-entropy and Expectation-Maximization (EM) operators. This decomposition yields an EM-related low-noise gradient expansion with additional correction terms of latent variance. In the high-noise limit, we further characterize gradient descent on this limiting loss under isotropic covariance. Along fixed high signal-to-noise ratio rays, the score matching imbalance gradient and the latent-variance term tend to zero pointwise. Numerical experiments illustrate our theoretical findings and statistical guarantees.

</details>


### [60] [GraphNOSE: A Graph Transformer in Olfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05694)
*Mrityunjay Sharma,Sarabeshwar Balaji,Valentina Parma,Ritesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphNOSE是一个开源的图变换器框架，用于从SMILES字符串预测单分子和二元混合物的多标签气味描述符。该模型在参数数量比标准GNN少六倍的情况下，平均AUROC超越线性模型、分子语言模型嵌入、分子指纹和基线GNN达4.52%，在分布外化合物上达到84%的AUROC，超越了当前最优的Open-POM（81%），并通过XAI方法提供了符合化学直觉的可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从分子结构预测气味质量是化学信息学中的开放问题。现有线性模型虽然能将分子特征与气味描述符关联，但在外推到新型化学骨架、极端分子量或复杂气味混合物时往往失效，因此需要更具泛化能力和可解释性的预测架构。

Method: GraphNOSE采用基于变换器的图架构，融合位置编码和结构编码，从SMILES字符串预测气味描述符；支持单分子和二元混合物；并应用可解释人工智能（XAI）方法识别驱动气味预测的关键子结构和分子特征。

Result: GraphNOSE在参数比标准GNN少6倍的情况下，平均AUROC超出各类基线模型4.52%（p<0.01）；在分布外化合物上达到84%的AUROC，显著优于当前最优的Open-POM（81%，p<0.001）；XAI分析发现的特征与化学直觉一致。

Conclusion: GraphNOSE确立了一种可扩展且可解释的嗅觉预测架构，能够泛化到当前感知数据库中代表性不足的结构差异化合物，为气味预测领域提供了性能更优且具备可解释性的解决方案。

Abstract: Predicting olfactory qualities from molecular structure is an open problem in chemoinformatics. Although linear models can link molecular features to odor descriptors, they often fail when extrapolating to novel chemical scaffolds, extreme molecular weights, or complex odor mixtures. To address this, we introduce GraphNOSE, an open-source graph transformer framework that predicts multi-label odor descriptors from simplified molecular-input line-entry system (SMILES) strings for single molecules and binary mixtures. By integrating positional and structural encodings within a transformer-based graph architecture, GraphNOSE achieves strong performance with six times fewer parameters than standard graph neural network (GNN) baseline while consistently outperforming linear models, molecular language model embeddings, molecular fingerprints, and baseline GNNs by an average area under the ROC curve (AUROC) margin of 4.52% (p < 0.01). GraphNOSE achieves an AUROC of 84% on out-of-distribution compounds (OODs). This exceeds the current state-of-the-art GNN for OOD in olfaction (Open-POM: 81%, p < 0.001), and identifies conditions under which linear models empirically fail. Finally, we apply XAI (explainable AI) methods to identify which substructures and molecular features drive odor predictions, yielding insights consistent with chemical intuition and grounded in the model's learned representations. Together, these results establish GraphNOSE as a scalable and interpretable architecture for olfactory prediction that generalizes to structurally distinct compounds underrepresented in current perceptual databases.

</details>

Result: 提出了三种神经网络架构并进行了评估，能够成功从ECG信号中提取呼吸波形并估计呼吸频率，实现了稳健且可扩展的无创呼吸监测。

Conclusion: 该深度学习方法为无创呼吸监测提供了一种稳健且可扩展的解决方案，有望应用于医疗保健和可穿戴技术领域。

Abstract: The paper introduces a neural network-based approach for analyzing ECG signals to estimate respiratory rate by leveraging the phe- nomenon of Respiratory Sinus Arrhythmia (RSA). Our method employs a deep learning model trained to predict respiratory waveforms directly from ECG input data. To achieve this, we developed and evaluated three different neural network architectures capable of automatically extract- ing relevant features from ECG signals without the need for manual preprocessing. The proposed approach offers a robust and scalable solu- tion for non-invasive respiratory monitoring, with potential applications in healthcare and wearable technology

</details>


### [62] [Protocol effects on feature-based hardware-Trojan detection across Trust-Hub families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07199)
*Hang Xiao,Chuhong Xu,Kainan Zhou,Gangzhen Qian,Lu Yi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对比三种测试边界（合并池、留出一整个网表、留出同一主机的所有变体）来量化基准集变体对硬件木马检测器性能评估的影响，发现剔除同一主机的全部变体会导致F1分数大幅下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Trust-Hub基准集中多个文件仅在被插入的木马方面不同而共享主机电路，当孪生变体同时出现在训练集和测试集中时，检测器可能从已见过的主机逻辑中获益，导致评估结果虚高。

Method: 系统实验：固定所有其他条件（解析器、36个门级特征、类别权重、模型设置、阈值及族级聚合），仅改变测试边界这一项。三种设置分别从合并语料池抽取测试门、留出一个完整网表、或剔除某一主机的全部变体。

Result: 随机森林F1/AP从合并池的0.914/0.978降至留出单网表的0.636/0.851，再降至留出主机族的0.460/0.577。XGBoost从0.946/0.976降至0.464/0.544。各主机族均呈相同的合并到族级下降趋势，多种稳健性检查均无法逆转该差距。

Conclusion: 孪生基准变体可能膨胀表面上的迁移性能。含有同一主机电路多个变体的基准应报告族级留出交叉验证以及全部五个族的完整结果，而不应只报告合并池分数。

Abstract: Trust-Hub reuses host circuits: several files differ mainly in the inserted Trojan. When gates from sibling variants enter both training and test folds, a detector can benefit from host logic it has already seen. We measure that effect instead of proposing another classifier. The corpus contains 49,124 gates from 16 netlists grouped into five host families. We left the parser, 36 gate features, class weighting, model settings, threshold, and family-level aggregation unchanged and altered one choice: the test boundary. The three settings draw test gates from the pooled corpus, withhold a complete netlist, or withhold every variant of one host. The choice matters. Random forest records F1/AP of 0.914/0.978 with pooled gates, 0.636/0.851 with one netlist held out, and 0.460/0.577 with a host family held out. XGBoost falls from 0.946/0.976 to 0.464/0.544 across the same comparison. Logistic regression loses AP, although its fixed-threshold F1 is not monotonic. Each family shows the same pooled-to-family direction. Feature removal, repeated model and simulator seeds, score normalization, parser-related exclusions, and a smaller sample change the size of the gap without reversing it. Aggregation also matters: a gate-weighted average is dominated by the larger ISCAS files, so the headline values give each host family one vote. Bootstrap and jackknife summaries keep the gap positive, but their folds reuse training families. We treat the five family rows as descriptive evidence rather than independent trials. Five host families are too few for a population claim, and the experiment says nothing about transfer to a new cell library or an industrial design. It supports a narrower conclusion: sibling benchmark variants can inflate apparent transfer. Benchmarks with several variants of one host circuit should report family-aware holdouts and all five family results beside pooled scores.

</details>


### [63] [Newton Matching for Generative Modeling: A Unified Framework for Fine-Tuning and Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05727)
*Zeyang Li,Yunan Wang,Paolo Giaretta,Navid Azizan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Newton Matching框架，统一了生成建模中的微调与采样。通过将标准条件匹配的总体极小化器视为规范模型，在Fisher-Rao度量和混合联络下证明反向KL的Hessian等于该度量，从而牛顿方向等同于负Fisher-Rao梯度。该框架在终端密度上通过正则化奖励驱动的切向步和保密度规范化实现，并证明了严格反向KL下降、全局收敛及局部二次收敛等理论性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成建模中的微调和采样方法依赖于孤立的损失函数设计，缺乏统一的理论框架。作者希望从"孤立损失"转向"规范模型上的迭代优化"，为奖励微调（目标为π∝μe^{τr}）和采样提供一个统一的数学基础，以桥接强化学习与生成模型的理论与算法。

Method: 作者建立从规范速度流形到密度流形的微分同胚映射，将Fisher-Rao度量和混合联络由此传输过来。在每个终端密度ρ处，算法执行：(1) 由正则化奖励r-1/τ log(ρ/μ)生成切向步；(2) 进行保终端密度规范化（canonicalization）。文中提出协方差与梯度两种形式，每种支持前向或反向回归对构造，得到具有相同总体极小化器的样本级切向更新损失，无需重要性采样或全轨迹反向传播。理论部分证明理想迭代在0<η≤τ下具有严格反向KL下降，在温和条件下全局收敛，η=τ满步长时局部二次收敛。

Result: 该方法恢复了多种代表性既有方法——它们分别被证明是精确实现、临界点一致近似或目标改变变体，从而支持模块化算法设计。批判点一致性定义为切向位移消失当且仅当ρ=π，为算法的收敛性提供了判定标准。实验结果展示了Newton Matching在细化和采样上的统一表现。

Conclusion: Newton Matching为生成模型的奖励微调和采样提供了统一的二阶优化视角，将牛顿方向的几何意义澄清为Fisher-Rao度量下的梯度下降，并建立了完整的收敛性理论。该框架不仅统一了现有方法，还为模块化算法设计与新型强化学习算法的推导提供了基础框架。

Abstract: We develop Newton Matching, a unified framework for fine-tuning and sampling in generative modeling. The target is $π\proptoμe^{τr}$, where $r$ is the reward, $τ>0$ the inverse temperature, and $μ$ denotes the pretrained model's terminal density for fine-tuning or the constant $1$ for sampling. We shift the paradigm from isolated losses to iterative optimization over canonical models: population minimizers of standard conditional matching for terminal densities. Under compatible smooth-realization assumptions, canonical velocities form a manifold diffeomorphic to the density manifold. Transporting the Fisher-Rao metric and mixture connection to this manifold, we show that the reverse-KL Hessian equals the metric, so the Newton direction coincides with the negative Fisher-Rao gradient. At terminal density $ρ$, each stage takes a tangential step generated by the regularized reward $r-\frac1τ\log(ρ/μ)$, followed by terminal-density-preserving canonicalization. This canonical retraction yields an exact finite-stepsize density characterization. For the ideal iteration, we prove strict reverse-KL descent away from the target for $0 < η\le τ$, global convergence under mild conditions, and local quadratic convergence for full steps ($η=τ$). Covariance and gradient forms, each with forward or reverse regression-pair constructions, yield sample-wise tangential-update losses with the same population minimizer, without importance sampling or full-trajectory backpropagation. We develop approximate updates and define critical-point consistency as vanishing tangential displacement if and only if $ρ=π$. We recover representative methods as exact realizations, critical-point-consistent approximations, or objective-altering variants, enabling modular algorithm design. Our work advances the theory and algorithms of reinforcement learning for generative models.

</details>


### [64] [A Multi-Source Ensemble Approach to Candidate Generation for Alternative Vacation Rental Property Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05748)
*Syed Mohammed Arshad Zaidi,Eric Rincon,Shayan Hassantabar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了度假租赁市场中替代房源候选生成（CG）的多种方法，包括协同过滤、浅层嵌入和图神经网络（GNN）。在超过200万活跃房源的平台上实验表明，融合基于物品的协同过滤与GNN检索的混合架构在Recall@300上比最强基线提升14.8%，并深入探讨了CG阶段收益如何传递到下游排序阶段的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度假租赁市场中的替代房源推荐对用户体验至关重要，但面临库存异构、地理约束、可用性快速变化和长尾分布等独特挑战，亟需高质量候选生成方法来解决这些问题。

Method: 对比了协同过滤、浅层嵌入（Hotel2Vec）和GNN三类候选生成方法，并提出了结合物品级协同过滤与GNN检索的混合架构，利用两者互补优势：CF擅长富有交互历史属性的早期召回，GNN能发现多样化非显而易见的替代品并更好处理冷启动场景。

Result: 混合架构在Recall@300上较最强基线提升14.8%；GNN嵌入单独比较浅层Hotel2Vec嵌入有48-68%的相对召回提升。此外发现更强的候选池能带来更高的下游排序质量，但这种效应因候选生成与排序器训练之间的耦合而难以归因。

Conclusion: 混合式候选生成架构在度假租赁场景中效果显著，GNN在发现多样化替代品和处理冷启动方面优势突出；论文揭示了候选生成阶段与排序阶段之间的"召回-转化差距"，提示部署者在两阶段推荐系统中需谨慎评估新检索方法的实际收益。

Abstract: Alternative property recommendations play a critical role in vacation rental marketplaces, helping users discover relevant options when viewing a specific listing. However, generating high-quality candidate alternatives presents unique challenges: heterogeneous inventory, geographic constraints, rapid availability changes, and long-tail property distributions. We present a comprehensive study of candidate generation (CG) approaches for vacation rental alternatives, comparing collaborative filtering, shallow embeddings, and graph neural network (GNN) methods.
  Our experiments on a large-scale vacation rental platform (over 2M active properties) show that a hybrid architecture combining item-based collaborative filtering with GNN-based retrieval improves Recall@300 by 14.8% over the strongest baseline, by leveraging the complementary strengths of the two sources: collaborative filtering excels at early recall for properties with rich interaction history, while GNNs discover diverse, non-obvious alternatives and handle cold-start scenarios more effectively. As a component result, GNN-based embeddings alone substantially outperform shallow Hotel2Vec embeddings (48-68% relative recall improvement across K), motivating their inclusion in the ensemble.
  Crucially, we examine how CG-stage gains carry through to the downstream ranking stage, and find that a stronger candidate pool yields higher downstream ranking quality, though attributing this effect cleanly is complicated by the coupling between candidate generation and ranker training. This recall-conversion gap is an important consideration for practitioners deploying new retrieval methods in two-stage recommendation systems.

</details>


### [65] [Data Scout: Targeted Web Crawling for Domain-Specific Pretraining Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05766)
*Chirag Garg,Eelaaf Zahid,Farhan Ahmed,Jay Pankaj Gala,Eric Butler,Heiko Ludwig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Data Scout方法，颠覆传统"过滤抓取档案"的思路，转而使用LLM扩展主题生成搜索查询来引导定向网络爬取，从根源上获取领域特定预训练语料。以数学领域为例，该方法爬取到的页面中21.9%为高质量数学内容（比过滤CommonCrawl高70倍），且63.2%的页面在CommonCrawl中不存在但同样有效。在1.9B token上继续预训练Llama-3.2-3B，GSM8k表现与FineMath语料相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流构建领域特定预训练语料的方法是通过过滤CommonCrawl等大型网络档案，但这在热门领域效果好、在专业领域效果差——相关内容稀少且往往超出流行度驱动型爬虫的覆盖范围。需要一种新方法来获取被传统方法遗漏的专业领域内容。

Method: Data Scout方法包含三个步骤：(1) LLM将根主题扩展为分类体系和数千个搜索查询；(2) 返回的URL（种子）按子域名分组；(3) 使用用户提供的分类器（probe）筛选每个子域名，仅基于小样本判断是否收录整个子域名。该方法基于"相关性在子域名层面具有清晰边界"的发现（数学领域子域名内页面相关性是兄弟子域名的21倍）。

Result: 使用FineMath分类器作为probe，Data Scout爬取页面中有21.9%为高质量数学内容，是从可比网络样本中过滤得到的0.31%比率的70倍。63.2%的爬取页面未出现在CommonCrawl中但同样可用于训练。以Llama-3.2-3B在1.9B Data Scout token上继续预训练，在GSM8k上的表现与使用FineMath语料相当。

Conclusion: Data Scout通过从源头定向爬取代替事后过滤，为专业领域预训练语料获取提供了更高效且更全面的方案。因为probe是唯一的领域特定组件，该方法原则上可适用于任何拥有此类分类器的领域。

Abstract: The dominant approach to building domain-specific pretraining corpora is to filter large web archives such as CommonCrawl. This works well for popular domains but breaks down for specialized ones, where relevant content is sparse and often beyond the reach of popularity-driven crawlers. We present Data Scout, which inverts this: instead of filtering an archive, it directs a targeted crawl. An LLM expands a root topic into a taxonomy and thousands of search queries; the returned URLs (seeds) are grouped by subdomain and screened with a user-supplied classifier (the probe), admitting each subdomain on the basis of a small sample. This works because relevance has a sharp boundary at the subdomain level: in mathematics, a page is 21x more likely to be relevant than one on a sibling subdomain. With the FineMath classifier as the probe, 21.9% of crawled pages are high-quality math content, 70x the 0.31% rate from filtering a comparable web sample, so the crawl wastes far less effort. But the payoff is not just efficiency: 63.2% of these pages are missing from CommonCrawl altogether, yet just as useful for training. Continued pretraining of Llama-3.2-3B on 1.9B Data Scout tokens matches FineMath corpus on GSM8k. Because the probe is the only domain-specific component, Data Scout can in principle apply to any domain with such a classifier.

</details>


### [66] [RAPTOR: Role-Aware Private Training for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05770)
*Duc Dm,Khai Le-Duc,Nguyen Do,Minh Son Hoang,Florent Draye,Thai Hoang,Hoang Phuong Dam,Jiarui Liu,Chris Ngo,Terry Jingchen Zhang,Anh Le Duc Tran,Nhat Do Minh,Minh Ngoc Le,My T. Thai,Ran Xu,Silvio Savarese,Mona Diab,Bernhard Schölkopf,Zhijing Jin,Huy L. Nguyen,Daeyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RAPTOR框架，针对差分隐私(DP)微调稀疏MoE模型时的三个失效模式（全局裁剪抑制专家梯度、批归一化稀释稀疏专家更新、固定隐私噪声降低低负载专家信噪比），采用角色感知的交替优化策略、专家级裁剪与噪声、公开期望分母和与计数无关的更新调度，证明了满足(ε,δ)-DP，并在多个模型和GLUE任务上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DP微调方法将MoE视为单个稠密块统一处理，忽略了共享层见所有数据而专家仅见路由记录的结构差异，导致三个已识别的失效模式严重影响稀疏专家的训练效果。

Method: 提出RAPTOR框架：交替优化共享层与专家层，分别针对三个失效模式采用专家特定裁剪与噪声机制、公开期望分母估计器、不依赖私有实现计数的更新调度；通过每个记录恰分配给一个专属专家的并行组合原理实现层内专家机制并行合成，无需额外隐私成本多专家更新；推导公开分母估计器的偏差-方差分解以确定需要保护的层。

Result: 在Switch Transformer、OLMoE和DeepSeek-VL2-Tiny上的GLUE任务实验中，RAPTOR在多个隐私预算ε下一致优于标准DP基线，在最严格的隐私预算下提升幅度最大。

Conclusion: RAPTOR通过角色感知的DP训练框架有效解决了MoE模型DP微调中的结构性问题，在不增加隐私成本的前提下提升了多专家更新效率，且代码和模型公开可用。

Abstract: Differentially private (DP) fine-tuning methods treat sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models as a single dense block, ignoring that shared layers see all data while experts only see routed records. We identify and formally characterize three resulting failure modes: global clipping suppresses expert gradients, batch-level normalization dilutes sparse expert updates, and fixed privacy noise degrades signal-to-noise ratio on low-load experts. We introduce RAPTOR - a Role-Aware Private Training framework, which alternates shared and expert optimization and targets each failure directly, using expert-specific clipping and noise together with a public expected-owner denominator and a count-independent update schedule that avoids conditioning on private, realized expert counts. We prove the resulting mechanism satisfies $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP: because each record is assigned to exactly one owner expert, per-expert mechanisms within a layer compose in parallel, so updating all $E$ experts costs no more, in privacy terms, than updating one, with shared and expert streams composing sequentially across training. We further derive a bias-variance decomposition of the public-denominator estimator showing its bias grows predictably with routing imbalance, yielding a privacy-free rule for selecting which layer to protect from routing entropy measured on a small public corpus. Experiments on Switch Transformer and OLMoE fine-tuning across GLUE tasks, and on DeepSeek-VL2-Tiny, show consistent gains over standard DP baselines across several privacy levels ($\varepsilon$), with the largest margins typically at the tightest budgets. Code and models are publicly available: https://github.com/leduckhai/RAPTOR

</details>


### [67] [Nonlinear elliptic homogenization with the parametric Deep Ritz method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05778)
*Conor Rowan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用参数化Deep Ritz方法求解非线性均匀化中的参数化胞元问题，通过神经网络同时离散空间和宏观状态依赖，获得连续可微的胞元响应表示，相比传统FE²方案显著加速宏尺度求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性均匀化问题需要求解参数化胞元问题，而传统数值方法（如FE²）计算代价高；同时宏尺度牛顿迭代需要胞元响应的可微表示，现有方法难以同时满足高效性和可微性需求。

Method: 利用胞元问题的变分结构，采用Deep Ritz方法，将胞元响应对空间坐标和宏观状态的依赖同时用神经网络离散化，通过强加边界条件，实现参数化Deep Ritz方法在一次求解中获得整个宏观状态范围内的胞元响应。

Result: 该方法在精度和效率上表现良好，提供了胞元响应关于宏观状态及其梯度的连续可微表示，相较于传统FE²方法显著加速了宏尺度问题的求解。

Conclusion: 参数化Deep Ritz方法为非线性均匀化的胞元问题提供了一种高效、准确且可微的求解框架，在宏尺度计算中具有明显的效率优势。

Abstract: Elliptic homogenization is used to determine coarse-grained properties of materials with features on small scales. When these small scale features have rapid, periodic fluctuations, the solution field corresponding to a homogenized constitutive relation closely resembles the true solution based on the heterogeneous material. This homogenized behavior of the material is computed from a cell problem, where a cell is defined to be one period of the fluctuating material. In the context of linear elliptic partial differential equations, the homogenized constitutive relation is defined simply by a constant coefficient tensor, but for nonlinear problems, the homogenized response depends on the macroscopic state and/or its gradient, thus requiring solutions to parametric cell problems. When computing a numerical solution with the homogenized constitutive relation, it is useful to have a differentiable representation of the solution to the cell problem, as derivatives of the homogenized constitutive relation are required in Newton iterations for the macroscopic state field. In this work, we use the Deep Ritz method to solve the parametric cell problems that arise from nonlinear homogenization. First, we exploit the variational structure of the cell problem, then we discretize the dependence of the cell response on both space and the macroscopic state with a neural network. Enforcing boundary conditions on the cell response strongly, we next use the parametric Deep Ritz method to simultaneously solve the cell problem over a range of macroscopic states. We show that this method is accurate, efficient, and offers a continuous and differentiable representation of the cell response over the macroscopic state and gradient. We then show that our parametric representation of the cell response significantly expedites macroscale solutions when compared to a traditional $\text{FE}^2$ scheme.

</details>


### [68] [Online Learning with LLM Experts from Limited Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05820)
