It separates evidence-free reference restoration from signed token-level evidence corrections. Fisher Evidence Contrast attenuates corrections along an estimated evidence-presence direction. A stopped token-wise ZPD controller scales acceptance according to local reward alignment and Fisher movement cost, while the reference channel remains independent of acceptance. Across five scientific-reasoning and tool-use tasks, the best variant on each backbone exceeds the strongest compared baseline in average score. The averages rise from 0.6826 to 0.6857 on Qwen3-4B, from 0.6895 to 0.7058 on Qwen3-8B, and from 0.4751 to 0.5657 on Llama-3.2-1B.

</details>


### [104] [Robust Decentralized Federated Distillation via Multi-Modality Knowledge Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07230)
*Xiao Ma,Hong Shen,Hui Tian,Wei Ke,Wenqi Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种鲁棒的分散式联邦蒸馏方法，使异构模型客户端能够通过共享的公共数据预测进行协作。该方法通过三种模态（类别预测、边界决策、预测相关性）评估接收到的预测，过滤不可靠客户端并分配可靠性权重，构建多类型知识教师，并通过私有数据监督梯度验证蒸馏梯度，移除冲突梯度以抵御拜占庭攻击。在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上的实验表明该方法在非IID数据和拜占庭攻击下显著提升了异构模型的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在去中心化联邦学习环境中（如边缘计算、任务导向的无人机协作），客户端可能面临接收端特异的拜占庭恶意预测消息，现有联邦蒸馏方法缺乏针对异构模型在不安全场景下的鲁棒性保障。

Method: 提出一种三模态（类别预测、边界决策、预测相关性）评估机制来过滤不可靠客户端；对保留的客户端赋予基于可靠性的权重，为每类知识构建教师模型；利用私有数据计算的监督梯度验证蒸馏梯度，移除冲突的预测与边界梯度，抑制冲突的关系梯度后再进行模型更新。

Result: 证明了提出方法在拜占庭蒸馏下的收敛性，并证明其能确保拜占庭影响在跨模态融合后对蒸馏梯度和客户端私有梯度均有界。在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上的大量实验表明，该方法在非IID数据分布和拜占庭攻击下提升了异构客户端模型的预测准确性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过跨模态知识融合与梯度验证机制，为去中心化联邦学习提供了一种可靠且鲁棒的部署方案，特别适用于客户端可能遭受接收端特异恶意预测的不安全现实场景，具有在边缘计算等DFL场景中广泛应用的潜力。

Abstract: This paper propose a robust decentralized federated distillation method that enables clients with heterogeneous models to collaborate through predictions on shared unlabeled public data. In the proposed method, each client first evaluates the received predictions in three modalities of class prediction, boundary decision, and prediction correlation. It then filters unreliable clients, assigns reliability-based weights to the retained clients, and constructs a teacher for each type of knowledge. Finally, the corresponding distillation gradients are validated using a supervised gradient computed from private data. Conflicting prediction and boundary gradients are removed, and conflicting relation gradients are suppressed before the final model update. We prove the convergence of the proposed method by showing stable local optimization for honest clients under Byzantine distillation. Particularly, we show that our method ensures a bounded Byzantine influence on both distillation gradients and individual client private gradients after cross-modality fusion, thereby enabling stable local optimization for honest clienunder Byzantine distillation. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 demonstrate that the proposed method improves the prediction accuracy of heterogeneous models of clients under non-IID data and Byzantine attacks. As the booming demands of federated learning in decentralized environments such as edge computing and mission-oriented UAV collaborations, our method has a great potential for adoption of DFL in unreliable real-world scenarios where clients are exposed to receiver-specific Byzantine messages of malicious predictions.

</details>


### [105] [Parallelism Strategy Chaining for Fast Training Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07236)
*Minchul Kang,Changyong Shin,Younghun Go,Hyunho Lee,Jinwoo Jeong,Chuck Yoo,Gyeongsik Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CONA方法，通过在线策略链式切换（不再离线固定单一并行策略），根据计算吞吐和梯度统计构建代理指标动态选择最优并行策略，使GPT-3 1.3B、BERT-Large和Llama-3.2-1B达到目标验证困惑度的速度比现有最优方法快1.4-9.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的并行策略搜索方法均为离线选择单一策略来最小化每迭代时间，但这忽略了目标验证困惑度和时间-困惑度（TTP）。分析发现，在训练过程中最优策略会多次变化，离线固定单一策略导致TTP比每迭代选择最优策略序列慢1.8-11.4倍。

Method: 提出CONA（在线策略链式切换）方法：在训练过程中实时排序候选策略，使用由计算吞吐量和梯度统计构建的代理指标来评估候选策略，当新策略的代理指标更高时切换到该策略。

Result: 在GPT-3 1.3B、BERT-Large和Llama-3.2-1B上进行评估，CONA达到目标验证困惑度的速度比现有最先进方法快1.4-9.6倍，且与每迭代选择最优策略序列的困惑度差距在2.6%以内。

Conclusion: 训练过程中最优并行策略会动态变化，离线选择单一策略是次优的；在线动态切换策略可以显著加速大语言模型的训练收敛（以TTP衡量），CONA验证了这一思路的有效性。

Abstract: Selecting a parallelism strategy - the configuration of data, tensor, and pipeline parallelism degrees together with micro- and global-batch sizes - largely determines the training efficiency of large language models. State-of-the-art methods search for a parallelism strategy offline and select the single strategy that minimizes per-iteration time. But we find that they neglect the target validation perplexity and time-to-perplexity (TTP). In particular, our analysis reveals that the best strategy yielding the fastest perplexity improvement changes multiple times during training. As a result, state-of-the-art methods are 1.8-11.4x slower in TTP than the strategy sequence that selects the best strategy at each iteration. This paper proposes CONA, a new training method that introduces online strategy chaining. Instead of a single strategy selected offline, CONA ranks candidate strategies during training using a surrogate metric built from compute throughput and gradient statistics, and switches the current strategy to a new strategy with a higher metric. In our evaluation with GPT-3 1.3B, BERT-Large, and Llama-3.2-1B, CONA reaches the target validation perplexity 1.4-9.6x faster than state-of-the-art methods. Moreover, CONA closely tracks the perplexity achieved by the sequence that selects the best strategy at each iteration, within 2.6%.

</details>


### [106] [DataFlex-RL: An Evaluation Platform for RLVR Data Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06107)
*Hao Liang,Mingrui Chen,Hengyi Feng,Meiyi Qiang,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DataFlex-RL评估平台，系统比较了RLVR（带可验证奖励的强化学习）中不同数据策略的效果。在统一GRPO训练配方下，评估了13种配置、12个匹配随机种子、12个数学/逻辑/科学基准。结果发现：均匀GRPO相比未训练基线提升7.76个百分点；所有8种rollout选择/重加权方法和3种自适应混合方法均未显著优于均匀采样；评估基准的选择强烈影响结论，数学为主的6基准摘要与12基准摘要的排名呈负相关（-0.33）。总体而言，改变数据策略虽可测量地改变训练过程，但未能在均匀训练之上产生可复现的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR中的数据策略（选择哪些rollout、如何加权、哪些领域进入训练批次）对训练效果影响重大，但目前缺乏系统、标准化的评估平台来公平比较不同数据策略选择。已有研究结果不一致，部分原因是缺少统一的训练配方和足够多的随机种子，导致统计功效不足，难以得出可靠结论。

Method: 作者构建DataFlex-RL评估平台，在统一GRPO配方下对所有方法进行比较。主实验使用Qwen2.5-7B-Base、12个数学/逻辑/科学基准、12个匹配种子，评估13种配置（包括8种rollout选择/重加权方法、3种自适应混合、均匀采样等）。另在Llama-3.1-8B-Base上做了12个种子的修正扩展实验，并通过重打分9个Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct运行来量化评估基准组合的敏感性（6基准vs 12基准摘要的相关性）。

Result: 均匀GRPO在领域均衡平均准确率上比未训练基线提升7.76个百分点。8种rollout选择/重加权方法均未达到排除零的95%配对置信区间；3种自适应混合也未能以同等精度超越固定的均匀混合。Llama-3.1-8B-Base扩展实验中各方法未出现稳定胜者。评估敏感性分析显示，6基准（数学为主、不含逻辑）摘要与12基准摘要的排名相关系数为-0.33（负相关），而保留全部12基准的摘要之间高度一致。

Conclusion: 在受控实验条件下，改变数据策略虽然可测量地影响训练过程，但没有任何方法能在均匀训练之上产生可复现的稳定提升。这表明该研究范围内均匀采样已是强基线，也提示评估基准的选择对方法排名有巨大（甚至反转）的影响，未来研究应谨慎选择评估摘要，并建议在更多数据策略空间和更大模型规模上进行探索。

Abstract: Data policies for reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) determine which rollouts are used, how strongly they are weighted, and which domains contribute to subsequent training batches. We introduce DataFlex-RL, an evaluation platform for comparing these choices under a common GRPO recipe. Our primary experiment evaluates 13 configurations across 12 matched seeds using Qwen2.5-7B-Base and 12 mathematics, logic, and science benchmarks. Uniform GRPO improves the domain-balanced average accuracy by 7.76 percentage points over the untrained checkpoint. None of the eight rollout-selection or reweighting methods achieves a paired 95% confidence interval that excludes zero relative to uniform sampling, and none of the three adaptive mixtures outperforms a fixed equal mixture at the same level of precision. A corrected 12-seed extension on Llama-3.1-8B-Base places the additional methods on the same score scale as the original controls, but does not reveal a consistent winner in terms of observed mean performance. We also quantify evaluation sensitivity by rescoring nine Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct runs using a math-heavy six-benchmark summary, consisting of five mathematics benchmarks and GPQA-Diamond but no logic benchmark, and comparing it with the domain-balanced 12-benchmark summary. The resulting rankings are negatively correlated, with a correlation coefficient of -0.33, whereas summaries that retain all 12 benchmarks largely agree. Across the controlled settings studied here, changing the data policy measurably changes the training process but does not produce a reproducible improvement over uniform training.

</details>


### [107] [Robust Decentralized Personalized Federated Learning via Prediction-Constrained Neighborhood Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07312)
*Xiao Ma,Hong Shen,Hui Tian,Wenqi Lyu,Wei Ke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种鲁棒的分布式个性化联邦学习方法R-DPFL，通过鲁棒邻域方向估计和基于历史值的更新趋势预测来抵御拜占庭攻击，而非像现有工作那样单纯聚合客户端模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式个性化联邦学习方法在面对拜占庭攻击时容易受到恶意客户端干扰，缺乏有效的鲁棒性保障机制。

Method: R-DPFL中每个客户端首先通过聚合接收到的邻域更新向量计算当前轮次的模型更新，然后基于历史值和局部模型变化预测该更新应有的值，最后计算两者之差并自适应裁剪后加入到局部更新中。

Result: 通过严格分析证明了学习过程的收敛性，并表明在拜占庭邻居扰动下诚实客户端无需邻居模型共识即可维持稳定的个性化下降动态。CIFAR-10上的大量实验表明，在异质性和对抗性设置下，R-DPFL持续优于最先进的分布式和个性化联邦学习基线方法。

Conclusion: R-DPFL通过结合鲁棒方向估计与历史趋势预测，有效提升了分布式个性化联邦学习在对抗环境中的鲁棒性和性能。

Abstract: This paper proposes a robust decentralized personalized federated learning method R-DPFL, that enables clients to reduce the impact of Byzantine attacks via robust neighborhood direction estimation and history-based update trend prediction, rather than purely aggregating client models as in the existing work. In R-DPFL, each client first computes the current-round model update by aggregating the received neighborhood update vectors. It then predicts what this update should be based on its historical values and local model changes. Finally, R-DPFL computes the difference between these two quantities, adaptively clips this difference, and adds it to the local update. We prove convergence of the learning process through rigorous analysis and show that honest clients maintain stable personalized descent dynamics under Byzantine neighbor perturbations without requiring consensus among neighboring models. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10 demonstrate that RDPFL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art decentralized and personalized federated learning baselines under heterogeneous and adversarial settings.

</details>


### [108] [Sparse Incident-Cluster Learning for 12-hour Port Flood Pre-warning in Digital-Twin Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06109)
*Jie Zhang,Qiang Ni,David Windridge,Huan X. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将12小时港口洪水预警建模为"事件簇学习"问题，并评估一个数字孪生分析模块。该模块使用八点水位历史、预测时点上下文协变量和可解释的动态特征，结合折叠特定的稀疏特征选择、预警簇分组、负标签控制、100次重复随机top-k控制及预警时段评估等协议。以利物浦为主要案例（四个簇），建立了F2=0.696的ElasticNet模型，验证了其可在仅用十个预测因子的情况下与非线性的XGBoost基线保持竞争力，最终产出一个可集成到港口数字孪生中的离线评估分析与验证模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 港口洪水数字孪生需要能在中断发生前提醒操作人员的分析功能，但官方预警事件往往很少，且相邻观测在时间上具有依赖性。逐行分类会因将同一事件的窗口同时放入模型开发和评估数据中而高估性能，因此需要一种考虑事件簇结构的学习与评估方案。

Method: 将12小时港口洪水预警形式化为事件簇学习问题；使用八点水位历史、预测时上下文协变量与可解释的短窗口动态特征；评估协议包括折叠特定的稀疏特征选择、预警簇分组、负标签控制、100次重复随机top-k控制，以及基于预警时段的评估；以利物浦为四簇主要案例，并采用Humber/Hull代理和Wessex South数据进行协议迁移验证。

Result: 利物浦各折叠中，top-10 ElasticNet模型平均F2=0.696，高于无top-k截断的0.633和全特征加权XGBoost的0.681；它是最强的ElasticNet变体，仅用十个预测因子即可与非线性参考模型竞争，并超过重复级随机子集的第95百分位。上下文协变量提供强预测锚点，可解释的局部动态特征作为补充。历史回放将风险分数转化为预警时段并度量预警持续时间和误报负担。

Conclusion: 研究产出一个经过离线评估的分析与验证模块，该模块设计用于集成到港口数字孪生系统之中，为实际部署前的预警性能提供了可靠的评估框架。

Abstract: Port flood digital twins require analytics that warn operators before disruption, but official warning incidents are often few and adjacent observations are temporally dependent. Row-level classification can therefore overstate performance by placing windows from the same event in both model-development and evaluation data. We formulate 12-hour port flood pre-warning as an incident-cluster learning problem and evaluate a digital-twin analytics module using eight-point water-level histories, prediction-time contextual covariates, and interpretable short-window dynamics. The protocol combines fold-specific sparse feature selection, warning-cluster grouping, negative-label controls, 100-repeat random top-k controls, and alert-episode evaluation. Liverpool is the primary four-cluster case study, with harmonised Humber/Hull-proxy and Wessex South data used for protocol-transfer checks. Across the Liverpool folds, the top-10 ElasticNet model achieves mean F2 = 0.696, compared with 0.633 without top-k truncation and 0.681 for full-feature weighted XGBoost. It is the strongest ElasticNet variant, remains competitive with the nonlinear reference using only ten predictors, and exceeds the repeat-level 95th percentile of broad and same-family random subsets. Contextual covariates provide a strong prediction-time anchor, complemented by physically interpretable local dynamics. Historical replay converts risk scores into alert episodes and measures alert duration and false-episode burden. The result is an offline-evaluated analytics and validation module designed for integration into a port digital twin.

</details>


### [109] [Compressed Recurrent Feedback in Tsetlin Machines: A Reproducible Boolean-FSM Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06133)
*Ankit Kumar,Utkarsh Raj,Rishad Shafik,Sudip Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种固定宽度的循环Tsetlin机（RTM）变体，通过XOR折叠将480个clause激活压缩为96个循环比特，并以两个时间尺度保留折合比特后阈值化回二值状态。在布尔有限状态机基准上144次运行显示，压缩模型与原始直接反馈的准确率差异不到1个百分点，但循环宽度减少十倍且执行时间降低约4倍。研究还强调无反馈对照在序列模型基准测试中的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型设备上的顺序推理需要模型在不反复处理长输入的情况下保留有用历史。RTM通过将上一时刻的布尔clause输出作为下一时刻输入来提供记忆，但直接反馈的规模随clause库增长而扩大，导致循环输入不必要地过宽，限制了在小型设备上的部署效率。

Method: 该方法结合clause激活使用XOR折叠技术来压缩特征维度，将折叠后的比特以两个时间尺度保留，再通过阈值化处理还原为二值状态。通过这种固定宽度设计，将480个clause激活减少到96个循环比特位，实现了显著的信息压缩。

Result: 在可复现的布尔有限状态机基准上（含明确的状态转移规则、数据划分和随机种子），144次运行中压缩模型在两个任务族上分别获得61.47±6.74%和62.94±9.92%的准确率。原始clause反馈与折叠方法相比，准确率均值变化不到1个百分点，但循环宽度增加十倍，且实测主机执行时间增加4.38倍和3.71倍。门控神经模型准确率更高，无反馈对照仅保留短期输入历史就能达到相当或略高的准确率。

Conclusion: 在该基准上，XOR折叠方法与原始反馈在较小的经验误差范围内相匹配，同时具有更窄的接口。研究同时强调在序列模型基准测试中加入无反馈对照的关键必要性，因为仅保留短期输入历史的简单模型即可达到相当或更好的效果，这提示许多序列基准可能并不过分依赖长期记忆。

Abstract: Sequential inference on small devices requires a model to retain useful history without repeatedly processing a long input record. A Recurrent Tsetlin Machine (RTM) provides this memory by returning Boolean clause outputs from one time step as inputs to the next. Direct feedback, however, grows with the clause bank and can make the recurrent input unnecessarily wide. This paper investigates a fixed-width alternative. We combine clause activations by exclusive-OR (XOR) folding, retain the folded bits at two time scales, and threshold them back to a binary state. The resulting design reduces 480 clause activations to 96 recurrent bits. We evaluate the method on a reproducible Boolean finite-state-machine benchmark with explicit transition rules, data splits, and random seeds. Across 144 runs, the compressed model obtains $61.47 \pm 6.74\%$ and $62.94 \pm 9.92\%$ accuracy on the two task families. Raw clause feedback changes these means by less than one percentage point, while increasing the recurrent width tenfold and measured host execution time by $4.38\times$ and $3.71\times$. Gated neural models remain more accurate, and a no-feedback control retaining only short input history achieves comparable or slightly higher accuracy. On this benchmark, folding matches raw feedback within small empirical margins at a much narrower interface; these findings also underscore the critical necessity of no-feedback recurrence controls when benchmarking sequence models.

</details>


### [110] [GraphFAS: A Distributed System for Automated Graph Feature Generation and Selection in Industrial Transaction Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08970)
*Yice Luo,Yun Zhu,Xi Chen,Yongchao Liu,Xintan Zeng,Chengying Huan,Kai Zhang,Jinrui Zhang,Juelu Zhang,Jiajun Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphFAS将无参数图特征生成与基于Boruta的分布式特征选择相结合，弥补了专家构造特征与端到端GNN在工业欺诈检测中的差距，已在支付宝大规模部署并取得显著工程效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业欺诈检测依赖昂贵的专家特征，忽视了图结构关系信号；而GNN又难以满足金融风控的可解释性和部署要求。因此需要一种既能捕捉图结构信息、又具备可解释性和工程可行性的方法。

Method: GraphFAS包含两个核心模块：(1)无参数图特征生成模块，通过多跳子图提取和多尺度聚合构造显式、可解释的结构特征，无需学习参数；(2)基于Boruta扩展的分布式自动特征选择算法，使用跨分区中位数聚合来稳健识别信息特征，降低对领域专家的依赖。该方法将特征聚合与模型训练解耦，可与表格模型和TreeSHAP解释直接集成。

Result: GraphFAS在支付宝部署后，与专家驱动和图表学习基线相比表现强劲，在工程效率上实现了数量级的提升，且在大规模图上展现了优于基线的性能。

Conclusion: GraphFAS成功弥合了传统特征工程与GNN在工业欺诈检测中的鸿沟，通过可解释的结构特征和自动选择机制，在保证性能的同时大幅提升了可解释性和工程效率，验证了其在大规模工业场景中的实用价值。

Abstract: Industrial fraud detection often relies on costly expert-crafted features that overlook graph-structured relational signals, while GNNs often do not meet the interpretability and deployment requirements of financial risk control. We propose GraphFAS (Graph Feature Automated Selection), a distributed feature selection procedure based on Boruta that bridges this gap through: (1) a non-parametric graph feature generation module that constructs explicit, interpretable structural features via multi-hop subgraph extraction and multi-scale aggregation without learned parameters; and (2) an automated distributed feature selection algorithm extending Boruta with median-based aggregation across partitions to robustly identify informative features at scale with minimal domain expertise. Compared with end-to-end GNN pipelines, GraphFAS decouples feature aggregation from model training, enabling direct integration with tabular models and direct compatibility with TreeSHAPbased explanations. Deployed in Alipay, GraphFAS delivers orderof-magnitude improvements in engineering efficiency while showing strong performance against expert-driven and graph-learning baselines on large-scale graphs.

</details>


### [111] [Rethinking One-Shot Federated Graph Learning: Training-Free Statistical Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06154)
*Shutong Zheng,Sijia Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPEAR，一个完全免训练的统计原型估计框架，用于解决单轮联邦图学习中的跨客户端表示不对齐问题。通过直接从局部图计算拓扑平滑的类原型，并在服务器端使用样本量自适应收缩估计器聚合，SPEAR在极端异构条件下实现最先进的准确率，且比所有基线快至少一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单轮联邦图学习方法过度依赖局部GNN训练，但在极端非IID条件下，局部GNN训练会导致严重的跨客户端表示不对齐，反而成为主要误差来源。作者通过实证观察发现这一问题，主张重新审视单轮联邦图学习问题。

Method: 将单轮联邦图学习重构为统计估计问题，提出SPEAR框架：1）从局部图原始特征空间直接计算拓扑平滑的类原型，无需任何训练；2）服务器端采用样本量自适应的收缩估计器聚合各客户端原型，降低不可靠局部估计的权重，生成鲁棒的全局类原型。

Result: 在七个基准数据集上，SPEAR在极端异构条件下 consistently 达到最先进的准确率。相比所有基线有至少一个数量级的速度提升，对比生成式和蒸馏方法可达多个数量级加速。

Conclusion: 免训练的统计估计方法而非局部GNN优化，是鲁棒且高效的单轮联邦图学习的关键。现有优化策略的前提假设（局部训练不可或缺）在这类场景下需要被重新审视。

Abstract: One-shot federated graph learning generally aims to train Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) across clients with disconnected subgraphs in a single communication round. Existing methods predominantly design advanced optimization strategies under the premise that local GNN training is indispensable. However, empirical observations reveal that under extreme non-IID conditions, local GNN training suffers from severe cross-client representation misalignment, becoming a major source of error rather than a remedy. Motivated by this, we reformulate one-shot FGL as a statistical estimation problem. We propose SPEAR (Statistical Prototype Estimation with Adaptive Reliability), a completely training-free framework that directly computes topology-smoothed class prototypes from local graphs in the original feature space. The server then aggregates these prototypes using a sample-size-adaptive shrinkage estimator that down-weights unreliable local estimates, producing robust global class prototypes. Extensive experiments across seven benchmarks demonstrate that SPEAR consistently achieves state-of-the-art accuracy under extreme heterogeneity. Moreover, SPEAR delivers at least an order-of-magnitude speedup over all baselines, reaching several orders of magnitude against generative and distillation-based methods. Our findings suggest that training-free statistical estimation, rather than local GNN optimization, provides the key to robust and efficient one-shot federated graph learning. The code is available at https://github.com/Yodeesy/SPEAR .

</details>


### [112] [All for 1-Bit: Towards Genuine 1-Bit Post-Training Quantization for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06161)
*Zhixiong Zhao,Zukang Xu,Guangyu Sun,Lifeng Liu,Dawei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AF1，一个真正的1-bit后训练量化（PTQ）框架，用于大语言模型（LLM）的高效部署。AF1包含两个核心组件：Null-space-Aware Binary Factorization (NABF)用于改进二值重建，以及Hierarchical Shapley Allocation (HiSA)用于结构容量分配，两者协同在严格的1.0-BPW预算下保持模型精度。实验表明，AF1在LLaMA、Qwen和Gemma模型家族上持续优于现有的二值化PTQ方法，相比BF16平均实现2.5倍推理加速和超过90%的内存缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型虽然取得了显著进步，但其庞大的存储和内存带宽需求阻碍了高效部署。权重二值化是一种有前景的解决方案，但现有的基于二值化的后训练量化方法通常因隐藏开销而远超名义上的1-bit存储目标，这促使作者开发一个真正的1-bit PTQ框架。

Method: AF1由两个互补组件构成：(1) Null-space-Aware Binary Factorization (NABF)，通过Hessian感知的替代重参数化、零空间感知的二值因子分解以及仅缩放参数的全局重建来改进二值重建质量；(2) Hierarchical Shapley Allocation (HiSA)，利用分层Shapley敏感性来分配结构容量。两者结合在PTQ设置中、严格的1.0-BPW预算下保持模型精度。

Result: 在LLaMA、Qwen和Gemma模型家族上的实验表明，AF1在困惑度和零样本准确率方面持续优于现有二值化PTQ方法。与BF16相比，AF1在评估模型上平均实现2.5倍推理加速和超过90%的内存缩减。

Conclusion: AF1为LLM提供了一条通往可部署的真正1-bit压缩的实用路径，通过NABF和HiSA两个组件的结合，在严格的1.0-BPW预算下实现了精度保持与显著的推理加速和内存缩减，并已开源代码以供复现。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable progress, yet their massive storage and memory-bandwidth demands still hinder efficient deployment. Weight binarization is a promising solution, but existing binarization-based post-training quantization (PTQ) methods usually far exceed the nominal 1-bit storage target due to hidden overhead. To address this gap, we propose All for 1-Bit (AF1), a genuine 1-bit PTQ framework for LLMs. AF1 comprises two complementary components: (1) Null-space-Aware Binary Factorization (NABF) for improving binary reconstruction through Hessian-aware surrogate reparameterization, null-space-aware binary factorization, and scale-only global reconstruction; and (2) Hierarchical Shapley Allocation (HiSA) for assigning structural capacity using hierarchical Shapley sensitivity. Together, they preserve model accuracy under a strict 1.0-BPW budget in the PTQ setting. Experiments on LLaMA, Qwen, and Gemma families show that AF1 consistently outperforms existing binarization-based PTQ methods in perplexity and zero-shot accuracy. Compared with BF16, AF1 achieves an average 2.5 times inference speedup and over 90% memory reduction across evaluated models, providing a practical path toward deployable genuine 1-bit compression for LLMs. The code for reproducibility is available at https://github.com/Kishon-zzx/AF1.

</details>


### [113] [Decision-Aware Suffix Prediction and Reasoning of Business Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06169)
*Henryk Mustroph,Stefanie Rinderle-Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种决策感知的后缀预测框架，将决策挖掘与神经网络预测相结合，通过神经符号方法利用挖掘的决策规则对预测事件进行推理，在短前缀和稀有流程变体场景下显著提升后缀预测性能，并增强了模型的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后缀预测方法主要依赖基于事件日志训练的神经网络，虽然在平均表现上不错，但在短前缀或稀有流程变体上表现不佳。这是因为这些场景中正确路径往往跨越多个分支决策，而这些决策主要由案例级和事件级属性决定，但神经网络倾向过度依赖（密集的）事件标签而低估这些属性信号。决策挖掘虽能提取此类决策规则，但此前仅用于事后分析和假设分析，尚未应用于后缀预测。

Method: 提出决策感知后缀预测框架，是一个神经符号方法：将决策挖掘融合到后缀预测流程中，利用从事件日志中挖掘的决策规则对神经网络预测的事件序列进行推理和修正，从而增强预测的合理性。

Result: 在三个（共四个）事件日志和三种后缀预测器的实验中，该框架能够改善后缀预测效果，尤其在短前缀场景下表现突出，同时对稀有流程变体也有改进，并且带来了内在的可解释性。

Conclusion: 决策感知后缀预测框架通过将决策规则融入神经网络预测过程，有效解决了传统后缀预测模型在短前缀和稀有变体上的短板，兼具性能提升与可解释性，验证了神经符号方法在后缀预测任务中的可行性。

Abstract: Suffix prediction forecasts the remaining sequence of events of a running case until completion. Most approaches rely on neural networks trained on event logs, which, on average, perform well but struggle with short prefixes or targets belonging to a rare process variant. In such scenarios, the correct path may cross multiple branching decisions, determined primarily by case- and event-level attributes, a signal that NN-based suffix prediction models tend to underweight because they may heavily weight (dense) event labels. Decision mining extracts rules for such decisions from the event log, but has so far been applied only to post-hoc and what-if analysis, not suffix prediction. We therefore extend suffix prediction with decision mining, introducing a decision-aware suffix prediction framework, a neuro-symbolic approach that enables reasoning about predicted events via mined decision rules. Experiments on three of four event logs and three suffix predictors show that the framework can improve suffix prediction, especially for short prefixes but also for rare process variants, and adds intrinsic interpretability.

</details>


### [114] [FMMO: Detecting the Divergence Between Local Attribution and Global Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06173)
*Muhammad Rehman Zafar,Ali El-Sharif,Naimul Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FMMO框架，揭示局部XAI方法（如TreeSHAP）在模型漂移导致公平性恶化的场景下会表现出误导性的稳定性，无法有效预警受保护群体的误报率飙升问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型部署后的漂移（尤其当真实标签延迟时）可能成为"静默故障"，而传统的局部可解释AI（XAI）方法在模型可靠性和公平性崩溃时可能呈现误导性的稳定性，形成公平性盲区。

Method: 提出模型监控与可观测性框架（FMMO），通过整合全局替代模型（global surrogate models）与模型利用率测量（model utilization measurements），用于暴露局部解释稳定性与全局分布漂移之间的分歧。

Result: 在基准、合成和真实世界数据集上验证，局部XAI方法无法标记由漂移引起的差异性影响（即受保护群体假阳性率飙升而特征归因保持不变的情况），而FMMO能够有效缓解这一公平性盲点。

Conclusion: FMMO框架能够帮助利益相关者检测到标准局部XAI工具所忽视的歧视性劣化，弥补了现有模型监控方法在公平性漂移检测方面的不足。

Abstract: Post-deployment drift poses a critical risk to algorithmic accountability, particularly when ground truth labels are delayed and performance degradation becomes a "silent failure". While Explainable AI (XAI) is often relied upon to audit these shifts, we demonstrate that popular local attribution methods (e.g., TreeSHAP) can exhibit misleading stability even as model reliability collapses. In this paper, we propose a Framework for Model Monitoring and Observability (FMMO) designed to expose the divergence between local explanation stability and global distribution shifts. Using benchmark, synthetic, and real-world datasets, we show that local XAI methods fail to flag drift-induced disparate impact, specifically where False Positive Rates spike for protected groups while feature attributions remain unchanged. By integrating global surrogate models with model utilization measurements, FMMO mitigates this fairness blind spot, ensuring that stakeholders can detect discriminatory deterioration that standard local XAI tools overlook.

</details>


### [115] [Spectral Prioritized Sweeping in Nonstationary Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06186)
*Hung Pham,Tuan Dam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GTA-PS（又称Spectral Prioritized Sweeping），通过图拓扑的解析函数（正则化拉普拉斯逆）传播Bellman残差，为优先清扫提供"即插即用"的排序分数，解决非平稳奖励设定下标准优先清扫（PS）目光短浅的问题，在Tabular实验（FourRooms和GARNET）中提升了重规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PS根据Bellman残差大小选择回放备份，但在非平稳奖励环境中，奖赏局部变化后残差只能通过已实现的备份逐步传播，导致瓶颈或拓扑距离较远的状态估计在有限重规划预算下可能长时间不更新，缺乏全局视野。

Method: 提出Graph Topology Augmentation框架：利用图的解析函数（resolvent）及其扩散语义来增强查询信号。具体地，GTA-PS使用平滑化的策略诱导转移链，结合入/出拉普拉斯算子，将标准优先级键与正则化拉普拉斯逆的混合（用于扩散残差幅度）相结合；同时基于第二最大特征值模（SLEM）设计退火调度器，使拓扑贡献的规模适应链的混合状态。

Result: 理论证明前向势能等同于几何折扣残差传播，且GTA-PS能立即对所有状态赋予活跃优先级。在FourRooms和GARNET领域上的Tabular实验表明，无论在精确DP还是Dyna式宿主规划器下，相比标准PS都展现出更优的重规划效率。

Conclusion: GTA-PS通过拓扑感知的残差扩散解决了标准PS在非平稳奖励下信息传播迟缓的缺陷，是一种通用、可即插即用的改进方案，有望在更复杂的环境中提升模型强化学习的重规划效率。

Abstract: Prioritized Sweeping (PS) accelerates model-based reinforcement learning by selecting backups according to Bellman residual magnitude. In nonstationary reward settings, however, the canonical priority score is shortsighted: after a localized reward shift, residuals propagate only through realized backups, so bottlenecked or topologically distant state estimates may remain static under a limited replanning budget. We introduce the Graph Topology Augmentation framework, which employ the graph's resolvent and its diffusion semantic, to augment the inquired signal. Our application, Graph Topology Augmentation for Prioritized Sweeping (GTA-PS), or which the alias Spectral Prioritized Sweeping (SPS) might be more universal, provides a drop-in ordering score for the setting of fixed dynamics and changing state rewards. GTA-PS uses a smootherized policy, inducing a transition chain, with its in- and out-Laplacian. The standard priority key is augmented with a mixing of regularized Laplacian inverses diffusing the residual magnitude. Furthermore, the topology contribution is annealed by a scheduler based on the Second Largest Eigenvalue Modulus (SLEM), allowing its scale to adapt to the chain's mixing regime. We prove that the forward potential coincides with geometric discounted residual propagation and show that GTA-PS gives active priority instantly to all states. Tabular experiments on FourRooms and GARNET domains demonstrate improved replanning efficiency over standard PS under both exact DP and Dyna-style host planners.

</details>


### [116] [EgoNeMo: Transferable Map of Pedestrian Dynamics via Egocentric LiDAR Scan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06195)
*Azusa Sawada,Allan Wang,Hideo Saito,Aaron Steinfeld*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可迁移的动力学地图（MoD）框架，仅利用以自我为中心的3D LiDAR点云即可推广到未知环境，克服了传统MoD方法依赖逐地点轨迹数据积累的局限性。通过神经隐式建模、位置平衡采样策略和多任务学习架构（联合预测运动分布与空间频率得分图），并引入可见性感知损失，该框架能在稀疏训练数据的条件下从单次瞬时LiDAR扫描中重建未知地点的运动地图，并提升下游轨迹预测的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoD方法虽然对编码人体运动特征以实现行人轨迹预测和机器人安全导航至关重要，但存在严重的站点特异性问题——每次在新地点部署都需要进行详尽的行人轨迹数据积累，成本高昂且效率低下，严重限制了其在实际场景中的泛化能力。

Method: 1）利用神经隐式建模技术，训练一个基于LiDAR的连续MoD估计器，在多样化环境中进行泛化训练；2）提出位置平衡采样策略以缓解真实世界轨迹数据的稀疏性和时间偏差问题；3）设计多任务学习架构，联合预测运动分布和空间频率得分图；4）对空间频率预测引入可见性感知损失，以补偿不完整观测数据带来的偏差。

Result: 综合实验表明，该框架即使在训练数据高度稀疏的情况下，也能从单个瞬时的LiDAR扫描中有效重建未知位置的底层运动地图；此外，该方法能显著增强下游行人轨迹预测任务的可靠性。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将MoD从站点特定的范式转向可迁移的通用框架，仅需一次LiDAR扫描即可在未知环境中估计行人运动特征，为机器人导航和轨迹预测在实际复杂场景中的部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a transferable Map of Dynamics (MoD) framework that generalizes to unknown environments using only egocentric 3D LiDAR point clouds to overcome the long-standing limitation of traditional MoD methods. While MoDs are essential for encoding human motion characteristics to enable accurate pedestrian trajectory prediction or safe robot navigation, traditional approaches suffer from site-specificity, requiring exhaustive trajectory accumulation at every new location. Extending recent advances in neural implicit modeling, our framework trains a continuous, LiDAR-based MoD estimator across diverse environments. To mitigate the inherent sparsity and temporal bias of real-world trajectory data, we introduce a position-balanced sampling strategy and a multi-task learning architecture that jointly predicts motion distributions and a spatial frequency score map. The latter is further augmented by visibility-aware losses to compensate for incomplete observation data. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that our method effectively reconstructs underlying motion maps even in unknown locations from a single instantaneous LiDAR scan, despite highly sparse training data. Finally, we show that our improvements enhance the reliability of downstream trajectory prediction.

</details>


### [117] [SeaCausal-FL: Federated Fuzzy Causal Learning for Maritime IoT Fault Diagnosis and Counterfactual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06257)
*Yuhang Qiu,Haihan Zhu,Koteeswaran Seerangan,Longsheng Zhu,Xiong Wang,Yijun Lu,Zheng Lin,Fangmin Ren,Jialiang Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SeaCausal-FL，一种用于海事物联网中船舶发动机故障诊断的联邦模糊因果学习框架，结合共享时间诊断路径与机制条件因果推理，在分布式数据所有权和异构故障分布下实现高精度因果诊断与反事实推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海事物联网中船舶发动机故障诊断面临三个核心挑战：数据分布在不同客户端难以集中共享、故障分布异构、运行条件持续变化。现有方法难以在联邦学习环境下同时处理机制不确定性、因果结构学习和异构联邦聚合问题。

Method: SeaCausal-FL将共享时间诊断路径与机制条件因果推理相结合。其核心组件包括：区间二型模糊层用于表示不确定且重叠的运行机制；每个机制关联一个物理约束的结构因果模型；聚合前采用运行上下文、因果结构和条件干预-响应签名对局部学习机制进行对齐；模型参数按样本、类别、机制和机制-类别证据加权聚合，而非仅基于客户端样本量；结构方程支持通过外展、行动和预测进行区间反事实推理。

Result: 在船舶发动机故障数据集上，SeaCausal-FL在四个客户端分区上平均F1分数达87.07%，AUROC和AUPRC分别为98.98%和94.81%；在未见负载和故障类型缺失训练时仍保持良好性能。在因果基准上，Edge-F1约0.58、Edge-AUPRC为0.68、系数RMSE降至约0.14，并提供良好的反事实估计和干预决策。

Conclusion: SeaCausal-FL通过机制感知的联邦因果学习有效解决了海事物联网中分布式异构故障诊断难题，在诊断精度、因果结构恢复、反事实推理和干预决策方面均展现出优越性能，为该领域提供了一种可靠且可解释的诊断方案。

Abstract: Reliable marine-engine fault diagnosis in maritime IoT is challenged by distributed data ownership, heterogeneous fault distributions, and continuously changing operating conditions. This paper proposes SeaCausal-FL, a federated fuzzy causal learning framework that combines a shared temporal diagnostic path with mechanism-conditioned causal reasoning. An interval type-2 fuzzy layer represents uncertain and overlapping operating mechanisms, while each mechanism is associated with a physics-constrained structural causal model. Before aggregation, locally learned mechanisms are aligned using operating context, causal structure, and conditional intervention-response signatures. Model parameters are then aggregated according to sample, class, mechanism, and mechanism-class evidence instead of client sample size alone. The learned structural equations further support interval counterfactual reasoning through abduction, action, and prediction. Experiments on a marine-engine fault dataset and a real-data-calibrated semi-synthetic causal benchmark show that SeaCausal-FL achieves an average F1 score of 87.07% across four client partitions, with AUROC and AUPRC of 98.98% and 94.81%, respectively. It also maintains strong performance under unseen loads and fault-type omission during training. On the causal benchmark, SeaCausal-FL reaches an Edge-F1 of approximately 0.58 and an Edge-AUPRC of 0.68, reduces coefficient RMSE to about 0.14, and provides favorable counterfactual estimation and intervention decisions.

</details>


### [118] [Representation Learning for Sample-Efficient CATE Estimation by Leveraging Multiple Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06294)
*Maitreyi Swaroop,Shikha Bhat,Samantha Rodriguez,Tamar Krishnamurti,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用历史数据中多种辅助结果来学习协变量的低维表示，从而更有效地估计条件平均处理效应（CATE），在理论上证明了识别性并刻画了偏差-方差权衡，实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实验样本有限的情况下，从高维协变量中估计CATE面临维度灾难，现成的降维方法可能丢失处理异质性信息。然而，许多领域拥有包含多种结果的历史大数据集，这些辅助监督信号在CATE估计中很少被利用。本文旨在利用这些历史数据来学习保持处理异质性信息的低维表示，提高CATE估计的样本效率。

Method: 遵循因果表示学习的思路：假设高维协变量域具有较低维度的潜在动力学结构，利用历史数据中测量的多样化结果作为辅助监督，学习协变量的低维表示。理论上，证明了当辅助结果满足代理条件（surrogacy conditions）且表示保留相关协变量信息时，用学习到的表示替换高维协变量后原始CATE仍可被识别；同时刻画了假设不完全成立时的偏差-方差权衡。实验上，在合成数据和半合成医学数据上进行了评估。

Result: 理论结果表明，结合已有的维度相关CATE估计速率，该方法在相同实验样本上实现了更高的样本效率；当代理条件不完全满足时，表示估计器在方差降低超过压缩引入的偏差时仍可达到更低的误差。实验在合成数据和半合成医学数据上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 利用历史数据中的多种辅助结果学习低维表示是提升有限实验样本下CATE估计效率的有效途径，该方法将维度灾难转嫁到数据丰富的历史数据上，实验数据仅需用于估计低维表示上的CATE，具有理论保证和实证支持。

Abstract: Estimating conditional average treatment effects (CATE) enables efficient targeting of interventions, but many applications have limited experimental samples, making it difficult to estimate heterogeneous effects from high-dimensional covariates. In such settings, policymakers and medical practitioners often succumb to the curse of dimensionality or apply off-the-shelf dimension reduction methods that may not preserve treatment heterogeneity. Yet these domains often come with large historical datasets measuring a wide range of outcomes -- a source of supervision that is rarely exploited in practice. Following causal representation learning, we hypothesize that such domains with high-dimensional covariates have lower-dimensional underlying dynamics. We can thus leverage the diverse outcomes measured in historical data to learn a lower-dimensional representation of the covariates. Theoretically, we prove that when the auxiliary outcomes satisfy a set of surrogacy conditions and the representation retains relevant covariate information, the original CATE is identified when the high-dimensional covariates are replaced by the learned representation. Combined with existing dimension-dependent rates for CATE estimation, the result implies greater sample-efficiency on the same experimental sample. Additionally, we characterize the bias-variance tradeoff when the assumptions do not hold perfectly, and show that the representation-based estimator can still achieve lower error when the reduction in estimator variance outweighs the bias due to compression. Empirically, we evaluate the method on synthetic data and semi-synthetic medical data.

</details>


### [119] [Linear Algebra Foundations of Efficient Attention: A Phase Reversal in Rank Collapse Under SVD Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06341)
*Anjaneya Teja Sarma Kalvakolanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文统一了十四篇关于Transformer基础模型中线性代数技术（矩阵秩、SVD、特征分解）应用的同行评审论文，聚焦于自注意力矩阵输出秩、压缩方法及低秩KV缓存投影三个领域。报告了一个原创发现：SVD压缩注意力投影在初始化时抑制秩坍缩，但在预训练模型上反而加速秩坍缩，通过受控因果分解证明该行为主要由SVD压缩时选择的子空间决定（而非算子范数降低），解释了约76%（初始化）和83%（预训练）的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到该文献中一个开放问题：压缩方法与网络自然秩坍缩之间的相互作用尚不明确，因此希望系统性地统一相关研究成果并深入探究这一交互机制。

Method: 统一分析了十四篇论文的相关工作，并在GPT-2 124M、GPT-2 Medium 355M和Pythia-160M模型上通过四种秩估计方法进行实验，采用受控因果分解来分离压缩子空间选择与算子范数降低两个因素对秩坍缩的影响。

Result: 发现SVD压缩在初始化时强烈抑制秩坍缩，但在预训练模型上加速秩坍缩；子空间选择因素解释了初始化时约76%和预训练时约83%的效果，且在所有压缩比下均未出现对象混叠伪影。

Conclusion: 该研究细化了校准感知压缩的观点，解释了为何它优于朴素SVD截断，为Transformer模型压缩与秩动态的理解提供了更深入的机理洞见。

Abstract: Linear algebra provides the framework of concepts (matrix rank, singular value decomposition (SVD), and eigendecomposition) that modern artificial intelligence employs to encode, compress, and propagate information through neural networks. This paper unifies fourteen separate peer-reviewed works analyzing the usage of these techniques in the context of transformer-based foundation model research, focusing on three areas of the topic: derivations and properties of self-attention matrices' output rank, compression methods that purposefully utilize this phenomenon, and the low-rank key-value (KV) cache projection and its semiseparable-matrix duality to linear attention and state-space structured models. We were motivated to conduct this work after observing an open problem in this literature: the interplay of the mentioned compression methods with natural rank collapse of the network. With this paper, we report an original finding that using SVD compression of attention projections actually has the opposite effect on the rank collapse of the network: while it strongly suppresses it at initialization, it accelerates on pretrained models (for GPT-2 124M, GPT-2 Medium 355M, and Pythia-160M) with minimal risk of object aliasing artifacts appearing (verified on all compression ratios) and is consistent across four rank estimation methods. A controlled causal decomposition of the effect in both settings showed that the reason for this behavior can be explained by the choice of the subspace SVD makes when compressing the matrix better than the reduction of the operator norm it achieves, explaining roughly 76% of the effect at initialization and 83% on the pretrained weights, providing a refinement to the calibration-aware compression viewpoint and an explanation of why it outperformed naive SVD truncation.

</details>


### [120] [Robust Dynamic Expansion for Continual Learning under Backdoor Attacks via Purification and Selective Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06346)
*Keyu Lin,Fei Ye,Qihe Liu,Shijie Zhou,Jiguo Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于后门攻击下持续学习的鲁棒动态扩展框架，通过样本净化、选择性恢复和鲁棒专家路由三个组件的协同，同时解决灾难性遗忘、适应性保持和恶意监督吸收三大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际场景中任务流可能包含来自不可信来源的后门投毒样本，现有持续学习和后门防御方法均无法应对这一组合挑战，即在顺序更新中同时实现灾难性遗忘抑制、适应能力保留和恶意监督防御。

Method: 提出动态扩展框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) Bi-Prototype Purification (BPP) 利用特征空间语义差异识别可疑样本；(2) Gradient Discrepancy-based Robustness Optimization (GDBRO) 通过伪标签纠正和梯度一致性评估选择性恢复有效投毒样本；(3) Robust Feature Consistency-based Expert Selection (RFCBES) 构建扰动感知类原型实现可靠的专家路由。

Result: 论文尚未展示具体实验结果，但框架设计在理论上解决了CLUBA设定下稳定性、可塑性与安全性的联合优化问题。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统研究后门攻击下的持续学习问题，提出的统一框架通过样本级净化、选择性恢复和路由级鲁棒策略，为安全持续的模型学习提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) enables models to acquire new knowledge from sequentially arriving tasks while retaining previously learned knowledge. However, in practical scenarios, task streams collected from untrusted sources may contain backdoor-poisoned samples, posing a critical challenge to the stability, plasticity, and security of continual learners. In this work, we investigate a challenging setting termed Continual Learning Under Backdoor Attack (CLUBA), where each incremental task may involve a small proportion of maliciously manipulated training samples. Unlike conventional continual learning or backdoor defense scenarios, CLUBA requires models to simultaneously mitigate catastrophic forgetting, preserve adaptation capability, and prevent the absorption of malicious supervision during sequential updates. To address this challenge, we propose a robust dynamic-expansion framework that integrates sample purification, selective recovery, and robust expert routing into a unified continual learning paradigm. Specifically, we introduce Bi-Prototype Purification (BPP) to identify suspicious samples by exploiting semantic discrepancies in feature space. Based on purified data, Gradient Discrepancy-based Robustness Optimization (GDBRO) selectively recovers informative poisoned samples through pseudo-label correction and gradient consistency evaluation, improving robustness while maintaining model plasticity. Furthermore, Robust Feature Consistency-based Expert Selection (RFCBES) constructs perturbation-aware class prototypes to enable reliable expert routing under corrupted or shifted inputs.

</details>


### [121] [Robust Conformal Consensus: Multi-Agent LLM-as-a-Judge Interval Evaluation with Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06367)
*Lihui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向多智能体LLM评估的鲁棒不确定性估计框架，利用保形预测为多个LLM评估者构建预测区间，以获得更稳定、更可靠的评估结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge已成为自然语言生成评估的重要范式，但其评估结果的固有不确定性尚未被充分探索，尤其在真实应用中缺乏可靠性保障。现有的保形预测方法仅应用于单一LLM评估者，忽视了不同LLM评估者之间引入的变异性。

Method: 提出一种多智能体LLM评估的鲁棒不确定性估计框架，该方法为多个LLM评估者分别构建保形预测区间，然后综合考虑不同LLM评估者的区间信息，以获得更稳定的不确定性估计，并采用基于区间的聚合策略整合多个评估者的结果。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提方法能够生成具有覆盖保证的有效预测区间，并且基于区间的多评估者聚合策略能够带来更稳定的评估结果。

Conclusion: 通过多LLM评估者的保形预测区间聚合，可以有效提升LLM-as-a-Judge评估的可靠性与稳定性，弥补了现有方法仅关注单一评估者的不足。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge has emerged as a promising paradigm for evaluating natural language generation. However, the uncertainty associated with such evaluations remains largely unexplored, which limits their reliability in real-world applications. Although conformal prediction offers a principled framework for uncertainty quantification, existing approaches typically apply it to a single LLM judge, overlooking the variability introduced by using different LLM evaluators. In this work, we propose a robust uncertainty estimation framework for multi-agent LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation. Our approach constructs conformal prediction intervals for LLM-based scores from multiple LLMs. By considering intervals from different LLM judges, we obtain more stable and reliable uncertainty estimates. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method produces valid prediction intervals with coverage guarantees, and that interval-based aggregation across multiple judges leads to more stable evaluation outcomes.

</details>


### [122] [Parameterized and Streaming Algorithms for Euclidean Fair $k$-Center Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06384)
*Zeyu Lin,Chaoqi Jia,Longkun Guo,Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究欧几里得空间中的公平k中心聚类问题，提出了参数化近似算法（近似比2.732），并将其集成到单遍流式框架中获得4.464的近似比，通过指数级运行时可进一步改善到2.414和3.828。同时设计了多项式时间的单遍流式算法，近似比从4.732改进到4.42，优于现有最先进方法，实验验证了聚类精度的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习中公平性日益重要，公平k中心聚类作为基础问题受到广泛关注，但针对欧几里得空间的高效近似算法仍存在改进空间，尤其是大规模数据场景下的处理需求尚未得到充分满足。

Method: 首先提出参数化近似算法用于欧几里得公平k中心聚类获得2.732的近似比；将该算法作为后处理阶段嵌入单遍流式框架获得4.464的近似比；通过牺牲运行时间（指数于k）可将两算法分别改进至2.414和3.828；进一步设计多项式时间的单遍流式算法，近似比从4.732改进至4.42。

Result: 参数化算法达到2.732近似比，流式算法达到4.464近似比；指数运行时可优化至2.414和3.828；多项式时间流式算法达到4.42近似比，超越最先进的现有结果；大量实验表明方法在聚类精度上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 在欧几里得空间中，通过参数化技术和流式框架的有机结合，公平k中心聚类在近似比与运行时间之间实现了更优的权衡，并在大规模数据场景中验证了算法的实用性和有效性。

Abstract: Motivated by the growing importance of fairness in machine learning, fair $k$-center clustering has attracted considerable research attention as a fundamental problem. In this problem, a dataset is partitioned into $m$ disjoint groups, and the objective is to select $k$ data points as centers, subject to upper bounds on the number of centers chosen from each group, aiming to minimize the maximum distance between any data point and its assigned center. Focusing on Euclidean spaces, which are ubiquitous in machine learning applications, we first develop a parameterized approximation algorithm for Euclidean fair $k$-center with an approximation ratio of $2.732$. By incorporating this algorithm as a post-processing stage into a one-pass streaming framework for large-scale data, we obtain an approximation ratio of $4.464$. These ratios can be further respectively improved to $2.414$ and $3.828$ with a runtime exponential on $k$. To ensure polynomial-time complexity, we further design a one-pass streaming algorithm with an approximation ratio of $4.732$, which can be further improved to $4.42$, outperforming the state-of-the-art ratio. Finally, extensive experiments show that our methods significantly outperform state-of-the-art approaches in terms of clustering accuracy.

</details>


### [123] [Are Verifier Errors Independent Within a GRPO Group? Evidence from Qwen2.5 Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06386)
*Esther Xin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对基于群的RLVR（可验证奖励的群组强化学习）中验证器错误的组内相关性展开分析，基于24,998组每组8个Qwen2.5-1.5B生成的补全样本，发现组内验证器错误相关系数为0.530，对应有效样本量仅为1.70（8个补全的群组），且不同答案形式（分数、根式、符号表达式、区间等）的聚类程度差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RLVR的分析通常假设验证器错误相互独立，但共享答案格式可能导致错误之间存在依赖关系，从而高估样本信息量和低估验证器噪声的影响，需要对此依赖程度进行实证调查。

Method: 在三类数据集（MATH、GSM8K、DeepMath-103K）上收集24,998组由Qwen2.5-1.5B生成的8个补全，估计合并的组内验证器错误相关性和按答案形式分层的相关性；采用可交换误差模型计算设计效应调整后的有效样本量；并在四种基于规则的验证器配置下对比组内相对优势，识别优势信号不一致的群组比例。

Result: 组内验证器错误相关系数为0.530（95% CI: 0.500-0.560）；8补全组的设计效应调整有效样本量为1.70。分数、根式、符号表达式和区间形式的聚类强于单位标注和百分号。在四种验证器配置下，最多有0.83%的群组出现至少一个优势信号不一致。

Conclusion: 验证器错误在同一提示的多个补全之间存在显著依赖，这种组内聚类可能反映共享的提示难度和答案形式，提示后续研究应采用基于提示和答案形式的验证器噪声分析，而非仅依赖总体错误率的刻画。

Abstract: Group-based reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) scoresmultiple completions per prompt using automatic verifiers. Analysesbased on independent verifier errors may overlook dependence associatedwith shared answer formats. We investigate this dependence in24,998 groups of eight completions generated by Qwen2.5-1.5B onMATH, GSM8K, and DeepMath-103K. We estimate a pooled within-groupverifier-error correlation of 0.530 (95% confidence interval:0.500--0.560). Under an exchangeable-error model, this correspondsto a design-effect-adjusted effective sample size of 1.70 for aneight-completion group. Dependence varies substantially across answerforms: fractions, radicals, symbolic expressions, and intervals exhibitstronger clustering than unit annotations and percent signs. Replayinggroup-relative advantages across four rule-based verifier configurationsidentifies at least one advantage-sign disagreement in up to 0.83% ofgroups. Because a group is repeated sampling for one prompt, thiswithin-group clustering may reflect shared prompt difficulty as well asshared answer form, and we do not attempt to separate the two here.Unlike studies of correlated judgments across multiple evaluators, ouranalysis examines dependence across completions scored by the sameverifier. These findings motivate prompt- and answer-form-aware analysesof verifier noise rather than characterizations based solely onaggregate error rates.

</details>


### [124] [MetaRSI / RSI2: A Meta-Recursive Self-Improving System for Recursive Self-Improving Systems Themselves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06396)
*Zihan Tan,Leixin Sun,Zitong Shi,Yitao Liu,Jiajun Wu,Nathaniel Brooks,Jiaru Qian,Xiaoran Shang,Suyuan Huang,Yi Ding,Yangxu Liao,Mukai Li,Qiushi Sun,Shudong Liu,Xuankun Rong,Xiaohang Yu,Zhuo Chen,Hejia Geng,Chenxin Li,Aozhou Wang,Zengji Tu,Robert Tang,Yuxin Zhan,Eric Jiang,Yuxin Wu,Jianqing Zhang,Xiao Liang,Fang Wu,Haochi Zhang,Alexander Marlow,Guancheng Wan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaRSI-v1框架，将递归自我改进从单一编码/数学领域扩展到通用科学工程领域，通过数据、支架、模型三种算子的组合调度实现跨生产管线的自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RSI(递归自我改进)仅在编码和形式化基准上被验证，局限于机器可检查的领域，无法推广到需要论证、复现或测量的通用科学场景中，这限制了自我改进的通用能力发展。

Method: MetaRSI-v1将改进定义为三个类型化算子的调度组合：Data-RSI增强现有能力并标记边界，Harness-RSI在不触碰权重的情况下编辑五槽支架，Model-RSI通过有界训练将能力内化到参数中。三者共享循环内核和工件词汇表，通过二维优化器联合决定算子顺序和提案策略，由元级策略跨周期修订调度。

Result: 在代码和封闭式科学的领域标准评估中验证了MetaRSI-v1，无需外部教师，目标模型在自身循环中扮演所有角色。

Conclusion: MetaRSI-v1将自我改进从单一表面编辑重构为跨完整模型生产管线的组合操作，开辟了通过训练内化能力的模型路径和无需权重更新的接口驱动支架路径，并产生关于循环位置、算子组合和监督价值的可证伪定律。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) lets a system improve the model-building machinery from its own failures, so every later model inherits the gain. Yet RSI has been validated almost exclusively on coding and formal benchmarks such as science QA and mathematics. This format bound limits RSI to improvement within a machine-checkable slice, not general capability where questions are open and correctness is settled by argument, replication, or measurement. We argue RSI must next operate across real, diverse scientific, engineering, and meta-scientific domains, not where formal evaluation is merely tractable. To that end we present MetaRSI-v1, where improvement is the scheduled composition of three typed operators over one unified paradigm. Data-RSI amplifies existing competence and marks its boundary; Harness-RSI edits a five-slot scaffold without touching weights; Model-RSI internalizes capability into parameters through bounded training. Sharing one loop kernel and artifact vocabulary, they make data, scaffold, and model changes composable rather than exclusive. A two-axis optimizer jointly decides operator order and each operator's proposal policy, while a meta-level policy revises the schedule across terms. We validate MetaRSI-v1 under the field's standard evaluations, on code and closed-form science, with no external teacher: the target model plays every role in its own loop. MetaRSI-v1 reframes self-improvement from a single-surface edit to a composition across the full model-production pipeline, opening two paths: a model route internalizing capability through training, and a harness route leaving weights untouched and thus extending self-improvement to any model reachable through an interface, with Data-RSI redefined as the shared substrate feeding both. The framework further yields refutable laws on where loops exist, how operators compose, and what supervision buys.

</details>


### [125] [On BatchNorm Forward Modes in Value-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06421)
*Daniel Palenicek,Mikael Henaff,Scott Fujimoto,Koustuv Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了批量归一化（BN）在离散动作价值学习中的性能退化问题，发现BN前向传播中运行统计量（running statistics）与批统计量（batch statistics）的选择是逆转该退化现象的关键。在C51中切换BN自举前向为批统计模式可显著提升性能并稳定扩展到高更新-数据比；在PQN中对动作选择和自举均使用批统计量可恢复失效的性能。在26个Atari游戏中、4亿帧条件下，该配置的聚合得分超过使用LayerNorm的PQN，表明精细配置的BN能大幅改善离散动作价值学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批归一化在连续控制领域（如CrossQ）显著提升样本效率，但在离散动作价值学习（如Atari）中却报告了性能退化，这一失败现象出乎意料且缺乏合理解释。研究者希望通过揭示BN统计量使用方式的差异来解释并解决这一退化问题。

Method: 针对目标型C51和无目标型PQN两种算法，系统对比了不同前向传播路径（动作选择、自举等）中采用运行统计量与批统计量的配置组合，并通过26个Atari游戏、4亿帧的大规模实验验证配置效果，同时考察更新-数据比的扩展性。

Result: C51在BN自举前向使用批统计模式后显著超越未归一化和LayerNorm基线，且能稳定扩展到更新-数据比高达12；PQN在动作选择和自举均使用批统计量时恢复性能，最终聚合得分高于使用LayerNorm的PQN配置。

Conclusion: BN的前向传播协议（运行统计量与批统计量的选择）是算法规范中不可忽视的组成部分。精心配置的BN能够显著改进离散动作价值学习，揭示了此前性能退化源于对BN统计量使用方式的粗浅配置而非BN本身的局限。

Abstract: Batch normalization (BN) substantially improves sample efficiency in continuous-control actor-critic methods such as CrossQ, yet recent studies report performance degradation in discrete-action value learning on Atari. These failures are surprising because discrete Q-networks lack the action-input distribution mismatch identified by CrossQ. We show for target-based C51 and target-free PQN that the simple choice between running and batch statistics at specific forward passes can reverse this degradation. In C51, switching the BN bootstrap forward to batch-statistic mode significantly improves performance over unnormalized and LayerNorm baselines and scales stably with update-to-data ratios up to 12. In PQN, using batch-statistics for both action selection and bootstrapping recovers performance from the failing running-statistic configuration. Across 26 Atari games at 400M frames, this configuration achieves a higher final aggregate score than PQN with LayerNorm. Our results show that carefully configured BN can substantially improve discrete-action value learning, and that its forward protocols are an essential part of the algorithm specification.

</details>


### [126] [Sparse Oblique Rule Boosting for Simpler Additive Rule Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06426)
*Shahrzad Behzadimanesh,Pierre Le Bodic,Geoffrey I. Webb,Mario Boley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展的规则集成方法，通过学习稀疏线性变换的命题（$x^Tw \geq t$）替代传统的轴平行阈值条件（$x \geq t$），使决策区域从轴平行多面体推广为具有斜面的通用多面体。基于加权逻辑回归的梯度提升学习方法在14个回归和分类任务上验证，相比基线方法在保持相似或更好精度的同时降低了模型复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统符号规则集成依赖轴平行的阈值条件，虽然单个规则可解释性强且可高效用梯度提升学习，但对输入特征的表达能力要求高。当缺乏精心构造的特征时，需要增加规则的数量和复杂度来保证精度，从而损害模型可解释性。

Method: 扩展经典规则集成：引入带可学习稀疏权重向量的逻辑命题 $x^Tw \geq t$，替代传统的单变量阈值命题 $x \geq t$，使决策区域支持斜面的通用多面体；采用基于加权逻辑回归的梯度提升方法进行学习。

Result: 在14个回归和分类任务上的实验表明，所提方法以更低的模型复杂度达到与竞争基线相当或更好的预测精度。

Conclusion: 该方法在可解释性与精度之间提供了更好的权衡，同时减少了对人工特征工程的依赖。

Abstract: Small additive ensembles of symbolic rules offer interpretable prediction models. Traditionally, these ensembles use rule conditions based on conjunctions of simple threshold propositions $x \geq t$ on a single input variable $x$ and threshold $t$, resulting geometrically in axis-parallel polytopes as decision regions. While this form ensures a high degree of interpretability for individual rules and can be learned efficiently using the gradient boosting approach, it relies on having access to a curated set of expressive input features so that a small ensemble of axis-parallel regions can describe the target variable well. Absent such features, reaching sufficient accuracy requires increasing the number and complexity of individual rules, which diminishes the interpretability of the model. Here, we extend classical rule ensembles by introducing logical propositions with learnable sparse linear transformations of input variables, i.e., propositions of the form $\mathbf{x}^T\mathbf{w} \geq t$, where $\mathbf{w}$ is a learnable sparse weight vector, enabling decision regions as general polyhedrons with oblique faces. We propose a learning method using gradient boosting based on a weighted logistic regression. Empirical results across 14 regression and classification tasks demonstrate that the proposed method achieves lower model complexity than competitive baselines while maintaining similar or better predictive accuracy. Hence, the approach provides a favorable trade-off between interpretability and accuracy and reduces the reliance on manual feature engineering.

</details>


### [127] [Stability and Generalization of Straight-Through Estimators for Training Two-Layer Quantized Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06430)
*Yiming Ying*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从统计学习理论（SLT）角度研究二元激活网络中使用身份直通估计器（STE）训练的泛化性质，通过将零初始化的逐样本STE递归等价于凸潜损失上的随机次梯度下降，推导出精确距离恒等式和近似非扩张性，进而获得显式的ℓ2平均模型稳定性和泛化界，以及O(n^{-1/2})的额外诱导风险率；在边缘可分假设下，还给出了最优阶O(R²/(γ²n))的期望额外误分类误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题在于：不连续的STE训练规则产生的估计器，其统计泛化性能否由算法稳定性来解释？这关乎二元激活网络这一非光滑、非凸训练系统泛化理论的基础理解。

Method: 将零初始化的逐样本STE递归在饱和输出区域精确表示为凸潜损失(-yu^Tx)_+上的随机次梯度下降，使其可进行稳定性分析；推导两个耦合更新的精确距离恒等式，证明公共示例映射的近似非扩张性（仅当两个潜边距跨越零时产生二次缺陷）；利用ℓ2平均模型稳定性界，将稳定性从潜向量等距传递到完整的第一层矩阵；结合优化界获得显式超额诱导风险保证；在边缘可分性条件下，通过互补论证推导随机单遍STE迭代的期望超额误分类误差及多数投票界。

Result: 获得显式的ℓ2平均模型稳定性与泛化界；结合稳定性与标准优化界，得到当T=n²时的O(n^{-1/2})额外诱导风险率；在边缘可分条件下，得到最优阶O(R²/(γ²n))的期望超额误分类误差界以及对应的多数投票界。

Conclusion: 算法稳定性确实可以解释STE训练二元激活网络所产生的估计器的统计泛化性，且获得的泛化速率在特定条件下达到最优阶。

Abstract: We study the identity straight-through estimator (STE) for training a two-layer binary-activation network with hinge loss from the perspective of Statistical Learning Theory (SLT). Our central question is whether algorithmic stability can explain the statistical generalization of the estimator produced by the discontinuous STE training rule. In the saturated-output regime, the zero-initialized samplewise STE recursion is exactly the stochastic subgradient descent on the convex latent loss $(-yu^\top x)_+$. This representation makes a stability analysis possible. We derive an exact distance identity for two coupled updates and prove approximate non-expansiveness of the common-example map, with a quadratic defect only when the two latent margins straddle zero. We then obtain explicit $\ell_2$ on-average model-stability and generalization bounds, transferring stability isometrically from the latent vector to the full first-layer matrix. Combining stability with a standard optimization bound yields an explicit excess induced-risk guarantee and the rate $O(n^{-1/2})$ when $T=n^2$. Under margin separability, a complementary argument gives the optimal-order $O(R^2/(γ^2n))$ expected excess misclassification error for a randomized one-pass STE iterate and a corresponding majority-vote bound.

</details>


### [128] [A Theoretical Framework for Masked Pretraining (MPT)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06460)
*Qi Zhang,Runyu Zhou,Yifei Wang,Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个理论框架来分析掩码预训练（MPT），建立了MPT与对比学习之间的理论联系，证明了掩码技术隐式创建语义相似的正样本对。同时指出MPT存在维度坍缩问题，并提出均匀性增强的U-MPT损失来解决该问题，还提出了新的掩码策略以提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码预训练（MPT）在多个领域的自监督学习中取得了优异表现，但其背后的工作机制缺乏理论理解，因此需要对MPT的理论基础进行深入研究。

Method: 作者提出新的理论框架分析MPT，建立其与对比学习的理论连接，证明掩码隐式创建语义相似的正样本对；发现维度坍缩问题并提出U-MPT损失；基于理论分析提出新的掩码策略，并建立下游任务的性能保证。

Result: U-MPT损失在真实数据集上的线性评估、跨数据集微调和分布外泛化等下游任务中带来了显著改进，新提出的掩码策略也增强了MPT的下游表现。

Conclusion: 该工作从理论上揭示了MPT的功能机制，解决了维度坍缩问题，并提供了理论指导下的掩码策略改进，为理解和发展掩码预训练范式提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Recently, Masked Pretraining (MPT) based on reconstruction pretraining tasks has risen to a promising self-supervised learning paradigm across various domains and achieves remarkable performance in multiple downstream tasks. However, the theoretical understanding of the working mechanism behind MPT is still limited. In this paper, we introduce a new theoretical framework to analyze MPT and understand the crucial role of masking in extracting meaningful representations. We establish theoretical connections between MPT and another popular self-supervised paradigm: contrastive learning. We prove that the masking technique implicitly creates positive pairs that are semantically similar and the reconstruction loss pulls them together in the feature space. Besides, as a result of the implicit alignment, we point out the dimensional collapse issue of MPT and propose a Uniformity-enhanced MPT (U-MPT) loss that can effectively address this issue and bring significant improvements in downstream tasks including linear evaluation, cross-dataset fine-tuning and out-of-distribution generalization on real-world data sets. Furthermore, we establish downstream guarantees of U-MPT and theoretically analyze the influence of masking strategies. Based on the theoretical analysis, we propose a new masking strategy which enhances the downstream performance of MPT and explains current improvements of masking strategies with our theoretical perspective.

</details>


### [129] [Local and Global Stability in Performative Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06467)
*Debmalya Mandal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了表现型强化学习中策略混合的稳定性问题，区分了局部混合稳定性和全局混合稳定性两种概念，证明了加权逐状态Hedge动态在任意（甚至不连续）环境映射下能以O(1/√T)的速率驱动局部稳定性间隙趋于零，并提出了比Lipschitz灵敏度更弱的"有界转移范围"假设以保证全局稳定性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表现型强化学习的收敛保证依赖于环境映射π↦(P_π, r_π)的Lipschitz灵敏度假设，但这些假设难以验证，且在多重智能体最优反应动态等场景中不成立。作者希望研究策略混合下的稳定性，探索是否能在不依赖灵敏度假设的情况下获得收敛保证。

Method: 作者区分了局部混合稳定性（基于占用权重的二阶松弛，等价于平稳性）和全局混合稳定性（可抵御任意偏离策略）。设计了加权逐状态Hedge动力学，并分别对其在精确反馈和轨迹反馈下分析了收敛性。此外提出比Lipschitz灵敏度更弱的"有界转移范围"假设来证明全局稳定性收敛，并将两种概念推广到n人表现型马尔可夫博弈。

Result: 加权逐状态Hedge动态在任意（可能不连续）环境映射下使局部稳定性间隙以O(1/√T)速率收敛到零（精确和轨迹反馈均适用）；证明了局部与全局稳定性确实不同（存在反例）；在有界转移范围假设下，无权重逐状态Hedge收敛至O(γε_P/(1-γ)³)的下限，并证明匹配的Ω(γε_P/(1-γ))下界不可回避；局部稳定性在n人表现型马尔可夫博弈中无需任何假设即可获得，全局稳定性在表现型马尔可夫势博弈中成立。

Conclusion: 与表现型预测不同，表现型强化学习中随机化并不能完全消除灵敏度假设的需求。局部稳定性对任意环境映射都有保证，但全局稳定性仍需要比Lipschitz灵敏度更弱的有界转移范围假设。两种稳定性概念在表现型马尔可夫博弈场景下也获得了相应的收敛保证。

Abstract: In performative reinforcement learning the deployed policy shapes the environment that generates the learner's future data, and the natural solution concept is a performatively stable policy that is optimal in the environment it induces. Existing convergence guarantees rely on Lipschitz sensitivity assumptions on the environment map $π\mapsto (P_π, r_π)$, which are hard to verify and fail in settings such as multi-agent best-response dynamics. We instead study stability for mixtures of policies, and show that the resulting picture is fundamentally different from performative prediction, where randomization removes the need for any sensitivity assumption. We distinguish local mixed stability, an occupancy-weighted first-order relaxation that we show is equivalent to stationarity, from global mixed stability, which certifies against arbitrary deviating policies. Our first result is that a weighted per-state Hedge dynamic drives the local stability gap to zero at an $O(1/\sqrt{T})$ rate for an arbitrary, possibly discontinuous, environment map, both with exact and with trajectory feedback. The two notions genuinely differ: we exhibit an instance where local stability is achieved exactly but every mixture has global stability gap bounded away from zero. For global stability we introduce a bounded transition range assumption, strictly weaker than Lipschitz sensitivity, under which unweighted per-state Hedge converges up to a floor of $O(γε_P/(1-γ)^3)$, and we prove a matching-in-$ε_P$ lower bound of $Ω(γε_P/(1-γ))$ under trajectory feedback, so this floor is unavoidable. Finally, we extend both notions to $n$-player performative Markov games, obtaining local stability with no assumption on the joint environment map or game structure, and global stability for performative Markov potential games.

</details>


### [130] [Sector-Mean: Deterministic Initialization of K-Means Centroids via Angular Sector Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06468)
*Abhiyan Dhakal,Pranish Kafle,Rajani Chulyadyo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Sector-Mean初始化为K-Means提供确定性初始质心选择，通过围绕全局质心将二维数据空间划分为角度扇区并计算扇区均值，时间复杂度为O(N)。实验表明该方法与K-Means++和Max-Min聚类质量相当，但初始化时间分别减少74.9%和59.8%，迭代次数减少约5%和16%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K-Means算法对初始质心选择高度敏感，不恰当的初始质心会严重影响收敛速度和聚类精度，现有初始化方法（如随机、K-Means++、Max-Min）在计算效率或确定性方面存在不足，亟需一种既高效又确定性的初始化策略。

Method: 提出Sector-Mean初始化算法：以全局质心为中心，将二维数据空间划分为若干角度扇区，然后利用各扇区内的数据均值作为初始质心。该策略为确定性方法，时间复杂度为O(N)。通过与随机、K-Means++和Max-Min在相同Lloyd迭代条件下进行对比实验。

Result: 在SIPU、Birch基准及真实数据集上的实验结果表明：Sector-Mean在聚类质量上与K-Means++和Max-Min相当（经Friedman检验p<0.05及Nemenyi事后检验确认）；初始化时间相比K-Means++减少74.9%，相比Max-Min减少59.8%；平均迭代次数最少，比K-Means++少约5%，比Max-Min少约16%。

Conclusion: Sector-Mean初始化提供了一种确定性强、计算高效的K-Means初始化方案，能在保持聚类质量的前提下显著降低计算开销和迭代次数，是一种实用且有竞争力的初始化策略。

Abstract: K-Means is one of the most widely used clustering algorithms, but its susceptibility to initial centroid selection remains a primary bottleneck for its convergence speed and clustering accuracy. This paper proposes Sector-Mean Initialization, a deterministic initialization strategy with O(N) time complexity that partitions the two-dimensional data space into angular sectors around the global centroid and initializes centroids using sector-wise means. We evaluate the method on established two-dimensional benchmarks (SIPU, Birch) and multiple real-world datasets, comparing against random, K-Means++, and Max-Min initialization under identical Lloyd iterations. The statistical analysis of Friedman's test (p<0.05) and Nemenyi post-hoc comparison indicates that, while delivering equivalent clustering quality as K-Means++ and Max-Min, Sector-Mean offers significant computational efficiency. Experimental results show that Sector-Mean reduces the initialization time by 74.9% and 59.8% in comparison to K-Means++ and max-min, respectively. And, it yields the lowest average number of iterations, achieving approximately 5% fewer iterations than K-Means++ and 16% fewer than max-min. These results highlight that Sector-Mean initialization offers a deterministic and computationally efficient initialization strategy while preserving cluster quality.

</details>


### [131] [One Step, One Lead: Mitigating Higher-Order Interference in Multi-Domain Reinforcement Learning via Cross-Step Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06469)
*Zihan Lin,Xiaohan Wang,Jie Cao,Jiajun Chai,Guojun Yin,Wei Lin,Ran He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OSOL方法，用于解决多领域强化学习训练中大语言模型（LLM）的跨领域干扰问题。作者发现传统的单步视角（基于一阶梯度的对齐或曲率代理）会遗漏一种关键的顺序干扰：同点领域梯度即使在输出空间中高度正交，也可能因为连续的真实更新而部分相互逆转。为此，论文提出利用相邻检查点的token对数概率足迹来直接捕获这种跨步交互（即输出空间中的局部二阶交互），无需显式重建同步曲率。OSOL在每次迭代中指定一个焦点领域，基于前一检查点的足迹对token级反弹风险进行排序，并在标准GRPO更新中应用漂移排序的自适应校正。实验表明，该方法在Qwen3-30B-A3B上将领域宏平均提升至0.4822，相比最强基线提高5.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多领域强化学习联合训练虽然能拓宽LLM的推理能力，但常导致个体领域性能下降和优化不稳定。现有诊断方法（如一阶梯度和曲率代理）仅从单步视角判断干扰，忽略了跨时间步的顺序干扰——同点梯度近乎正交不代表连续更新不会相互逆转。作者希望找到一种不依赖昂贵高阶求导的、能准确预测未来反弹风险的手段。

Method: OSOL方法的核心包括：（1）证明连续token对数概率足迹（相邻检查点间的差异）可以从局部二阶交互角度捕获跨步输出回溯，无需显式重建同步曲率；（2）每次迭代指定一个焦点领域，利用前一检查点的足迹对token级"反弹风险"（即后续更新可能逆转先前进展的风险）排序；（3）在标准GRPO更新中引入基于漂移排序的自适应缩放校正，定点压制跨步输出回溯分量。

Result: 对照实验显示：（1）跨步回溯比同点梯度诊断与后续任务损伤的关联更强；（2）前一检查点足迹对反弹风险的预测准确性优于Hessian代理方法。在实际模型Qwen3-30B-A3B上，OSOL达到0.4822的领域宏平均，比最强基线提升5.7%，且无需显式的高阶微分。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了一种新的多领域RL干扰类型——跨步顺序干扰，并证明了基于相邻检查点足迹的轻量化诊断比传统Hessian或梯度代理更有效。OSOL在不需要额外高阶微分的前提下有效缓解了跨领域训练中的性能回退，为多领域联合优化LLM推理能力提供了实用且高效的思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) across multiple domains can broaden the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs), yet joint training often degrades individual-domain performance and can destabilize optimization. Existing work typically diagnoses such interference from a single-step view using first-order gradient alignment or curvature-based proxies. We show that this view can miss a critical form of sequential interference: same-point domain gradients may remain nearly orthogonal even when consecutive realized updates partially reverse one another in output space. We further show that consecutive token log-probability footprints recover this interaction directly from adjacent checkpoints as a local second-order interaction in output space, without explicitly reconstructing same-step curvature. Building on this insight, we propose OSOL, which designates a focus domain at each iteration, uses the preceding checkpoint footprint to rank token-level rebound risk, and applies a drift-ranked, adaptively scaled correction within the standard GRPO update. Our analysis shows that this correction suppresses the targeted cross-step output backtracking component. Controlled studies further show that cross-step backtracking is more strongly associated with subsequent task damage than same-point gradient diagnostics, while the preceding footprint ranks future rebound risk more accurately than Hessian-based proxies. On Qwen3-30B-A3B, OSOL reaches a domain-macro average of 0.4822, improving by 5.7% over the strongest compared baseline, without explicit higher-order differentiation.

</details>


### [132] [Steering Under Compression: Dose-Response, Capability Cost, and Failure Asymmetry in Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06473)
*Saurav Bhandari,Benjamin Wade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统研究了激活引导（activation steering）在权重量化（INT8和NF4）条件下的行为鲁棒性。发现情感引导在量化后基本完好，但推理长度引导表现出不对称的剂量-反应行为（延长是渐进的但最终崩溃，缩短是阶跃函数并快速失败）。提出了删失（censored）等效能阶梯构来解决方法论陷阱，并揭示量化可能对基线能力产生显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导和权重量化分别是大模型行为控制和高效部署的关键技术，两者在实践中越来越常被同时使用，但其交互机制尚未被系统研究。

Method: 在四种7-9B开源模型上，对INT8和NF4权重量化下的激活引导进行系统评测，覆盖情感引导和推理长度引导两类行为目标。采用等效能（iso-effect）框架匹配行为效果后比较能力代价，提出删失等效能阶梯解决地板有界目标的阶梯锚定问题，并进行GSM8K v2.3.1统一重新评分修正解析器伪影。

Result: 情感引导在INT8下通过等价性检验（对比度-0.010），NF4下不结论；推理长度引导呈不对称剂量-反应：延长为渐变但会崩溃，缩短为阶跃函数（仅12-30%缩短后即失败）。Mistral-NF4基线能力大幅下降（0.545→0.365），但引导向量与FP16版本高度共线（余弦相似度0.945-0.998）。

Conclusion: 激活引导的行为方向在量化后得以保持，但其成本结构会因量化而改变；压缩可能压倒引导干预的效果。研究揭示了量化与引导协同使用时的关键交互特性及方法论陷阱，并公开了全部代码与数据。

Abstract: Inference-time activation steering enables behavioral control of large language models without parameter modification, while post-training quantization reduces memory and compute costs for deployment. Despite their growing convergence in practice, the interaction between these two techniques remains uncharacterized. We systematically study activation steering under weight-only quantization (INT8 and NF4) across four open-weight 7-9B models and two behavioral targets: judged sentiment and judge-free reasoning length. Using an iso-effect framework that compares capability costs at matched behavioral effect, we find that sentiment steering survives quantization intact. After correcting a GSM8K parser artifact with a uniform v2.3.1 rescore, the pooled INT8 contrast is -0.010 (90% CI [-0.026, +0.007]), descriptively Equivalent under the preregistered three-label rule, while NF4 remains Inconclusive at -0.017 ([-0.067, +0.033]). In contrast, reasoning length exhibits a surprising asymmetric dose-response: lengthening is graded but terminates in cap-runaway and collapse, while shortening is a step function with only 12-30% shortening (model-dependent) before discontinuous failure. We expose a methodological pitfall: the naive iso-effect ladder anchors on the collapse floor for floor-bounded targets, and we introduce a censored construction that restores interpretable crossings. We also quantify a substantial baseline capability shift for Mistral-NF4 (0.545 to 0.365 GSM8K at alpha=0), demonstrating that compression can dominate the steering intervention. Despite this, steering vectors remain highly collinear with their FP16 siblings (cosine similarity 0.989-0.998 for INT8, 0.945-0.990 for NF4), confirming that the behavioral direction survives quantization even when the cost structure does not. All code and data are released.

</details>


### [133] [Learning Kernels by Alignment for Multiclass Bayes Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06474)
*Hollan Haule,Alfredo Gonzalez-Sulser,Javier Escudero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将核方法中的核函数通过对齐方式学习得到，并基于Collaborative Learning and Inference (CLaI)框架进行了泛化，用学习的马氏距离替代余弦相似度并扩展到多分类场景，在多个基准数据集上取得了更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统核方法需要预先选定额外的核函数，这限制了其适用性和表达力。本文旨在证明核函数可以通过对齐方式自动学习，从而建立表示学习、核对齐与贝叶斯分类之间的统一理论框架。

Method: 将Collaborative Learning视为核对齐过程（训练嵌入使其诱导的相似度与标签派生的目标核匹配），将Collaborative Inference等价于带Parzen窗密度估计的核贝叶斯分类。在此基础上，用学习的马氏距离替换余弦相似度来泛化CLaI，并扩展至多分类设置。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、PathMNIST和SleepEDF数据集上，马氏距离版本相较余弦版本在准确率、收敛速度和校准误差方面均有改善。辅助实验表明CLaI产生的潜在信号与高斯过程形式一致，且在脓毒症预测上实现了有竞争力的校准性能。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个原理性驱动的学习核框架，统一了表示学习、核对齐和贝叶斯分类，并自然地扩展到多分类场景，为核方法的应用提供了理论依据和实践提升。

Abstract: Kernel methods separate data representation from decision-making, but typically require the kernel to be chosen in advance. We show that this kernel can instead be learned by alignment, and develop the resulting framework through the recently introduced Collaborative Learning and Inference (CLaI). We show that Collaborative Learning can be viewed as a kernel alignment process, in which an embedding is trained so that its induced similarity matches a label-derived target kernel. We also prove that Collaborative Inference is equivalent to kernel Bayes classification with Parzen-window density estimation. Motivated by these perspectives, we generalise CLaI by replacing cosine similarity with a learned Mahalanobis distance and extend it to multiclass classification. On CIFAR-10, PathMNIST, and SleepEDF, the Mahalanobis formulation improves accuracy, converges faster, and yields lower calibration error than the cosine-based variant. Auxiliary experiments further support these connections, showing that CLaI produces latent signals of the same form as a Gaussian process, while achieving competitive calibration on sepsis prediction. Together, these results establish a principled learned-kernel framework that unifies representation learning, kernel alignment, and Bayesian classification, and extends naturally to the multiclass setting.

</details>


### [134] [How Does Parameter Pruning Reshape DNN Representations? An Interaction-Driven Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06483)
*Fangbo Li,Junpeng Zhang,Qihan Ren,Quanshi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度神经网络（DNN）在不同参数剪枝时性能退化差异的内部因素，发现剪枝过程中模型编码的交互模式呈现三阶段动态：在剪枝去除低阶交互之前性能基本不受影响，且低阶交互具有强泛化性。性能对特定模块剪枝的高敏感性取决于该剪枝是否去除了可泛化的低阶交互模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解为什么剪枝某些参数会导致显著性能退化，而剪枝其他参数不会，旨在从内部机制层面解释DNN性能退化差异的根本原因。

Method: 通过研究剪枝操作如何影响DNN编码的交互模式，逐步增加剪枝比例观察交互模式的动态变化，并分析性能敏感性与低阶交互去除之间的关联。

Result: 发现DNN编码的交互模式在剪枝过程中呈现三阶段动态：性能在低阶交互被移除前基本不受影响；低阶交互表现出强泛化性；性能对特定模块剪枝的高敏感性源于该剪枝是否去除了可泛化的低阶交互模式。

Conclusion: DNN剪枝性能退化的关键决定性因素是低阶泛化交互模式是否被移除，这为理解剪枝的敏感性差异提供了统一的理论解释框架。

Abstract: This study focuses on the scientific problem of understanding internal factors that govern the diverse performance degradation of deep neural networks (DNNs) when different parameters are pruned. In order to explain why pruning certain parameters leads to significant performance degradation but pruning other parameters does not, we examine how the pruning operation affects the interaction patterns encoded by the DNN. We find that when we progressively increase the pruning ratio, the interaction patterns encoded by DNNs exhibit a distinct three-phase dynamics, \emph{i.e.}, model performance is not largely affected until the pruning operation begins to remove low-order interactions, and low-order interactions exhibit strong generalizability. Moreover, we find that the high sensitivity of DNN performance to the pruning of certain modules is attributed to whether the pruning operation removes generalizable low-order interaction patterns.

</details>


### [135] [Second-Order Smooth Planning with Optimal-Transport Bellman Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06484)
*Tuan Dam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SecondOrderSmoothCruiser算法，通过最优传输平滑的Bellman备份（具有闭式解、策略梯度和Lipschitz Hessian）实现二阶修正，将规划器的oracle复杂度从一阶SmoothCruiser的Õ(ε⁻⁴)提升到Õ(ε⁻³)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SmoothCruiser虽然实现了问题无关的Õ(ε⁻⁴)复杂度，但其估计器仅为一阶。作者希望探索更高阶估计是否能进一步降低样本复杂度，并发现复杂度指数由局部Taylor余项的阶数β决定。

Method: 证明SmoothCruiser型规划器的样本复杂度指数由局部Taylor余项阶数β决定（复杂度为Õ(ε^{-(2+2/(β-1))})）；提出基于最优传输平滑的Bellman备份（在动作分布上），该备份具有闭式解、策略梯度和Lipschitz Hessian，其二阶修正项可通过无偏叉积估计器计算；由此构造SecondOrderSmoothCruiser算法。

Result: SecondOrderSmoothCruiser在固定OT参数下实现Õ(ε⁻³)的oracle复杂度（对应β=3的二阶/三次余项情形），优于一阶SmoothCruiser的Õ(ε⁻⁴)；并给出了OT、熵正则化与无正则化目标之间显式的正则化偏差界。

Conclusion: 通过引入最优传输平滑与二阶修正，可以突破一阶SmoothCruiser的复杂度瓶颈，实现更高效的问题无关规划复杂度，且各目标之间的偏差有界可控。

Abstract: Planning with a generative model aims to estimate the value of a state using as few simulator calls as possible. SmoothCruiser achieves problem-independent complexity $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-4})$ by exploiting the smoothness of the entropy-regularized Bellman backup, but its estimator is only first-order. We show that the sample-complexity exponent of SmoothCruiser-type planners is governed by the order $β$ of the local Taylor remainder, giving oracle complexity $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-(2+2/(β-1))})$: the first-order case $β=2$ recovers SmoothCruiser, while a second-order/cubic remainder $β=3$ yields $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$. We reach this regime with an optimal-transport-smoothed Bellman backup over action distributions, which has a closed form, a policy gradient, and a Lipschitz Hessian, and whose quadratic correction admits an unbiased cross-product estimator. The resulting SecondOrderSmoothCruiser achieves $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ oracle complexity for fixed OT parameters, and we relate the OT, entropy-regularized, and unregularized objectives through explicit regularization-bias bounds.

</details>


### [136] [Power Mean Estimation in Stochastic Continuous Monte Carlo Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06489)
*Tuan Dam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的MCTS算法（\Algname），用于连续且随机MDP的在线规划。该算法结合幂均值作为价值备份算子与多项式探索奖励，在随机环境中实现了多项式收敛率O(n^{-ζ})，ζ∈(0,1/2)，将POLY-HOOT的非渐近收敛保证扩展到了随机环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCTS在确定性环境中表现优异，但适配连续状态-动作空间的随机MDP仍面临重大挑战。现有方法HOOT采用对数探索奖励，在非平稳随机场景缺乏理论保证；POLY-HOOT虽引入多项式奖励项实现确定性MDP收敛，但其对随机MDP的理论尚未建立。

Method: 提出\Algname算法，核心包括两点创新：(1) 使用幂均值（power mean）作为价值备份算子；(2) 结合多项式探索奖励来处理连续动作空间中的非平稳性问题。

Result: 理论分析证明\Algname以多项式速率O(n^{-ζ})收敛（ζ∈(0,1/2)，n为访问轨迹数），将POLY-HOOT的非渐近收敛保证扩展至随机环境。随机任务的实验结果验证了理论发现，展示了算法在连续随机域中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了MCTS在连续随机MDP中理论收敛保证的空白，通过幂均值备份与多项式奖励的有机结合，成功将确定性环境的收敛理论推广至更贴近实际应用的随机场景，并在实验中得到验证。

Abstract: Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) has demonstrated success in online planning for deterministic environments, yet significant challenges remain in adapting it to stochastic Markov Decision Processes (MDPs), particularly in continuous state-action spaces. Existing methods, such as HOOT, which combines MCTS with the Hierarchical Optimistic Optimization (HOO) bandit strategy, address continuous spaces but rely on a logarithmic exploration bonus that lacks theoretical guarantees in non-stationary, stochastic settings. Recent advancements, such as POLY-HOOT, introduced a polynomial bonus term to achieve convergence in deterministic MDPs, though a similar theory for stochastic MDPs remains undeveloped. In this paper, we propose a novel MCTS algorithm, \Algname, designed for continuous, stochastic MDPs. \Algname integrates a power mean as a value backup operator, alongside a polynomial exploration bonus to address the non-stationarity inherent in continuous action spaces. Our theoretical analysis establishes that \Algname converges at a polynomial rate of $\mathcal{O}(n^{-ζ})$, $ζ\in (0,1/2)$, where \( n \) is the number of visited trajectories, thereby extending the non-asymptotic convergence guarantees of POLY-HOOT to stochastic environments. Experimental results on stochastic tasks validate our theoretical findings, demonstrating the effectiveness of \Algname in continuous, stochastic domains.

</details>


### [137] [Structural Entropy-Driven Graph Diffusion Generation for One-Shot Federated Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06499)
*Shutong Zheng,Lele Fu,Sheng Huang,Wei Yang Bryan Lim,Chuan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPIRE是一种基于结构熵驱动的图扩散生成方法，用于一次性联邦图学习，通过拓扑感知的客户端差异化加权和服务器端图扩散模型生成伪图来训练全局GNN，在七个真实数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的一次性联邦图学习方法主要基于数据量进行客户端加权，忽略了图连接结构的组织方式差异，导致客户端贡献估计不准确，难以适应高度异质性的图数据分布。

Method: SPIRE方法包含两个核心组件：(1) 使用一阶度分布结构熵作为图拓扑的紧凑描述符，推导结构客户端权重，提供超越数据量的归纳偏置；(2) 在服务器端使用图扩散模型生成以加权客户端原型为条件的伪图，捕获语义和结构信息，通过不相交并集融合训练全局GNN。

Result: 在七个真实世界图数据集上的大量实验表明，SPIRE一致优于传统和一次性FGL方法，在高异质性（非IID）和图扰动场景下提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: SPIRE证明了将结构熵引入一次性联邦图学习的客户端加权和生成阶段，能够有效提升全局模型的性能，特别是在高度异构和扰动图数据场景下具有显著优势。

Abstract: One-shot federated graph learning (FGL) requires the server to estimate client contributions from highly compressed information, yet conventional volume-based weighting captures the amount of client data while overlooking how its connectivity is organized. In this paper, we propose SPIRE, a Structural Entropy-Driven Graph Diffusion Generation method that introduces topology-aware client differentiation into one-shot FGL. Specifically, we employ first-order degree-distribution structural entropy as a compact descriptor of degree-mass dispersion and use it to derive structural client weights, providing an inductive bias that accounts for differences in graph topology beyond data volume.
