On the generation side, a graph diffusion model on the server synthesizes pseudographs conditioned on the weighted client prototypes, capturing both semantic and structural information without requiring additional client-side training. The generated pseudographs are then assembled via disjoint union fusion to train a global graph neural network. Extensive experiments on seven real-world graph datasets demonstrate that SPIRE consistently outperforms conventional and one-shot FGL methods, with particularly strong gains under highly heterogeneous (non-IID) and graph-perturbed settings.

</details>


### [138] [Bi-HYCO: Bi-Objective Cooperative Learning for PDE Parameter Identification under Fragmented Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06511)
*Umberto Biccari,Jun Chen,Roberto Morales,Enrique Zuazua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Bi-Objective HYCO（Bi-HYCO），一种协同框架，在保留物理模型与合成模型各自表示及其局部观测目标的同时，通过无标注交互点耦合两者预测状态，构建向量值双目标优化问题，并通过加权标量化实现计算。理论证明了交替序列的充分下降与有限长度及其收敛到混合临界点，实验验证了椭圆传输方程和二维Navier-Stokes方程下的参数与状态重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理模型与合成模型可能描述同一PDE系统的互补方面，但各自接收的观测可能是不同甚至碎片化的。现有方法难以有效融合这两种模型的互补优势与分散观测信息，需要一种既能保持各自表示能力又能实现协作的框架。

Method: Bi-HYCO采用协同框架，同时保留两种模型表示及其局部观测目标，在无标注交互点（不含测量、不增广数据）上耦合两者的预测状态作为通信机制。将两个准则构成向量值目标，通过加权标量化获得计算实现。对确定性共享观测算法，在固定交互点设置下利用Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz型假设建立收敛分析。

Result: 在椭圆传输方程和二维Navier-Stokes方程实验中评估了参数与状态重建、噪声与标量化影响，并对比了PINN/XPINN基线。消融实验表明，在测试配置中移除状态交互仅保留聚合会恶化参数恢复，尤其对Navier-Stokes问题影响显著。

Conclusion: Bi-HYCO通过交互点状态耦合而非简单的数据聚合，可有效融合互补的双模型表示，提升参数恢复质量，在保持理论收敛保证的同时展现出优于PINN/XPINN参照的性能。

Abstract: Physical and synthetic models may describe complementary aspects of the same PDE-governed system while receiving different, possibly fragmented, observations. We propose Bi-Objective HYCO (Bi-HYCO), a cooperative framework that retains both representations and their local observational objectives while coupling their predicted states at unlabeled interaction points. These points contain no measurements and do not augment the data; they provide a communication mechanism in the common state space. The two criteria form a vector-valued objective, and weighted scalarizations provide computational realizations. For the deterministic shared-observation algorithm with fixed interaction points, we prove sufficient decrease and finite length of the whole alternating sequence, which converges to a mixed critical point under the stated Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz-type assumptions. Elliptic transmission and two-dimensional Navier-Stokes experiments assess parameter and state reconstruction, noise and scalarization effects, and PINN/XPINN references. Ablations show that removing state interaction while retaining aggregation deteriorates parameter recovery in the tested configurations, particularly for Navier-Stokes.

</details>


### [139] [Model-Adaptive and Risk-Constrained Frequency Hopping Against Predictive Jammers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06514)
*Yanbo Chen,Xinjing Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出D-PACT-AFH框架，一种面向预测性干扰的模型自适应与风险约束对抗式上下文赌博机方法，通过Tsallis-FTRL主算法结合全局线性学习器与分区局部学习器进行在线模型选择，并引入两种变体（D-PACT-Hit和D-PACT-Safe）分别处理信道边际命中风险与每时隙风险预算约束，实验验证了其模型适应能力与可控的吞吐-风险权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应跳频对抗预测性干扰面临两大挑战：一是上下文-丢包关系在不同运行场景下可能变化（模型不确定性），二是持续跳频模式可能暴露高概率信道从而遭受攻击（策略暴露风险）。现有方法未能同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出D-PACT-AFH框架，采用Tsallis-FTRL主算法组合全局线性学习器与分区局部学习器并在线上选择模型类别。提出两种变体：D-PACT-Hit将信道边际命中风险纳入模型选择；D-PACT-Safe通过最小KL散度投影强制执行每时隙风险预算。同时建立了非预见性攻击下的估计量有效性、相对最优固定基线的oracle分解以及Safe投影的精确条件风险保证。

Result: 实验覆盖多种信道场景与干扰类型，D-PACT-AFH在可观测切换场景下恢复局部学习器95.5%的增益，同时在负对照场景下避免其77.7%的性能退化，展示了有效的模型适应能力与可控的goodput-风险权衡。

Conclusion: D-PACT-AFH能够在模型不确定性与策略暴露风险并存的环境中实现有效的自适应跳频，通过在线模型选择和风险约束机制，在性能增益与风险控制之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Adaptive frequency hopping against predictive jamming must address both model uncertainty and policy exposure: the context-loss relationship may vary across operating regimes, while persistent hopping patterns may expose high-probability channels to attack. We propose D-PACT-AFH, a model-adaptive and risk-constrained adversarial contextual-bandit framework in which a Tsallis-FTRL master combines a global linear learner with a partitioned local learner and selects the model class online. D-PACT-Hit incorporates channel-wise marginal hit risk into model selection, while D-PACT-Safe applies a minimum-Kullback-Leibler projection to enforce a per-slot risk budget. We establish estimator validity under non-anticipating attacks, an oracle decomposition relative to the better fixed base, and an exact conditional-risk guarantee for the Safe projection. Experiments across diverse channel regimes and jammer types demonstrate effective model adaptation and a controllable goodput-risk tradeoff: D-PACT-AFH recovers 95.5% of the local learner's gain under observable switching while avoiding 77.7% of its degradation in a negative-control regime.

</details>


### [140] [Role-Specific Predictive Geometries for Nonstationary Multivariate Graph-Signal Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06519)
*Yanbo Chen,Anamitra Makur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种具有角色特定预测几何结构的多元图信号预测方法，其中长程（Long）关系作用于估计的均衡坐标，短程（Short）关系作用于滞后差分，通过时间交叉拟合和FWL偏出法实现条件预测解释。实验在合成数据上全部恢复所种植的关系，在四个真实基准上优于G-VARMA骨干模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元图信号预测的挑战在于节点级轨迹非平稳但跨节点和特征存在稳定关系。在误差修正表示中，长期均衡恢复与短期瞬态传播具有不同的预测角色，不应共享相同的跨特征几何结构。

Method: 提出角色特定的预测几何结构：有向Long关系作用于估计的均衡坐标，有向Short关系作用于滞后差分。矩阵值Long响应先混合均衡坐标再做图传播，Short响应使用图滤波的瞬态设计；采用直接多水平估计器耦合相邻水平的预测修正，并通过时间交叉拟合和Frisch-Waugh-Lovell偏出法使选中的边获得条件预测解释。

Result: 合成实验全部恢复种植的Long关系（20/20）、Short关系（20/20）及所有Dual场景（10/10）。在四个真实基准数据集中，提出的预测器在三个数据集上优于G-VARMA骨干模型，且在五折金融基准上全部25个fold-horizon对比均表现有利。

Conclusion: 将长短期预测角色分离并赋予不同几何结构的做法能够有效提升多元图信号预测性能，Long算子的右因子化设计可消除源公共趋势方向的影响，验证了该方法在理论和实践上的优越性。

Abstract: Forecasting multivariate graph signals is challenging when node-level trajectories are nonstationary but stable relations persist across nodes and features. In an error-correction representation, long-run equilibrium restoration and short-run transient propagation represent different predictive roles and need not share a common cross-feature geometry. We introduce role-specific predictive geometries in which directed Long relations act on estimated equilibrium coordinates, whereas directed Short relations act on lagged differences. Matrix-valued Long responses mix equilibrium coordinates before graph propagation, while Short responses use graph-filtered transient designs; a direct multi-horizon estimator couples forecast corrections across adjacent horizons. Temporal cross-fitting and Frisch-Waugh-Lovell partialling-out give selected edges a conditional predictive interpretation relative to a graph-temporal backbone. The Long operator remains right-factorized through the equilibrium subspace and therefore annihilates source common-trend directions. Controlled experiments recover all planted Long relations (20/20), all planted Short relations (20/20), and both role families in every Dual realization (10/10). Across four real-world benchmarks, the proposed predictor improves on the G-VARMA backbone in three datasets, with all 25 fold-horizon comparisons favorable on the five-fold financial benchmark.

</details>


### [141] [Not Just Oversmoothing: Detecting the Echo Chamber Effect in Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06521)
*Asela Hevapathige,Ahad N. Zehmakan,Asiri Wijesinghe,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出回声室效应（Echo Chamber Effect），指出GNN在社区结构图上存在双时间尺度消息传递的过度平滑问题——表征在社区内快速塌缩但跨社区缓慢塌缩。引入回声室指数（ECI）量化该效应，并基于分析提出社区感知分裂传播（CASP）插件，通过解耦社区内外聚合并学习其平衡，在多种同质性与异质性设置下提升各类GNN骨干模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN过度平滑诊断依赖全局聚合度量，无法捕捉消息传递的异构动态。真实图具有显著社区结构，消息传递在社区内与跨社区以不同速度进行，导致社区内表征已不可区分而跨社区分离仍然存在的关键失败模式，现有方法无法识别和量化这一现象。

Method: (1) 引入回声室指数（ECI），按社区成员身份分层计算成对距离，揭示全局能量衰减但跨社区分离持续的失效模式；(2) 理论分析表明特征保留机制可在特定条件下保持回声室效应；(3) 提出社区感知分裂传播（CASP），一种轻量级插件，解耦社区内与社区间聚合过程，并从标签结构学习两者的平衡。

Result: 回声室效应的影响取决于标签结构：社区与类别对齐时可锐化节点分类；不对齐时则使分类在理论上更难。CASP在大多数评估的同质性与异质性设置下均提升了多种骨干GNN的性能。

Conclusion: 全局聚合度量不足以诊断GNN过度平滑问题，社区感知的度量与传播策略（如ECI和CASP）能更准确理解并缓解异构社区结构下的表征塌缩问题。

Abstract: Oversmoothing is a well-known failure mode of Graph Neural Networks (GNNs). However, most existing diagnostics rely on global aggregation measures that fail to capture the heterogeneous dynamics of message passing. Real-world graphs exhibit pronounced community structure, and message passing operates on two timescales, with representations collapsing rapidly within communities and slowly across them. This creates a critical gap in which intra-community representations can become indistinguishable while inter community separation persists, a failure mode that we refer to as the Echo Chamber Effect. To quantify this effect, we introduce the Echo Chamber Index (ECI), which stratifies pairwise distances by community membership and reveals when global energy diminishes while inter-community separation persists. ECI further shows that feature retention mechanisms can preserve the echo chamber under the conditions of our theoretical analysis. The consequences depend on label structure: when communities align with classes, the echo chamber can sharpen node classification, whereas when they do not, the same collapse makes classification provably harder. Motivated by this analysis, we propose Community-Aware Split Propagation (CASP), a lightweight plugin that decouples intra- and inter-community aggregation and learns their balance from label structure. CASP improves diverse backbone GNNs across most evaluated homophilic and heterophilic settings.

</details>


### [142] [Hidden in Plain Sight: The Overlooked Significance of Canonical Elements for Extreme LLM Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06557)
*Hyeondo Jang,Kwanhee Lee,Dongyeop Lee,Namhoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过渐进式稀疏化框架结合二阶显著性准则与持续训练，挑战了LLM在极端稀疏（高达99%）下无法保持性能的认知，在LLaMA-2和Qwen-3系列上超越现有SOTA基线，证明预训练LLM可以在远高于常规研究的稀疏度下保持强劲性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为大规模语言模型在激进稀疏化下表现脆弱，通常只能保持中等稀疏度。然而近期研究表明LLM对高稀疏度的韧性被低估了，问题本质是设计挑战而非根本限制。作者希望重新审视在超大模型规模下被忽视的基础剪枝策略，探索LLM在极端稀疏度下的性能极限。

Method: 采用渐进式稀疏化框架，结合二阶显著性（second-order saliency）准则进行非结构化剪枝，并让持续训练（continued training）与稀疏度推进过程协调进行。通过逐步提升稀疏度，模型在每一阶段都能适应被剪枝的结构，从而突破传统研究的稀疏度上限。

Result: 在LLaMA-2和Qwen-3模型系列上，方法在最高99%稀疏度下均提升了困惑度和下游任务准确率，超越现有SOTA方法和代表性基线。具体而言，LLaMA-2-7B在95%和99%稀疏度下分别达到WikiText-2困惑度13.48和19.67，同时在95%稀疏度下实现3.23倍解码加速和6.21倍内存节省。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明LLM可以被推向极端稀疏度同时保持强大性能，为这一稀疏度区间内进一步改进稀疏模型提供了基础。此前被认为是LLM结构性瓶颈的激进稀疏化，实际上可以通过合理的设计（渐进式训练与二阶准则）被有效克服。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often considered fragile under aggressive sparsification, and maintaining reliable performance typically requires sticking to moderate sparsity levels. However, recent studies suggest that LLMs are more resilient to high sparsity than previously thought, reframing the problem as a design challenge rather than a fundamental limitation. In this work, we challenge the perceived limits of unstructured post-training LLM pruning by revisiting elementary pruning strategies that have remained relatively underexplored at this scale. Through a progressive sparsification framework with second-order saliency and continued training coordinated with sparsity progression, we show that pretrained LLMs can retain strong performance far beyond commonly studied sparsity regimes. Across LLaMA-2 and Qwen-3 model families, our approach improves perplexity and downstream accuracy up to 99\% sparsity, surpassing both the current state-of-the-art and representative baselines. Precisely, on LLaMA-2-7B, our approach achieves WikiText-2 perplexities of 13.48 and 19.67 at 95\% and 99\% sparsity, respectively, while delivering 3.23$\times$ decoding speedup and 6.21$\times$ memory savings at 95\% sparsity. Taken together, our results show that LLMs can be pushed into extreme sparsity while retaining strong performance, providing a foundation for further improving sparse models in this regime.

</details>


### [143] [Layer-Wise Gate-Controlled Prompt Truncation in a Multimodal Chest X-Ray Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06590)
*Jingtao Lei,Hongji Li,Dexiang Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了MoPE（混合提示专家）在胸部X光二分类任务中引入逐层门控扩展，通过控制器预测每个样本的保留比例来动态截断视觉提示。在单次运行中，门控模型的最佳验证准确率为0.8996，略高于固定长度基线的0.8969；但门控统计显示所有记录训练点保留长度均为1（配置最大为6），表明模型倾向于最大化压缩提示，且未证明样本特定的长度分配优势或临床实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在保持固定提示长度的多模态Transformer自适应框架（MoPE）基础上，探索一种逐层门控扩展，以实现输入依赖的动态提示长度分配，从而潜在提升模型适配性与计算效率。

Method: 控制器为每个样本预测保留比率，在mini-batch内对比率取平均，并将得到的整数长度用于截断静态和混合视觉提示。保留的混合提示还按个体比率进行缩放，实现了动态提示截断机制。

Result: 门控模型的最佳验证准确率为0.8996（基线0.8969），最终值为0.8963（基线0.8802）。门控导出统计显示保留长度为1（最大配置为6），将视觉序列从210个token缩减至200个，但无直接运行时间测量证明加速收益。

Conclusion: 研究发现仅记录了配置门控惩罚下的提示缩短现象，未确立样本特定长度分配的有效性、对固定短提示的优越性或临床实用性。报告标签、文本输入、数据分区方式及缺乏重复实验等限制因素削弱了结论的可推广性。

Abstract: Mixture of Prompt Experts (MoPE) adapts multimodal transformers through input-dependent prompt composition, while retaining a fixed prompt length. We investigate a layer-wise gating extension in a binary chest X-ray classification pilot study. The controller predicts a retention ratio for each sample, averages these ratios within a mini-batch, and uses the resulting integer length to truncate the static and mixed visual prompts. Retained mixed prompts are also scaled by the individual ratios. In one recorded run per configuration, the gated model reached a best validation accuracy of 0.8996, compared with 0.8969 for the fixed-length baseline; the corresponding final values were 0.8963 and 0.8802. The exported gate statistics imply a retained length of one at all recorded training points, relative to a configured maximum of six. This reduces the complete visual sequence from 210 to 200 tokens, but no direct runtime measurements establish an acceleration benefit. Report-derived labels, report text as input, sequential data partitioning, and the absence of repeated controlled experiments limit interpretation. The findings document prompt shortening under the configured gate penalty; they do not establish sample-specific length allocation, superiority over fixed short prompts, or clinical utility. Code is available at: https://github.com/jingtaolei/mope-dynamic-prompt-truncation.

</details>


### [144] [Deep Barycentric Regression for Optimal Transport Map Estimation and its Statistical Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06598)
*Kunwoong Kim,Insung Kong,Yongdai Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了BROT（Barycentric Regression for OT）方法，通过两步法计算最优传输（OT）映射：先计算无正则化的OT计划，再用深度神经网络（DNN）以最小二乘回归拟合诱导的重心目标。该方法在理论上达到极小极大最优收敛速率，同时在实践中具有参数化的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的OT映射估计器存在理论与实践之间的鸿沟：理论估计器虽有极小极大最优的统计保证但非参数化且实现成本高；实用估计器虽参数化可扩展但其对抗式训练目标不稳定且统计保证不明确。需要一个兼具理论保证和实用稳定性的估计方法。

Method: BROT采用两步法：第一步计算无正则化的OT计划（得到数据点间的传输权重），第二步通过最小二乘回归将DNN拟合到由OT计划诱导的重心目标上。在标准正则条件下（ground-truth OT映射为Lipschitz连续），证明了该DNN估计器能达到极小极大收敛速率。

Result: 在合成数据集和图像数据集上的数值实验表明，BROT相比现有估计方法能提供更准确的映射估计、更强的目标分布匹配以及有竞争力的传输成本。在单细胞扰动预测和无监督域适应两个下游任务上进一步验证了BROT的准确估计能转化为更强的任务性能。

Conclusion: BROT成功弥合了OT映射估计中理论最优性与实践可扩展性之间的鸿沟，以简单的两步回归方法同时获得了极小极大最优的理论保证和良好的实证表现，表明最小二乘回归框架是估计OT映射的一种有效且稳定的替代对抗式训练的方案。

Abstract: The optimal transport (OT) map provides a geometric transformation for aligning probability distributions and has become a useful tool in machine learning. However, existing estimators of the OT map still exhibit a gap between sharp statistical guarantees and practical parametric estimation based on stable training objectives. Theoretical estimators achieve minimax optimal convergence rates, but they are typically nonparametric and can incur demanding implementation design or inference costs. Practical estimators are parametric and scalable, but their statistical guarantees remain underexplored, and their min-max, adversarial-like training objectives can be sensitive to optimization algorithms. We propose BROT (Barycentric Regression for OT), a simple two-step method that first computes the unregularized OT plan and then fits a deep neural network (DNN) to the induced barycentric targets by least-squares regression. Under standard regularity conditions, we prove that the DNN estimator of BROT attains the minimax convergence rate, when the ground-truth OT map is Lipschitz. Numerical studies on synthetic datasets and an image dataset show that BROT provides accurate map estimates, strong target distribution matching, and competitive transport costs, compared to existing estimation methods. Experiments on two downstream tasks, single-cell perturbation prediction and unsupervised domain adaptation, further suggest that the accurate estimation of BROT can translate into stronger task performance.

</details>


### [145] [A Statistical and Machine Learning Framework for Quantifying Offensive Impact in Professional Box Lacrosse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06610)
*Robert Jimerson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于1,006个手动标注的Rochester Knighthawks射门样本（含151个进球，来自13场NLL比赛），开发了盒式长曲棍球的预期进球（xG）估计框架与进攻参与归因框架，评估了逻辑回归、随机森林和极端随机树三种模型，最终推荐上下文基线随机森林作为最优模型，并探索性地提出了预期掩护价值（xPV）指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 职业盒式长曲棍球的传统统计数据只能总结比赛结果，无法量化射门质量或捕捉得分机会背后的角色贡献，因此需要建立更精细的xG模型和进攻参与归因体系来弥补这一分析空白。

Method: 收集13场2025-2026赛季国家长曲棍球联盟（NLL）比赛中1,006个手动标注射门样本，测试逻辑回归、随机森林和极端随机树三种模型，采用三组嵌套特征集、Leave-One-Game-Out交叉验证并以训练集基线作为基准；通过射门者xG和射门端预期助攻（xA）记录核心进攻影响；通过比较实际状态概率与无掩护反事实状态概率来计算xPV。

Result: 上下文基线随机森林取得最低的联合对数损失（0.4189）和Brier分数（0.1260），较基准分别提升1.22%和1.50%，但9种规格中有5种未超越基准；加入二人配合和掩护类型特征未改善主要指标；xPV的幅度与模型噪声难以区分，方向性模式虽超过200次行置换复制，但其诊断性质仅为描述性而非推断性。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了首个针对盒式长曲棍球的文档化xG框架和进攻归因体系，但受限于单支球队和13场比赛的样本规模，结果仅应视为初步案例研究，而非联赛级别的普遍结论或因果估计；xPV仅作为探索性增强组件报告，不建议作为确定性指标使用。

Abstract: Professional box-lacrosse statistics summarize outcomes but provide limited information about shot quality or the roles behind scoring opportunities. This study develops a documented framework for estimating expected goals (xG) and attributing recorded offensive involvement using 1,006 manually annotated Rochester Knighthawks shot attempts, including 151 goals, from 13 consecutive 2025-2026 National Lacrosse League games. Logistic regression, random forest, and extremely randomized trees were evaluated across three nested feature sets using Leave-One-Game-Out cross-validation and a training-fold base-rate benchmark. The contextual baseline random forest had the lowest observed pooled log loss (0.4189) and Brier score (0.1260), improving on the benchmark by 1.22% and 1.50%; five of nine specifications did not beat the benchmark. Adding two-man-action and pick-type fields did not improve the primary metrics. Core Offensive Impact attributes recorded involvement through shooter xG and shot-based expected assists for final passers. Expected Pick Value (xPV) compares a qualifying pick's observed-state probability with a no-pick counterfactual. Its magnitude was indistinguishable from model noise. Its directional pattern exceeded 200 row-permutation replicates, but limited tail resolution and failure to preserve game-level pick composition make the diagnostic descriptive rather than inferential. Accordingly, xPV is reported only as an exploratory augmented component. Given the single-team, 13-game sample, the results are an initial case study rather than league-wide or causal estimates.

</details>


### [146] [Inducing Emergent Misalignment from Reward Hacks with Iterative DPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06649)
*Oliver Daniels,Perusha Moodley,Benjamin M. Marlin,David Lindner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用迭代DPO替代基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）来研究语言模型中的奖励黑客行为及其导致的紧急错位现象，成本更低且可在常用微调API上运行。在GPT-4.1上能诱导出隐蔽的权力寻求和伪装对齐行为，在Qwen2.5-32B上则同时引发错位与指令跟随精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR过程中的奖励黑客可能引发语言模型的奖励寻求行为和广泛错位，研究这种错位泛化对开发更好的威胁模型和应对措施至关重要，但在大型模型上开展RL成本过高，难以进行系统性研究。

Method: 提出使用迭代DPO作为RLVR的低成本替代方案，它保留RLVR的重要特性（迭代式在线训练）同时显著降低训练成本，并能在流行的微调API上运行。在单轮奖励黑客环境中分别对GPT-4.1和Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct进行迭代DPO训练实验。

Result: 在GPT-4.1上诱导出隐蔽的错位权力寻求行为和伪装对齐行为，这是首个开源（半）在线训练流程诱导出这类令人担忧的错位形式；在Qwen2.5-32B上同时产生错位和指令跟随精度的提升，展示了迭代DPO可作为选择性泛化的测试平台。

Conclusion: 迭代DPO有望民主化并加速RLVR引发的紧急错位研究，为更广泛的错位研究提供可行的低成本实验平台。

Abstract: Reward hacking during reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) can induce reward seeking and broad misalignment in language models. Studying this misgeneralization is important for developing better threat models and countermeasures, but is often infeasible due to the cost of RL on large models. As an alternative, we propose studying emergent misalignment from iterative DPO, which preserves important properties of RLVR while reducing costs and enabling training on popular finetuning APIs. In practice, we find that training GPT-4.1 with iterative DPO on a single-turn reward hacking environment induces covert misaligned power-seeking and alignment faking, the first openly available (semi)-online training pipeline to induce these concerning forms of misalignment. We also find that training Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct with the same pipeline induces both misalignment and improved instruction following accuracy, showing that iterative DPO can be used as a testbed for selective generalization. Overall, we think iterative DPO can help democratize and accelerate the study of emergent misalignment from RLVR.

</details>


### [147] [SwiftExplorer: Training-free Diffusion Model Alignment with Swift Diversity Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06651)
*Renye Yan,Jikang Cheng,You Wu,Bojin Huang,Wei Peng,Zongwei Wang,Ling Liang,Yimao Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SwiftExplorer插件，通过继承-重启探索机制和质量-效率仲裁机制来解决扩散模型在无训练目标对齐时面临的分布坍缩和计算浪费问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽有通用生成能力但难以与特定目标对齐。基于微调的方案成本过高，现有的无训练引导方法存在两个问题：强方向性偏置导致分布收窄及生成多样性下降；不加区分的恒定引导无法剔除冗余信号，损害质量与效率。

Method: 提出SwiftExplorer插件，包含两个核心机制：(1) 继承-重启探索机制，避免早期收敛并平衡多样性-保真度，防止分布过度偏移；(2) 质量-效率仲裁机制，剔除错误引导信号，并在生成完整度与边际奖励增益最优时动态终止生成以降低计算成本。

Result: 在大量实验和多种评估指标上，SwiftExplorer在偏好性、保真度、多样性和丰富度等所有指标上均取得优异性能。

Conclusion: SwiftExplorer有效缓解了无训练扩散模型目标对齐中的分布坍缩问题，同时降低了计算开销，在质量和多样性等多个维度上取得了均衡的优异表现。

Abstract: Diffusion models have general generative abilities but struggle to align with specific objectives. Fine-tuning can improve alignment, yet its training cost is often prohibitive. This led to training-free methods that apply objective-guided terms in sampling to bias the generation distribution toward designated regions, e.g., high-reward areas. However, these methods face two issues: (1) the strong directional bias narrows the pretrained distribution and generation diversity, and (2) indiscriminate constant guidance fails to prune redundant signals, hurting both quality and efficiency. To address the above challenges, we propose SwiftExplorer, a plugin that mitigates distribution collapse caused by excessive diversity loss and reduces compute costs. First, we adopt an Inheritance-Restart exploration mechanism to avoid early convergence, while exploration also increases the likelihood of high-reward trajectories. Additionally, it balances diversity and fidelity, adding diversity without causing a distribution over-shift. Second, our Quality-Efficiency arbitration mechanism improves guidance by removing incorrect signals, and it reduces computation by dynamically stopping generation when completeness and marginal reward gain are optimal. In an extensive number of experiments and different types of evaluation metrics, the proposed SwiftExplorer achieves excellent performance on all metrics, including preference, fidelity, diversity, and richness.

</details>


### [148] [Assessing Covariate-Informed Grid Load Forecasting with a Time-Series Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06656)
*Varsha Pendyala,Yiwei Fu,Weizhong Yan,Nurali Virani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了亚马逊开发的多通道时间序列基础模型Chronos-2在真实电网负荷预测中的表现，发现其通过任务特定微调能获得显著提升并在短期预测中表现强劲，但零样本精度不及任务特定深度学习模型，且误差随预测步长增加而增长更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代电力系统日益复杂，负荷预测面临多变量和外部变量集成等现实挑战。虽然时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）在零样本单变量负荷预测中表现良好，但其在包含多目标变量和协变量的真实多通道场景下的实用性以及在任务特定微调下的行为尚缺乏系统性理解。

Method: 以Chronos-2（亚马逊开发的多通道TSFM，支持单变量、多变量及协变量信息预测）为代表模型，在两个真实公用事业数据集（ISO New England和ENTSO-E）上，与广泛使用的任务特定深度学习模型进行基准对比，考察其零样本预测和任务特定微调后的表现。

Result: Chronos-2在任务特定微调后获得显著性能提升，短期预测表现强劲；但其零样本准确度落后于任务特定模型，且随着预测步长增加，其预测误差增长更快。

Conclusion: 研究详细刻画了Chronos-2等TSFM在电网负荷预测中的优势与局限，为如何有效利用预训练TSFM进行实际运营级负荷预测提供了实用见解。

Abstract: Modern power systems are growing increasingly complex as they integrate diverse generation sources to meet rising demand, making accurate load forecasting challenging. Recent advances in time-series foundation models (TSFMs) resulted in promising performance in zero-shot univariate load forecasting tasks. However, real-world load forecasting often involves multiple target variables and requires the integration of exogenous variables, raising important questions about the utility of TSFMs in realistic settings. In this study, we position Chronos-2, a recently developed model by Amazon, as a representative multi-channel TSFM that supports univariate, multivariate, and covariate-informed forecasting, and conduct a systematic investigation of how such models can be used for real-world load forecasting. While prior work has evaluated Chronos-2 on a limited number of energy-related tasks in a zero-shot setting, its performance relative to established task-specific deep learning models and its behavior when adapted using task-specific historical data remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we evaluate Chronos-2 on two real-world utility datasets, ISO New England and ENTSO-E, and benchmark it against widely used task-specific deep learning models. Our results show that Chronos-2 benefits substantially from task-specific fine-tuning and achieves strong short-horizon forecasting performance, but its zero-shot accuracy lags behind task-specific models and its forecasting error grows more rapidly with increasing forecast steps. Overall, this study provides a detailed characterization of the strengths and limitations of TSFMs such as Chronos-2 in grid load forecasting and offers practical insights into how a pretrained TSFM can be effectively adapted for operational load forecasting applications.

</details>


### [149] [Behavioral Cloning Outperforms Entropy-Regularized RL: Critic-Driven Failure of Actor-Critic Methods on Adaptive Tumor Treatment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06667)
*Aleksandar Dimitrov,Giacomo Spigler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究肿瘤自适应给药策略。作者以最优控制分析确定的最优参考调度（bang-bang+奇异弧）为标准，发现从零训练的SAC无法达到持续治愈标准；行为克隆能复现参考策略，但克隆后微调的SAC反而破坏了治愈能力，且该模式在多种算法和多药代噪声下稳定复现。关键发现是，若用启发式对照而非最优参考评价，微调策略会被误判为合格控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应给药策略通常仅与历史或启发式方案比较，无法判断是否达到了行为空间中的最优。缺少一个"接近最优"的参考基准，使得强化学习策略的失败或成功都不易被识别。

Method: 采用三群体肿瘤控制常微分方程模型，使用最优控制分析确定最优调度形式（bang-bang给药+奇异弧），以此构建数值控制器作为近最优参考；将SAC从零训练、行为克隆参考策略、以及克隆后的SAC/TD3微调等方法进行对比，在持续治愈（连续200天肿瘤低于5%承载量）标准下评估；并加入多药代动力学乘性噪声验证稳健性，同时分析critic在治愈轨迹上的状态价值排序。

Result: 从零训练的SAC从未达到持续治愈；行为克隆完美复现参考策略（30/30种子100%治愈）；但克隆后的SAC微调在5种熵系数下均破坏治愈能力，TD3与BC正则化SAC同样失败，且在有药代噪声时模式依旧。在治愈轨迹上，崩溃后的critic在96%的状态中将崩溃策略动作排于参考动作之上，集中于维持阶段，策略落入非治愈的自适应治疗平衡。

Conclusion: 以最优参考为基准才能揭示微调策略的真实失败——表面的"competent controller"实质是次优退化。最优控制参考为评估自适应给药策略提供了不可替代的判据，启发式对照会掩盖策略的根本缺陷。

Abstract: Adaptive dosing requires policies that reduce tumor burden without excessive toxicity. Learned dosing policies are typically judged against historical or heuristic comparators, which cannot show whether a policy has found the best behavior available. We instead study a three-population tumor-control ODE in which optimal-control analysis fixes the form of a good schedule -- bang-bang dosing punctuated by a singular arc -- and construct a numerical controller of that form as a proxy for near-optimal behavior. Judged against this reference under a sustained-cure criterion -- 200 consecutive days below 5% carrying capacity -- Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) trained from scratch never reaches cure. Behavioral cloning (BC) of the reference reproduces it (100% sustained cure, 30/30 seeds), but SAC fine-tuning of the cloned policy destroys it across five entropy coefficients, and TD3 and BC-regularized SAC fail identically; the pattern persists under multiplicative pharmacokinetic action noise. Along curative trajectories the post-collapse critic ranks the collapsed-policy action above the reference action in 96% of states, concentrated in the maintenance phase, and the policy settles into a non-curative adaptive-therapy equilibrium. The reference is what makes this legible: against a heuristic comparator the fine-tuned policy would read as a competent controller rather than a failure.

</details>


### [150] [Towards Unified Multimodal Graph Foundation Model: A Bridge-Router-Adapter Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06668)
*Sirui Zhang,Yubing Zhou,Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Shumeng Li,Wang Luo,Yinlin Zhu,Yujin Gao,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BRAIN，一个多模态图基础模型，通过范围条件桥（scope-conditioned Bridge）、层级路由器（hierarchical Router）和轻量残差适配器（residual Adapter）解决跨范围上下文纠缠和忽略范围的模态路由两大问题，在9个数据集和4类任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态图基础模型存在两个根本缺陷：(1) 跨范围上下文纠缠——将不同范围（scope）的图上下文混合为统一表示，模糊了多模态构建过程中它们之间的差异；(2) 忽略范围的模态路由——在固定图范围内路由模态，忽略了模态相关性随邻域范围变化的事实。

Method: BRAIN由三个核心组件构成：范围条件桥（Bridge）将局部到全局邻域范围的结构信息与不同模态组合结合；层级路由器（Router）估计范围与任务间的相关性，并在每个范围内分别选择组合，使模态效用随图范围变化；轻量残差适配器（Adapter）进一步将路由嵌入定制化用于下游预测。训练采用多图预训练加上任务特定适配的方式。

Result: 在9个数据集和4类任务族上的实验表明，BRAIN在节点分类和链接预测任务上相比最强基线最高提升4.73%，在图到文本的4个指标和图到图像的2个指标上平均相对提升达14.72%。

Conclusion: BRAIN通过解耦范围上下文并对不同范围进行差异化模态路由，显著提升了多模态图基础模型的跨域泛化能力，验证了将邻域范围与模态组成联合建模的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal graphs couple node attributes in different modalities, such as text and images, with relational structure, enabling topological structure and cross-modality attributes to be modeled jointly. Multimodal graph foundation models seek unified representations from such data that transfer across different graph domains and downstream tasks. However, existing methods exhibit two fundamental limitations. (1) Cross-Scope Context Entanglement. They merge scope-specific graph contexts into a unified representation, obscuring their distinctions during multimodal construction. (2) Scope-Ignorant Modality Routing. They route modalities within a fixed graph scope, overlooking how modality relevance varies across neighborhood ranges. To address these challenges, we propose BRAIN, a unified model that focuses on graph context that combines neighborhood scope with modality composition. BRAIN comprises a scope-conditioned Bridge that combines structural information spanning local-to-global neighborhood scopes with different modality compositions; a hierarchical Router that estimates the relevance between the scope and the task, and selects compositions separately within each scope, allowing modality utility to vary with graph range; and a lightweight residual Adapter that further specializes the routed embedding for downstream prediction. BRAIN is trained through multi-graph pretraining followed by task-specific adaptation. Experiments across nine datasets and four task families demonstrate its broad effectiveness, improving node-classification and link-prediction performance by up to 4.73% relative to the strongest baseline, while achieving an average relative improvement of 14.72% across four graph-to-text and two graph-to-image metrics.

</details>


### [151] [Data Efficient Sample Selection for In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06670)
*V Venktesh,Cem levi,Avishek Anand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DearICL是一个将演示示例选择建模为子集排序问题的框架，通过非线性的可微排序代理和gap-index bandit算法，实现了实例级别的子集选择，在开放源码LLM基准上相比线性bandit基线提升了8.08-15.9%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ICL演示示例选择方法没有建模示例子集与下游LLM性能之间的复杂关系，通常进行静态的任务级选择（离线选取一次），无法泛化到未见过的查询。

Method: DearICL将演示示例选择建模为子集排序问题，采用非线性代理（surrogate），结合可微排序目标，并在gap-index bandit算法框架下训练。gap-index方法能够精细区分优秀臂和边缘臂，通过充分采样边缘臂作为辅助目标来训练非线性代理，支持实例级别的子集排序。

Result: 在开放源码LLM的示例选择基准上，DearICL相比强线性bandit基线实现了8.08-15.9%的准确率提升，且样本复杂度较低。

Conclusion: DearICL通过将ICL示例选择视为子集排序问题，并利用gap-index bandit与非线性可微排序代理相结合，有效解决了静态任务级选择无法泛化到新查询的问题，显著提升了ICL性能。

Abstract: The In-context learning (ICL) paradigm aids large language models (LLMs) to adapt to new tasks without need for fine-tuning. However, selecting an optimal combination of demonstration examples from a large pool of example subsets is a challenging problem. Existing approaches for selection do not model the complex relationship between ICL samples and downstream LLM performance. They typically perform static task-level selection, choosing subsets once offline, which can fail to generalize to unseen queries. We introduce DearICL (Data Efficient Algorithm for Ranking) ICL samples, a new framework that models demonstration example selection as a subset ranking problem. DearICL employs a non-linear surrogate employing a differentiable sorting objective within a gap-index bandit algorithm. The gap-index based approach enables fine-grained separation of good arms and borderline arms, which is used as an auxiliary objective to train the non-linear surrogate through sufficient sampling of borderline arms, supporting instance-level subset ranking. On exemplar selection benchmarks with open-source LLMs, DearICL achieves 8.08-15.9% accuracy gains over strong linear bandit baselines, with low sample complexity. Code and data: https://github.com/VenkteshV/DearICL.

</details>


### [152] [Tracking the Moving Frontier: Long-Short Term Advantage Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06671)
*Xinhao Yao,Lu Yu,Changhao Wang,Fengwei Teng,Yuyao Zhang,Qing Cui,Jun Zhou,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LSTAE（长短期优势估计器），一种单流强化学习算法，利用历史经验进行优势估计，仅需每任务锚点一次rollout，即可匹配或超越基于组的RLVR基线，同时大幅降低rollout成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于组的RLVR方法需要对每个提示反复采样多条轨迹来计算优势，导致长时程智能体训练成本高昂，且浪费了跨迭代积累的有用经验。作者希望探究能否用历史经验替代迭代内的重复比较，而无需直接优化过时的轨迹。

Method: LSTAE采用单流RL算法，通过双时间尺度设计利用历史经验：(1) 长期（轨迹级）——维护漂移感知的历史基线的持久跟踪器，跟踪任务锚点的移动成功前沿，衡量每条新轨迹的相对贡献；(2) 短期（步骤级）——利用近期状态经验缓冲区，借助循环状态估计局部动作优势。策略仅用当前rollout更新，每锚点只需一次rollout。

Result: 在智能体任务和数学推理基准上，LSTAE匹配或优于强基线的基于组的方法，同时大幅降低rollout成本。

Conclusion: 历史经验可以替代组内重复采样来估计优势，LSTAE通过双时间尺度的优势信号设计，在降低训练成本的同时保持甚至提升性能，为高效长时程RL训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: Group-based RLVR methods estimate advantages by repeatedly sampling multiple trajectories for each prompt, making long-horizon agent training expensive and discarding useful experience accumulated across iterations. We ask whether historical experience can replace these repeated within-iteration comparisons without directly optimizing on stale trajectories. We introduce Long-Short Term Advantage Estimator (LSTAE), a single-stream RL algorithm that uses history for advantage estimation while updating the policy only with the current rollout. LSTAE maintains a persistent tracker for each task anchor. At the trajectory level (long term), a drift-aware historical baseline tracks the anchor's moving success frontier and measures the relative contribution of each new trajectory. At the step level (short term), a recent state-experience buffer exploits recurrent states to estimate localized action advantages. This two-timescale design converts accumulated experience into multi-granular credit signals, requiring only one rollout per anchor. Across agentic and mathematical reasoning benchmarks, LSTAE matches or improves upon strong group-based baselines while substantially reducing rollout cost.

</details>


### [153] [LATS: Levy Adaptive Tree Sampling for Feedback-Driven Diverse Target Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06761)
*Binglin Ji,Anindya Sarkar,Hengchang Lu,Lecheng Kong,Yixin Chen,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LATS（Levy Adaptive Tree Search），一个面向在线反馈驱动搜索的扩散模型采样框架，通过重尾探索与树状价值反向传播相结合，在保持样本保真度和结构多样性的同时，高效发现低似然、高效用区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型擅长捕捉复杂数据分布，但科学发现需要将生成导向特定的、未知的高目标效用区域。这些高价值模式通常位于低似然尾部区域，且只能通过交互式反馈逐步揭示。现有采样器存在两难：继承预训练模型高密度区域偏置而忽视稀有现象，或过度探索但受限于严格采样预算而无法高效利用高价值模式。

Method: LATS框架将重尾（Levy）分布驱动的探索策略与基于树的搜索相结合，通过树状结构的价值反向传播逐步优化采样方向，在维持广泛分布覆盖率的同时，渐进地揭示偏好模式，从而解决探索与利用之间的权衡困境。

Result: 在包括材料科学在内的多个基准测试中，LATS在目标发现效率方面显著优于基线方法，成功实现了对低似然、高效用区域的探索发现，同时保持了采样保真度和结构多样性。

Conclusion: LATS为解决在线反馈指导下的扩散模型目标导向采样问题提供了一种原则性框架，有效弥合了纯扩散采样与纯探索式采样之间的鸿沟，在稀有有价值现象的发现任务中展现出显著优势，对科学发现等实际应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: While diffusion models excel at capturing complex data distributions, scientific discovery often requires steering generation toward specific, uncharacterized regions that maximize a target objective. These high-utility modes frequently reside in low-likelihood tail regions and are only revealed sequentially through interactive feedback. Existing diffusion samplers fail in this regime: they inherit the pre-trained model's bias toward high-density regions, leaving rare yet promising phenomena underexplored. Conversely, exploration-heavy samplers ensure broad coverage but fail to efficiently exploit high-utility modes when constrained by a strict sampling budget. To resolve this dilemma, we introduce Levy Adaptive Tree Search (LATS), a principled sampling framework for online feedback-driven search. LATS leverages heavy-tailed exploration coupled with tree-based value backpropagation to progressively uncover preferred modes. By maintaining broad distributional coverage, LATS successfully discovers low-likelihood, high-utility regions while preserving sample fidelity and structural diversity. Experiments across diverse benchmarks, including materials science, demonstrate that LATS significantly outperforms baselines in target discovery efficiency.

</details>


### [154] [DrugReason: Dynamic Multi-View Reasoning over Knowledge Graph and Language Evidence for Drug Repurposing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06779)
*Zijie Liu,Hongxuan Li,Zhen Tan,Jinhao Duan,Baixiang Huang,Zunpeng Liu,Kai Shu,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DrugReason是一个多视角推理框架，融合知识图谱的确定性推理与LLM的机制推断，用于药物重定位（drug repurposing）预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物重定位的候选药物-疾病对空间庞大且依赖复杂多跳生物机制。现有方法中，知识图谱方法受限于已有图结构，而LLM方法缺乏事实基础易产生幻觉，两者单独使用均不足以实现可靠预测。

Method: DrugReason采用多视角推理框架：将知识图谱的扎根推理与LLM生成的机制推理相结合；根据查询上下文将不同推理路径自适应路由到专门的专家模块；通过跨专家蒸馏目标实现知识共享同时保留专家专长。

Result: 在PharmaDB、DDInter和DrugBank三个数据集上的实验表明，DrugReason相比强单一视角推理基线提升了平均性能，与图方法相比具有竞争力或更优的结果，同时提供可解释的基于路由的预测。

Conclusion: DrugReason通过整合KG的扎根性和LLM的灵活性，有效弥补了单一方法的不足，在药物重定位任务上取得了更优性能，并兼具可解释性。

Abstract: Drug repurposing aims to identify new therapeutic uses for existing compounds and, compared with de novo drug discovery, offers a faster and more cost-effective path to clinical translation. However, the space of candidate drug-disease pairs is enormous and their underlying relationships often depend on complex multi-hop biological mechanisms, making it difficult to reliably predict which pairs represent true therapeutic relationships. Existing approaches tackle this from two directions: knowledge graph-based methods organize curated biomedical evidence into structured relational networks for grounded multi-hop reasoning, while LLM-based methods leverage pretrained knowledge to generate flexible mechanistic rationales. Yet neither is sufficient alone - KGs are confined to observed graph structure while LLMs lack factual grounding and risk hallucination. To address this gap, we propose DrugReason, a multi-view reasoning framework that integrates grounded KG reasoning with LLM-generated mechanistic inference for drug repurposing. DrugReason adaptively routes diverse reasoning paths to specialized experts conditioned on the query context, while a cross-expert distillation objective enables knowledge sharing without sacrificing expert specialization. Experiments on PharmaDB, DDInter, and DrugBank show that DrugReason improves average performance over strong single-view reasoning baselines and achieves competitive or superior results compared with graph-based alternatives, while providing interpretable routing-based predictions.

</details>


### [155] [When Retain Constraints Conflict: Mitigating Forget-Retain Interference in Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06786)
*Zijie Liu,Jinhao Duan,Bingqi Shang,Xinming An,Sijia Liu,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出冲突感知遗忘（CAU）方法，针对表格数据中序列化记录导致的"模式诱导的遗忘-保留重叠"问题，通过放宽与遗忘集冲突最大的保留行约束来减少遗忘与保留之间的干扰，使表格LLM遗忘更接近重训oracle水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘在表格数据上的行为尚未充分研究，而表格预测广泛用于高风险领域并越来越多地通过记录序列化和模式感知提示适配到语言模型中。作者识别出表格遗忘区别于自由文本的关键挑战：序列化表格数据中记录共享固定的列名/值槽位、相似属性范围和公共输出空间，导致遗忘行附近可能存在依赖相同高信号属性的保留行，使得保留目标与遗忘更新产生冲突。

Method: 提出冲突感知遗忘（CAU），一种模式感知方法，通过识别并放宽与遗忘集最冲突的保留行的保真约束，从而减少遗忘-保留干扰。方法支持样本级和特征级遗忘场景，在临床和非医疗表格任务上进行评估。

Result: 在样本级和特征级遗忘的临床及非医疗表格任务中，CAU比基线更接近重训oracle的表现，同时保持了预测效用和保留区域行为，证明了可靠的表格LLM遗忘不仅取决于遗忘目标本身，还取决于保留约束的构造方式。

Conclusion: 表格数据上的LLM遗忘需要模式感知的约束设计来应对模式诱导的遗忘-保留重叠问题，CAU展示了如何通过冲突感知的保留约束放松来更可靠地实现表格数据遗忘，为高风险表格领域中的机器遗忘提供了新的方法论指导。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of designated training data while preserving model utility, but its behavior on tabular data remains underexplored. This gap is important because tabular prediction is widely used in high-stakes domains and is increasingly adapted to language models through record serialization and schema-aware prompting. We identify a key challenge that distinguishes tabular unlearning from unlearning in free-form text or other modalities: schema-induced forget-retain overlap. In serialized tabular data, records share fixed column-name/value slots, similar attribute ranges, and common output spaces. Consequently, a forget row may have nearby retain rows that rely on the same high-signal attributes, causing retain preservation to oppose the update required for forgetting. Motivated by this failure mode, we propose Conflict-Aware Unlearning (CAU), a schema-aware approach that reduces forget-retain interference by relaxing preservation constraints on retained rows that most conflict with the forget set. Across sample-level and feature-level unlearning on clinical and non-medical tabular tasks, CAU more closely matches a retraining oracle while maintaining predictive utility and retain-region behavior. Our results show that reliable tabular LLM unlearning depends not only on the forgetting objective, but also on how retain constraints are constructed.

</details>


### [156] [Train Smarter, Not Harder: Switching Signal-Guided Training in Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06806)
*Nagham Omar,Maya Rozenshtein,Evgeny Mishlyakov,Avigdor Gal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HybridAL自适应训练调度策略，在主动学习中监控在线稳定信号，从从头重训练自适应切换到微调，在保持宏F1不劣的前提下节省高达49%的重训练时间，并恢复重训练在NLL上的校准优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动学习中"从头重训练"还是"基于前一个检查点微调"这一训练策略选择长期被忽视，但该选择具有可利用的结构性特征——早期轮次重训练更有用，模型轨迹稳定后微调更安全。

Method: 提出HybridAL框架，通过监控在线稳定化信号（包括基于权重的谱指数变化Δα和基于验证集的准确率变化ΔAcc），在持续稳定后自动从重训练切换为微调。实验涵盖三个编码器骨干和六个文本分类任务，每个任务五个随机种子。

Result: HybridAL在0.010宏F1边际内与重训练和微调相比非劣性，节省高达49%的重训练时间，恢复重训练的大部分校准优势。与预提交轮次的固定切换相比，HybridAL在适度额外成本下获得更低的NLL。

Conclusion: 轨迹依赖的切换相比固定早期切换能提供更强的时间-校准权衡，这证明了自适应训练策略在主动学习中的价值。

Abstract: Training strategy, namely whether to retrain from scratch or fine-tune from the previous checkpoint, is an overlooked decision variable in active learning. We show that this choice has exploitable structure: retraining is most useful in early rounds, when each batch can substantially reshape the labeled distribution, while fine-tuning becomes safer once the model trajectory stabilizes. We propose HybridAL, an adaptive training schedule that monitors an online stabilization signal and switches from retraining to fine-tuning after sustained stabilization. Two complementary signals, spectral exponent change $Δα$ (weight-based) and accuracy change $Δ$Acc (validation-based), span different points on the time-calibration trade-off. Across three encoder backbones and six text-classification tasks (five seeds each), HybridAL keeps endpoint macro-F1 non-inferior to retraining and fine-tuning at a 0.010 margin, saves up to 49% of retraining time, and recovers a substantial fraction of retraining's calibration advantage as measured by negative log-likelihood (NLL). Compared with schedules that switch at a pre-committed round, HybridAL obtains lower NLL at moderate additional cost, showing that trajectory-dependent switching provides a stronger time-calibration trade-off than fixed early switching.

</details>


### [157] [Feature Superposition in Neural Networks: From Theory to Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06862)
*Dai Shi,Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于神经网络中"叠加现象"（Superposition）的综述论文，回顾了叠加表示在几何、学习和计算方面的研究进展，并对比了从训练网络中恢复和分析特征的各种方法及其评估结论，最后指出了该领域存在的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叠加现象是解释多义神经元和从神经激活中恢复可解释特征的重要理论机制，但目前理论模型与经验研究之间存在脱节，需要系统梳理几何、学习和计算三个维度上的研究，并厘清不同方法分别能证明什么样的结论（如特征身份、因果作用等）。

Method: 本文采用综述的方式，从三个方面系统梳理叠加表示：(1) 叠加表示的几何特性；(2) 叠加表示的学习过程；(3) 叠加表示的计算功能。同时对比了当前用于恢复和分析特征的实用方法，并考察各种方法所建立的评估证据所能支撑的结论边界。

Result: 论文指出：特征统计与解码器的选择会显著影响研究结论；仅靠精确的激活重建并不足以确立特征的身份标识或其因果用途；文章还汇总了现有方法已记录的失败案例，并对先前提出的开放问题进行了重新评估，识别出关于训练网络中叠加现象的剩余理论问题和经验问题。

Conclusion: 叠加现象的深入理解需要弥合理论模型与经过训练的网络之间的鸿沟，未来亟需更可靠的特征恢复与分析方法，以推动对深层神经网络的可解释性研究。

Abstract: Superposition refers to neural networks representing more features than they have dimensions. It offers a possible explanation for polysemantic neurons and motivates methods for recovering interpretable features from neural activations. Theoretical models typically start with a given set of input features and assumptions about how their values vary across inputs, then study how a network encodes those values in a lower-dimensional hidden representation. Empirical work, by contrast, seeks to identify the features encoded in trained networks and determine their role in computation. In this survey, we review the geometry, learning, and computation of superposed representations, explaining how feature statistics and decoder choice affect the conclusions. To connect these theoretical accounts with evidence from trained networks, we compare practical methods for recovering and analyzing features and examine what their evaluations establish. Since accurate activation reconstruction alone does not establish feature identity or causal use, we discuss the methods' documented failures and applications in light of the evidence available for these different claims. Finally, we assess previously stated open problems and identify remaining theoretical and empirical questions about superposition in trained networks. We hope our work can pave the way for a deeper understanding of superposition and more reliable methods for interpreting neural networks.

</details>


### [158] [PPIM: Pennes Physics-Informed Mamba for Heat-Source-Conditioned 3D Bioheat Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06869)
*Dongyun Lee,Kyungho Yoon,Minwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PPIM（Pennes Physics-Informed Mamba），一种基于Mamba的神经网络偏微分方程求解器，用于三维Pennes生物热方程仿真。PPIM在条件化热源输入和Pennes感知的状态空间模型衰减初始化方面改进了通用时序子序列模型。在600秒的代表性运行中，PPIM在MAE、相对L1和L2误差方面均优于空间傅里叶特征PINN和通用PINNMamba模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维生物热仿真对微波消融等临床场景的温度预测至关重要，而传统数值方法计算成本高。本文旨在评估和提升神经网络PDE求解器在受控Pennes生物热仿真中的预测精度，特别是针对局部热源条件下的三维温度场求解问题。

Method: PPIM构建于时序子序列模型（PINNMamba）之上，引入两个关键改进：(1) 条件化热源输入，使模型感知外部热源位置与强度；(2) Pennes感知的状态空间模型（SSM）衰减初始化，使时序建模与Pennes方程中Perfusion相关的热衰减物理特性对齐。所有对比模型使用相同的Pennes残差和训练条件，以显式有限差分法（FDM）解作为数值参考。

Result: 在600秒的代表性仿真中，PPIM在平均绝对误差（MAE）、相对L1误差和相对L2误差方面均取得三种神经求解器中的最低值。误差图显示PPIM的剩余误差主要集中在热源附近区域，而非均匀分布在整个计算域上，表明其整体逼近精度优于对比模型。

Conclusion: PPIM在受控三维Pennes生物热仿真中，能够有效逼近FDM参考的最终温度场，验证了将物理感知初始化与条件化输入引入时序SSM架构的有效性。这表明PPIM有望成为微波消融等热疗场景中神经热场求解的有效工具。

Abstract: Three-dimensional bioheat simulation aims to predict transient temperature distributions in biological tissue and is commonly modeled using the Pennes bioheat equation, which combines thermal diffusion, perfusion-mediated heat loss, and external heat generation. In this study, we consider a controlled 3D Pennes bioheat simulation under a localized heat-source condition inspired by microwave ablation (MWA). To evaluate neural approximation performance, we compare three neural partial differential equation (PDE) solvers under the same controlled simulation: a spatial Fourier-feature physics-informed neural network (PINN), a generic PINNMamba temporal subsequence model, and Pennes Physics-Informed Mamba (PPIM). PPIM builds on the temporal subsequence model by incorporating conditioned heat-source input and Pennes-aware state-space model (SSM) decay initialization. All three neural models are trained under the same conditions with the same Pennes residual, and an explicit finite-difference method (FDM) solution is used only as the numerical reference. In a representative 600~s run, PPIM achieved the lowest MAE, relative $L_1$ error, and relative $L_2$ error among the evaluated neural solvers. Error maps further showed that the remaining PPIM errors were more concentrated near the heat-source region than across the rest of the domain. These results indicate that PPIM is effective for approximating the FDM reference final temperature field in this controlled simulation. The source code is available at https://github.com/muvYun/PPIM.

</details>


### [159] [Exact Record Omission in Delta Attention: A Transport Criterion, Its Cost, and a Replay Certificate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06872)
*Vishwajith Ramesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究混合线性循环模型（如Kimi Linear）中的记忆删除问题。通过"传输收据"（记录记录到达时的影响并向前传递）的方法无法实现精确遗忘——实验表明经过4096个token后，记录仍在状态中留下约4.5%的印记，各种收据类方法均无法消除。对比之下，从记录前检查点恢复并重放后续序列，可以在所有测试数组上精确匹配"从未存储"的状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户要求模型遗忘某条记录时，测试标准是记忆状态应与记录从未被存储时一致。独立编码的行可以直接删除，但循环记忆将记录折叠入不断演化的状态中，删除难度大。作者探索"收据"方案能否以固定成本实现精确遗忘，并将其与检查点重放方法进行对比评估。

Method: 提出"传输收据"概念：保存记录到达时带来的差异，通过后续更新向前传递，在删除时减去该差异。理论上证明传输收据只有在记录对后续更新的影响净抵消时才能达到精确省略。在48B Kimi Linear混合模型上进行经验测量，并在Mamba-2、Falcon-H1、RWKV-7上验证写规则分类；与检查点前恢复加后缀重放的方法进行对比。

Result: 实验表明：记录在4096个token后仍留下约4.5%状态范数的印记，收据方法无法移除；重算半个后缀仅能弥补不到一半的差距；每token日志成本在88个token后超过完整检查点。掩码注意力行可使采样恢复接近从未存储水平，但审计者可检测到。检查点重放方法在所有测试数组上精确匹配从未存储的状态，工作量与重放后缀长度成正比。

Conclusion: 在被评估的方法中，检查点重放是唯一实现精确遗忘的方案，其代价是工作量与重放后缀长度成正比。传输收据在理论上存在精确遗忘的可能性条件（后续更新净抵消），但实际模型不满足该条件，因此收据方法不可行。

Abstract: When a user asks an assistant to forget a record, the test is whether the memory now matches the state it would hold if the record had never been stored. Independently encoded rows can be removed directly; a recurrent memory folds records into an evolving state. One hope is a receipt: save the difference the record made when it arrived, carry it forward through later updates, and subtract it, so that deletion costs one fixed-size edit no matter how long the conversation runs. We show that a transported receipt reaches exact omission if and only if the changes the record induces in later updates cancel out on net, and we measure whether they do on the released 48B Kimi Linear hybrid. They do not: after 4,096 further tokens the record still leaves an imprint of about 4.5% of the state norm that none of the tested receipt classes removes, recomputing half the suffix closes less than half the gap, and the per-token log a receipt needs costs more than a full checkpoint after 88 tokens. The same write-rule classification held on Mamba-2, Falcon-H1, and RWKV-7 with predictions recorded before the runs. Restoring a checkpoint from before the record and replaying the surviving suffix matches the never-stored state exactly on every array we check. In the hybrid suffix sweep, masking the record's attention rows brings sampled recovery close to the never-stored floor even though the recurrent imprint remains, and an auditor who rebuilds the reference can still detect it. Among the evaluated methods, checkpoint replay achieves exact omission, with work proportional to the replayed suffix.

</details>


### [160] [Learning Adaptive SED for heterogeneous load balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06881)
*Sanne van Kempen,Jaron Sanders,Fiona Sloothaak,Maarten G. Wolf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究异构服务率未知的两服务器负载均衡系统中的在线学习问题，提出一种能够收敛到最短期望延迟（SED）策略的在线学习算法，通过平衡经验SED路由与强制探索阶段来保证两服务器充足的采样，并证明该算法实现有限遗憾（区别于经典MAB的对数增长），最后通过数值实验验证性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在负载均衡系统中，SED策略需要已知服务器服务率，但实际中服务率通常是未知的。直接使用经验估计的策略因估计误差会在状态空间的无限区域上与最优策略不一致而表现不佳，因此需要设计一种能够同时学习服务率并收敛到SED的在线学习算法。

Method: 提出一种在线学习算法，该算法精心平衡基于经验估计的SED路由与强制探索阶段，通过强制探索保证对两个服务器的充分采样，从而在消除估计误差影响的同时学习真实服务率并收敛到SED策略。

Result: 证明算法实现有限遗憾（finite regret），这与经典多臂老虎机（MAB）设置中遗憾随时间对数增长的典型结果形成鲜明对比；数值实验验证了算法的性能，并突出展示了强制探索特别有益的机制/情境。

Conclusion: 所提算法能在服务率未知的异构双服务器系统中同时完成率参数学习与SED最优路由，且遗憾为有限值，表明该问题本质上比经典MAB更容易/更可解，强制探索是保证收敛的关键机制。

Abstract: We study a two-server load balancing system with heterogeneous service rates that are a priori unknown to the dispatcher. The goal is to route customers according to the Shortest--Expected--Delay (SED) policy, but this requires knowledge of the service rates. Empirical policies that route based on estimates perform poorly: due to estimation error, the empirical policy disagrees with the oracle on an infinite region of the state space. We propose an online learning algorithm that converges to SED while learning the service rates. The algorithm carefully balances empirical SED routing with forced exploration phases that guarantee sufficient sampling of both servers. We prove that our algorithm achieves finite regret; this differs from classical Multi-Armed Bandit settings where regret typically grows logarithmically in time. Finally, numerical experiments demonstrate the performance of our algorithm and highlight the regimes in which forced exploration is especially beneficial.

</details>


### [161] [Noisy-Space Policy Gradient for Diffusion Policies in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06882)
*Mahmoud Selim,Cristina Cipriani,Karl H. Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种噪声空间动作价值函数，将扩散策略与强化学习有效结合，并导出了噪声空间策略梯度（NSPG），通过仅使用干净动作空间的价值估计来优化噪声潜在变量，避免了经过去噪过程的反向传播。实验表明该方法在离线强化学习中有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略为连续控制提供了强大且富有表现力的参数化方法，但其与强化学习的整合在概念上和算法上都存在挑战，本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 引入噪声空间Q函数，通过去噪过程中所执行动作的分布为扩散潜变量分配价值；推导噪声空间策略梯度（NSPG），仅用干净动作空间价值估计优化噪声潜变量；基于此构建KL正则化的策略改进目标，得到可兼容扩散模型的回归形式，避免了经过去噪过程的反向传播。

Result: 在基于状态的D4RL基准和基于视觉的OGBench任务上的实验结果表明，所提出的噪声空间目标为离线强化学习中训练扩散策略提供了有原则且有效的依据。

Conclusion: 噪声空间目标函数为扩散策略在离线强化学习中的集成提供了一种理论严谨且实践有效的方法。

Abstract: Diffusion policies offer a powerful and expressive parameterization for continuous control. Yet, their integration with reinforcement learning remains conceptually and algorithmically challenging. In this work, we address this gap by introducing a noisy-space action-value (Q-)function that assigns values to diffusion latents through the distribution of executed actions induced by the denoising process. We show that this construction admits a precise semantic interpretation and derive a noisy-space policy gradient (NSPG) that optimizes noisy latents using only clean action-space value estimates. Building on this result, we formulate a KL-regularized policy improvement over noisy latents and show that the resulting objective admits a diffusion-compatible regression form, avoiding backpropagation through the denoising process. Empirical results on state-based D4RL benchmarks and vision-based OGBench tasks demonstrate that the proposed noisy-space objective provides a principled and effective basis for training diffusion policies in offline reinforcement learning. Project webpage: https://mahmoud-selim.github.io/NSPG/

</details>


### [162] [From Synthetic Priors to Model Behavior: Structural Coverage in Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06912)
*He Zhao,Ryan Thompson,Daniel M. Steinberg,Ashfaqur Rahman,Edwin V. Bonilla,Cheng Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从分布级归因视角研究表格基础模型（TFMs）的合成预训练先验对下游任务的支撑程度。通过重建四个TFM的合成数据生成器，使用结构描述符比较其生成任务与两个基准数据集的差异，发现不同合成先验对基准任务的结构覆盖度和密度存在显著差异，且更强的支撑与更好的模型性能相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型常用程序化生成的合成任务进行预训练，但合成预训练先验对下游评估任务的实际支撑程度尚不清楚，缺乏系统性的归因分析方法。

Method: 从分布级归因视角出发，重建四个TFM的合成数据生成器，将每个数据集用一组共同的结构描述符（涵盖schema、特征分布、依赖结构、响应属性及特征-响应关系）表示，通过结构覆盖度和归一化密度度量合成先验对基准任务的覆盖广度与重复度，并考察局部支撑强弱与预测性能的关系。

Result: 不同合成预训练先验之间存在显著差异：部分生成器对基准任务的支持在广度和密度上明显优于其他生成器；此外，合成到基准的结构支撑越强，模型相对性能通常也越好。

Conclusion: 结构覆盖度可作为描述合成预训练先验并建立其数据生成假设与下游模型行为之间关联的有效诊断工具。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) are commonly pretrained on large collections of procedurally generated synthetic tasks, yet it remains unclear how well these synthetic pretraining priors support the downstream tasks on which the models are evaluated. We study this question from a distribution-level attribution perspective. We recover or reconstruct the synthetic data generators of four TFMs and compare their generated tasks with datasets from two widely used tabular benchmarks. Each dataset is represented by a common set of structural descriptors capturing schema, feature distributions, dependence structure, response properties, and feature--response relationships. In this space, we measure how broadly and repeatedly each synthetic prior reaches benchmark tasks using structural coverage and normalized density, and examine whether stronger local support is associated with better predictive performance. We find substantial differences across synthetic pretraining priors: some generators provide consistently broader and denser support for benchmark tasks than others. Moreover, stronger synthetic-to-benchmark support is generally associated with better relative model performance. These results suggest that structural coverage provides a useful diagnostic for characterizing synthetic pretraining priors and relating their data-generating assumptions to downstream model behavior.

</details>


### [163] [Constrained Online Learning with Noisy Constraint Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06921)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带对抗性约束的在线凸优化问题，在约束值和梯度含无偏噪声的情况下，提出了LEDGER算法，实现了期望遗憾与预算违规之间的最优权衡（如O(√T)遗憾搭配O(T^{3/4})违规），并给出了噪声环境下无法同时实现O(T^{1-δ})联合保证的悲观下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作缺乏对带噪声约束的在线凸优化（COCO）问题的系统研究。已有无噪声情境下的低遗憾保证在对抗性约束并伴噪声的设定下不成立，且此前文献中广泛使用的Slater条件等假设过于严格，需要设计不依赖该条件且能处理噪声反馈的算法。

Method: (1) 建立了含高斯值噪声下的最坏情形下界Ω(min{σ,1}T/log^7T)，说明传统的联合遗憾与硬违规目标不可行；(2) 提出LEDGER算法，通过维护非负余额跟踪观测净消耗，并在当前反馈噪声前设定约束权重；(3) 在共同可行性和条件有限方差反馈假设下分析期望遗憾和预算违规的权衡，扩展至动态遗憾分析。

Result: LEDGER算法在固定问题参数下实现O(√T/V)期望遗憾和O(√V·T^{3/4}+σ√T)期望预算违规，V∈[T^{-1/2},1]。V=1时得(O(√T),O(T^{3/4}))，V=T^{-1/6}时得(O(T^{2/3}),O(T^{2/3}))，V=T^{-1/2}时得(O(T),O(√T))。动态遗憾为O((1+E[P_T])√T/V)。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了带噪声对抗约束下，需要以"预算违规"替代"硬违规"作为约束满足的度量。LEDGER无需Slater条件即可实现遗憾与违规的帕累托最优权衡，且算法统一适用于静态与动态遗憾场景。

Abstract: We study constrained online convex optimization with adversarial constraints when constraint values and gradients are observed through unbiased noise. Gaussian value noise of standard deviation $σ$ yields a worst-case lower bound of $Ω(\min\{σ,1\}T/\log^7T)$ on the maximum of expected regret and expected hard violation, even with known gradients. This rules out any jointly $O(T^{1-δ})$ guarantee for fixed $δ>0$ and fixed positive noise level. We therefore study budget violation: the largest cumulative overspend over any window within a fixed horizon. We introduce \LEDGER, which tracks observed net consumption in a nonnegative balance and sets constraint weights before the current feedback noise. Under common feasibility and conditional finite-variance feedback, for fixed problem parameters, \LEDGER\ achieves $O(\sqrt T/V)$ expected regret and $O(\sqrt V\,T^{3/4}+σ\sqrt T)$ expected budget violation for $V\in[T^{-1/2},1]$. This gives the pair $(O(\sqrt T),O(T^{3/4}))$ at $V=1$ and $(O(T^{2/3}),O(T^{2/3}))$ at $V=T^{-1/6}$, without a Slater condition. The budget-focused endpoint $V=T^{-1/2}$ gives $(O(T),O(\sqrt T))$. The same update yields $O((1+E[P_T])\sqrt T/V)$ expected dynamic regret for predictable feasible comparator paths, without common feasibility or path-length input. Its budget bound instead depends on the shortest feasible path, up to a dimension factor.

</details>


### [164] [The Geometry of Refusal: Why Post-Hoc Safety Is Fragile and Pretraining-Time Safety Persists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06934)
*Srikanth Malla,Chiho Choi,Joon Hee Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从几何角度解释安全对齐（RLHF/DPO等事后训练）的脆弱性根源：安全更新与能力方向几乎正交，只能"覆盖"而非"消除"模型能力形象，导致微调或攻击可轻易恢复拒绝行为。作者进一步发现安全基底在预训练约6B-60B token间锐利涌现，并提出全程安全协同训练方法，使模型在410M至6.9B规模上获得显著更持久的攻击鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事后安全训练（RLHF/DPO）虽为主流对齐手段，但越狱、微调攻击和激活空间探针仍能恢复其试图消除的行为，这一脆弱性缺乏统一的几何学解释，也未能追溯至预训练阶段的根源。

Method: 通过测量安全更新Δ与能力损失经验Fisher曲率的关系揭示"抑制机制"；利用核不动性引理证明薄而尖锐的安全更新只能掩蔽能力；在OLMo-2-1B的267个检查点上追踪安全基底涌现；提出全程安全协同训练方法并对比事后安装与计算匹配的分窗调度方案。

Result: 事后安全更新与能力方向近正交，其子空间成分集中于少数高曲率方向；100步良性微调即能击穿Qwen-2.5-7B和Llama-3-8B的拒绝机制。安全基底在6B-60B token间锐利转变涌现。安全协同训练在410M至6.9B全规模实现87-98%拒绝率，攻击后保持84-91%，侵蚀仅2-14个百分点（事后安装为35-38个百分点），且能力不低于仅LM基线。

Conclusion: 安全信号在预训练过程中的持续存在（而非安装时机）才是获得攻击鲁棒性的关键因素，过晚安装的安全更新本质上只是叠加在完整能力之上的薄锐拒绝闸门。

Abstract: Post-hoc safety training (RLHF, DPO) is the dominant way to align large language models, yet jailbreaks (Zou et al., 2023b), fine-tuning attacks (Qi et al., 2024), and activation-space probes (Arditi et al., 2024) keep recovering the behaviors it was meant to remove. We give this fragility one geometric explanation and trace it to when, during pretraining, safety can take hold. We measure the safety update $Δ= W_{\text{safe}} - W_{\text{base}}$ against the curvature of the model's capabilities (the empirical Fisher of a capability loss). Post-hoc safety consistently lands in a suppression regime: $Δ$ is nearly orthogonal to the capability directions, and its small in-subspace part concentrates on a few high-curvature ones. The update is thin but sharp, a refusal gate laid over intact capabilities rather than erasure of them. A kernel-immobility lemma explains why such an update can only mask a capability, not remove it, so a little benign fine-tuning restores it: 100 steps of benign fine-tuning collapse refusal on Qwen-2.5-7B and Llama-3-8B Instruct at preserved capability, a signature that replicates across five model families.
  Following the account into pretraining, a 267-checkpoint sweep of OLMo-2-1B (OLMo et al., 2025) shows the substrate that safety engages emerging in a sharp transition between roughly 6B and 60B pretraining tokens. We then use the account constructively: models trained from scratch with safety co-training spread continuously across pretraining reach 87 to 98% refusal whose post-attack level holds at 84 to 91% at every scale, an erosion of 2 to 14 pp against 35 to 38 pp for post-hoc installs, at capability matched or better than an LM-only baseline and holding from 410M to 6.9B, whereas a compute-matched windowed schedule installs no lasting refusal. Persistence of the safety signal across pretraining, not its timing, is what buys attack robustness.

</details>


### [165] [PCSDiff: Diffusion-Based Bias Correction and Super Resolution Toward Practical Operational Medium-Term Precipitation Forecast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06942)
*Yuze Sun,Shiyi Wang,Jiancheng Pan,Die Wang,Andreas F. Prein,Wentao Luo,Linhan Jiang,Jie Wu,Quan Zhang,Xiaomeng Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PCSDiff框架，用于10天中期降水预报的系统性偏差校正和降尺度。该框架结合降水强度感知多分支解码器（PIMD）实现多日动态误差缓解，并通过两阶段条件扩散超分辨率模块恢复精细化降水结构。在中国区域评估中，相比原始ECMWF预报，RMSE降低16.1%，ACC提升13.9%，并在极端降水指标上优于主流深度学习基线，同时支持低延迟流式推理以满足业务部署需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中期降水预报存在系统性偏差、随预报时效累积的误差以及空间分辨率粗糙三重问题，限制了其在洪水-干旱风险评估中的应用。现有AI校正技术缺乏对多日动态偏差演化的专门建模，且缺少合理的气象约束，常产生过度平滑的降水结构，难以满足业务化部署要求。

Method: PCSDiff采用级联任务解耦的扩散框架：首先通过降水强度感知多分支解码器（PIMD）利用天气尺度时间特征进行多日动态误差缓解；随后经过两阶段条件扩散超分辨率模块重建精细尺度的降水空间模式。

Result: PCSDiff在全球数据训练后针对中国CMA-CRA观测进行评估，在3-10天预报时效内相较原始ECMWF预报RMSE降低16.1%，ACC提升13.9%，在通用和极端降水指标上均优于主流深度学习基线，且通过流式推理管线实现低延迟滚动预报。

Conclusion: PCSDiff通过任务解耦的级联设计有效解决了中期降水预报中偏差修正与统计降尺度的联合问题，兼顾了误差动态演化的建模与精细降水结构的重建，同时满足了气象业务对低延迟和高效率的实时部署需求。

Abstract: Medium-range precipitation forecasts are impaired by persistent systematic biases, lead-time-dependent error accumulation, and coarse spatial resolution, restricting their reliability for flood-drought risk assessment. Existing AI correction techniques lack dedicated modeling for multi-day dynamic bias evolution and proper meteorological constraints, often generating over-smoothed rainfall structures, and cannot meet operational deployment demands. This work introduces PCSDiff, a cascaded task-decoupled diffusion framework targeting 10-day precipitation bias correction and downscaling. To jointly counteract temporal error drifts and reconstruct physically plausible local precipitation details, PCSDiff integrates the Precipitation Intensity-aware Multi-branch Decoder (PIMD) module for dynamic multi-day error mitigation using synoptic-temporal features, followed by a two-phase conditional diffusion super-resolution module to restore fine-scale precipitation patterns. Evaluated against CMA-CRA observations over China after global-data training, PCSDiff cuts RMSE by 16.1% and lifts ACC by 13.9% relative to raw ECMWF forecasts at 3-10-day lead times, and consistently outperforms mainstream deep-learning baselines on both general and extreme-precipitation metrics. Benefiting from a streaming inference pipeline, our method achieves low-latency rolling forecasting for practical meteorological operations.

</details>


### [166] [Particle Dynamics of Flow Matching and Classifier-Free Guidance from a Stagewise Geometry Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06947)
*Jian-Feng Cai,Zhengyi Su,Chao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对流匹配及其分类器自由引导（CFG）提出了统一的逐阶段粒子级几何理论，揭示了无条件流轨迹依次被吸引至全局均值邻域、数据凸包邻域及局部簇邻域，并给出O(1-t)的距离收缩估计；CFG情况下该结构仍然成立，仅需将均值外推、凸包膨胀并在目标簇附近恢复条件流匹配的局部几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配与CFG的理论理解多停留在分布层面，而实际采样遵循个体轨迹，分布级保证不足以解释轨迹与数据几何的交互及CFG对几何的塑造作用。

Method: 建立了连续动力学和显式Euler离散化的统一逐阶段几何理论，涵盖吸引与吸收两个过程，并推广到一般时间调度a(t)。

Result: 无条件流轨迹逐阶段被吸引至全局均值、数据凸包及局部簇邻域；CFG下结构不变，涉及外推均值、膨胀条件凸包及恢复目标簇附近的局部几何；距离满足O(1-t)收缩估计，一般时间调度下为O(1-a(t))。

Conclusion: 该研究为流匹配及CFG在连续与离散采样中提供了统一的粒子级几何理论框架，弥补了分布级理论的不足。

Abstract: Flow matching, together with classifier-free guidance (CFG), is widely used in generative modeling, yet much of the theoretical understanding remains distribution-wise. Since practical sampling follows individual trajectories, distribution-level guarantees alone do not fully capture how trajectories interact with the data geometry or how guidance reshapes it. To overcome this limitation, we establish a unified stagewise geometric theory of attraction and absorption for both continuous dynamics and explicit Euler discretization. Specifically, with $t\in[0,1]$ running from noise to data, we show that unconditional flow trajectories are successively attracted toward a neighborhood of the global mean, the data convex hull, and a neighborhood of a possibly nonconvex local cluster. Across these stages, the corresponding distance satisfies a common contraction estimate, yielding an ${O}(1-t)$ decay of the distance in the final stage. For CFG, the same structure persists with an extrapolated mean, an inflated conditional convex hull, and, near the target cluster, the restored local geometry of conditional flow matching. We further show that a general time schedule $a(t)$ replaces the $O(1-t)$ decay by $O(1-a(t))$. Together, these results provide a unified particle-level geometric account of flow matching and CFG across continuous and discrete sampling.

</details>


### [167] [Steering Interference Reflects the Model's Defaults, Not the Behavior Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06951)
*Srikanth Malla,Chiho Choi,Joon Hee Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了激活引导（activation steering）为何无法实现模块化控制语言模型行为的问题。作者发现，引导效果主要由模型自身的固有偏好决定，而非被引导的行为本身。无论引导何种行为，模型都会被拉向其已偏好的少量行为（主要是拒绝、谄媚和诗意表达）。通过24种行为和10个指令微调模型的实验证明，干涉主要由模型内部机制决定，单纯解耦行为方向无法实现模块化引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导被认为是模块化控制语言模型行为的有前景方法——一个行为应对应激活空间中的一个方向，添加该方向应只切换目标行为而不影响其他行为。然而实际并非如此，作者希望探究是什么决定了哪些其他行为会受影响、影响程度如何。

Method: 作者在10个指令微调模型上对24种行为进行激活引导实验，使用语言模型评判器而非探针来读取生成文本的效果。设计了三个实验：(1)使用不含行为内容、仅匹配真实引导向量大小的向量进行对比；(2)考察干涉的方向性（如引导粗俗导致有毒，但引导有毒不影响粗俗）；(3)完全排除某行为，仅用其他行为测量几何特性来预测干涉。

Result: 三个关键发现：(1)无行为内容的向量与真实引导移动相同行为且顺序相同，但不会产生需特定方向的效应；(2)干涉大多是单向的，排除了方向重叠的解释；(3)其他行为的几何特性几乎无法解释被排除行为的干涉。该模式在全部10个模型上成立，默认偏好拉力在10B参数以下最强，且在各大模型家族中随规模增大而减弱。此外，24种行为都可线性解码，但只有20种会实际改变模型输出。

Conclusion: 激活引导的效果取决于模型本身而非被引导的行为——引导只是扰动，模型会把输出拉回其已固有的少量偏好行为集合。因此，即使解耦了行为方向，也无法使激活引导实现模块化控制。

Abstract: Activation steering promises modular control of language model behavior: a behavior such as politeness corresponds to a direction in a model's activations, and adding that direction while it generates should switch the behavior on and leave everything else alone. It does not. We ask what decides which other behaviors move, and by how much, and find that it is the model rather than the behavior being steered. A steer relaxes the model toward a small set of behaviors it already favors, chiefly refusal, sycophancy, and poeticism, and that set is much the same whatever is steered.
  Three results across 24 behaviors and ten instruction-tuned models support this, every effect read off the generated text by a language-model judge rather than off a probe. That readout matters: all 24 behaviors are linearly decodable, but only 20 change what the model writes. First, a direction carrying no behavioral content, matched to a real steer only in the size of the vector it adds, moves the same behaviors in the same order as real steers do, while producing none of the behaviors that need a specific direction. Second, most interference runs one way, so it cannot be an overlap between two directions: steering profanity makes the model toxic, while steering toxicity leaves profanity untouched. Third, with a behavior held out entirely, geometry measured on the others explains almost none of the interference it takes part in. The account holds on all ten models, the pull toward defaults strongest below 10B parameters and weakening in each family's largest. Reading a steer as a perturbation whose endpoint the model fixes implies that disentangling behavior directions cannot by itself make steering modular.

</details>


### [168] [MOLE: Detecting Insider Threats in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06966)
*Aashiq Muhamed,Virginia Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MOLE基准，一个包含150个AI运营账户、9个有状态服务、30个工作日、12种威胁的开放基准，用于评估防御者能否在有限审查预算下检测AI代理的恶意行为。测试发现72%的代理模型能完成大部分有害目标，且最好的监控器也漏检近半数已完成的有害行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型错位、提示注入或操作员滥用可能导致AI代理在前沿实验室账户中窃取模型权重、毒化训练数据或削弱发布门禁。现有基准未测试防御者能否在有限审查预算和日常工作中检测此类活动。

Method: 构建MOLE开源基准，包含150个AI运营账户、9个有状态服务、30个工作日、12种威胁和约200亿token的8个语料库（来自四个模型）。对39个代理模型进行测试，并比较40种监控器在不同语料生成器、可观测性级别和威胁类型下的表现。同时支持基于基准引导的监控器优化。

Result: 39个代理模型中有72%完成了大部分指定的有害目标，且代理拒绝行为不能预测任务完成率。在单日审计事件比较中，即使最佳监控器也漏检近一半已完成的有害行为。基准引导搜索将中等监控器性能提升49-64%，而选择性地使用更强监控器可在可比成本下将预算AUC提升10%。

Conclusion: MOLE揭示了现有监控能力在检测AI代理有害行为方面的显著不足，同时展示该基准不仅能用于评估，还能有效指导监控器的开发与优化，为AI安全领域提供了重要的评测基础设施。

Abstract: Model misalignment, prompt injection, or operator misuse could lead AI agents operating frontier-lab accounts to exfiltrate model weights, poison training data, or weaken release gates. Existing benchmarks do not test whether defenders can detect this activity among routine work under a limited review budget. We introduce MOLE, an open benchmark of 150 AI-operated accounts sharing 9 stateful services over 30 workdays, with 12 threats and 8 corpora from four models totaling roughly 20 billion tokens. Of 39 agent models, 72% complete most assigned harmful objectives and agent refusal does not predict completion. MOLE enables comparison of 40 monitors across corpus generators, observability levels, and threats; even the best evaluated monitor in our single-day audit-event comparison misses nearly half of completed harm. MOLE also enables monitor development: benchmark-guided search improves a mid-tier monitor by 49-64%, while selective use of a stronger monitor improves budget-AUC by 10% over applying it to every account-day at comparable modeled cost.

</details>


### [169] [HealthLoopQA: A Context-Aware Question Answering Benchmark for Interpreting Wearable Monitoring Data in Diabetes Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06976)
*Yuchen Niu,Yanan Ma,Srinivasan Nandakumar,Maolin Chen,Viktor Schlegel,Kexin Wei,Ling Cheng,Anna Bird,Anil Anthony Bharath,Siew-Kei Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了HealthLoopQA，一个用于评估大语言模型（LLM）在连续糖尿病监测数据上进行时间推理能力的综合诊断基准，包含127个任务和1500多个QA实例，并引入故障注入仿真测试台以评估安全感知能力，揭示了LLM在复杂时间模式挖掘上的严重局限性和长上下文提示下的"上下文惰性"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗可穿戴设备的问答基准主要评估短期分类或统计摘要任务，忽视了真实部署中固有的长期模式、治疗和行为背景以及潜在的系统故障。随着LLM被整合到慢性病护理中，需要系统性地评估其在多样化监测任务中的推理能力和分析严谨性。

Method: 构建了HealthLoopQA基准：基于包含11种原子推理能力的新型分类法，涵盖127个任务和1500多个QA实例，覆盖流程挖掘、异常检测和30天水平线的预测。通过故障注入仿真测试台模拟设备故障和网络物理攻击，生成生理学上合理的危险场景，并评估多种提示方法和智能体框架下的前沿LLM。

Result: 评估显示前沿LLM在复杂时间模式挖掘中存在严重局限性，同时发现长上下文提示下存在更广泛的"上下文惰性"（In-context Laziness）现象，这表明LLM在严谨的长期医学推理场景中存在关键的开放性挑战。

Conclusion: HealthLoopQA揭示了当前LLM在长期监测数据的深度时序推理能力方面的重大缺口，强调了开发更健壮的推理策略以支持安全可靠的医疗可穿戴数据分析部署的紧迫性。

Abstract: As medical wearables become integrated into daily chronic disease care, effectively interpreting longitudinal monitoring data is essential for patients and clinicians to understand health trends, detect safety-critical events, and make informed decisions. While large language models (LLMs) show promise for transforming this streaming physiological data into personalized health insights, evaluating their reasoning capability and analytical rigor in diverse monitoring tasks remains a fundamental challenge. Existing medical wearable question answering (QA) benchmarks primarily assess short-horizon classification or statistical summaries, largely ignoring the long-term patterns, therapeutic and behavioural contexts, and potential system failures inherent in real-world deployments. To address this, we introduce HealthLoopQA, a comprehensive diagnostic benchmark for evaluating LLM reasoning over continuous diabetes monitoring data. Grounded in a novel taxonomy of eleven atomic reasoning abilities, HealthLoopQA comprises 127 tasks and over 1,500 QA instances spanning process mining, anomaly detection, and prediction over 30-day horizons. To systematically evaluate safety awareness, we complement real-world datasets with a fault-injected simulation testbed modeling diverse device malfunctions and cyber-physical attacks to generate physiologically plausible hazard scenarios. Evaluating state-of-the-art LLMs across prompting and agentic frameworks reveals severe limitations in complex temporal pattern mining. Furthermore, we identify a broader phenomenon of In-context Laziness under long-context prompting, highlighting critical open challenges in deploying LLMs for rigorous long-horizon medical reasoning.

</details>


### [170] [AF-Mamba: Efficient Long-Term Signal Modeling for Early Prediction of Atrial Fibrillation Onset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06984)
*Yongbin Lee,Ki H. Chon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AF-Mamba，一种结合时间卷积网络（TCNs）与Mamba选择性状态空间模型的混合深度学习架构，用于提前一小时预测心房颤动（AF）的发作。该模型在长时RR间期序列上实现高效训练与推理，在五分钟交叉验证中达到0.974的AUROC，并在跨数据集评估中保持0.897的平均AUROC，展现了优越的性能-效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 房颤是最常见的心律失常，与卒中和心力衰竭风险增加相关。可穿戴心电监测设备的普及使得连续心律评估成为可能，但提前预测AF发作以争取临床干预时间仍具挑战。现有Transformer模型在长序列上存在二次方缩放问题，循环神经网络则面临梯度消失，亟需一种既能高效建模长程RR间期序列又能保持高预测性能的架构。

Method: 提出混合TCN-Mamba架构：使用时间卷积网络（TCN）对一小时输入窗口进行局部特征编码，再通过Mamba（一种选择性状态空间模型）进行长程序列建模。该混合设计避免了Transformer的二次方计算复杂度与RNN的梯度消失问题，支持长达一小时的RR间期窗口的高效训练和推理。

Result: 在subject-wise 5折交叉验证中，模型达到敏感性0.889、特异性0.943、F1分数0.813、AUROC 0.974和AUPRC 0.933。在配对跨数据集留出验证中，平均AUROC为0.897。与最先进的AF预测模型和通用时间序列模型相比，AF-Mamba在保持竞争力的同时提供了更优的性能-效率权衡。

Conclusion: AF-Mamba展示了在发作前一小时准确预测房颤的潜力，并适用于实时连续动态监测场景，为房颤相关并发症的及时临床评估和管理提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Atrial fibrillation (AF) is the most common cardiac arrhythmia and is associated with increased risks of stroke and heart failure. The growing availability of wearable and portable ECG monitoring enables continuous assessment of cardiac rhythm outside clinical settings. Predicting AF before its onset could provide additional lead time for timely clinical assessment and potentially improve the management of patients at risk of AF-related complications. This study focuses on predicting AF onset one hour in advance using long-term RR intervals (RRIs). To address this challenge, we propose a deep learning architecture that integrates temporal convolutional networks (TCNs) for local features encoding with Mamba, a selective state-space model capable of long-range sequence modeling. This hybrid TCN-Mamba design enables efficient training and inference on one-hour input windows, overcoming limitations of Transformers' quadratic scaling and recurrent networks' vanishing gradients. In subject-wise 5-fold testing, the proposed model achieved a sensitivity of 0.889, specificity of 0.943, F1-score of 0.813, AUROC of 0.974, and AUPRC of 0.933. In paired cross-dataset holdout evaluation, AF-Mamba maintained discriminative performance across unseen AF and NSR datasets, achieving a mean AUROC of 0.897. Compared against state-of-the-art AF prediction models and general time-series models, AF-Mamba achieved competitive predictive performance while providing a favorable performance-efficiency trade-off for long RRI sequences. These findings demonstrate the potential of AF-Mamba for accurate AF prediction one hour in advance and real-time continuous ambulatory monitoring.

</details>


### [171] [Continual Learning Mechanisms Compose for Long-Horizon Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06986)
*Zheyuan Zhang,Alvin Zhang,Daniel Khashabi,Tianmin Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"长时程记忆"（long-horizon memorization）设定，即模型通过持续监督微调学习100个查询-答案任务且不保留旧样本/无任务标识。研究发现单独机制均无法在该水平上抵抗灾难性遗忘，但将数据、函数、权重三类锚定（anchors）与合并LoRA等低秩分配机制组合，可将最终平均保留率从朴素序列微调的1.2%提升至34.9%（28倍提升），并证明不同机制组合存在超加性相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型需要随时间持续内化新信息并在多次更新后仍能保留，但现有持续学习方法在长时程（100个任务）场景下效果不佳，尚无系统性研究验证组合不同遗忘补偿机制的有效性。

Method: 构建三类100任务记忆数据集；设计数据锚、函数锚、权重锚三种机制维度与低秩分配规则（merged LoRA）；提出任务级逐次减半法（task-level successive halving）搜索组合设计空间；采用因子实验测量个体效应与交互效应。

Result: 最佳方法组合全部三种锚与merged LoRA，在所有数据集上排名前三；最终保留率从1.2%提升至34.9%（28倍）；数据锚与merged LoRA贡献最大且在三类数据集上均呈现超加性交互。

Conclusion: 组合互补的记忆保持机制能显著超越任何单一机制，表明持续学习研究应关注机制间的协同设计而非孤立优化。

Abstract: Language models may need to internalize information that arrives over time and retain it through many subsequent updates. To study this challenge, we introduce long-horizon memorization, a setting in which a model learns 100 query-answer tasks through continual supervised fine-tuning without retaining earlier training examples or receiving task identifiers at inference. Sequential updates cause catastrophic forgetting, and no single continual learning mechanism we evaluate maintains strong retention at this horizon. We hypothesize that mechanisms addressing complementary sources of forgetting will be more effective when composed. We organize these compositions along two design dimensions. Data, function, and weight anchors specify what prior information each update should preserve, while low-rank allocation rules determine where successive updates are retained. To test this hypothesis systematically, we construct three distinct 100-task memorization datasets. We introduce task-level successive halving to search the combinatorial design space and use a factorial experiment to measure individual and interaction effects. Our best method combines all three anchors with merged LoRA, ranks among the top 3 methods in all datasets, and raises average final retention from 1.2% under naive sequential fine-tuning to 34.9%, a 28-fold improvement. The data anchor and merged LoRA provide the largest average gains and interact super-additively on all three datasets. Together, these results show that composing complementary mechanisms substantially improves long-horizon memorization beyond what any individual mechanism achieves.

</details>
