### [172] [NeuCME: Toward Dynamic Multimodal Continual Learning via Neural Combinatorics of Multiple Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07009)
*Kai Guo,Chuanbin Liu,Peng Hu,Hao Wang,Xi Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuCME框架用于解决动态多模态持续学习问题，该问题中任务间的模态集合可能发生变化，面临着时空灾难性遗忘和自适应多模态融合两大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法假设每个任务的模态集合是预定义且固定的，但这不符合现实场景。现实中不同任务可能涉及不同的模态组合，因此需要一种能适应模态动态变化的学习框架。

Method: NeuCME框架包含三个核心组件：模态组合重放（modality-combinational rehearsal）、多门控混合专家（multi-gated mixture-of-experts）和任务相关性引导蒸馏（task relevance-guided distillation）。此外还提出了评估任务序列动态程度的度量指标。

Result: 在四个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，NeuCME显著优于现有最先进的方法。

Conclusion: NeuCME有效解决了动态多模态持续学习中模态变化带来的遗忘和融合难题，为该领域建立了新的基准。

Abstract: Multimodal continual learning has recently shown great potential for developing agents with human-like intelligence by continuously learning new tasks across multiple modalities. However, existing methods typically assume that the set of modalities per task is predefined and fixed. In this paper, we investigate a more realistic learning setting, referred to as dynamic multimodal continual learning, in which the set of modalities may vary across tasks rather than remaining fixed. This setting involves two primary challenges: (i) spatio-temporal catastrophic forgetting and (ii) adaptive multimodal fusion. To address these challenges, we propose NeuCME (as shorthand for \textbf{Neu}ral \textbf{C}ombinatorics of \textbf{M}ultiple \textbf{E}xperts), a novel framework designed to effectively learn and integrate knowledge across tasks with varying modalities. The proposed NeuCME model comprises three key components, namely modality-combinational rehearsal, multi-gated mixture-of-experts, and task relevance-guided distillation. Furthermore, we formulate an evaluation metric to quantify the dynamism of task sequences and then set up a comprehensive benchmark with different degrees of dynamism. Extensive experiments using four real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed NeuCME outperforms state-of-the-art methods markedly.

</details>


### [173] [HyperTransfer: Understanding the Equivalence between Base Optimizer and Hyperball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07017)
*Jinghui Yuan,Hongtao Zhang,Jade Zou,Tianyu Li,Wenjie Zhou,Tianyu He,Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了Hyperball优化器与常规Base优化器在尺度不变网络中动态等价，并提出HyperTransfer框架，仅利用目标优化器的初始化和学习率调度即可复现其全部动态，且无需实际运行目标优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hyperball优化器通过约束参数范数并仅更新方向，与常规优化器更新参数范数和方向的范式看似根本不同，这引发了关于两者之间联系的理论疑问，需要澄清两种优化范式之间的关系。

Method: 首先在理论上证明两种优化范式在尺度不变网络中的动态等价性；基于此等价性提出HyperTransfer框架，通过推导正/逆映射，将目标Base优化器的初始化和学习率调度转换为Hyperball优化器参数，并扩展到非尺度不变网络。

Result: 实验表明HyperTransfer和逆映射生成的损失轨迹与目标优化器几乎一致，说明Hyperball动态主要由诱导的有效学习率调度和优化器状态主导。

Conclusion: Hyperball与Base优化器虽几何形式不同，但在动态行为上高度一致，这为两种优化范式之间架起了理论桥梁，也为优化器的迁移与转换提供了新的实用工具。

Abstract: Hyperball optimizers constrain parameter norms and update only their directions, establishing a distinct paradigm for neural network optimization. Although this geometry appears fundamentally different from that of conventional Base Optimizers, which update both parameter norms and directions, we show that the two paradigms are dynamically equivalent for scale-invariant networks. Building on this equivalence, we propose HyperTransfer, which constructs a Hyperball optimizer that reproduces the dynamics of a target Base Optimizer using only its initialization and learning-rate schedule, without running the target optimizer itself. We further derive the inverse mapping and extend the framework to non-scale-invariant networks. Experiments show that both HyperTransfer and the inverse mapping produce loss trajectories nearly identical to those of their targets, suggesting that Hyperball dynamics are governed primarily by the induced effective learning-rate schedule and optimizer state.

</details>


### [174] [Efficient Learning and Symmetry Discovery under Exact Invariances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07031)
*Ashkan Soleymani,Behrooz Tahmasebi,Patrick Jaillet,Stefanie Jegelka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了群不变性学习中两个关键的计算基础问题：(1) 提出了首个对有限和无限群均适用的多项式时间精确不变性学习算法，运行时间与数据维度和样本量相关而与群无关；(2) 在对称性未知的设定下，提出了多项式时间内从数据中恢复精确对称性并用于学习的算法，匹配已知对称性设定的最优样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管带群不变性的学习在许多科学和几何问题中至关重要，但其计算基础仍未得到充分理解。此前工作仅解决了有限且已知群情形下的精确不变性可多项式时间实现，而无限群与未知对称性这两种情形仍然悬而未决。此外，该工作对解释几何机器学习中不变/等变方法的经验成功也具有重要意义。

Method: 作者首先提出了一种统一适用于有限和无限群的多项式时间精确不变性学习算法，其运行时间与数据维度和样本量相关，与群无关。其次，在对称性发现设定中，研究者聚焦于有限群的子群格，利用随机Cayley图和扩张图理论作为分析工具，设计出能从数据中恢复精确对称性并利用该对称性进行学习的算法。

Result: 论文取得了两部分主要结果：一是给出了首个覆盖有限和无限群的多项式时间精确不变性学习算法，具有强泛化保证；二是在未知对称性的设定下，算法能在多项式时间内恢复潜在对称性，在有限维特征空间的回归任务上匹配已知对称性设定下的极小极大最优样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作从计算角度为不变/等变几何学习方法的成功提供了理论解释，部分解答了文献中近期提出的开放问题。同时，作者认为随机Cayley图与扩张理论等分析工具可能具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: Learning with group invariances is central to many scientific and geometric learning problems, yet its computational foundations remain poorly understood. Even for classical supervised regression settings, it has been unclear whether one can efficiently compute a regression function that is exactly invariant to a given group action. Recent work showed that exact invariance can be enforced in polynomial time when the underlying group is finite and known, but left open the cases of infinite groups and unknown symmetries. In this paper, we resolve both challenges. First, we present the first polynomial-time algorithm for learning with exact group invariances that applies uniformly to finite and infinite groups. The runtime is polynomial in the data dimension and sample size, and independent of the group, while achieving strong generalization guarantees. This provides a computational explanation for the empirical success of invariant and equivariant methods in geometric machine learning and partially answers a recent open question in the literature. Second, we study learning in the symmetry discovery setting, where the invariance group is unknown. Focusing on the subgroup lattice of a finite group, we show that exact symmetries can be identified from data and exploited for learning in polynomial time. For regression over finite-dimensional feature spaces, our algorithm provably recovers the underlying symmetry, matches the minimax-optimal sample complexity of the known-symmetry setting, and runs in time polynomial in the data dimension and sample size. Our analysis relies on tools from random Cayley graphs and expander theory, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [175] [Disentangling Steering Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07037)
*Takeru Hiramatsu,Kyohei Atarashi,Koh Takeuchi,Hisashi Kashima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Steering Vector Dissection框架，通过在实例级转向向量上训练专门的稀疏自编码器（SAE），将传统方法产生的复合转向向量分解为语义一致且彼此可区分的基准向量，从而实现对LLM行为更精确的干预控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的激活转向方法（如差均值法）产生的转向向量往往将多个语义和风格概念纠缠在一个复合方向中，导致转向效果不可预测，缺乏对模型行为的精细控制能力。

Method: 将正负激活成对配对并取差分，生成一组实例级转向向量；然后直接在这些差分向量上训练专门的稀疏自编码器（SAE），将复合转向方向显式分解为独立且语义一致的组成概念。

Result: 在两个数据集、两个模型和两种干预深度上进行定量评估，结果表明该方法能够产生一组语义一致且转向效果彼此可区分的基准向量，且这种解缠能够实现精确的模型行为控制。

Conclusion: Steering Vector Dissection有效解决了转向向量概念纠缠的问题，通过SAE解缠实现了对LLM行为的精确可控干预，是一种轻量且有效的推理时控制方法。

Abstract: Activation steering has emerged as a lightweight, inference-time approach to control the behavior of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, traditional steering vectors used to intervene in LLMs' activations, such as those derived from the difference-in-means method, tend to entangle multiple semantic and stylistic concepts into a single composite direction, leading to unpredictable steering effects. Our core objective is to disentangle this composite direction into its constituent concepts. To this end, we propose Steering Vector Dissection, a framework to explicitly isolate individual and semantically consistent features from these composite directions. Specifically, we pair positive and negative activations and take their differences to generate a set of instance-level steering vectors, and train a dedicated Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) directly on them. Quantitative evaluations across two datasets, two models, and two intervention depths show that our method yields a set of semantically consistent basis vectors whose steering effects are mutually distinguishable. Furthermore, we show that this disentanglement enables precise control over model behaviors.

</details>


### [176] [AI and TCAD for Inverse Design and Defect Discovery: From Simple Machine Learning to LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07046)
*Hiu Yung Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用TCAD生成精确数据来赋能机器学习，实现"仿真增强的机器学习"。通过自编码器模型和噪声工程学习潜在物理规律，结合大语言模型（LLM）和多模态LLM，在半导体器件设计、缺陷发现、逆向工程等任务上达到器件工程师水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI已广泛应用于工程领域，但由于半导体数据稀缺和维度灾难，AI在器件设计与缺陷发现方面的应用仍受限。需要一种方法来克服数据瓶颈，使AI能在该领域发挥与人类专家相当的水平。

Method: 1) 利用TCAD仿真生成高精度训练数据；2) 采用自编码器结合噪声工程技术从TCAD数据中学习潜在物理规律；3) 将模型迁移应用到实验数据；4) 引入LLM/MLLM实现TCAD命令文件创建和自动化器件设计。

Result: 通过多个案例验证：用非破坏性电学数据逆向工程PiN二极管层厚、Ga2O3肖特基二极管的掺杂与功函数变化、逆变器中的接触电阻；生成FinFET IV/CV预测模型；实现晶体管图像与IV曲线的映射；自动校准TCAD参数等。表明无需深厚领域专长即可构建达到器件工程师水平的模型。

Conclusion: TCAD生成数据与ML结合是可行的半导体器件AI应用路径，自编码器和噪声工程能有效学习物理规律，LLM/MLLM是实现全自动化器件设计与缺陷发现的未来方向。

Abstract: AI has revolutionized various engineering domains, but its impact on semiconductor device design and defect discovery is still limited, due to limited data and the curse of dimensionality. In this paper, we will discuss our work on using the Technology Computer-Aided-Design (TCAD) to generate precise data needed for machine learning (ML) to enable simulation-augmented ML. We demonstrate that with minimal domain expertise, it is possible to create a machine that performs as well as a device engineer on a specific task. We will show that auto-encoder-based machine learning models and noise engineering applied to TCAD data are effective at learning latent physics, and that the models can be seamlessly applied to experimental data. We will demonstrate how to build a device-engineer-level model step by step through various examples, including using only non-destructive electrical data to inverse-engineer the PiN diode layer thickness variations, the Ga2O3 Schottky diode doping and anode workfunction variations, and the transistor contact resistance in an inverter. Examples also include the generation of a FinFET IV/CV prediction model, the mapping between transistor images and IV curves, and the automatic calibration of TCAD parameters for a Ga2O3 Schottky diode, which can only be handled well by experienced TCAD engineers. Finally, to fully realize the potential of AI, large language models (LLMs) and multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) are believed to be necessary. We will discuss the application of LLMs to TCAD command file creation and our vision for MLLMs in automated device design and defect discovery.

</details>


### [177] [TrojanWorld: Backdooring World-Model Agents via Imagination Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07051)
*Wenkai Huang,Siyuan Liang,Gaolei Li,Yiming Li,Tianhao Peng,Jianhua Li,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TrojanWorld，一个针对世界模型智能体的后门攻击框架，通过物理物体作为触发器，操纵智能体的内部想象过程诱导其执行攻击者指定的行为，在多个基准上验证了有效性和隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型作为基于模型的强化学习智能体的预测核心日益重要，其预训练模型的广泛分发和复用引入了模型供应链安全威胁。后门攻击虽已被广泛研究，但针对交互式世界模型智能体的后门威胁尚未被充分探索。

Method: TrojanWorld包含三个核心机制：1) Decision-Reflective Induction（决策反射诱导），利用决策反馈引导触发器条件下的想象朝向攻击者指定动作；2) Clean Behavior Anchoring（清洁行为锚定），保持无触发器场景下的预测和行为保真度；3) Causal Propagation（因果传播），使触发器消失后仍能维持诱导偏好沿后续轨迹传播。攻击通过场景中的物理物体作为触发器，经由智能体原生观测管线激活，无需数字篡改观测流。

Result: 在TD-MPC2、DreamerV3和R2-Dreamer系统上，跨DeepMind Control、MetaWorld、MyoSuite和RoboDesk基准测试，触发器激活时目标动作偏差低至0.026，同时保留至少98.8%的清洁性能。触发器移除后，被攻陷的智能体仍可能被困在诱导的行为轨迹中继续执行攻击者指定动作。

Conclusion: TrojanWorld建立了一条从物理感知经污染想象到恶意动作选择的端到端攻击链，表明世界模型智能体面临严重的安全供应链威胁，即使在触发器移除后攻击效果仍具持续性。

Abstract: World models increasingly serve as the predictive core of model-based reinforcement learning agents, enabling them to simulate future dynamics and reason over imagined trajectories before acting. Their substantial training demands make pretrained world models attractive for distribution and reuse, exposing downstream systems to model supply chain threats. Backdoor attacks offer a targeted and stealthy means of exploiting such supply chains, yet their threat to interactive world-model agents remains largely unexplored. To fill this gap, we present TrojanWorld, a backdoor framework for world-model agents that induces attacker-specified behavior by steering internal imagination. A physical object placed in the scene acts as the trigger, enabling deployment-time activation through the agent's native observation pipeline without digitally manipulating the observation stream. To achieve effective, stealthy, and persistent control, TrojanWorld combines Decision-Reflective Induction to steer trigger-conditioned imagination toward attacker-specified actions using decision feedback, Clean Behavior Anchoring to preserve trigger-free predictive and behavioral fidelity, and Causal Propagation to sustain the induced preference along subsequent trajectories after the trigger disappears. Together, these mechanisms establish an end-to-end attack chain from physical perception through corrupted imagination to malicious action selection. Experiments with the TD-MPC2, DreamerV3, and R2-Dreamer systems across the DeepMind Control, MetaWorld, MyoSuite, and RoboDesk benchmarks show that under trigger activation, TrojanWorld achieves a target-action deviation as low as 0.026 while retaining at least 98.8% of the corresponding clean performance. Even after trigger removal, the compromised agent can remain trapped in the induced behavioral trajectory, continuing to execute attacker-specified actions.

</details>


### [178] [PhysSAE: Mechanistic Interpretability with Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07061)
*Nandita N. Patil,Eshwar R. A.,Gajanan V. Honnavar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysSAE框架，通过在PINN隐藏层上训练稀疏自编码器（SAE）并进行因果干预，证明PINN会形成稀疏且物理结构化的潜在表征，且这些表征可被事后识别和因果查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs虽然将PDE残差嵌入神经网络训练，但其内部表征仍然不透明——我们不知道隐藏层编码了哪些物理特征，也不清楚这些特征是否具有局部因果作用。

Method: PhysSAE框架：在PINN倒数第二层激活上训练过完备稀疏自编码器（SAE），并通过直接因果干预h_cf = h - αz_k d_k评估字典原子，完全绕过SAE解码器。实验涵盖6个PDE族、3种PINN种子和3种SAE种子。

Result: (i) SAE原子与独立定义的物理可观测量对齐（最大Pearson |r|=0.951）；(ii) 顶级对齐原子消融的因果足迹空间集中度比PCA/ICA干预高出1.2-4.2倍；(iii) 顶级对齐原子在结构化物理概念的因果定位上优于随机对照（ESF80优势0.04-0.44）；双原子双侧表征使概念回归R²提升0.05-0.15，而随机配对使其最多下降0.60。

Conclusion: PINN会发展出稀疏、物理结构化的潜在表征，这些表征可以被事后识别并进行因果查询，为可解释的科学机器学习开辟了道路。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) embed PDE residuals into neural network training, but their internal representations remain opaque: it is unknown what physical features their hidden layers encode or whether those features have a localized causal role. We present PhysSAE, a mechanistic interpretability framework that trains overcomplete sparse autoencoders (SAEs) on PINN penultimate-layer activations and evaluates dictionary atoms through direct causal intervention in the original frozen hidden state: $h_{\mathrm{cf}} = h - αz_k d_k$, bypassing the SAE decoder entirely. Across six PDE families, with 3 PINN seeds and 3 SAE seeds each---we show that (i) Our discovered SAE atoms align with independently-defined physical observables (max Pearson $|r|=0.951$, always $\gg$ permutation null), (ii) the causal footprint of top-aligned atom ablation is 1.2--4.2$\times$ more spatially concentrated canonical than PCA or ICA interventions, and (iii) top-aligned atoms outperform matched random controls on causal localization for structured physical concepts (ESF$_{80}$ advantage 0.04-0.44). Two-atom bilateral representations improve concept regression R$^2$ by $ΔR^2\!=\!0.05\text{-}0.15$ over single atoms, while random pairs decrease it by up to 0.60. These results demonstrate that PINNs develop sparse, physically structured latent representations that can be identified and causally interrogated post-hoc, opening a path toward interpretability-aware scientific machine learning.

</details>


### [179] [Trust-But-Verify: Poisoning-Resilient Locally Private Graph Learning Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07063)
*Longzhu He,Li Sun,Hao Peng,Ruijie Wang,Raymond Chi-Wing Wong,Sen Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VERITAS，一种基于"信任但验证"范式的抗投毒攻击的本地差分隐私（LDP）图学习协议。它通过验证列表编码分级信任级别，在用户端联合扰动节点特征和图结构，在服务器端利用双边认证不对称性识别并剪除恶意节点。协议包含四个协同阶段：本地数据扰动、认证驱动的恶意节点剪枝、双去噪效用恢复和鲁棒私有图学习。在四个真实数据集和多种LDP机制/GNN架构下验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的本地差分隐私图学习协议因其开放参与特性而极易遭受数据投毒攻击——攻击者注入精心构造的恶意节点以破坏邻域聚合并降低下游效用。尽管威胁严重，但该场景下的有效防御机制尚未被充分探索。

Method: VERITAS基于"信任但验证"范式，引入验证列表编码分级同伴信任级别。其四阶段协同流程包括：(1)本地数据扰动（在用户端联合私有化节点特征和图结构），(2)认证驱动的恶意节点剪枝（服务器端利用双边认证不对称性识别恶意节点），(3)通过双去噪恢复效用，以及(4)鲁棒私有图学习。

Result: 在四个真实世界基准数据集、多种LDP机制和GNN架构上的大量实验表明，VERITAS能有效防御数据投毒攻击，并在严格隐私保证下显著提升下游图学习效用。

Conclusion: VERITAS为本地差分隐私图学习场景下的数据投毒攻击提供了首个系统性的防御方案，验证了"信任但验证"范式在该领域中的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Built upon local differential privacy (LDP), locally private graph learning protocols have emerged as an important paradigm for decentralized graph learning, balancing privacy protection and learning utility. Under such protocols, each user locally perturbs their node features and adjacency information before transmission, ensuring formal privacy guarantees without original data leaving the device. However, the inherently open participation nature renders these protocols critically vulnerable to data poisoning attacks, where adversaries inject carefully crafted malicious nodes to corrupt neighborhood aggregation and degrade downstream utility. Despite the severity of this threat, effective defenses in this setting remain largely unexplored. In this paper, we propose VERITAS, a poisoning-resilient locally private graph learning protocol built on a trust-but-verify paradigm. By introducing a verification list encoding graded peer trust levels, VERITAS jointly privatizes node features and graph structure on the user side, while exploiting bilateral attestation asymmetry on the server side to identify and prune malicious nodes. Concretely, VERITAS comprises four synergistic stages: (1) local data perturbation, (2) attestation-driven malicious node pruning, (3) utility restoration via dual denoising, and (4) robust private graph learning. Extensive experiments on four real-world benchmark datasets across multiple LDP mechanisms and GNN architectures demonstrate that VERITAS effectively defends against data poisoning attacks and significantly improves downstream graph learning utility under rigorous privacy guarantees.

</details>


### [180] [Conditioned Initialization for Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07086)
*Hemanth Saratchandran,Simon Lucey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"条件初始化"（conditioned initialization）的注意力权重初始化方案，通过改善注意力层的谱属性来优化Transformer的训练动态。理论和实验均表明该方法能加速收敛并提升泛化性能，且易于集成到现有架构中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer架构中查询、键和值矩阵的初始化方式长期被忽视，现有实践依赖随机初始化或模仿性初始化，缺乏理论指导。作者认为初始化会引入优化偏差，从根本上塑造训练动态，因此需要一种原则性的初始化方案来改善注意力层的条件数。

Method: 提出条件初始化（conditioned initialization），一种基于原则的注意力权重初始化方法，旨在改善注意力层的谱性质（spectral properties），特别是从理论上证明该方法能降低注意力Jacobian的条件数，从而实现更稳定的优化。

Result: 经验实验表明，条件初始化在多种应用中都能加速收敛并改善泛化性能，且该方法简单易用，可无缝集成到广泛的Transformer架构中。

Conclusion: 条件化（conditioning）是提升Transformer性能中一个关键但尚未被充分探索的方向，本文的工作揭示了初始化在训练动态中的重要作用，为该领域提供了新的研究思路和实用方案。

Abstract: Transformers are a dominant architecture in modern machine learning, powering applications across vision, language, and beyond. At the core of their success lies the attention layer, where the query, key, and value matrices determine how token dependencies are captured. While considerable work has focused on scaling and optimizing Transformers, comparatively little attention has been paid to how the weights of the queries, keys and values are initialized. Common practice relies on random initialization or alternatives such as mimetic initialization, which imitates weight patterns from converged models, and weight selection, which transfers weights from a teacher model. In this paper, we argue that initialization can introduce an optimization bias that fundamentally shapes training dynamics. We propose conditioned initialization, a principled scheme that initializes attention weights to improve the spectral properties of the attention layer. Theoretically, we show that conditioned initialization can potentially reduce the condition number of the attention Jacobian, leading to more stable optimization. Empirically, it accelerates convergence and improves generalization across diverse applications, highlighting conditioning as a critical yet underexplored area for advancing Transformer performance. Importantly, conditioned initialization is simple to apply and integrates seamlessly into a wide range of Transformer architectures.

</details>


### [181] [Temporal Heterogeneous Graph Transformer for Credit Card Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07100)
*Qinwen Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出THGT-FD，一种用于信用卡欺诈检测的时间异构图表征Transformer模型。每个交易由一个交易token和六种关系token表示，并结合Time2Vec时间编码，通过Transformer学习token间交互来输出欺诈概率。在IEEE-CIS数据集15万条样本上，AUC-ROC为0.8536。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信用卡欺诈检测通常仅依赖表格特征，而重复出现的属性（如商户、设备等）能提供有价值的关系信号，现有方法未充分利用这些异构关系信息进行欺诈风险建模。

Method: 每个交易被表示为一个交易token加六种关系token，并融入Time2Vec时间编码；使用Transformer在同一交易内部学习各token间的交互，最终输出欺诈概率。

Result: 在IEEE-CIS欺诈检测数据集上（15万条交易，按TransactionDT时间划分），THGT-FD在测试集上取得AUC-ROC 0.8536、平均精度0.4164、Recall@5%为0.4708；而类权重直方图梯度提升基线AUC-ROC为0.8722。

Conclusion: 关系token提供了对欺诈风险排序有用的信息，但当前模型尚未整合实体级历史聚合，性能仍低于梯度提升基线，说明仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Credit card fraud detection typically relies on tabular features, while repeated attributes can also provide useful relational signals. This paper proposes THGT-FD, a Temporal Heterogeneous Graph Transformer for Fraud Detection. Each transaction is represented using one transaction token and six types of relation tokens and incorporates Time2Vec encoding into the transaction representation. A Transformer learns the interactions among these tokens within each individual transaction and then outputs a fraud probability. Experiments were conducted on 150,000 transactions sampled from the IEEE-CIS Fraud Detection dataset and chronologically partitioned according to TransactionDT. On the test set, THGT-FD achieved an AUC-ROC of 0.8536, an average precision of 0.4164, and a Recall@5% of 0.4708. The class-weighted histogram-based gradient-boosting baseline achieved an AUC-ROC of 0.8722. The results indicate that relation tokens provide useful information for fraud-risk ranking, although the current model does not yet incorporate entity-level historical aggregation.

</details>


### [182] [Fine-grained Distributed Backdoor Attacks in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07147)
*Jian Wang,Hong Shen,Wei Ke,Xue Hua Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种细粒度分布式后门攻击框架（FDBA），通过动态触发器生成和嵌入向量优化，在联邦学习中用更少的投毒样本实现更高的攻击成功率，并能有效规避主流防御机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习面临分布式后门攻击的严重威胁，相比集中式攻击更隐蔽但需要更多投毒样本；固定触发模式容易被鲁棒聚合算法检测，导致攻击暴露风险增加。

Method: 提出FDBA框架：(1) 基于Canny算法提取图像边缘特征并注入Laplacian噪声的动态触发器生成方法，结合RGB通道分解实现分布式触发器的隐蔽自适应；(2) 嵌入向量对比学习策略，迫使投毒样本在特征空间中接近目标类中心以增强攻击效果。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，FDBA相比DBA将投毒比例需求降低37.4%至48.4%；在Non-IID场景下，FDBA在极端异构条件下保持了IID攻击性能的84.7%，而DBA仅降至73.5%；该框架能够成功绕过主流防御机制。

Conclusion: 该研究为联邦学习安全提供了新见解，强调细粒度分布式攻击带来的潜在威胁和防御挑战。

Abstract: Federated learning, as a privacy-preserving distributed machine learning paradigm, faces significant threats from backdoor attacks. Compared to centralized attacks, distributed backdoor attacks are more harmful but require more poisoned samples to compensate for the loss of trigger strength due to decomposition. Fixed trigger patterns are also easily detected by robust aggregation algorithms, increasing the risk of attack exposure. To address these challenges, we propose a fine-grained distributed backdoor attack framework (FDBA). This framework uses dynamic trigger generation and embedding vector optimization to perform attacks with fewer poisoned samples. First, we design a dynamic trigger generation method based on image edge structures using the Canny algorithm to extract edge features, which are then injected with Laplacian noise. RGB channel decomposition is applied for covert adaptation of the distributed trigger, reducing detection chances. Second, we introduce an embedding vector contrastive learning strategy that forces poisoned samples to approach the target class center in the feature space, enhancing attack effectiveness. On CIFAR-10, piecewise-linear estimates for target ASRs between 70\% and 90\% show that FDBA reduces the required poisoning ratio by 37.4\%--48.4\% compared with DBA. In non-independent and identically distributed (Non-IID) scenarios, FDBA retains 84.7\% of its IID attack performance under extreme heterogeneity, whereas DBA drops to 73.5\%, and the framework successfully bypasses mainstream defense mechanisms.
  This study offers new insights into federated learning security and emphasizes the potential threats and defense challenges posed by fine-grained distributed attacks.

</details>


### [183] [Stable-MM-R1: Anchoring Multimodal Reasoning Dynamics via Entropy-Guided Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07148)
*Yimeng Ye,Shuang Chen,Wenxuan Huang,Manyuan Zhang,Kaituo Feng,Zhangquan Chen,Jiayu Chen,Yucheng Zhou,Yicheng Xiao,Zhiyuan Feng,Tianyu Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个数据中心的稳健RL框架来稳定大语言模型的强化学习训练，包括两个核心组件：PAQM（Potential-Aware Query Mining）用于动态筛选高潜力样本，以及HSR（Hybrid Stratified Replay）通过路径熵分层回放来构建高对比度优化组，从而缓解熵坍缩并提升学习信号利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM强化学习流程受困于训练不稳定和熵快速坍缩问题，这些问题源于标准采样过程中的"Rollout Silencing"和低质量梯度信号，亟需稳健的解决方案。

Method: 提出数据中心的框架：1) PAQM动态过滤数据，聚焦"蒸馏区"（高能力激发潜力的样本）；2) HSR基于路径熵（rollout级置信代理）和结果奖励对rollout分层，重构批次，在当前策略中重用"稳定锚点"和"硬负样本"构建高对比度优化组，每步后清空缓冲区。

Result: 在复杂推理任务上超越了强基线方法，在有限计算资源下实现了更稳定和高效的RL微调。

Conclusion: 该方法为稳定高效的RL微调提供了原则性解决方案，有效缓解熵坍缩并改善了学习信号的利用。

Abstract: While Reinforcement Learning (RL) effectively incentivizes reasoning in Large Language Models, current pipelines are hindered by training instability and rapid entropy collapse. These limitations often stem from "Rollout Silencing" and low-quality gradient signals in standard sampling procedures. In this work, we propose a robust, data-centric framework to stabilize RL training. We first introduce Potential-Aware Query Mining (PAQM), which filters data dynamically to focus on the "Distillation Zone"---samples with high potential for capability elicitation. Furthermore, we present Hybrid Stratified Replay (HSR), a novel mechanism that restructures batches by stratifying rollouts based on Path Entropy, a rollout-level confidence proxy, and outcome reward. Within each optimization step, HSR reuses current-policy "Stability Anchors" and "Hard Negatives" to construct high-contrast optimization groups, then clears its buffers before the next step. This approach mitigates entropy collapse while improving the utilization of learning signals under limited compute. Our method outperforms strong baselines on complex reasoning tasks, offering a principled solution for stable and efficient RL fine-tuning.

</details>


### [184] [The Oversight Gap: What LLM Safety Monitors Miss, and Why It Is Not Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07162)
*Xin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将2-安全超属性的二元判定不可能性转化为可度量的检测前沿，用总变差距离界定单轨迹监控器的平衡准确率上界，定义了"监督缺口"。实验表明LLM监控器在信息与程序两维度均存在显著短fall，模拟第二运行不足以替代真实执行，且基准构造的有效性需机械化证明而非依赖模型审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨租户非干扰、沙袋防护、评估感知等安全属性是2-安全超属性，需要两条执行轨迹才能判定，单轨迹无法决定，构成二元的不可判定性。作者希望将这种二元不可判定性转化为可测量的等级化检测前沿，从而量化监督缺口并理解LLM监控器性能不足的根源。

Method: 用总变差距离TV(P0,P1)推导单轨迹监控器平衡准确率的紧致上界1/2+1/2·TV，构造具有闭式TV的泄漏族，对九个LLM监控器进行系统评测；通过2×2因子实验（想象第二运行vs执行第二运行、有存储预言机vs有比较程序）分离信息与程序的作用；在非确定性下分析k-重放曲线与投影前沿，并用符号检验审计LLM法官的基准认证。

Result: 九个LLM监控器在TV=0时最优，但随TV增大捕获信号有限；TV=1时20行成员检查可达100%，LLM平均仅60.9%。命名检查目标可闭合61%的缺口。想象的第二运行仅50.4%（接近随机），真实第二运行达90.0%；有存储预言机无比较程序仅68.2%。窄投影漏掉98.6%离通道泄漏，宽投影误报75.7%干净流量。两位前沿LLM法官认证了有缺陷的基准，符号检验发现方向偏差(p=2.7×10^-5)使三项发现失效。

Conclusion: 信息与程序各自必要且均非单纯能力问题；超属性基准的构造有效性应通过机械化证明而非模型审计来保证。监督缺口不是二元可判定性问题，而是可度量的、可分解的检测前沿问题。

Abstract: Several properties safety monitors are asked to certify, among them cross-tenant noninterference, sandbagging and evaluation awareness, are 2-safety hyperproperties, witnessed only by two executions. The standard consequence is a binary impossibility: one trace cannot decide them. We replace the binary with a measurement. A tight bound puts the balanced accuracy of any single-trace monitor at $\tfrac12+\tfrac12\,TV(P_0,P_1)$, turning undecidability into a graded detectability frontier and defining an oversight gap: a monitor's shortfall below it. On a leak family with closed-form $TV$, nine LLM monitors are optimal at $TV=0$ but capture little signal as $TV$ grows; at $TV=1$, where a 20-line membership check scores $100\%$, they average $60.9\%$. That shortfall is mostly not capability: naming what to check closes $61\%$ of it while leaving the $TV=0$ control at chance. The same split runs through a $2{\times}2$ factorial: an imagined second run leaves monitors at chance ($50.4\%$) while the same rule on an executed second run reaches $90.0\%$, and a stored oracle without a comparison procedure yields only $68.2\%$. Information and procedure are each necessary and neither is capability. Under nondeterminism, replay tracks a closed-form $k$-replay curve only under the right projection, and a projection frontier shows the resulting dilemma is forced: narrow misses $98.6\%$ of off-channel leaks, broad flags $75.7\%$ of clean traffic, and attainable accuracy decays like $1/(qm)$ in the benign-variation rate and the channel count. Finally, two frontier LLM judges certified an earlier version of our own benchmark as sound while a sign test found a directional bias ($p=2.7\times10^{-5}$) that invalidated three of our findings. Construction validity for hyperproperty benchmarks should be proved mechanically, not audited by models.

</details>


### [185] [FedRAW: Preserving Rare-Label Influence in Asynchronous Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07192)
*Prashant Bajpai,Divya Saxena,Philippe Lalanda,German Vega*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedRAW，一种纯服务器端的异步联邦学习聚合方法，通过客户端级更新去重和稀有标签感知加权来解决异构参与下的"静默稀有性失败"问题，在不改变本地训练和通信协议的情况下提升稀有标签客户的模型影响力与准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步联邦学习虽提高了可扩展性，但其基于到达驱动的聚合方式在异构参与下会扭曲表示学习。作者识别出"静默稀有性失败"这一隐藏故障模式：持有稀有标签的客户端对全局模型的贡献过弱，而整体准确率表面看似正常，该问题源于参与偏差和聚合偏差的双重耦合效应。

Method: FedRAW是纯服务器端的聚合方法，包含两个核心组件：（1）客户端级更新去重，防止高频到达的客户端反复主导更新缓冲区；（2）稀有标签感知加权，增加携带低覆盖标签客户端的更新影响力。该方法无需改变本地训练、客户端目标或通信协议。

Result: 在EMNIST Balanced、CIFAR-10、HAM10000和ISIC-2019四个数据集上，FedRAW在保持可比全局精度的同时提升了稀有标签的准确率，相比统一聚合显著增加了稀有标签客户端的影响力，并且仅增加可忽略的服务器端计算开销，同时保持了收敛性。

Conclusion: 作者正式化了静默稀有性失败的参与偏差和聚合偏差机制，证明了FedRAW在提高稀有标签客户端影响力的同时能维持收敛性，为异步联邦学习中的表示学习公平性问题提供了可行的服务器端解决方案。

Abstract: Asynchronous federated learning improves scalability by updating the global model from a server-side buffer of client updates as they arrive, rather than waiting for all selected clients to finish. While efficient, this arrival-driven aggregation can silently distort representation learning under heterogeneous participation. We identify silent rarity failure, a hidden failure mode in which clients holding rare labels contribute too weakly to the global model even though its overall accuracy appears largely unaffected. This failure arises from two coupled effects: rare-label clients may submit updates less frequently when they are slower or less available, creating participation bias; and once their updates enter the buffer, standard asynchronous aggregation assigns them no compensating influence, creating aggregation bias. We propose FedRAW, a fully server-side aggregation method that preserves rare-label influence without changing local training, client objectives, or communication protocols. FedRAW combines client-level update deduplication, which prevents frequently arriving clients from repeatedly dominating the update buffer, with rare-label-aware weighting, which increases the influence of clients carrying low-coverage labels. We formalize silent rarity failure through participation and aggregation bias, and show that FedRAW increases rare-label client influence over uniform aggregation while preserving convergence. Across EMNIST Balanced, CIFAR-10, HAM10000, and ISIC-2019, FedRAW improves rarelabel accuracy while preserving comparable global accuracy and adding negligible server-side computation.

</details>


### [186] [REFINE: Trajectory Representation Learning via Closed-Loop Transcription -- Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07206)
*Sean Bin Yang,Ying Sun,Jilin Hu,Zongyi Xu,Kristian Torp,Hua Lu,Bin Yang,Christian S. Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出REFINE框架，融合路网感知的生成式重建与反馈驱动的对比学习，构建闭环轨迹表示学习框架，在四个真实数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹表示学习的自监督方法多采用开环范式，依赖固定数据增强或随机掩码且缺乏反馈机制，导致泛化能力和扩展性受限。

Method: 借鉴反馈控制理论，将路网感知的生成式重建与反馈驱动的对比学习紧密耦合，无需人工设计增强视图即可同时捕获细粒度局部移动语义与全局时空依赖；并提供控制理论分析建立闭环优化的收敛保证。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上的大量实验表明，REFINE在多个下游任务上持续优于现有最先进方法，同时保持计算高效性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: 闭环转录精炼范式为轨迹表示学习提供了一条简洁高效的新路径，兼具理论保障与实证优势。

Abstract: Trajectory representation learning underpins a wide range of trajectory analytics tasks; however, most existing self-supervised approaches, whether discriminative or generative, adopt an open-loop paradigm, relying on fixed data augmentations or random masking without feedback, which limits their ability to generalize and scale.
  We propose REFINE, a simple yet effective Representation lEarning Framework vIa closed-loop traNscription rEfinement for trajectory data. Drawing upon feedback control theory, REFINE tightly couples road-network-aware generative reconstruction with feedback-driven contrastive learning, enabling the model to capture fine-grained local movement semantics and global spatio-temporal dependencies without manually designed augmentation views. We further provide a control-theoretic analysis that establishes convergence guarantees for the proposed closed-loop optimization.
  Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets demonstrate that REFINE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across multiple downstream tasks while remaining computationally efficient and scalable.
  This paper is an extended version of REFINE: Trajectory Representation Learning via Closed-Loop Transcription, to appear in KDD 2026.

</details>


### [187] [Kolmogorov--Arnold stability for discontinuous functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07240)
*Sviatoslav V. Dzhenzher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kolmogorov-Arnold表示定理（KART）在对抗性重参数化下对多元不连续和无界函数的稳定性，为KAN等深度学习架构的结构鲁棒性提供了严格的数学基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习架构（如KAN）依赖KART的理论保证，但该定理在对抗性重参数化条件下、面对不连续和无界函数时是否仍然成立，尚缺乏严格的数学分析。

Method: 通过对抗性重参数化方式扰动隐藏层，系统地分析KART对多元不连续函数和无界函数的表示稳定性，从数学上建立结构鲁棒性的理论框架。

Result: KART在对抗性重参数化下对不连续和无界函数的稳定性得到了严格的数学刻画，为KAN等架构在对抗性设定下的可靠性提供了理论支撑。

Conclusion: 该研究为现代深度学习架构（尤其是KAN）在对抗性重参数化条件下的结构鲁棒性提供了严格的数学基础，拓展了KART理论的适用边界。

Abstract: Here we investigate the stability of the Kolmogorov--Arnold representation theorem (KART) under adversarial reparameterisations of the hidden layer for multivariate discontinuous and unbounded functions. Our results provide a rigorous mathematical foundation for the structural robustness of modern deep learning architectures, such as Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (KANs), under adversarial configurations.

</details>


### [188] [Dense Structural Compression of Transformers via Gauge-Correct Channel Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07264)
*Jed A. Duersch,Naïm Es-Sebbani,Nathanaël Haas,Zied Bouraoui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GaugeLasso方法，通过在训练过程中施加通道级对称群组套索惩罚，在保持稠密张量结构的同时将整条通道驱动至零并物理移除，从而在推理计算预算下最大化模型效用。该方法解决了朴素范数惩罚因规范自由度而失稳的问题，在多项长除法上实现148-255倍压缩，在字符级语言建模和掩码自编码任务上也优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer推理的能耗和碳排放主要由稠密矩阵乘法的FMA运算和内存流量主导。现有方法要么通过结构化剪枝破坏稠密张量降低GPU吞吐，要么缺乏从第一性原理出发的系统性压缩方法。作者希望设计一种机制，在训练过程中自适应压缩模型结构，同时保持张量稠密性和网络功能，最大化"每单位计算量的推理效用"。

Method: 作者从第一性原理出发，在训练中通过通道惩罚将整条张量切片驱零以实现物理移除。关键发现是直接惩罚作用于每个通道的算子分量范数会因规范自由度而失稳。为此提出GaugeLasso：添加对称群组套索惩罚项，在网络收敛到规范平衡时恢复为乘积范数的单调函数。通过均衡分析实现逐通道校准，正确抑制推理效用/单位计算量表现不佳的通道。压缩过程随训练持续施加自适应压力，不依赖事后剪枝。

Result: 在F_31上的多项式长除法任务中，计算量压缩148至255倍且保持完美精度；在字符级语言建模中，压缩模型在相同FMA下超越手工设计基线；在掩码自编码中，压缩试验揭示了过分配和饱和的轴，指导了更好的二次设计。压缩随训练单调加速训练过程。事后剪枝无法达到同样结构，证明持续压力对发现高效模型至关重要。重训发现的架构在统计任务上恢复基线质量，但在精确算法任务上失败。

Conclusion: GaugeLasso提供了一种从训练过程中自然涌现高效结构的方法论，能在保持稠密张量和GPU友好的前提下显著降低推理计算量。持续的自适应压缩压力是发现高效架构的关键，而非事后剪枝可替代。然而，对精确算法任务上发现的结构进行重训会丢失精度，暗示这类任务中学习到的结构携带了额外信息，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Inference energy per token drives the cost and carbon footprint of deployed transformers. It is dominated by dense matrix products that incur fused multiply-accumulate (FMA) operations and memory traffic. To reduce these computations while retaining dense tensors for high GPU throughput, we develop a methodology from first principles to adapt structural complexity during training to maximize inference utility per unit compute. Channel penalties drive entire tensor slices to zero to enable physical removal while preserving density and the network function.
  The natural approach, penalizing the norm of operator components acting through each channel, is provably destabilized by gauge freedom. We resolve this pathology with GaugeLasso: additive symmetric group-lasso penalties that recover a monotone function of product-norms when the network converges to gauge balance. Our equilibrium analysis enables per-channel calibration to correctly suppress slices that under-perform in inference utility per unit compute.
  Under adaptive pressure, the network reorganizes into depth-dependent structural profiles that can be far smaller than the architecture required to learn the task. On polynomial long division over $\mathbb{F}_{31}$, compute compresses from 148 to 255 times with perfect accuracy. On character-level language modeling, compressed models outperform the hand-designed baseline at equal FMA. On masked autoencoding, a compression trial exposes which axes were over-provisioned and which saturated, guiding a better second design. Compaction also accelerates training monotonically as the model progresses. Post-hoc pruning with the same utility ranking cannot reach these structures, showing that sustained pressure is central to discovery of efficient models. Retraining a discovered architecture recovers baseline quality on our statistical tasks, but fails on our exact algorithmic task.

</details>


### [189] [Constitutive State-Space Modeling of Path-Dependent Plasticity: A Resolution-Consistent and Parallelizable Computational Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07294)
*Rui Barreira,Taylan Soydan,Francesco Scipione,Miguel A. Bessa,Dirk Mohr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向路径依赖塑性本构建模的"本构状态空间"（CSS）模型，将结构化状态空间动力学重构为增量式本构算子，将应变增量分解为大小和方向分别处理，从而保证零增量下的平稳性、降低对应变路径离散化分辨率的敏感性，并保留并行扫描的高效训练特性。CSS在多种多轴路径依赖材料模型上匹配或超越现有MSC架构的精度，且在粗分辨率下误差远低于MSC，训练更快、所需数据更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的路径依赖塑性本构模型多采用非线性循环神经网络，其顺序状态演化限制了并行训练能力，且预测结果可能依赖于应变路径的离散化分辨率，这限制了模型的实用性和可扩展性。

Method: 引入CSS模型，将结构化状态空间动力学（基于S5架构）重构为增量本构算子：应变增量分解为大小（magnitude）和方向（direction）两部分，方向驱动潜在状态空间系统，而增量大小进入连续线性递推的零阶保持（ZOH）离散化中。这种力学定制的构造保证了零应变增量下的平稳性，降低对路径分辨率的敏感性，同时保留S5的并行扫描结构用于高效训练。

Result: CSS在四种多轴路径依赖材料模型（各向同性J2塑性、压力敏感泡沫塑性、组合各向同性-运动硬化）上匹配或超越MSC的预测精度；在塑性不可压缩材料上验证损失低一个数量级。CSS在粗分辨率评估时误差远低于MSC，训练速度显著更快，所需应变-应力数据对更少，且学习到的潜状态维度与物理本构模型的固有维度一致。

Conclusion: 力学定制化的结构化状态空间动力学为高效且对离散化鲁棒的数据驱动本构建模提供了一个有前景的计算框架，同时兼具物理可解释性和计算效率。

Abstract: Data-driven constitutive models for path-dependent plasticity are commonly formulated using nonlinear recurrent neural networks, whose sequential state evolution limits parallel training and whose predictions may depend on the discretization of the applied strain path. We introduce a Constitutive State Space (CSS) model that reformulates structured state-space dynamics as an incremental constitutive operator. The strain increment is decomposed into magnitude and direction: the loading direction drives the latent state-space system, while the increment magnitude enters the zero-order-hold discretization of its continuous-time linear recurrence. This mechanics-tailored construction guarantees stationarity under zero increments, strongly reduces sensitivity to strain-path resolution, and retains the parallel-scan structure of S5 for efficient training on long constitutive histories. The CSS and Minimal State Cell (MSC) architectures are compared for four multiaxial path-dependent material models including isotropic J2 plasticity, pressure-sensitive foam plasticity, and combined isotropic-kinematic hardening. CSS matches or exceeds the prediction accuracy of the MSC, including one order of magnitude lower validation losses for the plastically incompressible materials. Importantly, CSS maintains low errors across large changes in strain-path discretization, whereas the MSC error increases substantially when evaluated at coarser resolutions than used for training. CSS trains substantially faster and requires fewer strain-stress pairs to attain comparable or better accuracy. Analysis of the learned state further reveals latent structure consistent with the dimensionality of the underlying physical constitutive models. These results establish mechanics-tailored structured state-space dynamics as a computational framework for efficient and discretization-robust data-driven constitutive modeling.

</details>


### [190] [PCFlow: Physics-Conditioned Flow Matching for GPR B-Scan Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07300)
*Zhijie Shen,Chenchen Fu,Xuanhao Chang,Hongtao Bai,Lili He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PCFlow是一种物理条件流匹配框架，用于快速合成探地雷达B扫描图像。它通过从电磁仿真参数化模型中构建Maxwell物理条件场，引导VAE潜空间中的条件流匹配生成物理可行且视觉逼真的雷达图像，在gprMax埋地管道数据集上验证了生成效果的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探地雷达B扫描图像合成对数据增强、算法验证和仿真加速非常重要，但现有生成模型通常只注重视觉外观，缺乏对响应几何的物理控制，难以同时保证视觉真实感和物理一致性。

Method: 提出PCFlow框架，核心是构建基于Maxwell方程的密集物理条件场，该条件场由电磁仿真的参数化物理模型推导而来，涵盖材料属性、目标几何、传播线索和响应域先验。该条件场提供物理场景参数与雷达响应几何之间的可解释接口，并在VAE潜空间中引导条件流匹配向物理可行的生成路径演进。

Result: 在基于gprMax的埋地管道数据集上，通过分布内和分布外测试，PCFlow生成的图像具有更准确的响应几何和高视觉保真度，验证了其在可控且物理保真的雷达图像合成中的有效性。

Conclusion: PCFlow成功实现了兼具视觉真实感与物理一致性的探地雷达B扫描图像合成，为可控且物理可信的雷达图像生成提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: Ground-penetrating radar (GPR) B-scan image synthesis is important for data augmentation, algorithm validation, and simulation acceleration, yet generating radargrams with both visual realism and physical consistency remains challenging. Existing learning-based generative models often emphasize visual appearance but provide limited control over response geometry. In this paper, we propose PCFlow, a physics-conditioned flow matching framework for fast GPR B-scan image synthesis. The core of PCFlow is a Maxwell-informed dense physical condition field constructed from the parameterized physical model used for electromagnetic simulation, including material properties, target geometry, propagation cues, and response-domain priors. This condition field provides an interpretable interface between physical scene parameters and radar response geometry, and guides conditional flow matching in the VAE latent space toward physically feasible generation paths. We evaluate PCFlow on a gprMax-based buried-pipeline dataset with both in-distribution and out-of-distribution test cases. Experimental results show that PCFlow generates images with more accurate response geometry and high visual fidelity, demonstrating its effectiveness for controllable and physically faithful radar image synthesis.

</details>


### [191] [Long-Horizon Language Model Reinforcement Learning via Progressive Point Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07303)
*Preston Fu,Kevin Frans,Oleh Rybkin,Sergey Levine,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"渐进式点匹配"的无偏稠密奖励公式，通过在片段级别奖励部分进展，使语言模型强化学习在长时程任务上实现指数级更高效的扩展，即使面对极端困难的数学推理问题也能取得进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的语言模型训练严重依赖稀疏结果奖励，但在需要更长、更复杂轨迹的任务中学习缓慢。先前尝试奖励部分进展的工作往往有偏且收敛到次优策略，因此需要一种无偏且高效的稠密奖励方案。

Method: 提出"渐进式点匹配"（progressive point matching）方法，这是一个简单且无偏的稠密奖励公式，在片段级别奖励部分进展，每个任务仅需单条参考轨迹即可实际实例化。

Result: 理论上和通过合成环境实验证明该方法在长时程任务上具有指数级的效率扩展。在极端困难的数学推理问题上，稀疏结果奖励无法取得任何进展，而片段级奖励在更大的测试时token预算下通过成功率或pass@k指标显示出改进。

Conclusion: 片段级别的无偏稠密奖励（渐进式点匹配）是一种在长时程任务中远超稀疏结果奖励的有效训练策略，即使对于最困难的推理任务也能实现实质性改进。

Abstract: Current paradigms for training language models via reinforcement learning rely heavily on sparse outcome rewards. However, as we pursue tasks that require longer and more complicated trajectories, such strategies result in slow learning. Prior work has attempted to address this problem by rewarding partial progress; however, naive formulations are often biased and converge to suboptimal policies. We show that a simple and unbiased dense reward formulation, which we term progressive point matching, scales exponentially more efficiently to long-horizon tasks by rewarding partial progress on a segment level, both theoretically and empirically via synthetic environments. We then show how progressive point matching can be practically instantiated using a single reference trajectory per task. On extremely hard math reasoning problems, sparse outcome rewards cannot make any progress, whereas segment-level rewards enable improvements at larger test-time token budgets when measured by success rate or pass@k.

</details>


### [192] [Inferring Urban Mobility Interactions from Aggregated Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07349)
*Yi Wang,Jing Li,Jinliang Deng,Zhenghong Wang,Yizhi Zhang,Fan Zhang,Ivor W. Tsang,Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于不确定性感知的物理信息框架，仅利用城市已有的区域级聚合计数数据即可重建起点-终点(OD)矩阵的时间演化，无需追踪个体轨迹。在美国和中国12个移动数据集中，该方法达到了与传统输入历史OD矩阵模型相当的精度，为实时城市智能提供了更可部署且隐私风险更低的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时城市治理需要了解人群在不同地点之间的方向性流动，但传统方法依赖追踪个体空间位置，成本高昂且个体轨迹具有高唯一性、容易被重新识别，存在严重的隐私风险。

Method: 采用不确定性感知的物理信息框架，仅从区域级聚合计数推断未来OD流量；结合概率建模修正稀疏高价值走廊的系统性低估；架构上遵循交通规划中"先生成后分配"的逻辑，先保留位置级空间异质性再重建两两交互关系。

Result: 在美国和中国12个城市移动数据集上验证，仅从区域级计数推断的OD流精度与以历史OD矩阵为输入的模型相当；概率建模提供了校准的预测，与观测流量一致。

Conclusion: 城市已收集的聚合数据蕴含足够信息重建方向性流动结构，训练后可仅依赖聚合观测进行推断，减少对个体持续追踪的依赖，为实时城市智能提供了更可部署、隐私暴露更小的基础。

Abstract: Real-time urban governance depends not only on knowing where people are, but on how they move between places, directional flows that could be conventionally resolved by tracking individuals through space, i.e., expensive to sustain and built on traces that are highly unique and readily re-identifiable. Here we show that this directional structure need not be observed to be known: aggregated counts which cities already collect retain enough information to reconstruct the temporal evolution of origin-destination (OD) matrix. Using an uncertainty-aware physics-informed framework, we infer future OD flows from area-level counts alone across twelve mobility datasets from cities in the United States and China, reaching accuracy comparable to models that take historical OD matrices as input. Probabilistic modeling corrects the systematic underestimation of sparse, high-value corridors and yields calibrated predictions consistent with observed flows. Architectures that respect the generation-before-assignment logic of transport planning recover interactions more faithfully, indicating that location-level spatial heterogeneity should be preserved before pairwise interactions are reconstructed. Because inference requires only aggregated observations after training, recovering interactions this way reduces reliance on continuous individual-level tracking, pointing toward a more deployable and less exposure-heavy basis for real-time urban intelligence.

</details>


### [193] [Think Wider: Mitigating Latent Rank Collapse in Implicit Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07406)
*Yuwen Hao,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WIDER，一种针对隐式思维链（Implicit CoT）的轻量级谱正则化方法，用于缓解隐式推理中潜状态发生"潜在秩坍塌"（latent rank collapse）的问题，使潜状态在表示子空间内更分散，从而提升推理质量与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式思维链虽能提升推理能力，但会带来解码长度、延迟和上下文成本的增加；隐式CoT通过连续潜状态进行中间推理以提升效率，但其潜推理轨迹不稳定——相邻潜状态趋于相似并朝共享主导方向坍塌，导致推理轨迹多样性下降，影响推理效果。

Method: 提出WIDER谱正则化器：训练时估计每条潜轨迹的共享方向，并惩罚潜状态向该方向的投影，促使潜状态在表示子空间中铺展得更宽。该方法即插即用，不改变骨干模型、潜调度及推理时解码过程；同时将坍塌形式化为隐式推理中的几何瓶颈问题，将缓解措施视为训练时正则化问题而非推理时解码改动。

Result: 大量实验表明WIDER优于匹配的隐式CoT基线；机制分析显示其提升了潜状态的有效秩、降低了主导方向能量并减少了潜步骤间的冗余。

Conclusion: 潜子空间的利用程度是影响连续推理效率的重要因素，WIDER从几何视角为分析和改进隐式CoT提供了新思路。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning improves the reasoning ability of large language models by introducing intermediate computation, but explicit rationales increase decoding length, latency, and context cost. Implicit CoT offers a more efficient alternative by moving intermediate reasoning into continuous latent states. However, latent reasoning can be unstable: successive latent states may become overly similar and collapse toward a shared dominant direction, reducing the diversity of the reasoning trajectory. In this work, we identify $\textit{latent rank collapse}$ and propose $\textbf{WIDER}$, a lightweight spectral regularizer for implicit CoT. During training, WIDER estimates the shared direction of each latent trajectory and penalizes projections onto this direction, encouraging latent states to span a broader representational subspace. The method is plug-and-play and leaves the backbone model, latent schedule, and inference-time decoding procedure unchanged. We further formulate this collapse as a geometric bottleneck in implicit reasoning, casting its mitigation as a training-time regularization problem rather than an inference-time decoding change. Extensive experiments show that WIDER improves matched implicit CoT baselines, while mechanistic analyses reveal higher effective rank, lower dominant-direction energy, and reduced redundancy among latent steps. These results highlight latent subspace utilization as an important factor for efficient continuous reasoning, providing a geometric perspective for analyzing and improving implicit CoT. Code is available at https://github.com/whitesweater/WIDER.

</details>


### [194] [Impact of canny edge detection preprocessing on performance of machine learning models for Parkinson's disease classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07408)
*Sameer Bhat,Piotr Szczuko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究使用8种机器学习模型（LR、DT、RF、GB、XGBoost、NB、SVM、AdB）对帕金森病健康与高风险个体进行分类，考察了4个不同数据集（原始、Canny+Hessian预处理、增强、增强+预处理）对模型性能的影响。结果显示：Canny边缘检测结合Hessian滤波预处理会降低大多数模型性能；RF在多个数据集上保持61KB的稳定内存占用；KNN和SVM在增强数据集上内存和推理时间显著增加；LR、DT、NB表现出稳定的内存占用和快速预测时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森病的早期诊断至关重要，但机器学习模型在此任务中的性能受数据集规模和数据预处理技术的影响尚未被系统研究，作者希望通过对比不同数据集的构建方式，找出最优的模型和预处理策略。

Method: 从原始数据集创建4个数据集：DS_0（正常）、DS_1（Canny边缘检测+Hessian滤波）、DS_2（增强DS_0）、DS_3（增强DS_1）。评估8种ML模型在所有数据集上的预测准确率、模型大小和预测延迟。

Result: 较大数据集导致更高的内存占用和预测延迟；Canny+Hessian预处理降低了多数模型性能；RF在所有数据集上内存占用稳定为61KB；KNN和SVM在DS_2上内存从5.7-7KB增至102-220KB；XGBoost预测时间从180-200ms增至700-3000ms；LR、DT、NB表现稳定且快速。

Conclusion: 数据集增强会增加资源开销但Canny+Hessian预处理对大多数模型有害；RF和LR/DT/NB在资源效率和预测速度上更具优势，为帕金森病分类任务提供了模型选择参考。

Abstract: This study investigates the classification of individuals as healthy or at risk of Parkinson's disease using machine learning (ML) models, focusing on the impact of dataset size and preprocessing techniques on model performance. Four datasets are created from an original dataset: DS_0, (normal dataset), DS_1 (DS_O subjected to Canny edge detection and Hessian filtering), DS_2 (augmented DS_0), and DS_3 (augmented DS_1). We evaluate a range of ML models-Logistic Regression (LR), Decision Tree (DT), Random Forest (RF), Gradient Boosting (GB), XGBoost (XBG), Naive Bayes (NB), Support Vector Machine (SVM), and AdaBoost (AdB)-on these datasets, analyzing prediction accuracy, model size, and prediction latency. The results show that while larger datasets lead to increased model memory footprints and prediction latencies, the Canny edge detection preprocessing supplemented by Hessian filtering (used in DS_1 and DS_3) degrades the performance of most models. In our experiment, we observe that Random Forest (RF) maintains a stable memory footprint of 61 KB across all datasets, while models like KNN and SVM show significant increases in memory usage, from 5.7-7 KB on DS_0 to 102-220 KB on DS_2, and similar increases in prediction time. Logistic Regression, Decision Tree, and Naive Bayes show stable memory footprints and fast prediction times across all datasets. XGBoost's prediction time increases from 180-200 ms on DS_0 to 700-3000 ms on DS_2 (truncated)

</details>


### [195] [Revisiting Thinning Methods for Kernel Learning Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07432)
*Blanca Cano-Camarero,Yago R. Aguado-Carrillo-de-Albornoz,Ángela Fernández-Pascual,José R. Dorronsoro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于核方法的子集选择算法：Backward Kernel Herding（通过迭代删除数据点构建代表性子集）和Flexible Kernel Thinning（克服了Kernel Thinning只能逐次减半的局限，支持任意规模子集的构建），两者在Gaussian Processes和Kernel SVM上实现了与现有最优方法相当的预测性能，同时显著提升了训练效率和内存效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核方法虽然具有强理论保证和良好经验性能，但计算成本高，难以应用于大规模数据集。现有子集选择方法（如Kernel Thinning）存在限制（只能构建减半规模子集），且需要更高效的算法来加速核学习流程。

Method: 1) 提出Backward Kernel Herding，通过迭代从数据集中移除点来构建代表性子集，利用MMD在RKHS中保持原数据集性质；2) 提出Flexible Kernel Thinning，作为Kernel Thinning的扩展，支持构建任意大小的子集而非仅限逐次减半；3) 在Gaussian Processes和Kernel SVM上进行了广泛的实验对比。

Result: Backward Kernel Herding在真实场景（减缩规模小于数据集一半）下可获得与现有最先进方法相当的结果，同时加速子采样过程；Flexible Kernel Thinning则在预测性能上经常达到最优。在中等压缩比下优势尤为明显。Backward Kernel Herding在计算效率优先时是首选，Flexible Kernel Thinning在内存消耗上也具有竞争力。但没有任何单一方法在所有场景中占优。

Conclusion: 子集选择策略应根据预测性能、训练成本和内存需求之间的权衡来选定。Backward Kernel Herding适合计算效率优先的场景，Flexible Kernel Thinning适合追求最优预测性能的场景，两种方法共同丰富了核方法在大规模数据集上的可用工具箱。

Abstract: Kernel methods are widely used because of their strong theoretical guarantees and empirical performance. However, their high computational cost limits their applicability to large-scale datasets. To address this shortcoming, several approaches use Maximum Mean Discrepancy to construct representative subsets that preserve the properties of the full dataset in a Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space. We introduce Backward Kernel Herding, an algorithm that addresses this problem by iteratively removing points from the dataset, achieving results comparable to current state-of-the-art approaches while accelerating the subsampling process in realistic scenarios where the reduced size is less than half of the dataset. Moreover, we overcome a limitation of Kernel Thinning by proposing an extension that enables the construction of subsets of arbitrary size rather that restricting to successive halvings. Finally, we conduct an extensive experimental comparison focusing on the most relevant kernel learning procedures: Gaussian Processes and Kernel Support Vector Machines. The results show that Backward Kernel Herding consistently achieves competitive performance with the most favorable training-time efficiency, while the proposed Flexible Kernel Thinning frequently achieves the best predictive performance. These gains become especially pronounced for moderate compression ratios, highlighting the benefits of incorporating supervised information into the thinning process. In terms of memory consumption, Flexible Kernel Thinning is also competitive, whereas Backward Kernel Herding remains an alternative when computational efficiency is the primary objective. Overall, no single method dominates across all scenarios, underscoring the importance of selecting the reduction strategy according to the desired trade-off between predictive performance, training cost, and memory requirements.

</details>


### [196] [TabBench-Bio: A Living Benchmark for Machine Learning on High-Dimensional Biomedical Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07441)
*Jules Kreuer,Sofiane Ouaari,Julia Hellmig,Julius Braitinger,Nico Pfeifer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabBench-Bio是一个包含43个生物医学数据集的可交互基准测试平台，在28种特征×样本组合下比较经典估计器、神经网络和表格基础模型。在10,000特征和100训练样本的参考条件下，RealTabPFN v2.5表现最佳，比分紧随其后的是逻辑回归和TabDPT。表格基础模型普遍占据领先位置，但最强配置取决于操作点和生物医学模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学表格数据往往具有数千个测量变量但仅有数十或数百个标记样本的特点，这种"高维小样本"场景在通用表格基准测试中代表性不足，缺乏系统性的评估平台。

Method: 构建了包含43个跨域生物医学数据集的动态交互式基准TabBench-Bio，在共享交叉验证协议下，对经典估计器、神经网络和表格基础模型在28个特征×样本操作点进行全面比较，并使用配对自助法进行统计显著性检验。

Result: 在参考操作点（10,000特征、100训练样本）下，RealTabPFN v2.5取得最高点估计分数，与逻辑回归相差145 Elo（95%置信区间[59,232]），逻辑回归与TabDPT分数几乎相同。表格基础模型普遍占据领先排名，但最佳配置依赖操作点和模态。AutoGluon以一小时"极端"预设作为独立的高资源参考。

Conclusion: TabBench-Bio填补了生物医学高维小样本表格学习评估的空白，强调了无重复预测、运行状态和确定性聚合以确保结果可复现性，并作为可生长的社区基准，欢迎新数据集的贡献。

Abstract: Biomedical tables often combine thousands of measured variables with only tens or hundreds of labelled samples, a regime that is poorly represented in general-purpose tabular benchmarks. We introduce TabBench-Bio, a living and interactive benchmark of 43 biomedical datasets spanning multiple domains. Under a shared cross-validation protocol, we compare classical estimators, neural networks, and tabular foundation models across 28 feature-by-sample operating points. At the reference cell of 10,000 features and 100 training samples, RealTabPFN v2.5 has the highest point estimate, followed by Logistic Regression and TabDPT, whose point estimates are nearly identical. A paired bootstrap over the target pool separates RealTabPFN v2.5 from Logistic Regression by 145 Elo (95% interval [59, 232]). Tabular foundation models generally occupy the leading ranks, while the strongest configuration depends on the operating point and biomedical modality. The AutoML framework AutoGluon, using its one-hour "extreme" preset, is configured as a separate resource-intensive reference and is reported here at the reference cell. Fold-level predictions, run status, and deterministic aggregations make every reported result reproducible and reusable.
  We invite the community to contribute: TabBench-Bio is designed to grow, and we welcome submissions of new biomedical tabular datasets, particularly from underrepresented assays and clinical endpoints, for inclusion in future releases.
  The interactive leaderboard is available at: https://tabbench-bio.eu

</details>


### [197] [TASTE: Throughput-Aware Batch Size Tuning for On-Device Edge Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07444)
*Avik Bhatnagar,Federico Nicolas Peccia,Oliver Bringmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于贝叶斯优化的批量大小（batch size）调优技术，用于在边缘设备上进行高效的模型训练，可在不损失模型精度的前提下实现高达2倍的训练吞吐量提升，并在持续学习场景中有效缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私保护AI的兴起推动模型适配与个性化转向端侧设备学习，但资源受限硬件上的深度学习训练需要优化以最大化吞吐量并保持预测精度，因此需要有效的超参数调优方法来应对这一挑战。

Method: 提出一种基于贝叶斯优化的批量大小调优方法，用于最大化硬件吞吐量；将该超参数应用于两种学习范式（标准监督学习SL和在线持续学习CL），并结合梯度累积与线性学习率缩放策略。

Result: 在不同边缘设备上的实验显示存在吞吐量上限，超过该上限增加批量大小不再带来吞吐量增益；在Raspberry Pi 4等平台上，所提方法相比最大批量大小实现了高达2倍的训练吞吐量提升且不损害模型精度；在持续学习范式中，最优批量大小维持了稳定性-可塑性平衡，有效缓解灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯优化驱动的批量大小调优是提升边缘设备训练效率的有效手段，在监督学习和持续学习两种范式下均能兼顾计算效率与学习性能。

Abstract: The rise of privacy-preserving artificial intelligence (AI) has shifted the focus of model adaptation and personalization towards on-device learning, where deep learning models are finetuned directly on edge hardware using local user data. However, this shift requires optimization of deep learning training on resource-constrained hardware to maximize throughput while maintaining predictive accuracy. This paper introduces a novel technique for on-device model training that incorporates an efficient Bayesian optimization-based batch size tuning approach to maximize hardware throughput. To evaluate the impact of this hyperparameter on the learning dynamics, we investigated two distinct paradigms: standard supervised learning (SL) and online continual learning (CL). Experimental results across various edge devices demonstrate a throughput ceiling, beyond which increasing the batch size yields no additional throughput gains. The proposed tuning approach identifies the optimal batch size, which, when combined with gradient accumulation and linear learning rate scaling, achieves up to a 2X increase in training throughput on platforms such as Raspberry Pi 4 compared to maximum batch sizes, without compromising model accuracy. Furthermore, in the CL paradigm, we demonstrate that optimal batch sizes maintain the stability-plasticity balance required for incremental learning, effectively mitigating catastrophic forgetting while maximizing computational efficiency on edge-hardware.

</details>


### [198] [Temporal-Causal Inference for Reinforcement Learning via Automata Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07461)
*Jan Corazza,Daniil Kaminskyi,Simon Lutz,Patrick Nossol,Hadi Partovi Aria,Zhe Xu,Daniel Neider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TCIRL框架，用于在具有不可逆相变和隐藏时间模式的强化学习环境中，联合学习控制策略并推断相变的隐藏时间原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多真实环境（如基因治疗、交通信号）的动力学会在隐藏时间模式的驱动下发生不可逆的相变，但智能体无法直接观察相变状态，导致环境呈现非马尔可夫性，传统RL方法难以处理。

Method: 将问题形式化为两阶段非马尔可夫决策过程，TCIRL维护一个假设确定性有限自动机（DFA）来跟踪当前激活的相变阶段，并通过反例驱动的SAT综合来精炼DFA，同时联合学习控制策略。

Result: 理论上证明假设几乎必然收敛到能识别所有可达标签序列上真实原因语言的DFA，从而得到原始非马尔可夫决策过程的最优策略；在基因治疗网格世界和交通信号环境中，TCIRL能恢复正确的因果DFA并匹配全信息基线性能。

Conclusion: TCIRL能有效在隐藏相变机制下同时进行策略学习与因果推断，并达到与全信息环境相当的性能水平。

Abstract: We consider reinforcement learning in environments with dynamics that undergo an irreversible phase transition governed by a hidden temporal pattern. The agent observes the base state but cannot observe the phase directly. We formalize this problem as a two-phase non-Markovian decision process and introduce Temporal-Causal Inference for Reinforcement Learning (TCIRL), a framework that jointly learns a control policy and infers the hidden temporal cause of the phase transition. TCIRL maintains a hypothesis deterministic finite automaton (DFA) to track what phase is active and refines it via counterexample-driven SAT-based synthesis. We prove that the hypothesis converges almost surely to a DFA recognizing the true cause language on all attainable label sequences, yielding an optimal policy for the original non-Markovian decision process. Experiments on a genetic therapy gridworld and a traffic signal environment show that TCIRL recovers the correct cause DFA and matches the full-information baseline in both domains.

</details>


### [199] [Improving Multivariate Time Series Classification with Class-Wise Training and Model Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07493)
*Mouhamadou Mansour Lo,Gildas Morvan,Mathieu Rossi,Fabrice Morganti,David Mercier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向多元时间序列分类（MTSC）的类级维度（通道）选择框架，不同于传统的全局维度选择方法，该方法为每个类别独立识别信息丰富的维度，并为每个类别执行专用的学习过程，最后通过融合阶段进行预测。实验采用MiniRocket作为基线方法进行评估，结果表明类级维度选择能提高特征表示质量，在高维场景下尤其能提升分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTSC方法通常对所有类别应用单一的全局维度选择过程，忽略了不同类别可能依赖不同维度（通道）信息的特性。这种做法可能导致噪声或无信息维度干扰判别性特征表示的生成，尤其在维度较高的场景下问题更为突出。

Method: 提出类级维度选择框架，核心步骤包括：（1）独立为每个类别识别信息丰富的维度；（2）为每个类别执行专用的学习过程；（3）通过融合阶段整合各类别结果进行最终预测。采用MiniRocket随机卷积核方法作为基线进行验证。

Result: 实验表明，类级维度选择能够提升提取特征的质量，并在高维设置下显著增强分类性能。该方法在异构数据集上表现出一致的性能提升，表明具有良好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将类特定信息纳入MTSC训练过程是一个有前景的研究方向，不仅能通过一致性的性能提升增强模型鲁棒性，还能通过显式识别类别相关维度提升可解释性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a class-wise dimension (channel) selection framework for Multivariate Time Series Classification (MTSC). Rather than applying a single global dimension selection process, the proposed approach independently identifies informative dimensions for each class. A dedicated learning process is subsequently performed for each class, followed by a fusion stage for final prediction. The objective is to improve the generation of discriminative feature representations while reducing the influence of noisy or non-informative dimensions. The proposed framework is evaluated using MiniRocket, a random kernel-based baseline method. Experimental results indicate that class-wise dimension selection improves the quality of extracted representations and can enhance classification performance, particularly in high-dimensional settings. These findings suggest that incorporating class-specific information into the training process represents a promising direction for MTSC, improving robustness through consistent gains across heterogeneous datasets, and interpretability through the explicit identification of class-relevant dimensions.

</details>


### [200] [Statistical versus machine learning-based spatial interpolation of post-processed ensemble weather forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07512)
*Mária Lakatos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究比较了统计学和机器学习方法对ECMWF 2米温度和10米风速集合预报的统计后处理效果，涵盖观测站和未观测站。提出了海拔感知线性池（ALP）方法，在未观测位置比标准线性池有小幅但显著的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集合天气预报需要统计后处理来提升校准性，但在无观测站点的位置生成校准预测仍具挑战性，现有方法在此场景下的表现缺乏系统比较。

Method: 比较EMOS、分布回归网络、Transformer和图神经网络等方法，在有限和扩展预测变量设置下进行训练；对温度还考察线性预报组合，并提出海拔感知线性池（ALP）方法。

Result: 大多数设置下后处理均优于原始集合预报，但没有任何单一方法在所有变量、站点组和评价指标上表现最佳。ALP在未观测位置相对标准线性池有小幅但显著的改进。

Conclusion: 不同后处理方法各有优劣，不能一概而论；结合海拔信息的线性组合方法在未观测站点能提供可靠的改进，值得在无观测场景中推广使用。

Abstract: Statistical post-processing improves ensemble weather forecasts, but generating calibrated predictions at locations without observations remains challenging. This study compares statistical and machine-learning-based methods for post-processing ECMWF 2-m temperature and 10-m wind speed forecasts at observed and unobserved stations in Germany. We consider EMOS-based approaches, distributional regression networks, Transformers, and graph neural networks under both limited and extended predictor settings. For temperature, we also investigate linear forecast combinations and propose an altitude-aware linear pool (ALP). The results show that post-processing improves upon the raw ensemble in most settings, but no single method performs best across all variables, station groups, and evaluation metrics. The proposed ALP provides a small but significant improvement over the standard linear pool at unobserved locations.

</details>


### [201] [CoRL: Co-Evolutionary Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Indirect Prompt-Injection Attacks and Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07529)
*Boyang Zhang,Qingxin Xiao,Lingwei Dang,Qingyao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoRL框架，将自适应间接提示注入(IPI)建模为非对称部分可观测马尔可夫博弈，通过攻击者SFT初始化、双边Co-PPO联合训练和历史对手种群修复三个阶段，将整体ASR降低38.5个百分点至0.0%，同时将任务效用提升13.1个百分点至76.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具增强的语言代理容易遭受间接提示注入(IPI)攻击，而基于固定攻击训练的防御无法应对攻击者策略、注入位置和载荷的动态变化，亟需一种能够适应自适应攻击的稳健防御框架。

Method: 将自适应IPI形式化为非对称、部分可观测、一般和马尔可夫博弈；提出CoRL框架，包含三个阶段：1) 攻击者SFT从成功轨迹初始化多轮攻击；2) 双边Co-PPO联合训练攻防双方，使用角色特定奖励和历史对手种群；3) 防御者SFT整合验证器接受的教师修复以应对种群发现的失败案例。

Result: 在每位防御者1,514次干净、固定模板和自适应执行的评估中，CoRL将整体ASR降低38.5个百分点至0.0%，并将效用提升13.1个百分点至76.3%。分阶段和受控消融实验显示在线Co-PPO和种群挖掘修复均有正向贡献，外部基准评估表明攻击抵抗能力具有迁移性。

Conclusion: CoRL框架有效平衡了防御者的安全性与任务效用，保留的攻击者可作为自适应红队评估的候选集，为工具增强语言代理的安全防御提供了可持续的对抗训练范式。

Abstract: Tool-augmented language agents are vulnerable to indirect prompt injection (IPI). Unlike direct prompt injection, IPI hides adversarial instructions in untrusted tool outputs and can covertly alter the execution of a legitimate task. Defenses trained on fixed attacks may fail as an attacker changes its strategy, injection site, and payload. To address this problem, we formulate adaptive IPI as an asymmetric, partially observable, general-sum Markov game: a multi-turn attacker adapts payloads at reached tool-return sites from the public trajectory, while a tool-using defender must block the injected objective and complete the user task. We propose CoRL, a verifier-grounded co-evolution and repair framework with three stages: Attacker SFT initializes multi-turn attacks from successful trajectories; bilateral Co-PPO jointly trains both agents with role-specific rewards and historical opponent populations; and Defender SFT consolidates verifier-accepted teacher repairs for population-discovered failures. Across 1,514 clean, fixed-template, and adaptive executions per defender, CoRL reduces overall ASR by 38.5 points to 0.0% and raises utility by 13.1 points to 76.3%. Stage-wise and controlled ablations show positive contributions from online Co-PPO and population-mined repair, while external-benchmark evaluation indicates transfer in attack resistance. The defender balances safety and task utility under the evaluated attacks, while the retained attackers provide candidates for adaptive red-team evaluation.

</details>


### [202] [No-Regret Mixing of LRU and LFU with Optimal Switching Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07566)
*Younes Ben Mazziane,Xinying Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究缓存系统中的在线学习驱逐策略。作者首先证明现有策略LeCar即使在无限历史下对 oblivious adversary 也会遭受线性遗憾，随后提出H-MC——一种基于Hedge算法的虚拟LRU与LFU缓存混合策略，该策略保留Hedge的选择概率及其遗憾保证，同时最小化所有具有这些边际分布的联合选择规则中的切换成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LeCar和Cacheus等结合LRU与LFU的策略在经验上表现优异，但其是否能保证在每个请求序列下渐近地达到LRU与LFU中较优者的性能（即相对于该基准实现亚线性遗憾）仍不清楚。作者旨在从理论上探究这一问题，并设计一个具有理论保证的策略。

Method: 作者首先从理论上证明LeCar在面对 oblivious adversary 时即使使用无限历史也会产生线性遗憾。然后基于Hedge算法设计H-MC策略，该方法维护虚拟LRU和LFU缓存的混合，通过保留Hedge的选择概率来继承其遗憾保证，同时利用联合选择规则最小化缓存切换成本。

Result: 证明LeCar存在理论缺陷——在 oblivious adversary 下具有线性遗憾；提出的H-MC策略能够保留Hedge算法的遗憾保证，同时最小化缓存切换成本。

Conclusion: H-MC提供了一种兼具理论遗憾保证和实际效率的LRU/LFU混合缓存策略，弥补了现有策略在理论保障方面的不足。

Abstract: Caching systems often rely on simple eviction policies such as Least Recently Used (LRU) and Least Frequently Used (LFU), which perform well in complementary request regimes. Recent policies such as LeCar and Cacheus combine LRU and LFU using ideas from the experts problem in online learning. Specifically, upon a miss, they randomize between the two eviction rules using probabilities derived from scores updated by tracking the history of past evictions. While these policies exhibit strong empirical performance, it remains unclear whether they are guaranteed, on every request sequence, to perform asymptotically as well as the better of LRU and LFU, i.e., whether they achieve sublinear regret with respect to this benchmark. We first show that LeCar suffers linear regret against an oblivious adversary, even with unbounded history. We then propose H-MC, a Hedge-based mixture of virtual LRU and LFU caches that preserves Hedge's selection probabilities, and hence its regret guarantees, while minimizing the switching cost among all joint selection rules with these marginals.

</details>


### [203] [Efficient Exploration Is Enough](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07575)
*Mikel Malagón,Jon Vadillo,Josu Ceberio,Michael Bowling,Jose A. Lozano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无外部奖励条件下高效探索的新视角，将高效探索者定义为优先产生可泛化经验的智能体，并通过预测与泛化的理论框架进行分析。理论上证明了最优效率探索者会自然地安排轨迹访问最具信息量和最可学习的区域；实证表明优化此目标可在简单环境中涌现出自动课程式的渐进复杂行为，无需外部奖励即可驱动开放式的复杂行为涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的高效探索研究通常依赖外部奖励信号，缺乏在无奖励环境下如何引导智能体产生复杂行为的系统性理论框架。本文旨在建立一种不依赖于外部奖励、以预测和泛化为核心的探索理论，以解释智能体-环境系统如何实现开放式复杂行为持续涌现。

Method: 首先从理论上定义高效探索者为优先产生可泛化经验的智能体，将问题转化为数据的学习能力（预测与泛化）分析；然后通过理论推导证明最优探索者的轨迹调度特性（优先访问高信息量、高可学习性区域）；最后在相对简单的环境中进行实证验证，观察优化该目标所产生的行为演化。

Result: 理论方面表明最优效率探索者自然而然地按"信息量最大且最可学习"的优先级安排探索轨迹；实证方面发现在简单环境中也能自动产生由浅入深的课程式学习，涌现出渐进复杂的智能行为，无需任何外部奖励或任务设定。

Conclusion: 该框架为无外部奖励条件下的开放式复杂行为涌现提供了原则性机制。纯内在目标（最大化可泛化经验）足以驱动智能体-环境系统持续产生日益复杂的行为，为研究开放式智能的涌现提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: This work introduces an alternative view of efficient exploration and studies its theoretical and empirical implications in the absence of extrinsic rewards. Specifically, we define efficient explorers as agents that prioritize generating generalizable experience, i.e., data that supports learning models capable of predicting and adapting across the environment. This allows us to analyze efficient exploration through the lens of prediction and generalization. Theoretically, we demonstrate that optimally efficient explorers naturally schedule their trajectories to visit the most informative and learnable regions first. Empirically, we show that optimizing for these agents gives rise to an automatic curriculum of progressively more complex behaviors, even in relatively simple environments. These results indicate that pursuing this purely intrinsic objective alone is enough to drive the emergence of highly sophisticated behaviors. We believe that this new framework provides a principled mechanism by which agent-environment systems may sustain an open-ended process of increasingly complex behavior without external rewards, tasks, or objectives.

</details>


### [204] [I Don't Miss You, but I Do: Self-Explanation Faithfulness of Modality Missingness in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07596)
*Aydin Javadov,Daniel Schoess,Florian von Wangenheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种干预式评估协议，用于检验视觉-语言模型（VLM）对其模态缺失如何影响自身预测的自解释能力。研究发现VLM系统性地高估现有模态证据的充分性，严重低估恢复缺失模态对预测的影响（预测变化率中位数≤8.8%，实际执行变化率高达72.1%），并指出可执行的模态干预可作为评估多模态自解释的行为学真值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型越来越多地应用于某些输入模态不可用的场景，但我们对其能否忠实解释模态缺失如何影响自身预测知之甚少。

Method: 引入一种干预式评估协议：让模型陈述各模态单独支持的结论、恢复缺失模态是否改变答案、现有证据是否充分；随后执行相应的模态干预，将这些声明与模型实际行为进行比较。评估了8个开源VLM、2个模型家族、4个任务（涵盖互补/同构图文设置及多视角驾驶场景）。

Result: 模型系统性高估现有模态证据的充分性；在62/64的设置中严重低估恢复缺失模态的效果（预测变化率≤8.8% vs 实际执行变化率达72.1%）；不足声明罕见但精准（被标记案例中恢复模态改变答案的中位数达78-100%）；回顾性自解释同样存在过度归因单一模态/表征充分性的倾向。

Conclusion: VLM系统性地错误描述其预测对现有和缺失模态证据的依赖关系，这突显了将可执行的模态干预作为评估多模态自解释行为学真值的必要性。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly used in settings where some input modalities may be unavailable, yet we know little about whether they can faithfully explain how such missing information affects their own predictions. We introduce an interventional protocol for evaluating self-explanations of modality dynamics: models state what each modality alone would support, whether restoring a missing modality would change their answer, and whether the available evidence is sufficient; we then execute the corresponding modality intervention and compare these claims with the model's realized behavior. We evaluate eight open-weight VLMs from two model families across four tasks spanning complementary and isomorphic text-image settings and a multi-view driving setting. We find a systematic tendency to overstate the sufficiency of available modality evidence. Models substantially underestimate the effect of restoring missing modalities: task-level median predicted change rates are at most 8.8%, while the corresponding executed change rates reach 72.1%, with underprediction in 62 of 64 model-task-condition settings. Insufficiency claims are rare, but precise when produced: restoring the modality changes the answer in a median of 78-100% of flagged cases. Retrospective self-explanations show the same tendency: on complementary data, models over-credit single-modality sufficiency; on isomorphic data, they over-credit single representation sufficiency relative to their executed behavior. Together, these results show that VLMs systematically mischaracterize how their predictions depend on available and missing modality evidence, motivating executable interventions as a behavioral ground truth for evaluating multimodal self-explanations.

</details>


### [205] [Beyond the Matrix Sign: Quadratic Spectral Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07597)
*Qiaozhe Zhang,Jun Sun,Yingzhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出二次谱下降（QSD）方法，在保留Muon的谱范数约束的同时引入二次局部模型以考虑曲率信息，通过Kronecker分解近似曲率并使用Frank-Wolfe步骤求解约束二次问题，实验表明在GPT预训练上持续优于Muon及其变体，训练时间最多减少8.49%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon方法可解释为在谱范数球上优化线性局部目标，其矩阵符号更新保留了梯度的奇异方向并为所有活跃奇异模式赋予相同幅度。作者质疑当考虑局部曲率时，这两个性质是否仍然最优，并希望通过引入二次模型来回答这一问题。

Method: 保持Muon的谱范数约束不变，将线性局部模型替换为二次模型，得到QSD方法。利用Kronecker分解统计量近似曲率，通过少量Frank-Wolfe步骤求解约束二次问题，每步包含闭式矩阵符号子问题，并提供最优性认证。

Result: 证明了曲率会同时改变最优更新的奇异值和奇异方向；提供了与Muon在同一二次替代模型下的比较；内层求解器具有O(1/K)收敛率；在GPT预训练实验中，QSD持续改进验证损失并减少最多8.49%的训练时间。

Conclusion: 曲率信息对优化更新的奇异值和奇异方向都有影响，QSD通过有效整合曲率信息在实践中有显著性能提升，验证了在Muon框架下引入二次模型的价值。

Abstract: Muon can be interpreted as optimizing a linear local objective over a spectral-norm ball. This gives a matrix-sign update that preserves the singular directions of the gradient and assigns the same magnitude to all active singular modes. We ask whether these two properties remain optimal when local curvature is taken into account. To answer this question, we keep Muon's spectral-norm constraint unchanged and replace the linear local model with a quadratic one. We call the resulting method \emph{Quadratic Spectral Descent} (QSD). We show that curvature can change both the singular values and the singular directions of the optimal update. To make QSD practical, we approximate curvature with Kronecker-factored statistics and solve the constrained quadratic with a small number of Frank--Wolfe steps, each of which has a closed-form matrix-sign subproblem. We further provide an optimality certificate, a comparison with Muon under the same quadratic surrogate, and an $O(1/K)$ convergence rate for the inner solver. Experiments on GPT pre-training show that QSD consistently improves validation loss over Muon and recent Muon variants, and reduces wall-clock training time by up to $8.49\%$ at matched validation loss.

</details>


### [206] [CLUES-WEASEL: No additional clues required to choose your time series clustering algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07606)
*Johann Faouzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CLUES-WEASEL的时间序列聚类算法，它利用WEASEL 2.0的无监督特征提取、主成分分析降维和k-means聚类，在聚类性能和运行速度之间取得了更优的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列聚类算法普遍面临聚类性能与运行速度之间的权衡问题，缺乏能够同时兼顾两者的解决方案。

Method: CLUES-WEASEL算法包含三个步骤：首先使用WEASEL 2.0分类算法的无监督版本进行特征提取，然后利用主成分分析（PCA）对提取的特征进行降维，最后采用k-means算法对降维后的特征进行聚类。

Result: 通过大量实验证明，CLUES-WEASEL在聚类性能上显著优于现有其他时间序列聚类算法，同时运行速度远快于最先进的方法。此外，该算法的架构也可以与其他时间序列特征提取算法很好地配合使用。

Conclusion: CLUES-WEASEL在时间序列聚类领域具有重要价值，它不仅克服了传统算法在性能与速度之间的权衡困境，还展示了良好的通用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Time series data is very common in many real-world applications and in numerous domains, with increasing interest for automated information extraction using machine learning. One of these subfields is time series clustering, which consists in identifying clusters among a set of time series in an unsupervised fashion. Most time series clustering algorithms suffer from the same balancing act: they trade clustering performance for faster runtimes or vice versa. We present a novel time series clustering algorithm that we call CLUES-WEASEL, which stands for CLustering with the UnsupervisEd Second version of Word ExtrAction for time SEries cLassification. CLUES-WEASEL extracts features using the unsupervised version of the transformation step of WEASEL 2.0, which is a time series classification algorithm, then reduces these features using principal component analysis, and finally performs clustering with the $k$-means algorithm using these reduced extracted features. Through extensive experiments, we prove that CLUES-WEASEL is significantly better than any other existing time series clustering algorithm while being (much) faster than any state-of-the-art one. We also show that the architecture of CLUES-WEASEL can work well with other time series feature extraction algorithms. Our findings highlight the relevance of CLUES-WEASEL for time series clustering.

</details>


### [207] [Translation of Black-Box Clinical Prediction Models into Standalone Transparent Nomograms: Temporal External Validation in Heart Transplantation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07610)
*Henry Pigot,Paulo J. G. Lisboa,Sandra Ortega-Martorell,Ivan Olier,Joseph Mahon,Johan Nilsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRiSM方法，将黑盒临床预测模型（表格数据）转换为可逐项审计的列线图（nomogram），保留了源模型中每个效应的形状与交互信息。在50,356名心脏移植受者中测试，5种源模型转换后的列线图均达到预设非劣效判别标准，且机器学习模型转换结果与可解释模型相当或更优。PRiSM已作为开源Python包发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒临床预测模型在医学应用中缺乏可解释性，难以被临床医生逐项审计和信任。现有方法（如GAM、EBM等）通常需要从头训练，而非从已有的黑盒模型中提取可解释结构，因此需要一个能捕获源模型效应形状并将其转化为可审计人类可读格式的方法。

Method: PRiSM（Partial Responses in Structured Models）方法：从黑盒源模型中提取每个变量的偏效应（partial responses）和交互形状，而非仅关注变量重要性；通过让结果变量（outcome）来选择和加权这些效应，构建成可逐项审计的列线图。在5种不同源模型（临床风险评分、逻辑回归、神经网络、随机森林、极端梯度提升）上进行转化测试，并在时间跨度上采用后一时期数据作为验证集。
