Result: 在50,356名心脏移植受者中，5种源模型转化的列线图均满足预设的非劣效判别标准，且多数保持了校准度和临床净收益。3种机器学习源模型（神经网络、随机森林、XGBoost）转化的列线图与从头训练的GAM和EBM相比判别力无显著差异，优于神经加法模型（NAMs），且比EBM携带更少的项数。

Conclusion: PRiSM能够在不做进一步简化的情况下，将多种黑盒临床预测模型转化为满足非劣效标准的可审计列线图，弥合了预测性能与临床可解释性之间的鸿沟。该方法已以开源Python包形式发布，可广泛用于临床预测模型的透明化部署。

Abstract: We convert black-box clinical prediction models for tabular data into standalone nomograms that can be audited term by term. PRiSM (Partial Responses in Structured Models) takes the shape of each effect and interaction from the source model, not merely which variables mattered, and lets the outcome select and weight them. We tested this in 50,356 heart transplant recipients, with validation in a later era than training. Nomograms from all 5 source models - a public clinical risk score, logistic regression, neural networks, random forests and extreme gradient boosting - met a prespecified noninferiority criterion for discrimination before any further simplification, and generally preserved calibration and clinical net benefit. Those from the 3 machine-learning models showed no detectable difference in discrimination from de novo generalized additive and explainable boosting models, exceeded neural additive models, and carried fewer terms than the explainable boosting model. PRiSM is released as an open-source Python package.

</details>


### [208] [Forecasting the Winner of a Live Tennis Match](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07617)
*Charles Xie,Aneesh Muppidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用8,222场大满贯赛事（共1,505,355个积分）的数据，评估五种网球比赛实时胜率预测模型。提出的混合模型Trace在比赛进行到25%、50%和75%时分别达到76.06%、82.15%和88.34%的预测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着实时体育博彩的兴起，网球预测已从赛前预测扩展到赛事进行中的动态胜率更新。核心挑战在于模型需要持续适应比分和表现变化，因此需要探索如何将赛前信息与实时信息最有效地整合。

Method: 使用2011-2024年的大满贯比赛数据，按时间顺序划分训练集（2011-2021）、验证集（2022）和测试集（2023-2024），评估五种模型在不同比赛进度（25%、50%、75%）下的实时胜率预测性能。其中Trace为赛前与实时信息相结合的混合模型。

Result: 混合模型Trace在25%、50%和75%比赛进度时分别实现了76.06%、82.15%和88.34%的准确率，优于其他对比模型。

Conclusion: 研究表明混合建模是实时网球预测的实用方法，通过有效整合赛前和比赛中的实时信息，可以在赛事推进过程中持续提供准确的胜率估计。

Abstract: With the rise of live sports betting in recent years, tennis forecasting has expanded from pre-match prediction to models that update win probabilities as a match unfolds. A central challenge in creating such a model is the constant need for models to adapt to score and performance changes. This study examines how pre-match and live information can be most effectively integrated into a model to produce accurate win-probability estimates. The analysis uses 8,222 Grand Slam matches containing a total of 1,505,355 points. Five models were evaluated using a chronological split, with matches from 2011-2021 used for training, 2022 for validation, and 2023-2024 for testing. Trace, a hybrid model, achieved accuracies of 76.06%, 82.15%, and 88.34% at 25%, 50%, and 75% match progress, suggesting that hybrid modeling is a practical approach to live tennis forecasting.

</details>


### [209] [Online Surrogate Repair: Decoupling High-Fidelity Feedback from Search Length in Closed-Loop Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07655)
*Xiaotang Feng,Philip Torr,Bruno Andreis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在线代理修复（OSR）算法，在闭环AI科学家搜索过程中，通过稀疏高保真度评估来持续更新代理模型，以平衡低成本候选生成与昂贵的可靠反馈之间的不对等，实现在固定代理与每轮高保真反馈之间的第三种反馈机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环AI科学家可以以极低计算成本生成候选设计，但可靠反馈（如湿实验合成、表征或高保真计算）成本高昂。现有的两种方案各有缺陷：定制实验室自动化基础设施密集且昂贵；使用固定替代模型则会累积模型误差并被优化过程放大。

Method: OSR算法在主要使用廉价代理反馈的长时间agent搜索中，通过采集规则从agent累积的提案中选择哪些设计接受高保真评估，并将得到的标签用于更新后续episode中使用的代理模型。在受控合成环境中对比了多种采集规则（如Q90-UCB和期望改进EI）对全局代理拟合与遗憾值的影响。

Result: 在受控合成环境中发现，改进全局代理拟合并不一定能减少最大遗憾值；Q90-UCB和EI通过将评估导向决定优化器决策的区域，能显著降低遗憾值。在MADE基准上，每episode后都接受高保真反馈的对照组需要6.36-7.23倍（两种LLM编排器下）和10.27倍（非LLM Chemeleon+MLIP工作流下）的oracle查询才能匹配在线EI的表现。

Conclusion: 在线代理修复引入了介于固定代理操作和每episode高保真反馈之间的第三种反馈机制，将高保真评估的频率与agent搜索的持续时间分离，为闭环AI科学研究提供了更高效的反馈策略。

Abstract: Closed-loop AI scientists can generate candidate designs at low marginal computational cost, whereas reliable feedback may require wet-lab synthesis, characterization, or high-fidelity computation. Addressing this imbalance through custom laboratory automation remains infrastructure-intensive and costly, while replacing new experiments with a fixed surrogate leaves persistent model errors that can be amplified by optimization. We propose \emph{online surrogate repair} (OSR), a closed-loop algorithm that uses sparse high-fidelity evaluations to update the surrogate throughout a longer agent search conducted primarily with inexpensive surrogate feedback. An acquisition rule selects which designs from the agent's accumulated proposals receive high-fidelity evaluation, and the resulting labels update the surrogate used in subsequent episodes. Across controlled synthetic environments, we demonstrate that improving global surrogate fit does not necessarily reduce maximum regret, whereas Q90-UCB and expected improvement (EI) substantially reduce regret by directing evaluations toward regions that determine the optimizer's decisions. On MADE, controls receiving high-fidelity feedback after every episode require $6.36$--$7.23\times$ more oracle queries to match Online EI under two LLM orchestrators and $10.27\times$ more under the non-LLM Chemeleon+MLIP workflow. Online surrogate repair introduces a novel third feedback regime between fixed-surrogate operation and high-fidelity feedback after every episode, separating the frequency of high-fidelity evaluation from the duration of the agent's search.

</details>


### [210] [Accuracy is Not Enough: A Divergence-Based Approach to Evaluate Fidelity Loss in Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07664)
*Shahzeb Qamar,Lorenz Sparrenberg,Christian Bauckhage,Baha Rababah,Carson Leung,Murat Kantarcioglu,Cuneyt Gurcan Akcora,Rafet Sifa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个分布敏感的评估框架，通过计算量化LLM与全精度模型之间全词表预测分布的Jensen-Shannon散度和总变差距离，来量化量化导致的信息损失，弥补了传统top-1准确率对分布变化不敏感的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对量化LLM的评估主要依赖零样本任务准确率，仅基于argmax预测，对底层预测分布的变化不敏感，导致在渐进量化下准确率呈现不稳定、非单调行为，掩盖了相对于BF16基线的显著保真度损失，提供误导性的部署信号。

Method: 提出分布敏感的评估框架，在token决策边界处计算全词表预测分布之间的统计距离（Jensen-Shannon散度和总变差距离），量化概率质量迁移和分布漂移；在5种基础架构和4个推理基准上，针对从BF16到Q2_K的渐进量化设置进行了120次实验。

Result: 散度指标在更强量化下总体增加，可与任务准确率互补提供保真度信号；在llama.cpp量化方案中，混合精度Q4_K在相似内存占用下比均匀Q4_0产生更低的散度。

Conclusion: 分布感知评估可作为任务准确率之外实用的诊断补充工具；但作者指出结果不直接证明正确性、校准、安全性或用户感知质量。

Abstract: Deployment of Large Language Models (LLMs) on memory-constrained edge devices relies heavily on aggressive post-training quantization. However, evaluating these models is largely based on zero-shot task accuracy, which depends solely on argmax predictions and is insensitive to changes in the underlying predictive distribution. Consequently, accuracy can exhibit unstable, non-monotonic behavior under progressive quantization, masking substantial fidelity loss relative to the BFloat16 (BF16) uncompressed base model and providing misleading deployment signals. We introduce a distribution-sensitive evaluation framework quantifying information loss in quantized LLMs as the divergence between full-vocabulary predictive distributions at the token decision boundary. We compute statistical distances, including Jensen-Shannon Divergence and Total Variation Distance, between outputs of full-precision and quantized models, enabling a fine-grained analysis of distributional shift. Using this framework, we quantify probability mass displacement and distributional drift relative to the BF16 reference, capturing predictive distribution changes not reflected in top-1 accuracy. We conduct a 120-run experimental matrix across five foundation architectures and four reasoning benchmarks under progressive quantization regimes, from uncompressed BF16 to Q2_K, providing a systematic fidelity analysis. Our results show divergence metrics generally increase under stronger quantization, complementing task accuracy with a fidelity signal. Across tested llama.cpp schemes, mixed-precision Q4_K generally yields lower divergence than uniform Q4_0 at similar memory footprints. These findings motivate distribution-aware evaluation as a practical diagnostic complement to task accuracy; they do not directly establish correctness, calibration, safety, or user-perceived quality.

</details>


### [211] [MpSub: A Momentum $p$-Dimensional Subspace Trust-Region Method for Derivative-Free Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07666)
*Yuyang Wang,Haoyu Yao,Pengcheng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出动量p维子空间信任域方法（MpSub），通过随机子空间搜索替代全参数反向传播，无需学习率调参即可高效微调大语言模型。在OPT-125M和OPT-350M上以相同预设参数达到与经过调参的MeZO相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型全参数微调因反向传播存储激活值和梯度而内存开销巨大；零阶优化虽避免此问题，但现有方法需要对每个模型和任务手动调整敏感的学习率，缺乏自适应能力。

Method: 提出MpSub方法：每轮迭代在p维子空间中搜索，一个方向保留最近接受步的历史动量，其余方向通过随机采样探索；用中心差分估计子空间梯度，基于线性信任域模型计算试探步，信任域半径根据预测与实际损失下降的一致性自适应调整，从而无需学习率。

Result: 在8,400次训练目标前向传播预算下微调OPT-125M和OPT-350M（CommitmentBank数据集），两个模型规模使用相同预设参数，平均测试精度分别达到0.673和0.690，匹配经调参的MeZO（0.685），且无需任何学习率搜索。理论上证明了有限差分误差上界、子空间梯度能量量化及平滑确定性目标下的几乎必然收敛性。

Conclusion: MpSub通过信任域半径自适应机制消除了学习率调参需求，在匹配的预算下达到与超参数调优后的MeZO相当的精度，同时实现了理论收敛保证，为大模型零阶微调提供了无需敏感超参数的实用方案。

Abstract: Full-parameter fine-tuning of large language models has substantial memory costs because backpropagation stores activations and gradients. Zeroth-order optimization avoids this by estimating update directions from loss evaluations, but existing methods require tuning a sensitive learning rate for each model and task. We propose the momentum $p$-dimensional subspace trust-region method (MpSub). At each iteration, MpSub searches within a $p$-dimensional subspace: one direction preserves historical momentum from the most recent accepted step, while the remaining directions explore via fresh random sampling. The subspace gradient is estimated by central differences, a trial step is computed from a linear trust-region model, and the trust-region radius adapts according to the agreement between predicted and observed loss reduction, eliminating the learning rate. For LLM fine-tuning, evaluations within an iteration share a minibatch, and directions are regenerated in place from seeds, using forward passes alone. For smooth deterministic objectives under unorthogonalized Gaussian directions, we bound the finite-difference error, quantify gradient energy captured by the subspace, and prove that $\lim_{k\to\infty} \|\nabla f(x_k)\|_2 = 0$ almost surely under a safeguarded radius update. Under a matched budget of 8,400 training-objective forward passes, we fine-tune OPT-125M and OPT-350M on CommitmentBank. With the same preset parameters at both model sizes, MpSub attains mean test accuracies of 0.673 and 0.690 over three seeds, matching tuned MeZO (0.685) without any learning-rate search.

</details>


### [212] [On the Recall Scaling Laws in Mamba: A Theoretical and Mechanistic Study via Hashing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07681)
*Yuval Koren,Assaf Ben-Kish,Raja Giryes,Lior Wolf,Itamar Zimerman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从机制可解释性角度逆向工程Mamba架构中关联回忆（AR）的内部算法，发现Mamba通过隐式学习线性哈希函数来实现回忆，并基于此提出了"回忆缩放定律"（Recall Scaling Laws）理论框架，可预测模型维度、回忆成功概率及多层/多头的AR容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关联回忆（AR）是评估Mamba等架构上下文记忆能力的重要基准，且与语言建模性能密切相关，但其内部实现机制尚不明确。作者希望从机制可解释性角度揭示Mamba执行回忆的确切内部算法。

Method: 首先识别出Mamba执行回忆的核心洞察——隐式学习线性哈希函数，并定位底层电路。然后借鉴相似性保持哈希中的理论工具（如Johnson-Lindenstrauss引理），构建名为"Recall Scaling Laws"的理论分析框架，用于预测嵌入维度、状态维度、回忆成功概率，并分析多层及多头SSM模式。

Result: 实证结果表明理论发现准确且具有预测性，能够有效解释AR容量如何随词汇量、状态维度、嵌入维度以及架构配置而变化。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅揭示了Mamba执行记忆回忆的底层机制（线性哈希函数），还提供了可预测AR性能的缩放定律框架，对理解状态空间模型的能力边界和架构设计具有理论指导意义。

Abstract: Associative Recall (AR) is the cognitive ability to learn and retrieve links between items in memory. In NLP, AR is used as a benchmark for evaluating the in-context memory capacity of architectures such as Mamba, and has been found to strongly correlate with language modeling performance. This paper explores AR from the perspective of mechanistic interpretability, aiming to reverse-engineer the exact internal algorithm used by Mamba to perform recall. Our key insight is that Mamba performs recall by implicitly learning linear hash functions, and we identify the low-level circuit that enables this behavior. Building on these findings and inspired by theoretical tools in similarity-preserving hashing, such as the Johnson-Lindenstrauss lemma, we develop a theoretical framework for analyzing AR, which we term Recall Scaling Laws. Given the vocabulary size and the number of facts in context, this framework allows us to (1) predict the embedding and state dimensions required for Mamba to achieve perfect recall, (2) predict recall success probability given the model dimensions, and (3) analyze multi-layer models and multi-head SSM patterns. Empirical results show that our theoretical findings are accurate and predictive, offering insights into how AR capacity scales with vocabulary, state, embedding size, and architecture.

</details>


### [213] [Emergent Charging Coordination in Electric Delivery Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07689)
*Javier Vales-Alonso,Juan J. Alcaraz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种纯局部控制的强化学习范式解决电动配送车队的中段充电协调问题。每辆车辆独立运行同一策略，仅依据自身时间预算和广播的站点占用率做决策，无需集中调度或预约机制，即可涌现出协作行为。基于20个真实城市的OpenStreetMap路网仿真，NEAT和PPO训练的代理零样本迁移到未见城市，分别完成96.8%和98.6%的班次，接近Oracle的99.5%水平，而朴素贪心规则仅73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动配送车队中段充电具有挑战性：每辆车需决定何时、何地、充多少电以按时完成任务且保持电池不低于安全阈值，但选择是耦合的（过多车辆选同一站点会造成排队）。现有方法依赖集中调度、预计算计划或预订机制，而现实中充电基础设施很少支持这些功能，需要一种不需要集中控制通信的局部协调方案。

Method: 采用纯局部控制的同构学习代理：每辆车辆运行相同策略，独立根据自身时间预算和站点广播占用率做决策。分别使用神经进化（NEAT）和策略梯度（PPO）两种方法在四个城市训练，并零样本部署到全部20个城市，其中16个从未出现在训练中。

Result: NEAT和PPO代理零样本部署后分别完成96.8%和98.6%的班次，接近Oracle的99.5%；朴素贪心规则（低电量时去最近站点）仅完成73%；针对性地调优的阈值启发式在拥挤城市仅约80%；PPO在需求或车辆特征漂移出训练分布时更为鲁棒。训练后代理自发学会了部分充电、短期机会性充电以及绕开繁忙站点，将每会话排队等待从约45分钟降至2分钟以内。

Conclusion: 该纯局部控制的学习式协调范式能够平衡局部紧迫性与公共占用率信息，以极低的实现成本达到接近Oracle的性能，为充电基础设施有限的电动配送车队提供了一种可行且可扩展的协调方案。

Abstract: In electric delivery fleets, mid-shift charging is non-trivial: each vehicle must decide when, where and how much to charge to finish on time with battery above a safety floor. The choices are coupled: queues build where too many vehicles pick the same station. Prior work resolves this coupling with central dispatching, precomputed schedules or reservations, machinery that charging infrastructure rarely supports. Instead, we use a family of learning agents under purely local control: every vehicle runs the same policy, deciding alone from its time budgets and broadcast station occupancies, leading to emergent coordination without central control or messaging. We validate this paradigm in simulation on real OpenStreetMap networks of twenty cities, each with a frozen scenario calibrated by an omniscient Oracle (99.5% of shifts completed on time), whereas a naive greedy rule (nearest station on low battery) completes just 73%. Agents trained with neuroevolution (NEAT) and policy gradients (PPO) on four cities and deployed zero-shot across all twenty, sixteen never seen in training, complete 96.8% and 98.6% of shifts, with the policy-gradient controllers proving more robust when demand or vehicle characteristics drift beyond the trained regime. In contrast, tuned threshold heuristics that read vehicle urgency alone fall short in contended cities (~80%). Through training, these learning agents rediscover partial charging and short opportunistic sessions, and route around busy stations, cutting per-session queue waits from about 45 minutes to under 2. In summary, this coordination paradigm balances local urgency against public occupancy, reaching near-Oracle performance at minimal implementation cost.

</details>


### [214] [ParetoTransport: Generative Optimization by Mass Transport Toward The Pareto Front](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07706)
*Stephanie Holly,Sepp Hochreiter,Werner Zellinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ParetoTransport，一种针对预训练流匹配模型的免训练引导方法，通过在目标空间中显式指定并细化群体级分布，将经验离线分布迭代传输至帕累托前沿，在多个离线多目标优化基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式离线多目标优化方法大多沿用经典的样本级引导策略，未能充分利用生成模型在分布级建模方面的能力，导致目标向量在帕累托前沿上的分布质量不佳。

Method: ParetoTransport利用Wasserstein匹配将流匹配采样器引导至中间代理分布，通过迭代方式将经验离线分布传输至帕累托前沿，直接控制分布位移以及沿前沿的质量分配，且无需额外训练。

Result: 在标准离线多目标优化基准上达到最先进性能，并将评估指标从超体积扩展到世代距离、逆世代距离和Wasserstein距离，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: ParetoTransport通过群体级分布引导充分发挥了生成方法的分布建模优势，成功提升了离线多目标优化中解集在帕累托前沿上的分布质量。

Abstract: Offline multi-objective optimization requires not only moving the objective vectors of candidate designs toward the Pareto front, but also distributing them effectively along it. Generative methods have recently emerged as a natural approach because they learn a distribution over feasible designs while allowing generation to be steered toward promising designs. Existing methods, however, largely retain classical sample-wise guidance strategies, leaving the distribution-level modeling capability of generative methods underused. We propose ParetoTransport, a training-free guidance method for pre-trained flow-matching models that explicitly specifies and refines a population-level distribution in objective space. ParetoTransport guides a flow-matching sampler to iteratively transport the empirical offline distribution toward the Pareto front, with Wasserstein matching to intermediate proxy distributions. This directly controls distributional displacement and mass allocation along the front. We establish a convergence result and demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on standard offline MOO benchmarks, extending recent evaluations beyond hypervolume to generational distance, inverted generational distance, and Wasserstein distance.

</details>


### [215] [Attributing Cohen's d: Training Data Attribution for Disease-Related Effects in Normative Age Biomarkers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07729)
*Jakob Snel,Marc-Andre Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将疾病相关效应量直接归因于个体训练样本的影响函数方法（基于Cohen's d的闭式解），而非传统的预测层损失归因。在UK Biobank的四种疾病和两种生物标志物模态上，移除最具影响的10%训练样本后，所有种子的留出疾病效应量均提升；对2型糖尿病的代谢组学年龄效应提升超过一倍，对多发性硬化的脑年龄效应提升约三分之一。被标记的受试者携带模型未见过的亚临床心血管代谢负担。作者发布了pyinfluence工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统规范年龄模型通过预测年龄与实际年龄的差距来评估疾病风险，但现有方法仅使用预测层损失作为归因目标，无法直接衡量训练样本对病例-对照分离（疾病效应量）的影响。作者希望将疾病相关效应量直接归因到个体训练样本，从而识别和移除对疾病效应产生负面影响的训练样本。

Method: 提出针对Cohen's d的闭式影响函数（closed-form influence functional），将疾病相关效应量（而非预测损失）作为归因目标，直接对个体训练样本进行排序。该方法通过留一法重训练进行验证，并在UK Biobank的四种疾病和两种生物标志物模态（代谢组学年龄、脑年龄）上评估了移除最具影响样本前后留出病例-对照效应量的变化。

Result: 在UK Biobank中，移除10%最具影响的训练样本在所有种子和所有任务中均提升了留出疾病效应量：2型糖尿病的代谢组学年龄效应提升超过一倍，多发性硬化的脑年龄效应提升约三分之一。随机移除50%样本时效应量保持不变，证明收益来自移除的样本选择而非样本数量。被标记的受试者携带模型未见的亚临床心血管代谢负担（如2型糖尿病对应的HbA1c标志物）。

Conclusion: 通过将影响归因目标从预测损失改为疾病效应量，可以更有效地识别和剔除损害病例-对照分离能力的训练样本，从而显著增强年龄模型作为疾病风险生物标志物的判别力。该方法还能揭示诊断排除标准遗漏的亚临床疾病负担，并发布了pyinfluence工具包以供复现和进一步应用。

Abstract: Normative age models are trained to predict chronological age in a nominally healthy cohort. Applied to patients, they deviate, and the gap between predicted and chronological age is read as disease risk. Here, we attribute the disease-related effect size of the age gap directly to individual training samples, rather than using a prediction-level loss as the attribution target. For Cohen's $d$, the resulting closed-form influence functional, validated against leave-one-out retraining, ranks training samples by their effect on held-out case-control separation. Across four diseases and two biomarker modalities in UK Biobank, removing the 10% most influential training samples raises held-out disease-related effect size in every seed. It more than doubles the metabolomic-age effect for type-2 diabetes and raises the brain-age effect for multiple sclerosis by roughly a third. Random removal leaves effect size flat even at 50% removal, confirming the gain comes from which samples are removed, not how many. Flagged subjects carry subclinical cardiometabolic burden that diagnosis-based exclusion misses, on markers the model never sees. For type-2 diabetes, where the method gains most, the marker recovered is HbA1c, the standard measure of blood sugar control. We release pyinfluence, our influence-function package, for reproducibility and reuse.

</details>


### [216] [Guiding Worker Self-Selection in Crowdsourcing Contests: An LLM-Augmented Algorithmic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07749)
*Nguyen Thach,Hau Chan,David Parkes,Karim Lakhani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合贪心框架（GRAF）与LLM驱动的进化搜索（LLMScore）的方法，用于众包平台在Tullock竞赛场景下向工人推荐竞赛，在保证工人零后悔和平台收益方面实现高质量（通常接近最优）的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 众包平台中工人会策略性地选择参与哪些竞赛以及投入多少努力，这种自选择可能导致重要竞赛参与者不足或投入不够，且工人可能后悔选择了比现有替代方案更差的竞赛。现有方法难以在工人异质性下设计有效的评分排序机制。

Method: 提出SSTC两阶段模型（先选竞赛再竞争）；设计GRAF贪心多项式时间框架，通过评分向量对工人排序来构建自选择结果，保证零工人后悔及特殊情形下平台最优；进一步提出LLMScore，利用LLM驱动的进化框架自动设计GRAF的评分算法，同时优化平台效用和工人满意度，并在精确评估不可行时近似评估工人后悔。

Result: LLMScore仅在单一设置的小规模实例上训练，即可迁移到更大规模和结构不同的设置；输出人类可读代码。在涵盖四种设置的1000个合成实例上，GRAF+LLMScore持续获得高质量、通常接近最优的结果，且工人后悔度低。

Conclusion: 该方法能同时惠及平台和工人，有效解决众包竞赛推荐中的战略自选择问题，且其可解释、可修改的代码输出便于平台运营者实际部署。

Abstract: Crowdsourcing platforms coordinate large pools of online workers who strategically choose which contests to enter and how much effort to invest. This self-selection can leave important contests with too few participants or too little effort, while workers may regret entering contests that leave them worse off than available alternatives. We study how platforms can recommend contests to workers using self-selection in Tullock contests (SSTC), a two-stage model in which workers first choose contests and then compete within them. We introduce GRAF, a greedy polynomial-time framework that constructs self-selection outcomes by ordering workers according to a score vector, with guarantees of zero worker regret and platform optimality in special cases of SSTC. Because effective orderings are difficult to design under worker heterogeneity, we propose LLMScore, an LLM-driven evolutionary framework that automatically designs GRAF's scoring algorithm. LLMScore addresses two challenges: jointly optimizing platform utility and worker satisfaction, and evaluating worker regret when exact computation is intractable. Trained only on small instances of one setting, it transfers to larger and structurally different settings; moreover, its output is human-readable code that platform operators can inspect and modify. Across 1,000 synthetic instances spanning four settings, GRAF with LLMScore consistently achieves high-quality, often near-optimal, outcomes with low worker regret, benefiting both platforms and workers.

</details>


### [217] [Local gradient neural operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07752)
*Baiming Zhang,Jinsong Tang,Ying Xu,Lihua Chen,Shiying Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出局部梯度神经算子（LGNO），一种轻量级、可解释的神经网络算子方法，用于动力学系统中场的时间演化预测和源识别任务，在各类PDE基准上展现出高精度、参数效率和滚动稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的场时间预测和源识别依赖对PDE的深入理解；现有的全局神经算子虽然提供数据驱动方案，但需要大规模训练数据和大量可学习参数，且泛化能力和可解释性有限。

Method: 基于非线性梯度离散化的先验知识，使用多层感知机卷积层学习平移不变的局部核（类似离散模板）；通过零一致模板分解将系数学习与场重建分离，增强算子透明性；利用网络折叠对对称问题共享等价组件并减少参数数量。

Result: 在覆盖线性和非线性、静态和动态、低维和高维的PDE基准上，LGNO在准确性、参数效率和滚动稳定性方面均表现良好，并广泛适用于扩散、流动和量子现象等机械问题。

Conclusion: LGNO作为轻量且可解释的替代方案，能够有效解决场时间演化预测和源识别问题，兼具精度与效率优势，并展现出广泛的工程适用性。

Abstract: Field temporal prediction and source identification constitute canonical problems in dynamical systems. Conventional approaches to these problems depend on a thorough understanding of the governing partial differential equations (PDEs). Recently, deep learning, as represented by neural operators, has provided a data-driven paradigm for addressing such tasks. However, most existing global neural operators for PDEs require large training datasets and many learnable parameters, with limited interpretability and generalization. We propose the local gradient neural operator (LGNO) as a lightweight and interpretable alternative for field temporal evolution prediction and source identification in typical mechanical problems. The method builds on priors from nonlinear gradient discretization and uses multilayer perceptron convolutional layers to learn translation-invariant local kernels that resemble discrete stencils. A zero consistent stencil factorization separates coefficient learning from field reconstruction, rendering the learned operators more transparent. For problems with symmetries, network folding shares equivalent components and reduces parameter counts. We evaluate the method on PDE benchmarks covering linear and nonlinear, static and dynamic, and low and high dimensional cases. Results show that LGNO maintains accuracy, parameter efficiency, and rollout stability across these tasks, and further exhibits wide applicability to mechanical problems including diffusion, flow, and quantum phenomena.

</details>


### [218] [A Theoretical Analysis of Generalization Dynamics in Neural Networks under Gradient Descent with Weight Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07755)
*Yuqing Wang,Ioannis G. Kevrekidis,Mikhail Belkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个理论框架，研究数据、架构和训练动态如何共同影响神经网络的泛化性能。作者证明了在ℓ²损失下用梯度下降加权重衰减训练的广泛神经网络类收敛到经验损失全局最小值的邻域，并将总体误差分解为数据误差、优化误差和预测变化误差分别进行界定。特别地，针对预测变化误差，提出了（局部）近似齐质性概念，推导了其沿训练轨迹的逐单元和逐层界，进而给出两个重要含义：改进泛化的必要条件解释了逐层泛化行为差异，充分条件描述了延迟泛化并为grokking现象提供了理论刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解泛化是机器学习领域的核心挑战，因为它需要同时考虑数据、架构和训练动态三者的相互作用，而现有理论通常难以全面捕捉这些因素如何联合影响泛化性能。

Method: 研究在ℓ²损失下通过梯度下降（GD）配合权重衰减训练的广泛神经网络类别，证明GD收敛到经验损失全局最小值邻域；通过基于输入数据划分空间，将总体误差分解为数据误差、优化误差和预测变化误差并分别界定；针对预测变化误差提出（局部）近似齐质性概念，推导其沿训练轨迹演化的逐单元和逐层显式界。

Result: 得到了预测变化误差在训练过程中的逐单元和逐层演化界；由此推导出两个重要结论：改进泛化的必要条件可解释不同网络层泛化行为的差异；充分条件描述了延迟泛化现象，为grokking提供了理论表征。

Conclusion: 该理论框架实现了对泛化误差的系统性分解与界定，既解释了逐层泛化行为的差异性，也为grokking这一延迟泛化现象提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: Understanding generalization remains a central challenge in machine learning because it requires jointly considering data, architecture, and training dynamics. In this paper, we develop a theoretical framework that characterizes how these factors jointly shape generalization performance throughout training. More precisely, we study a broad class of neural networks trained under the $\ell^2$ loss by gradient descent (GD) with weight decay, and prove the convergence of GD to a neighbourhood of the global minimizers of the empirical loss. By partitioning the space based on the input data, we then decompose the population error into data error, optimization error, and prediction variation error, and bound them separately. In particular, for the prediction variation error, which measures the oscillations of the learned function, we propose (local) approximate homogeneity and derive explicit cellwise and layerwise bounds for its evolution along the training trajectory. These bounds yield two important implications: a necessary condition of improved generalization explains differences in layerwise generalization behavior; a sufficient condition describes delayed generalization and provides a theoretical characterization of grokking.

</details>


### [219] [Decomposition-Guided Diffusion Language Models for Inertial Confinement Fusion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07756)
*Xiang Zhang,Varchas Gopalaswamy,Rahman Ejaz,Riccardo Betti,Dongfang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ICF-DLM，据作者所知是首个基于语言模型的惯性约束聚变（ICF）中子率波形预测器。该方法通过物理类型化分解（产率、峰时、局部波形）、双向去噪和基于物理的PPO奖励，在仅232个实验样本和50K模拟数据的条件下，将峰值时间误差从11.6步降至9.2步，优于经典序列模型和LLM时序预测器。其配方对低数据、稀疏事件的科学领域具有潜在推广价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICF是实现清洁能源的重要途径，但美国国家点火装置（NIF）每次实验成本约一百万美元，且每个实验仅能获得少量真实波形数据。传统时序预测器难以应对三个挑战：(1)时间稀疏性——皮秒级峰值出现在纳秒级窗口中；(2)输入输出尺度失配——真实数据不足300次实验；(3)峰值灵敏度——皮秒级时序偏差即影响结果。因此需要高精度的AI代理模型直接由激光脉冲和目标设计参数预测中子率波形，而无历史响应观测。

Method: 提出ICF-DLM模型，包含三个关键组件：(1)物理类型化分解：将波形分解为产率Y_DT、峰值时间t_peak和局部波形w_local三部分，降低建模复杂度和词汇表维度；(2)双向去噪机制：延迟对峰值位置的最终确认，同时利用前后上下文信息编码峰值不确定性；(3)物理驱动的PPO奖励函数：将聚变物理指标结构重新注入数值token的强化学习过程中，引导模型遵守物理约束。在ICFBench基准（50K模拟+232实验数据）上训练与评估。

Result: 在ICFBench基准上，ICF-DLM将峰值时间误差从11.6步降至9.2步（与匹配的自回归LLaMA-3-8B相比），并且优于经典序列模型（如LSTM等）以及其他基于LLM的时间序列预测方法。实验数据仅232个真实实验样本，验证了模型在小样本极端场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: ICF-DLM是首个LM-based ICF预测器，展示了在超低数据、时间稀疏、高灵敏度场景下语言模型结合物理先验的可行性。其"物理分解+双向去噪+物理奖励"的设计范式有望推广至其他低数据、稀疏事件驱动的科学领域，为AI代理在昂贵物理实验中的部署提供了新路线。

Abstract: Inertial confinement fusion (ICF) is a leading pathway toward clean energy, but each shot at the National Ignition Facility costs on the order of one million dollars, making accurate AI surrogates a high-value target. We study exogenous-driven ICF waveform prediction, where a 512-step neutron-rate diagnostic must be inferred directly from a laser pulse and target design parameters, with no historical response observed. The regime stresses standard time-series predictors with temporal sparsity (picosecond peak in a nanosecond window), input-output scale mismatch (under 300 real shots), and peak sensitivity (picosecond timing). We propose ICF-DLM, to our knowledge the first LM-based ICF predictor, combining (i) a physics-typed decomposition into yield $Y_{DT}$, peak timing $t_{\mathrm{peak}}$, and local waveform $w_{\mathrm{local}}$; (ii) bidirectional denoising that defers commitment to peak location; and (iii) a physics-driven PPO reward re-injecting metric structure across numeric tokens. On ICFBench (50K simulations + 232 experimental shots), ICF-DLM cuts peak-timing error from 11.6 to 9.2 steps over a matched autoregressive LLaMA-3-8B and outperforms classical sequence models and LLM-based time-series predictors. Beyond ICF, the recipe shows potential to address science domains with low data and sparse events.

</details>


### [220] [Latent-MoE: Domain-Aware Mixture-of-Experts for PDEs with Multi-Regime Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07814)
*Hanwen Wang,Paris Perdikaris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent-MoE架构，通过具有中心化紧凑支撑路由的混合专家（MoE）结构，使神经网络的NTK呈均匀带状分布，从而将学习局部化，解决了PINN在处理域内物理特性变化（如多阶段时变物理）的PDE时因NTK平移变异导致的远距离耦合和梯度冲突问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准坐标网络的NTK具有平移变异性，导致大坐标幅度的训练点过度影响其他位置的预测，产生远距离耦合和训练中的梯度冲突，使得PINN在物理特性随域变化（多阶段时变物理）的PDE上表现不佳。

Method: 作者从理论上分析并实证验证：采用中心化、紧凑支撑路由器的MoE架构可以产生均匀带状NTK，其核回归权重随距离指数衰减，从而实现学习局部化。在此基础上提出Latent-MoE，在共享骨干网络中间隔插入域感知的MoE块，既能保留域感知路由的局部化优势，又允许容量通过共享骨干在区域间流动。与FB-PINNs和X-PINNs（刚性地划分域和参数，各子域参数独立更新）不同，Latent-MoE在保持局部化的同时实现了跨区域的能力共享。

Result: 在标准均匀物理基准测试上，Latent-MoE与既有基线方法竞争力相当；在多阶段时变物理基准测试上（全局模型和刚性域分解方法均陷入伪解），Latent-MoE相较基线的改进超过一个数量级，并且训练过程中的梯度冲突显著减少。

Conclusion: Latent-MoE通过在共享骨干中内插域感知MoE块，实现了局部化学习与跨区域容量共享之间的平衡，有效解决了PINN在非均匀物理PDE上的失效问题，为该领域提供了一种新的架构设计思路。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) struggle on PDEs whose governing physics varies across the domain. We trace this to a structural property of standard coordinate networks: their neural tangent kernel (NTK) is translation-variant and lets training points of large coordinate magnitude disproportionately influence predictions elsewhere, producing long-range coupling and gradient conflict during training. We show analytically and empirically that mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures with centered, compact-support routers yield a uniformly banded NTK whose kernel-regression weights decay exponentially with distance, localizing the learning. Building on this, we propose \emph{Latent-MoE}, which interleaves domain-aware MoE blocks within a shared backbone. Unlike FB-PINNs or X-PINNs, which rigidly partition both the domain and the parameters so that the parameters on different subdomains are updated independently, Latent-MoE is designed to preserve the localization benefit of domain-aware routing while allowing capacity to flow across regions through the shared backbone. On standard homogeneous-physics benchmarks Latent-MoE is competitive with established baselines; on benchmarks with multi-stage time-variable physics, where global models and rigid domain decompositions both fall into spurious solutions, it improves over them by more than an order of magnitude, with markedly reduced gradient conflict during training.

</details>


### [221] [Kalman Delta Networks: Uncertainty-aware Associative Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07816)
*Ngoc Bui,Tinglin Huang,Rex Ying*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kalman Delta Networks (KDNs)，将线性注意力中的递归联想记忆重新表述为线性-高斯状态空间模型，利用卡尔曼滤波进行最优递归估计，显式建模记忆不确定性。作者设计了两种可并行扫描的KDN近似（对角和等方向），在750M和1.3B参数规模的预训练中一致优于现有线性注意力模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关注力模型中的Delta-rule模型虽然能学习写入强度，但无法跟踪记忆估计的置信度，导致每次写入无法根据累积证据自适应调整。现有方法缺乏对不确定性建模的能力。

Method: 将递归联想记忆重新表述为线性-高斯状态空间模型，用卡尔曼滤波作为最优递归估计器。提出KDN新模型族，通过卡尔曼增益按累积证据和观测可靠性加权残差写入。为解决精确跟踪导致的稠密Riccati递推不适配GPU并行扫描的问题，提出两种扫描兼容近似：Diagonal KDN（在线均值场变分推断投影到对角高斯族）和Isotropic KDN（每个头使用单一不确定标量的等方向近似），其不确定性递推为Mobius映射，支持对数并行深度的关联扫描。

Result: 在750M和1.3B参数的受控预训练实验中，KDN变体在困惑度和下游平均准确率上均一致优于最先进的线性注意力模型。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模记忆不确定性并用卡尔曼滤波最优加权写入，KDN在保持线性注意力高效长上下文推理和恒定内存解码优势的同时，显著提升了模型质量。Delta式更新可视为KDN在特定简化条件下的特例。

Abstract: Linear attention is increasingly used in frontier language models for efficient long-context inference and constant-memory decoding. Its fixed-size recurrent memory, however, requires an online decision at each token: what to write and how strongly to overwrite existing associations before knowing which information future queries will require. Delta-rule models learn this strength from the current token embedding but do not track confidence in the memory estimate, preventing each write from adapting to accumulated evidence. To represent this uncertainty explicitly, we reformulate recurrent associative memory as a linear--Gaussian state-space model, for which the Kalman filter is the optimal recursive estimator, and introduce a new family of models, Kalman Delta Networks (KDNs). Within KDNs, the transition propagates both the memory state and its uncertainty, allowing the Kalman gain to weight each residual write by accumulated evidence and observation reliability. Under this formulation, Delta-style updates emerge as a special case that substitutes a token-wise isotropic surrogate for predictive covariance and omits covariance tracking. Exact tracking, however, entails a dense, state-dependent Riccati recursion that is poorly suited to GPU-parallel linear-attention scans. To address this issue, we introduce two scan-compatible KDN approximations. Diagonal KDN projects each one-step posterior onto the diagonal Gaussian family through online mean-field variational inference, whereas Isotropic KDN uses an isotropic approximation with a single uncertainty scalar per head. Their uncertainty recurrences are Mobius maps, enabling associative scans with logarithmic parallel depth. Across controlled pretraining at 750M and 1.3B parameters, KDN variants consistently improve perplexity and mean downstream accuracy over state-of-the-art linear-attention models.

</details>


### [222] [Foundation Models for Generalizable Semantic and Goal-Oriented Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07853)
*Boliang Liu,Wint Yi Poe,Riccardo Trivisonno,Giuseppe Caire*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FMSGOC框架，利用视觉-语言基础模型的先验知识，通过稀疏语义锚点选择和生成式扩散模型重建，实现极低码率（0.039 BPP）下保持高语义保真度和跨数据集泛化能力的目标导向通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有6G语义/目标导向通信系统在极低码率下严重退化，原因在于模型过拟合训练数据且试图压缩整个信号。在有限码率预算下，泛化能力成为关键弱点，亟需解决。

Method: FMSGOC框架采用解耦设计：发送端利用视觉-语言基础模型选择并传输稀疏的目标对齐语义锚点（决定发什么），接收端利用预训练扩散模型（经掩码补全微调）重建被遮挡区域（决定如何重建），充分借助基础模型的广泛先验知识来缓解过拟合并提升码率效率。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上达到0.039 BPP且余弦相似度0.87-0.90；在未见过的ImageNet数据上保持0.83-0.86的语义保真度；感知相似度指标为0.1278/0.1558（CIFAR-10/ImageNet），在更低码率下优于强端到端基线。

Conclusion: FMSGOC通过将"发什么"与"如何重建"解耦，借助生成式基础模型实现极低码率下的高语义保真与良好泛化，为语义通信在资源受限场景中的应用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Semantic and goal-oriented communication is increasingly studied for 6G, but generalization beyond seen data remains a key weakness under tight rate budgets. Many existing systems overfit their training data and degrade sharply at very low bit rates because they attempt to compress the entire signal. We introduce Foundation Model-Guided Semantic and Goal-Oriented Communication (FMSGOC), a framework that uses broad visual-linguistic Foundation Model priors to mitigate overfitting. It further improves rate efficiency by concentrating bits on sparse, goal-aligned anchors and relying on generative foundation-model priors to reconstruct the masked regions. By decoupling what to send from how to reconstruct, a vision-language foundation model selects and transmits a sparse set of semantic anchors, while a pretrained diffusion model, fine-tuned for masked completion, reconstructs the image at the receiver. In our experiments, FMSGOC reaches 0.039 bits per pixel (BPP), maintains high semantic fidelity (cosine similarity 0.87-0.90 on CIFAR-10), remains robust on previously unseen inputs (0.83-0.86 on ImageNet), and shows good perceptual similarity (0.1278/0.1558, CIFAR-10/ImageNet), outperforming strong end-to-end baselines at lower bit rates.

</details>


### [223] [InfluenceField: A Differentiable Field with Interventionally Identifiable Causal Structure for Multimodal World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07874)
*Zihao Yang,Zijia Wang,Zhiqiu Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出InfluenceField，一种插入在视觉编码器与语言解码器之间的干预感知潜在场，通过连续空间表示预测局部视觉干预对下游答案的影响，在CausalVQA上相比基线提升13.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型虽然能捕捉视觉-语言相关性，但难以预测局部视觉干预如何传播并影响下游答案，缺乏对干预效果的因果推理能力。

Method: 将patch特征提升到连续空间表示，通过共享转移算子进行多步定向影响传播并预测局部干预效应；联合优化语言建模、跨环境不变性、反事实rollout监督和结构正则化。理论上给出非线性有限基种群模型的可识别性条件以及线性特化的精确部分覆盖刻画和有限损失稳定性界。

Result: 在CausalVQA上整体准确率较基线提升13.1个百分点，规划与假设类别的提升最为显著；容量匹配的基线和结构对照证明增益源自因果目标而非额外容量。

Conclusion: InfluenceField通过插入干预感知潜在场并配合因果驱动的训练目标，能有效恢复干预的传播结构，显著提升多模态模型的因果推理鲁棒性与事实-反事实一致性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models often capture visual-linguistic correlations but struggle to predict how local visual interventions propagate and affect downstream answers. We introduce InfluenceField, an intervention-aware latent field inserted between the visual encoder and language decoder. It lifts patch features into a continuous spatial representation, propagates directed influence over multiple steps, and predicts local intervention effects through a shared transition operator. Training jointly optimizes language modeling, cross-environment invariance, counterfactual rollout supervision, and structural regularization. For a nonlinear finite-basis population model, we show that target-aligned interventional supervision, together with a one-step separation condition on the transition, restricts admissible representations to within-location reparameterizations, so that the directed dependency graph of the full transition is recovered exactly. A linear specialization gives an exact partial-coverage characterization and a finite-loss stability bound, and the field analysis derives the spatial profile of coefficient interventions together with a shared-channel calibration result. On CausalVQA, InfluenceField improves overall accuracy over its backbone by 13.1 percentage points, with the largest gains on the planning and hypothetical categories. Capacity-matched baselines and structural controls attribute the gains in robustness and factual-counterfactual consistency to the causal objectives rather than to added capacity.

</details>


### [224] [The Accuracy Paradox: Empirical Diagnostic of Default Decision Thresholds in Multi-Label Enzyme Commission Prediction [With Code]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07897)
*Bilal Ahmad,Rajed Mehmood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对酶学委员会（EC）编号自动预测任务中的类别不平衡问题，对14,096种化合物的六类主要EC分类进行了系统性实证诊断，揭示了固定决策阈值（t=0.50）导致的"准确率悖论"：系统虽达到77.16%的平均准确率，但宏观F1分数仅0.3976，EC6类召回率完全崩溃（0.00%）。研究提出目标特异性阈值优化和后验共形校准作为必要防护措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的多元标签机器学习管道常默认使用0.50的决策阈值，假设目标头之间具有平衡的先验分布。然而在EC编号预测这类严重类别不平衡的真实生物信息学任务中，这种默认假设会导致预测性能的严重失真，因此需要系统性地诊断不平衡条件下固定决策边界的失效模式。

Method: 对N=14,096种注释化合物按照六个主要EC类别（EC1-EC6）进行系统性实证诊断；对比平均准确率与宏观F1、宏观召回等更能反映类别不平衡下性能的指标；分析多数类和少数类在不同阈值下的预测行为差异；结合拓扑指数与指纹密度度量的特征相关性分析进行冗余性评估，并提出目标特异性阈值优化与共形校准方案。

Result: 系统在默认阈值下表现出77.16%的高平均准确率（"准确率悖论"），但宏观F1仅为0.3976、宏观召回为0.3872。多数类严重过预测，少数类召回率急剧衰减，EC6类别尽管具有潜在判别力（ROC-AUC=0.5857），召回率仍完全崩溃至0.00%。特征相关分析发现拓扑指数之间存在高线性冗余。

Conclusion: 标准点预测结果掩盖了生物信息学工作流中的关键错误。目标特异性阈值优化和开放源码的后验共形校准是确保可靠的应用机器学习和深度学习架构的必备后处理保障措施。

Abstract: Automated prediction of Enzyme Commission (EC) numbers plays a central role in functional annotation and computational drug discovery. However, standard multi-label machine learning pipelines frequently rely on default decision thresholds (t=0.50), assuming balanced prior distributions across target heads. In this study, we present a systematic empirical diagnostic of uncalibrated fixed decision boundaries operating under severe class imbalance across N = 14,096 annotated compounds categorized into six primary EC classes (EC1-EC6). Our results highlight a pronounced Accuracy Paradox: while the multi-label system achieves a deceivingly high mean accuracy of 77.16%, the macro F1-score (0.3976) and macro recall (0.3872) reveal severe predictive breakdown. Majority target classes suffer from hyper-sensitivity and over-prediction, whereas minority classes exhibit sharp recall decay, culminating in a total decision boundary collapse for EC6 (Recall = 0.00%) despite underlying discriminative power (ROC-AUC = 0.5857). Feature correlation analysis further reveals high linear redundancy among topological indices relative to fingerprint density metrics. Ultimately, this diagnostic study demonstrates that standard point predictions mask critical errors in bioinformatics workflows. We establish target-specific threshold optimization and post-hoc conformal calibration as essential, open-source post-processing safeguards for reliable applied machine learning and deep learning architectures.

</details>


### [225] [AVCG: A Generalized Variational Framework for Counterfactual Generation under Hypothesis Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07917)
*Jamie Duell,Alejandro Jimenez Rodriguez,Mahault Albarracin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AVCG（Amortized Variational Counterfactual Generator），一个将反事实生成建模为对任意预测假设分布进行优化的广义框架，而非依赖单一确定性预测器，从而生成在模型重训练或更新后依然有效、鲁棒的反事实解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的反事实解释方法（无论是实例特定优化还是摊销单次模型）都依赖于单一的确定性点估计预测器，忽略了预测不确定性和假设可变性，导致生成的解释是脆弱的——当底层模型被重训练或更新时，这些解释常常变得无效。

Method: 提出AVCG框架，将反事实生成形式化为对任意分布（而非单个确定性预测器）上可行的预测假设集合进行优化。该框架统一支持贝叶斯后验、Rashomon受限假设空间及其他不确定性表示形式。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的评估表明，AVCG框架生成的反事实解释在预测不确定性和模型变化下保持高度有效性，同时维持了有竞争力的合理性和单次推理运行时间性能。

Conclusion: AVCG通过将预测不确定性纳入反事实生成过程，有效解决了传统方法在模型更新后解释失效的脆弱性问题，为鲁棒反事实解释提供了一种统一的广义优化框架。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations formalize "what-if" scenarios by identifying modifications to an input instance that obtain a desired alternative prediction. Traditionally, whether generated via instance-specific optimization or amortized single pass models, these approaches rely on a single, deterministic point-estimate predictor. However, this ignores predictive uncertainty and hypothesis variability, leading to brittle explanations that frequently become invalid if the underlying model is retrained or updated. To address this fragility, we propose the Amortized Variational Counterfactual Generator (AVCG), a generalized optimization framework that formulates counterfactual generation as optimization over an arbitrary distribution of plausible predictive hypotheses rather than a single deterministic predictor. This formulation naturally accommodates Bayesian posteriors, Rashomon-restricted hypothesis spaces, and other uncertainty representations within a unified optimization framework. Evaluation across multiple benchmark datasets demonstrates that the AVCG framework produces counterfactual explanations that remain highly valid under predictive uncertainty and model changes, while maintaining competitive plausibility and single-pass runtime performance.

</details>


### [226] [Prevalence calibration as shortcut mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07922)
*Mohamed Amine Kina,Eike Petersen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将捷径学习重新定义为校准问题，通过对不同捷径组进行患病率均衡校准来缓解模型依赖虚假相关性而非诊断特征的问题。作者提出了两种与编码器无关的方法（训练中正则化器和训练后重校准），在胸管气胸基准数据集上大幅超越现有基线，其中对标准ERM训练的DenseNet进行后处理重校准可将错位组AUROC从0.23提升至0.73，证明捷径依赖主要损害的是分类头而非底层表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有捷径学习缓解策略大多旨在学习对捷径不变的表示，但实证效果有限，且无法应用于使用冻结基础模型编码器的分类器，因此需要一种新的解决框架。

Method: 作者提出两个与编码器无关的方法：(1)训练中的正则化器（in-processing regularizer）；(2)训练后的患病率均衡重校准步骤（post-hoc prevalence-equalized recalibration），通过对捷径组之间的校准进行患病率均衡来缓解捷径学习。

Result: 在CheXpert和SIIM-ACR胸管气胸基准上，跨越微调CNN和冻结基础模型骨干，两种方法均大幅超越所有基线。后处理重校准使标准ERM训练的DenseNet错位组AUROC从0.23提升到0.73。

Conclusion: 捷径依赖主要损害的是分类头而非底层表征，该工作将捷径学习与校准理论及算法公平性建立了更根本的联系，并提出了两种最新的捷径缓解方法。

Abstract: Shortcut learning denotes the widespread situation in which a classifier exploits spurious correlations rather than diagnostic features. Existing mitigation strategies mostly aim to learn shortcut-invariant representations; their empirical success is limited and they cannot be applied to classifiers using frozen foundation model encoders. We propose to reframe shortcut learning as fundamentally a calibration problem: unconstrained learning implicitly calibrates each shortcut group to its training set disease prevalence, rendering the resulting classifier necessarily over-confident in one group and under-confident in the other. Building on this insight, we prevalence-equalize calibration between shortcut groups through two encoder-agnostic methods, an in-processing regularizer and a post-hoc prevalence-equalized recalibration step. Across chest-drain-pneumothorax benchmarks on CheXpert and SIIM-ACR, spanning fine-tuned CNNs and frozen foundation-model backbones, both methods substantially outperform all baselines. Post-hoc recalibration of a standard ERM-trained DenseNet raises misaligned-group AUROC from 0.23 to 0.73, indicating that shortcut reliance degrades the classification head rather than the underlying representation. Besides two new state-of-the-art shortcut mitigation approaches, our findings more fundamentally connect shortcut learning to calibration theory and algorithmic fairness.

</details>


### [227] [Structured Extrema Errors in Classical Surrogates for Viscous Burgers: A Physics-Consistent Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07952)
*Youssef Oubari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究一维黏性Burgers方程时间演化的机器学习代理模型的局部误差。比较四种模型（RBF核岭回归、线性Ridge、ExtraTrees和随机森林），发现误差在预测极值附近形成弯曲分支，且与二阶空间导数（局部曲率）密切相关。对KRR的深入分析揭示了预测值-曲率-残差之间的折叠结构，从而得出近抛物线关系。基于曲率的残差建模表明，KRR和Ridge存在黏性扩散不足的问题，而树模型此解释较弱。提出仅使用预测量的修正项可同时降低一步误差和递归展开误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解机器学习代理模型在逼近偏微分方程（特别是Burgers方程）时产生的系统性局部误差结构，探究这些误差的物理根源，并开发有效的误差修正方法以提升递归预测的长期稳定性。

Method: 在空间网格值上直接比较RBF核岭回归、线性Ridge、ExtraTrees和随机森林四种代理模型；通过分析一步残差与一阶/二阶空间导数的关系，建立基于局部曲率的残差模型；利用保留轨迹上的回归测试、打破扩散项空间对齐的对照组和谱分析测试来验证物理假设；提出基于预测量的修正项。

Result: 四种模型的一步残差在预测极值附近形成清晰的弯曲分支；KRR误差与二阶空间导数的相关性远超一阶导数；在光滑极值处预测值与曲率形成两分支折叠结构，推出预测值-残差间的前导阶近抛物线关系。回归测试、对照组和谱测试一致表明KRR和Ridge在中高黏度下存在黏性平滑不足的问题。该物理解释对树模型较弱。提出的修正项同时降低了一步误差和递归展开误差。

Conclusion: Burgers方程代理模型的系统性误差源于黏性扩散项建模不足，表现为极值附近特征性的曲率相关残差结构。基于此物理理解开发的修正方法能有效改善模型在递归预测中的性能。这一框架为理解物理信息代理模型的系统误差并设计纠正策略提供了有价值的视角。

Abstract: We study the local errors of classical machine-learning surrogate models, which approximate the time evolution of the one-dimensional viscous Burgers equation. Four models are compared on the same prediction task, using the spatial grid values directly: radial basis function (RBF) kernel ridge regression (KRR), linear Ridge, ExtraTrees, and Random Forests. Across all four models, the one-step residual, defined here as the true value minus the predicted value at each grid point, forms clear curved branches near predicted maxima and minima. A more detailed analysis of KRR shows that these errors are much more strongly related to the second spatial derivative, which measures local curvature, than to the first spatial derivative. Near a smooth extremum, predicted value and curvature form a local two-branch fold. Under our local curvature-based model of the residual, this fold predicts a leading-order near-parabolic relation between predicted value and residual. This geometric result motivates a direct test of the Burgers advection (transport) and diffusion (smoothing) terms. For KRR and Ridge, regression tests on held-out trajectories, a control that breaks the spatial alignment of the diffusion term, and a spectral test of high-frequency content are consistent with insufficient viscous smoothing at moderate and high viscosity. In this case, the surrogate retains more small-scale structure than the true future state. The same physical explanation is much weaker for the tree models. Finally, a correction that uses only predicted quantities reduces both one-step error and error during recursive rollout, where each prediction is used as the next input.

</details>


### [228] [Streaming Hierarchical Inference with Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07956)
*Vitor Crista,Afonso Lourenço,Diogo Martinho,Goreti Marreiros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HINT是一个面向表格基础模型(TFM)的分层推理框架，通过边缘端基于滑动窗口的图近似最近邻检索进行本地预测，仅在置信度不足时将样本及其检索上下文卸载到云端TFM，从而在高吞吐数据流场景下平衡预测性能与通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TFM虽然在上下文学习中表现优异，但其部署在高吞吐量数据流场景中面临通信开销和延迟的巨大挑战，亟需一种能够在保证预测性能的同时降低通信成本的推理方案。

Method: HINT采用分层推理架构：边缘端维护基于滑动窗口的图近似最近邻记忆，提供本地预测和不确定性估计；置信度高的样本在本地处理，不确定样本连同检索到的上下文被选择性卸载到云端TFM；通过卸载阈值和邻域检索策略可灵活调节预测性能与通信成本之间的权衡。

Result: 实验表明，HINT能够持续识别出预测性能与通信成本之间的有利权衡，即在不同场景下找到比基线更优的性能-通信折中点。

Conclusion: HINT证明了边缘检索与云端TFM推断相结合的分层框架能够有效缓解TFM在高吞吐数据流部署中的通信瓶颈问题，为表格基础模型的实际落地提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) have recently demonstrated strong predictive performance through in-context learning, but their deployment in high-throughput data streams remains challenging due to communication overhead and latency. We propose \textit{HINT}, a hierarchical inference framework that combines edge-based retrieval with cloud-based TFM inference. A graph-based approximate nearest neighbor memory maintained over a sliding window provides local predictions and uncertainty estimates, allowing confident samples to be processed locally while uncertain instances are selectively offloaded, together with their retrieved context, to a cloud-hosted TFM. The framework exposes an offloading threshold and a neighborhood retrieval policy that can be varied to balance predictive performance and communication cost. Experiments show \textit{HINT} consistently identifies favorable trade-offs.

</details>


### [229] [$α$-Graph: Attention-Infused Normalizing Flow Approach to Tractable Graph Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07961)
*Thanh-Dat Truong,Sarah Alharbi,Susan Gauch,Xinghui Zhao,Marios Savvides,Khoa Luu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于注意力机制和归一化流的图建模方法（ANFA/α-Graph），通过可逆注意力机制和可学习查询的条件图归一化流，显式、可解释地建模图数据的复杂关系结构，并在三个基准数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图建模方法依赖传统图神经网络和预训练方法隐式学习图的潜在关系结构，无法充分捕捉复杂的图结构和输入间的相关性。

Method: 提出α-Graph方法，包含两部分：(1) 无条件图归一化流，集成可逆注意力机制以捕捉复杂关系结构；(2) 带可学习查询的条件图归一化流，用于高效建模图结构数据中的相关性。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验结果表明，α-Graph方法达到状态最优（SoTA）性能。

Conclusion: 条件图归一化流与无条件图归一化流行为相似，能在增强模型表达能力的同时保持训练稳定性和效率。

Abstract: Graph modeling, a crucial task for representing complex relationships in graph-structured data, has achieved significant success in recent years. However, current graph modeling methods rely on traditional Graph Neural Networks and pre-training approaches to implicitly learn the underlying relational structure of graph data. Thus, these prior methods cannot capture the complex graph structure and correlations among inputs. In this paper, we introduce a novel Attention-based Normalizing Flow-based Approach\footnote{Our implementation and models will be released publicly for research reproducibility.} (ANFA or $α$) that provides an explicit, interpretable, and tractable Graph Modeling ($α$-Graph). In particular, we propose a new Unconditional Graph Normalizing Flow with an Invertible Attention Mechanism to capture the complex relational structure of graph data. To further enhance the expressiveness of the model, we introduce Conditional Graph Normalizing Flow with Learnable Queries that enables efficient modeling of correlations in graph-structured data. We show that our Conditional Graph Normalizing Flows behave similarly to Unconditional Graph Normalizing Flows, enhancing expressiveness while maintaining training stability and efficiency. Our experimental results on three benchmarks will illustrate the effectiveness and the state-of-the-art (SoTA) performance of the proposed $α$-Graph method.

</details>


### [230] [MetaKV: Adaptive KV Cache Compression for Constrained LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07966)
*Michael Wang,Keith Li,Roozbeh Bostandoost*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MetaKV是一个自适应框架，能够根据用户指定的延迟和峰值内存预算，为每个输入提示词选择合适的KV缓存压缩配置，在满足资源约束的同时保持准确性。实验表明，MetaKV在各种延迟和内存约束下，相比最佳静态配置，约束成功率平均提升约0.07，最高提升0.135。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的KV缓存压缩方法在准确性、推理延迟和峰值内存利用之间做出了不同的权衡，单一的固定配置无法适应不同提示词和资源约束的需求，因此需要一种自适应选择机制。

Method: MetaKV使用轻量级预测模型来估计每个候选配置的端到端延迟、峰值内存和正确响应概率，然后选择在满足延迟-内存约束条件下最能保持准确性的配置。评估覆盖了三种代表性KV缓存压缩方法（KVQuant、H₂O和RocketKV）的十种配置以及未压缩的FP16配置。

Result: 在数学、科学、常识推理和阅读理解四个数据集上，跨广泛的延迟和峰值内存约束，MetaKV一致优于最佳静态配置，约束成功率（CSR）平均提升约0.07，最高提升0.135。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，将KV缓存压缩自适应地适配到单个提示词及延迟-内存约束条件是有益的，代码已开源。

Abstract: Key--value (KV) cache compression is an effective way to reduce the memory overhead of large language model (LLM) inference, particularly for long-context workloads. However, existing compression methods make different trade-offs among accuracy, inference latency, and peak KV cache memory utilization, making a single fixed configuration unsuitable across different prompts and resource constraints. We introduce MetaKV, an adaptive framework that selects a KV cache compression configuration for each input prompt based on user-specified latency and peak memory budgets. MetaKV uses lightweight prediction models to estimate the end-to-end latency, peak memory, and probability of a correct response for each candidate configuration, and selects the configuration that best satisfies the latency-memory constraints while preserving accuracy. We evaluate MetaKV across ten configurations from three representative KV cache compression methods, KVQuant, H$_2$O, and RocketKV, together with an uncompressed FP16 configuration, on four datasets covering mathematics, science, commonsense reasoning, and reading comprehension. Across a wide range of latency and peak memory constraints, MetaKV consistently outperforms the best static configuration, improving constrained success rate (CSR), the fraction of prompts answered correctly while satisfying both constraints, by approximately 0.07 on average and up to 0.135. These results demonstrate the benefit of adapting KV cache compression to individual prompts and latency-memory constraints. Code is available at https://github.com/MichaelWang0505/MetaKV.git

</details>


### [231] [Heat Field Signatures: From Point Clouds to Smooth Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07975)
*Yuanqing Wang,Yapeng Tian,Baris Coskunuzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Heat Field Signatures (HFS)，将点云提升为多尺度平滑环境热场，直接从成对距离计算闭式全局与局部几何签名，涵盖热浓度、内蕴维数、各向异性和尺度转换。同时引入Heat Dimension Spectrum (HDS)作为多尺度几何组成的紧凑摘要。HFS可作为闭式描述符、轻量学习表示或几何特征通道，在多个基准上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将多尺度几何分析直接应用于不规则点云仍然困难：局部分数维、各向异性、密度变化和几何转换等量通常需要通过显式的邻域、流形或图结构来估计，或留给神经网络从坐标中推断，缺乏直接且高效的接口。

Method: HFS将点云提升为一个多尺度平滑环境热场族，从该场中直接从成对距离计算闭式全局和局部签名，捕获热浓度、内蕴维数、各向异性和尺度转换。另有HDS提供多尺度几何组成的紧凑摘要。HFS支持三种使用方式：闭式描述符、轻量学习表示、神经点云模型的几何特征通道。

Result: 在涵盖亚细胞、神经元、树木和蛋白质数据的合成与真实基准上，HFS优于强点云和多参数持久性基线，同时大幅降低端到端成本。在SCOP蛋白质折叠分类上，仅用坐标就比最强深度基线提升近24个百分点，且HFS表示天然具有旋转不变性。

Conclusion: HFS将经典热场转化为现代点云学习中进行多尺度几何分析的实用接口，在不依赖显式结构构建的前提下实现高效、鲁棒且旋转不变的几何特征提取。

Abstract: Bringing multiscale geometric analysis directly to irregular point clouds remains difficult: quantities such as local dimension, anisotropy, density variation, and geometric transitions are typically estimated through explicit neighborhood, manifold, or graph constructions, or left for neural networks to infer from coordinates. We introduce Heat Field Signatures (HFS), which lift a point cloud to a multiscale family of smooth ambient heat fields, providing a direct interface from discrete samples to geometric analysis.
  From this field, HFS computes closed-form global and local signatures directly from pairwise distances, capturing heat concentration, intrinsic dimension, anisotropy, and scale transitions. We further introduce the Heat Dimension Spectrum (HDS), a compact summary of multiscale geometric composition. HFS can be used as a closed-form descriptor, a lightweight learned representation, or a geometric feature channel for neural point-cloud models.
  Across synthetic and real-world benchmarks spanning subcellular, neuronal, tree, and protein data, HFS outperforms strong point-cloud and multiparameter-persistence baselines while substantially reducing end-to-end cost. On SCOP protein-fold classification, HFS improves over the strongest deep baseline by nearly $24$ percentage points using coordinates alone, while standalone HFS representations are exactly rotation-invariant by construction. More broadly, HFS turns a classical heat field into a practical interface for multiscale geometric analysis in modern point-cloud learning.

</details>


### [232] [Semi-Supervised Learning under Spatially Biased Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07982)
*Bright Wiredu Nuakoh,Francky Fouedjio,Stephen Bradshaw,Yaw Kwaafo Awuah-Mensah,Wei Hong Tan,Emet Arya,Ebenezer Afrifa-Yamoah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了半监督学习（SSL）在空间有偏标签采样下的性能退化问题，发现性能并非渐进下降而是在失配程度达到约0.71-0.77时发生阈值式崩溃，并提出核加权局部散度指标作为空间失配的稳定诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准半监督学习假设有标签与无标签数据共享相同边际分布，但空间有偏采样（如优先选址）会违反该假设。现有研究缺乏对这一失配如何影响SSL性能的系统性理解，特别是在空间自相关和非平稳性交织的情况下。

Method: 作者将边际失配、空间自相关和空间非平稳性视为三种独立机制，通过标签采样集中参数、空间长度尺度和非平稳性强度参数分别控制。采用受控合成框架及四个真实数据集（PovertyMap-WILDS、加州房价、社会经济、美国空气质量监测），运用分段回归变点分析等方法系统研究SSL退化行为，并提出核加权局部散度度量。

Result: SSL性能并非渐进退化，而是在分布失配严重程度超过0.71-0.77范围时出现阈值式崩溃；空间非平稳性独立于边际失配导致性能损失；模型在无标签区域外变得过度自信；核加权局部散度指标比朴素局部化方法提供更稳定的空间失配估计。

Conclusion: 研究为空间数据在半监督学习工作流中的使用风险提供了实证证据和诊断工具，有助于更好地记录和防范空间有偏采样带来的模型失效问题。

Abstract: Standard semi-supervised learning (SSL) typically relies on labelled and unlabelled data sharing a common marginal distribution. This assumption is often violated by biased spatial sampling mechanism, when labels are collected under spatially biased or preferential site selection. We treat this marginal mismatch, spatial autocorrelation, and spatial non-stationarity as three distinct mechanisms, varied independently via a labelled-sampling concentration parameter, a spatial length scale, and a non-stationarity strength parameter, and ask how mismatch degrades SSL, whether the cluster and manifold assumptions survive it, and how the resulting failure can be diagnosed. Using a controlled synthetic framework alongside PovertyMap-WILDS, California housing, socio-economic and US air quality monitoring datasets, we systematically vary the degree of mismatch while accounting for spatial autocorrelation and non-stationarity. Through a series of analyses including a segmented-regression changepoint, we show that in the synthetic generator, SSL performance does not degrade gradually but instead exhibits a threshold-like breakdown between approximately 0.71 and 0.77 once distribution mismatch becomes sufficiently severe. We further demonstrate that spatial non-stationarity contributes to performance loss independently of marginal mismatch and that models become increasingly overconfident outside the regions where labels are available. To support practical deployment, we evaluate several distribution-divergence measures as indicators of reliability and introduce a kernel-weighted local divergence metric that provides a more stable estimate of spatial mismatch than a naïve localised approach. These findings provide empirical evidence and diagnostic tools for better documenting the risk of incorporating unlabelled spatial data into semi-supervised learning workflows.

</details>


### [233] [Solving the Elastic Wave Equation with Physics-Informed Neural Networks: A Robust and Critical Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07983)
*Davide Staub,Ben Moseley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对PINN在弹性波方程求解中的应用进行了全面评估，发现将波动物理知识嵌入网络架构（如自定义小波层或平面波层）能显著提升精度，相对L2误差约为标准PINN的一半，并成功实现了对震源位置的条件化训练，推动了快速地震灾害检测的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然PINN在求解偏微分方程方面展现出巨大潜力，但存在频谱偏差和收敛不稳定等问题，且在地震学领域的应用尚待探索。作者旨在系统评估PINN在弹性波方程上的表现，并探究将物理原理嵌入网络架构是否能提升收敛性与精度。

Method: 作者对不同复杂度的地震源和参数模型（从恒定介质到高度非均质）进行了系统的PINN性能测试，比较了从无物理约束的标准PINN到高度专业化网络架构的一系列设计，并引入包含自定义小波层或平面波层、配合编码器和解码器层的创新网络结构。

Result: 将波动物理知识嵌入网络设计可显著提升精度，引入自定义小波层或平面波层配合编解码器层，相对L2误差约为标准PINN的一半；该架构在声波方程上也表现出良好的泛化能力；成功实现了对地震源位置的条件化训练。

Conclusion: 物理知识嵌入网络架构是提升PINN求解弹性波方程精度与收敛性的有效策略，条件化震源位置的PINN为快速地震灾害检测和地震分析提供了切实可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) have recently emerged as a promising approach for solving Partial Differential Equations (PDEs), offering a meshfree alternative that integrates physical principles into the learning process. This presents a new paradigm compared to traditional discretization methods and purely data-driven machine learning techniques. While promising, PINNs are not a panacea; they inherit challenges such as spectral bias and unstable convergence. Moreover, their potential in seismology remains largely unexplored. In this work, we provide a robust and critical assessment of PINNs for solving the elastic wave equation in seismology. We investigate the performance of PINNs on problems with varying degrees of complexity across various seismic sources and parameter models, from constant to highly heterogeneous settings. A pivotal aspect of our work involves investigating whether embedding physical principles directly into the network architecture enhances convergence and accuracy. We test an extensive range of neural architecture designs, from unrestricted, uninformed PINNs to highly specialized ones. We find that integrating an understanding of wave physics into the network design significantly improves accuracy. For instance, introducing a custom wavelet or plane wave layer, coupled with encoder and decoder layers, consistently yields a relative $L_2$ error approximately half that of the standard PINN, as evidenced across numerous experiments. We further demonstrate that this novel architecture enhances accuracy when applied to the acoustic wave equation, underlying the versatility of our network. Another key contribution of our research is the successful conditioning of PINNs on seismic source locations. This signifies a considerable advancement towards rapid seismic hazard detection and seismic analysis.

</details>


### [234] [Automated Chest CT Protocol Selection via Large Language Model Derived Text Embeddings from Imaging Request Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07986)
*Zahra Hosseini,Mahan Pouromidi,Farzad Khalvati,Patrik Rogalla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出基于大语言模型（LLaMA-3.1-70B）特征的决策支持系统，用于从自由文本临床指征中推荐胸部CT扫描协议。在285,123例胸部CT请求上，该系统达到79%的总体准确率和0.81的加权F1分数，与放射科医生表现无显著差异（p=0.263）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前CT协议选择依赖人工操作，耗时且易出错。传统机器学习方法基于关键词或词袋模型，缺乏上下文理解能力，在罕见协议上表现不佳。因此需要一种能够捕捉临床细微差别和措辞变异的方法来提高协议选择的准确性和一致性。

Method: 回顾性研究，使用2017-2024年间285,123例胸部CT请求（80%训练，20%测试）。将临床文本通过微调的Meta LLaMA-3.1-70B进行嵌入，输入逻辑回归分类器预测18种CT协议标签（如PE、LDCT等）。

Result: 系统在18种CT协议上达到加权精确率0.84、加权F1分数0.81和79%总体准确率。在300例独立专家共识案例中，LLM准确率80%与放射科医生83%无显著差异（p=0.263）。在部分具有挑战性的类别上LLM表现超过放射科医生，熵分析表明其协议使用更平衡、变异更小。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的推荐系统能够利用大规模自然文本语料的通用知识，准确地从自由文本影像请求中分配胸部CT协议，可作为需要语言理解的协议推荐工具的有效基础。

Abstract: Purpose: Accurate CT protocol selection is critical for diagnostic quality and patient safety, yet the current process is manual, time-consuming, and prone to inconsistencies. Prior Machine Learning methods using keywords or bag-of-words lack contextual understanding and perform poorly on rare protocols. We propose a decision support system using large language model (LLM) features to recommend protocols from free-text clinical indications, capturing clinical nuance and phrasing variation for more consistent, efficient selection.
  Methods: In this REB-approved retrospective study, 285,123 chest CT imaging requests from a large academic medical center (2017-2024) were split into training (228,099, 80%) and held-out test (57,024, 20%) sets. Each request included procedure names, clinical indication, HIS comments, and the selected protocol. Clinical text was embedded using a fine-tuned LLM, Meta's LLaMA-3.1-70B; these features input a logistic regression classifier predicting 18 protocol labels (e.g., PE, LDCT).
  Results: The pipeline achieved a weighted precision of 0.84, weighted F1-score of 0.81, and overall accuracy of 79% across 18 CT protocols. On 300 independent cases with expert consensus, the LLM reached an overall accuracy of 80% versus 83% for radiologists, with no significant difference (p = 0.263). Performance was comparable across most classes, with the LLM exceeding radiologists for some challenging categories, and entropy analyses indicated more balanced protocol use, suggesting reduced variability.
  Conclusion: An LLM-based recommendation system can leverage general knowledge from a large natural-text corpus to accurately assign chest CT protocols from free-text imaging requests, and may serve as a viable foundation for protocol recommendation tools where inputs require language understanding.

</details>


### [235] [HyCO: A Hybrid Neural Solver for Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07990)
*Yuheng Li,Di Yang,Haipeng Chen,Yanhai Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HyCO混合推理算法：先用RL求解器构建解前缀，再自适应切换到条件扩散模型完成剩余决策，在理论上证明其遗憾值低于任一单一方法，并设计轻量级自适应切换机制，实验验证效果优于两大基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序RL求解器和全局扩散模型求解器在神经组合优化中具有互补的失败模式：RL在早期构建阶段边缘遗憾小但随求解深度存在超线性复合误差累积；扩散模型避免时序复合误差但遗憾值随剩余未解子空间维度线性或次线性增长。如何融合两者优势，取长补短成为一个自然且重要的问题。

Method: 首先提出混合推理算法HyCO，先用RL求解器构建解前缀，再在某一触发步骤切换到条件扩散模型完成剩余决策。其次，建立统一误差缩放理论框架，在显式误差缩放假设下证明混合结构期望遗憾严格低于任一单独方法，且存在唯一最优切换步。最后，设计基于策略熵和RL-DM不一致性的轻量级自适应触发器，在轨迹层面近似检测最优切换时机。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验结果表明，HyCO相较于单一RL和单一扩散模型基线的结果均有一致的性能提升，同时验证了自适应触发机制在实际中的有效性。

Conclusion: HyCO通过RL与扩散模型的混合推理策略，从理论和实践两个层面证明了两者在神经组合优化中的互补性可以被有效利用，为后续研究提供了新的框架与实证基础。

Abstract: Sequential reinforcement learning (RL) solvers and global diffusion model (DM) solvers for neural combinatorial optimization exhibit complementary failure modes under an optimization-regret view. The former enjoys small marginal regret in the early construction stage, but suffers from horizon-wise compounding errors with super-linear regret growth; the latter avoids horizon compounding but incurs linear or sublinear regret w.r.t. the dimension of the remaining unsolved subspace. We propose Hybrid Neural Solver for Combinatorial Optimization (HyCO), a hybrid inference algorithm that constructs a solution prefix with an RL solver and adaptively switches to a conditional DM to complete the remaining decisions. To characterize why such hybridization helps, when to trigger the handover, and how to realize it in practice, we first develop a unified error-scaling theoretical framework and prove that, under explicit error-scaling assumptions, i) the hybrid structure achieves strictly lower expected regret than either backbone alone, and ii) there exists a unique optimal trigger step that minimizes the hybrid regret. We then design a lightweight adaptive trigger that combines policy entropy and RL-DM disagreement to detect trajectory-level signals of the regime shift as a practical proxy, since the optimal trigger step is defined at the expected-regret level and is not directly computable on individual trajectories. Experimental results on diverse benchmarks demonstrate that HyCO achieves consistent improvements over both backbones and support the empirical effectiveness of adaptive triggering.

</details>


### [236] [Sharp Structure-Agnostic Minimax Risk for Partial Linear Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07997)
*Haichen Hu,David Simchi-Levi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文刻画了部分线性模型中系数估计在结构无关意义下的极小极大风险，其中结果变量和处理的扰动函数由两个不同的黑箱学习器估计。通过引入每个学习器的逼近误差预算和随机误差预算（由局部Rademacher复杂度控制），推导出最优均方误差率，并解决了Gu (2025)提出的双机器学习开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gu (2025)提出了双机器学习中两个不同黑箱学习器条件下目标估计极小极大风险的开放问题。标准双机器学习可能高估目标估计的内在难度，因此需要精确刻画在结构无关条件下的最优估计率。

Method: 核心贡献是一个针对双学习器一般问题的新型下界。通过使用正交编码函数构造四个有限混合测试实验，将隐藏扰动分别置于：两个学习器类之外、仅治疗学习器类之外、仅结果学习器类之外、以及两个学习器类之内。这四个构型分别捕捉了两个逼近误差之间的交互、两个不对称的跨学习器交互（一个学习器的逼近误差与另一个学习器的学习误差），以及联合学习两个扰动函数的联合估计难度。将四个下界组合得到最终的风险率。

Result: 推导出的极小极大均方误差率为 E_n ≍ 1 ∧ {1/n + (a_μa_π + min{a_πs_μ + s_π², a_μs_π + s_μ²})²}，该结果与Gu (2026)的最新上界匹配。下界和上界吻合，证明确凿了该率的最优性。

Conclusion: 研究表明标准双机器学习可能高估目标估计的内在难度，并提供了目标特定的学习器选择原则：逼近误差与随机复杂性必须在两个扰动学习器之间联合平衡，而非分别单独优化。

Abstract: We characterize the sharp structure-agnostic minimax risk for coefficient estimation in the partial linear model when the outcome and treatment nuisances are learned by two distinct black-box learners, which resolves the open problem in double machine learning posed by Gu (2025). For each nuisance \(q\in\{μ,π\}\), we characterize the available learner by an approximation-error budget \(a_q\) and a stochastic-error budget \(s_q\), with the latter controlled through localized Rademacher complexity. Writing \(\mathcal E_n\) for the minimax mean-squared error, we show that \[\mathcal E_n\asymp1\wedge\left\{\frac1n+\left(a_μa_π+\min\left\{a_πs_μ+s_π^2,\,a_μs_π+s_μ^2\right\}\right)^2\right\}.\] The main new ingredient is a novel lower bound for the general two-learner problem. Our proof constructs four finite-mixture testing experiments using orthogonal code functions. Across these experiments, the hidden perturbations are placed outside both learner classes, outside only the treatment learner class, outside only the outcome learner class, or inside both learner classes. These four configurations capture, respectively, the interaction between the two approximation errors, the two asymmetric interactions between one learner's approximation error and the other learner's learning error, and the joint estimation difficulty of learning both nuisances. Combining the four resulting lower bounds yields the displayed rate, which matches the latest upper bound in Gu (2026). Our result shows that standard double machine learning can overstate the intrinsic difficulty of target estimation and provides a target-specific principle for learner selection: approximation error and stochastic complexity must be jointly balanced across the two nuisance learners rather than optimized separately.

</details>


### [237] [Two-Scale Localized PCA-Net: Coarse-Global and Local-Residual Representations for Artifact-Reduced PDE Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08034)
*Mrigank Dhingra,Jordan Stout,Omer San*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双尺度局部化PCA-Net方法，将PDE解分解为粗粒度全局分量和局部残差修正，通过紧凑全局PCA基捕获全域结构、非重叠局部PCA基表征细尺度残差，配合块平衡潜在目标和界面感知微调，有效减少局部解码带来的块偏移、界面不匹配和伪高频内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部化降维虽能提升高维PDE算子学习的高维可扩展性，但独立解码的局部块会引入块偏移、界面不匹配和虚假的高波数内容，影响解的连续性和精度。需要一种既能保持局部化可扩展性又能消除这些伪影的表征方法。

Method: 提出双尺度局部化PCA-Net（Two-Scale Localized PCA-Net），将解分解为粗全局分量与局部残差修正两部分：使用紧凑的全局PCA基表征全域尺度结构，用非重叠局部PCA基表示残余细尺度细节；通过块平衡潜在目标耦合并表征，并采用可选的界面感知微调（重建损失与迹损失）增强连续性。

Result: 在Poisson基准上，该双尺度表征相比普通和基于重叠的局部化PCA-Net显著降低了重建误差和可见块伪影，且PCA拟合成本约为重叠方法的一半。在非均匀Darcy流上，大幅降低了界面误差和离散残差误差，重建精度改善较为温和。消融实验表明主要改进来自双尺度输出表征，界面感知微调提供互补的连续性优化。

Conclusion: 将全局相干结构与局部残差细节分离，是一种高效且能减少伪影的PDE算子学习表征策略，在保持可扩展性的同时有效提升了解的表示质量。

Abstract: Localized dimensionality reduction improves the scalability of operator learning for high-dimensional partial differential equations (PDEs), but independently decoded local patches can introduce block offsets, interface mismatches, and spurious high-wavenumber content. We introduce Two-Scale Localized PCA-Net, which decomposes the solution into a coarse-global component and local residual corrections. A compact global PCA basis captures domain-scale structure, while nonoverlapping local PCA bases represent the remaining fine-scale residual. A block-balanced latent objective couples the two representations, and optional interface-aware fine-tuning further promotes continuity through reconstruction and trace losses. On Poisson benchmarks, the two-scale representation substantially reduces reconstruction error and visible block artifacts relative to plain and overlap-based localized PCA-Net while approximately halving PCA fitting cost relative to overlap. On heterogeneous Darcy flow, it strongly reduces interface and discrete-residual errors, with more modest reconstruction gains. Ablations show that the primary improvement arises from the two-scale output representation, while interface-aware fine-tuning provides complementary continuity refinement. Overall, separating globally coherent structure from localized residual detail provides an efficient representation for artifact-reduced PDE operator learning.

</details>


### [238] [Risk-Conditioned Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08064)
*Zixuan Liu,Fangzheng Wu,Brian Summa,Zizhan zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出risk-conditioned RLHF框架，通过训练单一策略提供连续的风险控制接口，使用户在推理时无需重新训练即可调整不同的风险厌恶程度，更灵活地部署风险感知的大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Risk-Averse RLHF方法通过优化CVaR来解决LLM生成稀有但严重有害内容的问题，但只为固定风险水平训练策略，无法在推理时调整风险厌恶程度，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 提出风险条件化的RLHF框架，训练一个带连续风险控制接口的单一策略，使不同风险厌恶程度可在推理阶段被选择，无需重新训练或部署多个风险特定模型。

Result: 在多个基准上的实验表明，单一风险条件化策略能够在推理时适应不同的风险水平，实现更灵活和风险感知的LLM部署。

Conclusion: 风险条件化RLHF提供了一种统一的解决方案，克服了固定风险水平训练的局限，使单一模型能在推理时动态调整风险厌恶程度。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in settings where rare but severe harmful generations can have significant consequences. Existing Risk-Averse RLHF addresses this issue by optimizing Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR), but it trains policies for fixed risk levels and therefore cannot adjust the desired degree of risk aversion at inference time. In this paper, we propose risk-conditioned RLHF, a framework that trains a single policy that provides a continuous risk-control interface, enabling users to select different degrees of risk aversion without retraining or deploying multiple risk-specific models. Experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that a single risk-conditioned policy can adapt to different risk levels at inference time, enabling more flexible and risk-aware LLM deployment.

</details>


### [239] [A Machine Learning Framework for Predicting Restaurant Food Waste to Support Sustainable Food Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08078)
*Md Mehedi Hasan Naeem,Md Ashraful Islam,Moumita Barua,Ishtiyak Ahmmad Araf,Md. Arefin Haque Mahir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个探索性的机器学习框架，用于根据运营与情境特征估算餐厅每日食物浪费量。研究整合了餐厅需求记录、气象数据和事件指标构建了77,980条记录、27个特征的数据集，评估了四种监督回归模型（线性回归、决策树、随机森林和梯度提升）。随机森林表现最佳，在消除代数泄漏变量后达到MAE 6.19 kg、RMSE 8.36 kg、R²为0.817。研究公开了完整数据集、目标构建公式和代码库以支持可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 餐厅部门的食物浪费对环境的可持续性和经济效率构成重大挑战，而大规模的真实食物浪费测量数据不可公开获取，因此需要开发基于可获得的运营特征来估算每日食物浪费量的方法。

Method: 整合餐厅需求记录、气象数据和事件指标构建67特征数据集（77,980条记录）；基于运营上有依据的假设推导目标变量（完整公式和随机变异公开）；采用按时间顺序的70-30划分训练-测试集，辅以5折时间序列交叉验证；评估四个监督回归模型，并系统排除具有代数泄漏风险的变量。

Result: 集成方法性能一致优于线性基线。随机森林在现实特征子集上达到最优性能：MAE为6.19 kg、RMSE为8.36 kg、R²为0.817。特征重要性分析识别出菜单多样性、运营面积和时间活动模式是主要预测驱动因素。

Conclusion: 该框架展现了对餐厅食物浪费进行估算的可行性，所有结果明确限定为针对构造目标的性能而非实测数据的验证。完整数据集、公式、代码和实验配置已公开，为未来扩展到实测浪费数据的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Food waste in the restaurant sector poses a substantial challenge to environmental sustainability and economic efficiency. This paper presents an exploratory machine learning framework for estimating daily restaurant food waste quantities from operational and contextual features. A structured dataset was constructed by integrating restaurant demand records, meteorological data and temporal event indicators, yielding 77,980 records across 27 features. Because large-scale ground-truth food waste measurements are not publicly available, the target variable was derived from operationally justified assumptions, with the complete construction formula and controlled stochastic variability disclosed for full reproducibility. Four supervised regression models, namely Linear Regression, Decision Tree, Random Forest and Gradient Boosting, were evaluated under a chronological 70-30 train-test split that respects the temporal ordering of restaurant operations, augmented by 5-fold time-series cross-validation. All reported metrics are explicitly scoped to performance against the constructed target and do not imply validation against measured food waste. Ensemble methods consistently outperformed linear baselines. Random Forest attained an MAE of 6.19 kg, RMSE of 8.36 kg and $R^2$ of 0.817 on the realistic feature subset following systematic exclusion of algebraically leakage-prone variables. Feature importance analysis identified menu diversity, operational area and temporal activity patterns as the primary predictive drivers. The full dataset, target construction formula, codebase and experimental configurations are publicly released to support reproducibility and future extension to empirically measured waste data.

</details>


### [240] [Proactive Context-Forecasted Safety Constraints for Nonstationary Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08080)
*Tim Tomashevskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于上下文预测的前瞻性安全约束生成框架，用于非平稳强化学习环境中的驾驶安全问题。该框架通过推断潜在环境上下文并预测其未来演变，提前构建适应预期条件的安全约束，使智能体在安全违规发生前主动避开危险区域，在驾驶仿真实验中显著降低了碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全强化学习依赖设计时定义的安全约束或在执行过程中被动更新，但非平稳环境中这些约束可能失效。因此需要一种能主动预测风险变化并提前调整安全约束的方法。

Method: 提出基于上下文预测的前瞻性安全约束生成框架，分为三步：从观测中推断潜在的潜在环境上下文，预测其未来演变趋势，并根据预测结果构建适应预期条件的安全约束，从而引导智能体主动避开不安全区域。

Result: 在包含高速公路、交叉路口和赛道等场景的驾驶环境中进行实验，对非平稳强度进行扫掠并测试未见过的驾驶布局。结果表明前瞻性约束生成在已见和训练外非平稳强度下均显著减少碰撞，并在各类未见驾驶布局中保持有效性，同时维持可用任务性能。

Conclusion: 基于上下文预测的约束生成是应对非平稳环境下安全强化学习的一种有前景的方法，能够实现主动而非被动的安全保护。

Abstract: Ensuring safety in reinforcement learning under nonstationarity requires anticipating changes in risk before they lead to unsafe behavior. Existing approaches typically rely on safety constraints defined at design time or updated reactively during execution, assuming that such constraints remain valid over time. However, in nonstationary environments with evolving contexts and changing driving layouts, these assumptions may fail.
  We propose a framework for proactive safety constraint generation based on context forecasting. The approach infers latent environmental context from observations, predicts its future evolution, and constructs safety constraints adapted to anticipated conditions. This enables the agent to proactively avoid unsafe regions instead of reacting only after safety violations occur.
  We evaluate the method in driving environments with structured context variation. The experiments include a sweep over nonstationarity intensities and additional held-out driving layouts, including highway, intersection, and racetrack scenarios. Results show that proactive constraint generation substantially reduces collisions under both seen and out-of-training nonstationarity intensities and generally remains effective across held-out driving layouts while maintaining usable task performance. These findings suggest that context-based constraint generation is a promising approach for safe reinforcement learning under nonstationarity.

</details>


### [241] [Learning Metamaterial Eigenmodes with Wavelet-Encoded Fourier Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08102)
*Han Zhang,Alexander Ogren,Cynthia Rudin,Johann Guilleminot,L. Catherine Brinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将傅里叶神经算子（FNO）与小波编码相结合，用于学习和预测弹性波方程的多重特征模态（本征值问题）。该方法能够在单一模型中确定性地选择连续值和二值化超材料几何上的多个形变模态，相比有限元分析在消费级CPU上加速三个数量级，同时保持高保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络算子代理模型在正向偏微分方程求解中已广泛应用，但特征值问题要求同时求解特征参数和多个有效特征模态，而标准算子学习框架假设唯一输入-输出映射，无法直接处理此类多模态问题。

Method: 将傅里叶神经算子（FNO）与小波编码的PDE输入相结合。小波编码与FNO的双重空间-频谱结构相匹配，实现确定性模态选择，适用于连续值和二值化几何结构。
