Result: 在单一模型中实现了超材料几何上多个弹性波特征模态的确定性选择与预测；代理模型在消费级CPU上比有限元分析快三个数量级且保持高保真度；预测精度与几何不连续性相关。

Conclusion: 小波编码与FNO的结合为多模态PDE求解器提供了有效的输入编码设计思路，对基于谱神经算子的其他多模态PDE求解器具有更广泛的启示意义。

Abstract: Machine learning surrogates based on neural operators have shown broad applicability in solving forward PDE problems. However, eigenvalue problems, in which an eigenparameter and one of several valid eigenmodes must be simultaneously solved, remain difficult because standard operator learning formulations assume a unique input-output map. This work demonstrates that Fourier Neural Operators (FNOs), combined with wavelet-based encodings of PDE inputs, can learn and predict multiple eigenmodes of the elastic wave equation, corresponding to deformation modes of acoustic waves propagating through arbitrary metamaterial geometries. We provide a mechanistic explanation and experimental evidence for why wavelet encodings are well matched to the dual spatial-spectral structure of the FNO, enabling deterministic mode selection on both continuous-valued and binary-valued geometries within a single model, and for why prediction accuracy varies with geometric discontinuities. For metamaterial design, the resulting surrogate accelerates the simulation stage of the design cycle by three orders of magnitude relative to finite element analysis on a consumer-grade CPU, while preserving high fidelity. These results also carry broader implications for designing input encodings in other multi-mode PDE solvers based on spectral neural operators.

</details>


### [242] [Nyström Attention Matches Full Attention for Cross-Sectional Stock Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08106)
*Kunhan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了MASTER模型中跨股票多头注意力模块，发现其注意力接近均匀分布但微小的低秩偏差承载了全部预测价值。作者提出Nyström低秩注意力在O(mN)成本下匹配全O(N²)注意力的效果，并通过TOST统计检验验证等效性。同时发现注意力与收益相似性负相关（追求互补而非相关性），图结构方法均表现不佳，且在N≈3500的大规模场景下跨股票模块不再优于简单LSTM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MASTER模型的跨股票多头注意力占据了42.5%的参数和25%的预测价值，但其内部工作机制尚不明确。已有研究中稀疏近似持续失败，而注意力近似均匀却不可移除，这一矛盾促使作者深入解构该模块，以揭示其有效性的本质来源。

Method: 作者首先对注意力权重矩阵进行困惑度和谱分析，揭示近均匀性与低秩偏离并存的结构。随后提出Nyström低秩注意力近似（m=32个地标点），在不同规模（N=300和N=800）上用TOST检验验证与原方法的统计等效性。此外还进行了消融实验、相关性分析（Spearman秩相关）以及大规模（N≈3500）外推实验，对比GCN、Nyström与逐股LSTM基线。

Result: 主要发现包括：(1) 注意力近似均匀（困惑度278/300）但对均匀性的偏离是低秩的（有效秩~65，前10个模态占96.5%能量）；(2) Nyström低秩注意力在N=300和N=800均通过TOST认证等效于全注意力；(3) 注意力与收益相似性显著负相关（rho=-0.614），经行业、beta和波动率控制后依然稳健（-0.627）；(4) 所有图结构替代方案均降低性能，硬掩码比直接移除模块更差；(5) 在大规模（N≈3500）条件下，跨股票模块的优势消失。

Conclusion: 跨股票注意力的价值在于一种可压缩的、动态的、近乎全局的重分配机制——它偏好低秩近似但抵抗稀疏化。这一结论不仅解释了现有方法优劣的来源，也为设计更高效的金融序列模型提供了理论指引，同时提醒研究者注意跨股票建模优势在大规模场景下可能不可持续。

Abstract: MASTER's inter-stock multi-head attention -- the module responsible for modeling cross-sectional stock relationships -- accounts for 42.5% of model parameters and 25% of predictive value. We systematically decompose this module and uncover a surprising structure: the learned attention is near-uniform (perplexity 278/300), yet forcing exact uniformity eliminates all cross-sectional discrimination. Spectral analysis resolves this paradox: the deviation from uniformity is low-rank (effective rank ~65, top-10 modes capture 96.5% of energy), explaining why sparse approximations consistently fail while Nystrom low-rank attention (m=32 landmarks) matches full O(N^2) attention at O(mN) cost -- certified equivalent via TOST at both N=300 (5 seeds, Rank IC p=0.003) and N=800 (10 seeds, Rank IC p=0.034). Additional findings include: (i) attention anti-correlates with return similarity (Spearman rho = -0.614; on the industry-labeled subset, -0.645 unconditionally and -0.627 after controlling for industry, beta, and volatility), suggesting complementarity-seeking rather than correlation mining; (ii) all graph-based alternatives degrade performance, with hard masking worse than complete module removal; and (iii) at N ~ 3,500 with adapted architectures, no cross-stock module (GCN, Nystrom, or MASTER-style pipeline) significantly outperforms a per-stock LSTM baseline (n=4 seeds), indicating that the benefits observed at smaller scales do not trivially transfer. These results establish that the inter-stock attention's value resides in a compressible, dynamic, near-global redistribution that rewards low-rank approximation but resists sparsification.

</details>


### [243] [Sparse Data Augmentation for Optimization with Provable Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08133)
*Behrooz Tahmasebi,Melanie Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究非凸优化中能否用固定小样本变换近似完全数据增强。在适当正则条件下，使用固定稀疏增强的梯度下降达到完全增强目标ε稳定点的变换查询复杂度为O((log|G|+log(1/δ))/ε²)，优于每次迭代重新采样变换的group-SGD（O(1/ε⁴)）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何机器学习中的非凸优化问题常通过数据增强平均所有变换上的经验损失来促进不变性，但完全增强需要访问变换群G的每个元素，当G很大或只能通过采样访问时计算成本过高。研究者希望探索能否用优化前固定采样的小变换集合来近似完全增强目标。

Method: 在合适的正则条件下，预先从变换群G中固定采样一组小样本变换并在优化过程中复用（稀疏增强），对增强后的目标执行梯度下降。证明技术利用群诱导算子的谱性质和表示论工具，建立随机群平均对完整群平均梯度场的均匀逼近。

Result: 以至少1-δ的概率，稀疏增强GD在O((log|G|+log(1/δ))/ε²)次变换查询内达到完全增强目标的ε平稳点，查询数少于完全增强GD和group-SGD（后者需O(1/ε⁴)次）

Conclusion: 固定稀疏增强的梯度下降在变换查询效率上优于完全增强GD和group-SGD，为大规模几何机器学习中的数据增强提供了理论保障。

Abstract: In nonconvex optimization problems arising in geometric machine learning, data augmentation is commonly used to promote invariance by averaging empirical losses over transformations of the data. Computing the fully augmented objective, however, requires access to every element of the transformation group $G$, which may be prohibitively expensive when $G$ is large or accessible only through sampling. We study whether full augmentation can instead be approximated using a small, fixed sample of transformations acquired before optimization and reused thereafter. Under suitable regularity conditions, we show that, with probability at least $1-δ$, gradient descent (GD) on the resulting sparsely augmented objective returns an $\varepsilon$-stationary point of the fully augmented objective using $\mathcal{O}\bigl((\log |G|+\log(1/δ))/\varepsilon^2\bigr)$ group-transformation-oracle queries. By comparison, standard group stochastic gradient descent (group-SGD), which samples a fresh transformation at every iteration, uses $\mathcal{O}(1/\varepsilon^4)$ transformation queries. Therefore, gradient descent with fixed sparse augmentation requires fewer transformation queries than both GD applied to the fully augmented objective and group-SGD. Our proof techniques, which may be of independent interest, establish a uniform approximation of the full group-averaged gradient field by a random group average using spectral properties of group-induced operators and tools from representation theory.

</details>


### [244] [KBBQ: A Predictive Noise Law and the Limits of Spectrum Flattening in FP4 Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08135)
*Lexington Whalen,Yuki Ito,Ryo Sakamoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了矩阵乘法中量化噪声的二阶理论，将量化格式特征化为每个元素分配的方差。整数量化的恒定方差恢复现有理论；浮点量化的乘法特征将数据依赖性简化为标量"参与因子κ"，得到闭式信噪比定律及其上界κ*。基于此提出KBBQ方法，在W4A4下超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量化噪声理论主要针对整数量化，对浮点量化的分析不足，缺乏统一的二阶理论框架来指导低比特量化方法的设计。

Method: 构建量化噪声的二阶理论，用每个元素的方差刻画量化格式；通过参与因子κ简化浮点量化的数据依赖性，推导闭式SNR上界κ*；据此提出KBBQ方法，参数化线性变换逼近该理论上界的程度。

Result: 在W4A4设置下，跨四个基础模型和两种FP4格式，KBBQ超越先前最优方法，且无需额外的部署期计算开销。

Conclusion: 所提出的二阶量化噪声理论为理解和优化矩阵乘法量化提供了统一框架，KBBQ验证了理论指导下的方法在实际场景中的有效性。

Abstract: We develop a second-order theory of quantization noise in matrix multiplication in which the quantization format is characterized by the variance it assigns to each element. The constant variance profile of integer quantization recovers existing integer-noise theory, while the multiplicative profile of floating-point rounding reduces the data dependence to a scalar, the participation factor $κ$, yielding a closed-form signal-to-noise-ratio law. The resulting functional also admits a closed-form upper bound $κ^{*}$ that no function-preserving linear transform can exceed and that is attained by a recent state-of-the-art method. Building on this analysis, we introduce KBBQ (\textbf{K}appa-\textbf{B}raked \textbf{B}lockwise \textbf{Q}uantization), which parameterizes the extent to which a transform approaches this ceiling. At W4A4, across four base models and two FP4 formats, KBBQ outperforms the prior state of the art without additional deployment-time computation.

</details>


### [245] [GPU-Enabled Large-Scale Optimization Using Randomized Linear Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08136)
*Pratik Rathore,Zachary Frangella,Parth Nobel,Xuning Hu,Madeleine Udell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了rlaopt，一个基于PyTorch的开源优化与科学计算包，利用随机数值线性代数（RandNLA）技术，提供GPU加速的求解器（NystromPCG、NysADMM、SAPPHIRE），支持正定线性系统和带约束的凸经验风险最小化问题，并配备自然数学语法建模语言及可微迭代，在岭回归等任务上展现了显著加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管RandNLA算法已取得显著进展，但现有实现很少能同时兼顾GPU加速与简单的优化问题描述接口，存在工程上的空白。

Method: 该包提供三类GPU求解器：利用RandNLA加速共轭梯度法（NystromPCG）、算子分裂方法（NysADMM）和随机梯度方法（SAPPHIRE）；同时构建了自然数学语法建模语言，自动检查问题与求解器的兼容性并完成问题分解，且支持迭代过程的反向传播以实现超参数调优。

Result: 在岭回归、有界多项逻辑回归和有界弹性网络上进行的实验，识别了随机预条件何时能提升性能，并展示了GPU执行带来的显著加速。

Conclusion: rlaopt填补了RandNLA算法GPU实现与易用接口结合的空缺，作为Apache许可下的开源工具（已发布至PyPI）可供社区使用。

Abstract: This paper introduces rlaopt, a PyTorch-based package for large-scale optimization and scientific computing using randomized numerical linear algebra (RandNLA). Despite substantial progress in RandNLA-based algorithms, few implementations combine GPU acceleration with a simple interface for specifying optimization problems. rlaopt addresses this gap by providing GPU-enabled solvers for positive-definite linear systems and convex empirical risk minimization with constraints and regularizers. These solvers use RandNLA to accelerate conjugate gradient (NystromPCG), operator splitting (NysADMM), and stochastic gradient methods (SAPPHIRE). Moreover, rlaopt includes a modeling language that lets users specify problems using natural mathematical syntax. rlaopt automatically checks compatibility with the selected solver and performs the required problem decomposition. The solvers also support differentiation through their iterations, enabling applications such as hyperparameter tuning. Experiments on ridge regression, bounded multinomial logistic regression, and bounded elastic net identify when randomized preconditioning improves performance and demonstrate substantial speedups from GPU execution. The package is open-source under an Apache license, with source code at https://github.com/udellgroup/rlaopt and version 0.1.0 available on PyPI.

</details>


### [246] [Topology-induced Operators Reveal Complementary Graph Representations without Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08152)
*Meng Qin,Jinqiang Cui,Hongwei Zheng,Weihua Li,Sen Pei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明无需复杂模型设计和梯度训练，仅通过随机游走和匿名游走引入的隐式层次结构传播随机特征，即可获得信息丰富的图嵌入，在节点、边和图级别任务上与经典及最新方法表现相当，且计算成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表示学习大多聚焦于设计越来越复杂的模型来学习嵌入，但嵌入质量究竟取决于模型学习还是底层的拓扑变换尚不清楚。作者希望探究是否可以不经过训练就获得有信息量的嵌入。

Method: 将随机特征通过由随机游走和匿名游走诱导的隐式层次结构进行传播，生成两种无需训练的嵌入：前者捕捉节点邻近性，后者捕捉结构角色。两种嵌入可单独使用或联合使用。

Result: 这两种免训练嵌入在多种节点、边和图级别任务上与经典及最新方法表现相当，且通常计算量显著更低，具有更优的质量-效率权衡。融合两种嵌入可进一步提升部分任务的推理质量。

Conclusion: 信息丰富的图嵌入可以来源于在应用任何学习操作之前对拓扑变换的精心选择，而非必须依赖复杂的模型设计和梯度训练。

Abstract: Graph representation learning has largely focused on designing increasingly sophisticated models to transform graph topology into vector representations, or embeddings. However, the extent to which embedding quality depends on model learning, rather than on the underlying topological transformations, remains unclear. Here, we show that informative embeddings can be derived without complicated model design and gradient-based training. Propagating random features through implicit hierarchical structures induced by random walks and anonymous walks yields embeddings that capture node proximity and structural role, respectively. These two training-free embeddings preserve complementary aspects of graph organization and perform competitively with classic and recent methods across various node-, edge-, and graph-level tasks. They often require substantially less computation, resulting in a favorable quality-efficiency trade-off. Combining the two types of embeddings further improves inference quality of some tasks compared with using either embedding type alone. Our results suggest that informative graph embeddings can arise from carefully chosen topological transformations before any learning operation is applied.

</details>


### [247] [Geodesic-informed Generative Diffusion Model For Topology-preserved Image Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08153)
*Nian Wu,Nivetha Jayakumar,Jiarui Xing,Miaomiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IGG框架，将拓扑保持的测地线动力学原理整合到扩散生成模型中，在测地线变形空间中学习和合成多样样本，从而在图像生成过程中保证几何和拓扑一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成扩散模型存在两大局限：一是主要依赖图像强度与纹理信息，忽视底层物体几何结构，无法保证生成过程中的几何和拓扑一致性，这对计算解剖学、生物学和机器人等高风险领域至关重要；二是现有模型未能显式学习或表达生成过程中的形状变化，形变动力学隐藏在网络参数中，导致变换过程不可解释且缺乏物理意义。

Method: IGG框架将拓扑保持的测地线原理融入扩散生成过程。与常规在图像强度空间操作的方法不同，IGG在测地线变形空间中学习和合成多样样本，将几何对象变化学习为从给定模板/源图像到目标的光滑且可逆的映射。

Result: IGG框架成功解决了现有生成模型的几何一致性缺失和形变不可解释性问题，代码已公开在GitHub上。

Conclusion: 通过在测地线变形空间中引入测地线动力学，可以实现既保持拓扑/几何一致性又具备物理意义和可解释性的图像生成过程。

Abstract: Generative diffusion models have emerged as a class of powerful techniques for various imaging applications, including but not limited to synthesis, reconstruction, and segmentation. Despite their success, current generative models pose two key limitations. First, they primarily rely on image intensity and texture information, with limited attention to underlying object geometry. As a result, they do not guarantee geometric or topological consistency during the generation process, which is a crucial requirement for high-stakes domains such as computational anatomy, biology, and robotics, where preserving object structure is critical. Second, existing models fail to explicitly learn or represent shape changes in the generative process. Such deformation dynamics remain occluded within network parameters; hence leaving the transformation process uninterpretable and physically uninformed. To address these challenges, we introduce IGG (Image Generation informed by Geodesic dynamics), a novel framework that integrates topology-preserving geodesic principles into the diffusion-based generative process. In contrast to conventional methods that operate in image intensity space, IGG learns and synthesizes diverse samples within geodesic deformation spaces, where geometric object changes are learned as smooth and invertible smooth mappings from a given template/source image. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/nellie689/IGG.

</details>


### [248] [SIM: Subspace Interaction-based Method for Token-Level Text Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08200)
*Kehan Yan,Yue Tan,Qingfeng Chen,Shiyuan Li,Yu Zheng,Yixin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SIM方法，用于令牌级文本异常检测。SIM采用子空间交互式异常检测器将高维词嵌入解耦为多个低维子空间以放大局部异常信号，设计硬伪异常生成模块模拟被语义平滑掩盖的细微异常，并提出概率边界损失将异常分数标准化为统计距离。实验表明SIM在效率、鲁棒性和可解释性方面均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有令牌级文本异常检测方法仍依赖全局距离计算进行评分，导致局部异常信号被大量冗余的正常特征维度稀释；此外，预训练语言模型会不可避免地平滑表面异常，进一步限制令牌级异常的检测效果。

Method: SIM包含三个核心组件：(1)子空间交互式异常检测器，将高维令牌嵌入解耦为多个低维子空间，放大特定维度中隐藏的局部异常信号；(2)硬伪异常生成模块，构造伪异常令牌以模拟被语义平滑掩盖的细微异常；(3)概率边界损失，将异常分数标准化为统计距离，迫使异常实例显著偏离正常分布中心。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验验证了SIM的有效性，并展示了其在效率、鲁棒性和可解释性方面的显著优势。

Conclusion: SIM通过子空间交互、硬伪异常生成和概率边界损失三种策略，有效解决了令牌级文本异常检测中局部信号稀释和过度平滑两大关键问题，为该领域提供了高效、鲁棒且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Token-level text anomaly detection, as an emerging trend of text anomaly detection, moves beyond coarse-grained document-level detection by localizing anomalous tokens within text. By providing fine-grained abnormality prediction, token-level text anomaly detection plays a critical role in various real-world applications, such as spam filtering and fake news detection. However, existing methods still rely on the global distance calculation for scoring, during which the local anomaly signals are severely diluted by numerous redundant normal feature dimensions. Moreover, pre-trained language models used in these methods inevitably smooth out surface anomalies, further limiting their effectiveness in token-level anomaly detection. To address these limitations, we propose a Subspace Interaction-based Method (SIM for short) for token-level text anomaly detection. To prevent local signal dilution, SIM adopts a subspace interaction-based anomaly detector, which decouples high-dimensional token embeddings into multiple low-dimensional ones, amplifying localized anomaly signals hidden within specific dimensions. To counteract the over-smoothing effect, we design a hard pseudo-anomaly generation module to construct pseudo-anomalous tokens, simulating the subtle anomalies obscured by semantic smoothing. Also, a probabilistic boundary loss is developed to standardize anomaly scores into statistical distances, effectively enforcing anomalous instances to deviate significantly from the normal distribution center. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets verify the effectiveness of SIM and demonstrate its remarkable efficiency, robustness, and interpretability. The source code is available at: https://github.com/yankehan/SIM-TAD.

</details>


### [249] [CUNO: Curriculum and Preference Optimization for Stable Graph Unlearning under Mass Deletion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08244)
*Chenhan Zhang,Ali Braytee,Madhushi Bandara,Xin Hao,Paul J. Kennedy,Massimo Piccardi,Raymond Owen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CUNO，一种基于课程学习的图遗忘框架，通过按遗忘难度渐进地删除样本，并在每个课程阶段采用分布级负偏好优化（NPO）目标，有效缓解大规模删除（mass deletion）下现有图遗忘方法的"灾难性遗忘"问题，在20%删除率下保留74%的原始效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图遗忘方法在大规模删除场景下模型效用急剧下降（称为灾难性遗忘）。根本原因是现有方法对所有被删除样本采用统一处理，忽视了图学习中结构依赖导致不同节点在模型学习中扮演的角色差异巨大。

Method: 提出CUNO框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于课程的渐进式遗忘——按照估计的遗忘难度对删除集合中的样本分阶段排序并逐步移除；(2) 每个课程阶段采用分布级负偏好优化（NPO）目标，引导模型远离当前遗忘子集上的原始行为，同时保持保留集性能。理论分析表明课程设计在遗忘集跨度大时最有效，而这恰好是大规模删除的天然特征。

Result: 实验证明CUNO有效缓解灾难性遗忘：在20%删除率下保留74%的原始效用（现有方法仅26-53%），在50%删除率下仍能保持超过一半的原始效用。

Conclusion: 通过将难度感知的课程学习引入图遗忘，CUNO解决了大规模删除下模型效用骤降的挑战，为该领域提供了新的方法论方向。

Abstract: Graph unlearning removes the influence of designated training data from a trained graph model without retraining from scratch. However, existing methods suffer a sharp drop in model utility under large deletion ratios (mass deletion), a phenomenon we refer to as catastrophic unlearning. We find that a key cause is the uniform treatment of all deleted samples, which is particularly damaging in graph learning: structural dependencies cause different nodes to play vastly different roles in the learned model, yet existing methods apply the same forgetting operation to the entire forget set. Based on this insight, we propose CUNO, a curriculum-based graph unlearning framework that removes the forget set progressively, ordering samples by their estimated unlearning difficulty across multiple stages. CUNO further employs a distribution-level negative preference optimization (NPO) objective at each curriculum stage that steers the model away from its original behavior on the current forget subset while preserving retained performance. Our theoretical analysis shows that the curriculum design is most beneficial when the forget set spans a wide range of unlearning difficulty, a condition naturally satisfied under mass deletion. Comprehensive experiments confirm that CUNO consistently mitigates catastrophic unlearning: at 20% deletion, it retains 74% of the original utility compared to 26-53% for existing methods, and maintains more than half the original utility even at 50% deletion. Our code is publicly available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/cuno-D4FF.

</details>


### [250] [Revisiting Spectral Representations in Generative Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08253)
*Yuehao Wang,Peihao Wang,Hanwen Jiang,Ziyi Yang,Qixing Huang,Zhangyang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从扰动核的统一视角出发，揭示了自监督谱表示学习与扩散生成模型之间的内在联系，提出将谱正则项整合到扩散训练目标中，以提升图像和3D点云等多样数据的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在各类生成任务中表现优异，近年研究发现对扩散网络隐状态施加表示对齐既能加速训练收敛又能提升采样质量，但其背后的协同机制尚不清晰，缺乏理论层面的理解。

Method: 1) 提出自监督谱表示对齐方法用于扩散模型训练；2) 从几何视角阐明联合谱学习如何促进扩散训练；3) 发现谱对齐目标的优化在表示空间中等价于扩散分数蒸馏；4) 基于上述发现将谱正则项集成到扩散训练目标中。

Result: 在图像和3D点云等多个数据集上的实验一致性地展示了生成质量的提升，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 该工作通过扰动核的统一视角建立了自监督谱表示学习与扩散生成模型之间的理论联系，证实谱正则化作为扩散训练的有效辅助手段可泛化应用于多种数据类型。

Abstract: Diffusion models have shown remarkable performance on diverse generation tasks. Recent work finds that imposing representation alignment on the hidden states of diffusion networks can both facilitate training convergence and enhance sampling quality, yet the mechanism driving this synergy remains insufficiently understood. In this paper, we investigate the connection between self-supervised spectral representation learning and diffusion generative models through a shared perspective on perturbation kernels. On the diffusion side, samples (e.g., images, videos) are produced by reversing a stochastic noise-injection process specified by Gaussian kernels; on the spectral representation side, spectral embeddings emerge from contrasting positive and negative relations induced by random perturbation kernels. Motivated by this, we propose a self-supervised spectral representation alignment method to facilitate diffusion model training. In addition, we clarify how joint spectral learning can benefit diffusion training from a geometric perspective. Furthermore, we find that the optimization of the spectral alignment objective is in an equivalent form of diffusion score distillation in the representation space. Building on these findings, we integrate a spectral regularizer into diffusion training objectives to improve the performance of diffusion models on multiple datasets. Experiments across images and 3D point clouds show consistent gains in generation quality. Code is released at https://github.com/yuehaowang/spectral-reg-diffusion.

</details>


### [251] [Synergistic Fusion of Topological Structure and Temporal Semantics of Mobility for Urban Region Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08268)
*Namwoo Kim,Jeeyun Chang,Kanghoon Lee,Yoonjin Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了MoSS框架，通过从移动数据中提取序列视图（每小时入/出流量剖面）和结构视图（基于zigzag持久性图捕捉区域连接随时间涌现/持续/消散），并利用协同模块通过多度交互显式捕获跨视图的高阶信号，在仅使用移动数据的情况下于多个城市感知任务中达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市区域嵌入方法虽融合移动数据与辅助模态数据，但存在两个不足：(1) 人类移动的时间动态特性（区域流量日内波动、区域间连接随时间涌现/持续/消散）未被充分利用；(2) 主流融合策略对视图进行加性组合，忽略了视图共同出现时才涌现的联合信号。

Method: 提出MoSS框架：从移动数据中提取两个互补视图——Sequence视图保留每区域每小时入/出流量序列，Structure视图基于zigzag持久性图捕获区域连接的时间演化（涌现/持续/消散）；设计协同模块通过多度交互从两视图共现中提取涌现表示，显式捕获跨视图的高阶信号。

Result: 在纽约市和芝加哥的大规模实验中，MoSS在三个下游任务上仅使用移动数据即达到最先进性能，优于依赖辅助模态的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作表明充分建模移动数据自身的时间动态与视图间协同交互，可以在不依赖辅助模态的情况下获得更优的城市区域表示，为轻量化、数据高效的城市感知提供了新思路。

Abstract: Urban region embeddings have shown promising results in diverse urban sensing tasks such as crime, income, and service-call prediction. Recent methods improve representation quality by integrating mobility data with auxiliary modalities, using cross-view attention or contrastive objectives to align heterogeneous features into a unified region representation. However, leveraging the temporal dynamics of human mobility remains under-explored. Regional inflow and outflow fluctuate throughout the day, and inter-region connections emerge, persist, and dissolve over time. Moreover, prevailing fusion strategies combine views additively and miss the joint signal that emerges only when views co-occur. To address these gaps, we propose Mobility Stream-Structure Synergy (MoSS), which derives complementary views from mobility data: a Sequence view that preserves each region's hourly inflow/outflow profile, and a Structure view based on zigzag persistence diagrams that capture how regional connectivity emerges, persists, and dissolves over time. A synergy module then extracts emergent representations from the co-occurrence of these views through multi-degree interactions, explicitly capturing higher-order signal across views. Extensive experiments on New York City and Chicago show that MoSS achieves state-of-the-art performance across three downstream tasks using mobility data alone, outperforming baselines that rely on auxiliary modalities.

</details>


### [252] [Online Signature Verification Using Augmented Path Signature and T-Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08276)
*Ruiling Li,Danyu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将增强路径签名（APS）描述符与T-Mamba模型相结合的在线签名验证（OSV）框架。APS通过时间和基点增强后计算滑动窗口路径签名，捕捉几何结构和非线性跨通道交互；T-Mamba采用双TCN块与时间扫描Mamba的混合设计，同时学习局部时间模式和全局长程依赖，在三个公开基准数据集上取得最优EER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在在线签名验证中虽被广泛采用，但在捕捉高判别力特征和建模长程依赖方面存在困难，亟需新框架解决这些问题。

Method: 提出一个集成APS描述符和T-Mamba模型的框架。APS先进行时间和基点增强，再计算滑动窗口路径签名；T-Mamba采用混合设计，结合两个TCN块与时间扫描Mamba，同时捕捉局部时序模式和全局长程依赖。

Result: 在MCYT-100、SVC-2004 Task 2和DeepSignDB三个公开基准数据集上取得最优等错误率（EER），尤其在训练数据有限的条件下表现出色。

Conclusion: 该框架有效提升了在线签名验证的准确性，并通过公开代码验证了其有效性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Handwritten signature verification is vital for personal authentication across commercial and financial applications. Although deep learning methods are widely adopted for online signature verification (OSV), they often struggle with capturing highly discriminative features and modelling long-range dependencies. To address these issues, we propose a novel framework that integrates the augmented path signature (APS) descriptor with the T-Mamba model. The APS descriptor first applies time and basepoint augmentations, then computes sliding-window path signatures. The path signature is a non-parametric feature map from rough path theory that effectively captures geometric structures and nonlinear inter-channel interactions. Inspired by the efficacy of state space models (SSMs) in sequence modelling, our T-Mamba model employs a hybrid design combining two temporal convolutional network (TCN) blocks with a time-scanning Mamba. This design enables the model to learn both local temporal patterns and global long-range dependencies, substantially improving verification accuracy. Our framework achieves state-of-the-art EERs on three public benchmark datasets (MCYT-100, SVC-2004 Task 2, DeepSignDB), validating its effectiveness and robustness, especially when the training data is limited. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/DLRL04/OSV-using-APS-and-T-Mamba.

</details>


### [253] [Adaptively Incorporating Directional Hints into Zeroth-Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08277)
*Alexander Ryabchenko,Jian Qian,Wenlong Mou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Control-Variate Zeroth-Order Descent（CV-ZOD）框架，利用廉价但可能不准确的方向提示（directional hints）来改进非凸函数的零阶优化。该方法通过控制变量精炼经典零阶梯度估计器，实现了在最优一阶O(1/T)速率和零阶O(d/T)速率之间的自适应插值收敛，且无需预先知道提示质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化在高维非凸问题上收敛较慢（O(d/T)），而一阶方法虽快（O(1/T)）但需要完整梯度信息。实际场景中存在廉价的方向提示（如局部低维子空间近似），但如何自适应利用这些可能不准确的提示同时保持鲁棒性是一个未解决的问题。

Method: 提出CV-ZOD框架，使用控制变量（control variate）精炼经典零阶梯度估计器，参考向量和步长可基于方向提示设置。理论上分析了最优参考向量和步长设置的oracle算法的收敛率，并开发了无需先验提示知识即可达到oracle保证（差对数因子）的实用变体。

Result: 理论上证明了oracle算法收敛率在一阶O(1/T)与零阶O(d/T)之间插值，取决于轨迹上提示的质量；实用变体在无先验知识下达到与oracle相同的保证（至多差对数因子）；在仿真科学优化任务上验证了方法在非凸环境中优于零阶下降，且比现有引导方法更鲁棒。

Conclusion: CV-ZOD有效弥合了一阶与零阶优化之间的差距，通过方向提示的控制变量实现了自适应且鲁棒的收敛性能，尤其适用于仿真科学优化等需要零阶方法的场景。

Abstract: We study zeroth-order optimization of non-convex functions with the aid of directional hints, which are cheap but potentially inaccurate approximations of the true gradient direction, given by linear subspaces at each iteration. To leverage these hints adaptively while maintaining robustness to their quality, we introduce Control-Variate Zeroth-Order Descent (CV-ZOD), a new framework that refines the classical zeroth-order gradient estimator with a control variate that can be set based on the directional hints. We first show that the oracle algorithm that optimally sets the reference vector and step size at each iteration achieves a convergence rate that interpolates between the first-order $O(1/T)$ rate and the zeroth-order $O(d/T)$ rate, depending on the quality of the hints along the trajectory. We then develop a practical variant of CV-ZOD that achieves the same oracle guarantee up to logarithmic factors, without any prior knowledge of the hint quality. We validate the method empirically on simulation-based scientific optimization tasks, demonstrating sustained progress on non-convex landscapes where zeroth-order descent is slower and existing guided methods stall as guidance deteriorates.

</details>


### [254] [HypLTSF: A Hyperbolic Geometric View of Multi-Scale Hierarchies for Long-Term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08286)
*Namwoo Kim,Hyungryul Baik,Yoonjin Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HypLTSF框架，将多尺度时间序列预测中的层次结构显式建模为双曲空间（Poincaré球）中的几何结构，通过径向约束和角度约束对齐时空层次，在长期预测基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期时间序列预测中的多尺度建模方法虽然能在不同尺度间交换信息，但层次结构本身往往只是交互的副产品，未被显式地当作几何结构来建模和利用。

Method: HypLTSF将各尺度的时间表示嵌入到Poincaré球中，利用其指数增长的体积自然适配层次结构。具体施加两个约束：(1)径向约束，根据抽象程度对嵌入排序；(2)角度约束，将共享相同粗尺度祖先的细粒度模式聚合在一起。

Result: 在长期时间序列预测基准上，HypLTSF取得了最先进的预测性能，表明将多尺度层次显式建模为几何结构对预测任务有效。

Conclusion: 显式地将多尺度时间序列中的层次关系建模为双曲几何结构，比让层次自然涌现更具优势，为长期预测提供了新的有效思路。

Abstract: Multi-scale modeling has become an effective approach for long-term time series forecasting, capturing temporal patterns that range from fine-grained local dynamics to coarse global trends. Representations across these temporal scales are inherently hierarchical, with coarser scales abstracting and aggregating information from finer ones. While existing approaches readily exchange information across these scales, the hierarchy itself is typically left as an emergent byproduct of such interactions rather than captured as a geometric structure in its own right. In this paper, we introduce HypLTSF, a framework that endows the multi-scale hierarchy with a concrete geometric form by embedding scale-wise representations into the Poincaré ball, whose exponentially expanding volume naturally accommodates hierarchical structures. To align this geometry with the temporal hierarchy, HypLTSF imposes two constraints: (1) a radial constraint that orders embeddings by their level of abstraction, and (2) an angular constraint that groups fine-scale patterns sharing a common coarser-scale ancestor. Extensive experiments on long-term time series forecasting benchmarks show that HypLTSF achieves state-of-the-art performance, suggesting that explicitly modeling the multi-scale hierarchy as a geometric structure is effective for forecasting.

</details>


### [255] [EMBLEM: Enhancing Multi-script Table Detection through Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08330)
*Dhruv Kudale,Udhay Brahmi,Ganesh Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MANDALA数据集（覆盖18种语言、15种文字的2,323页多语言文档表格检测数据集）和EMBLEM方法（基于掩码的多文字表格检测范式），通过掩码隐藏文字和字体特征，使模型聚焦于与文字无关的页面布局，仅使用英文掩码图像微调即在MANDALA上获得20.8%的F1绝对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习表格检测模型在英文和中文文档上表现良好，但在多语言、多文字文档上性能欠佳，主要原因是文字多样性以及标注数据稀缺。

Method: 提出EMBLEM（掩码式多文字表格检测范式），生成掩码图像以隐藏文字和字体细节，使预训练于丰富英文文档的模型聚焦于文字无关的页面布局；同时构建MANDALA多语言手工标注数据集。

Result: EMBLEM在三种表格检测架构上均优于MANDALA上的强基线；仅用英文掩码图像微调（不包含多语言训练数据）即在MANDALA上获得20.8%的绝对F1提升，且在五个英文主导基准上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 掩码策略有效解决了多语言、多文字文档的表格检测问题，验证了文字无关的布局信息在跨脚本场景下的迁移能力，数据集和代码已开源。

Abstract: Table detection is a core task in document analysis, supporting downstream applications such as information retrieval, document reconstruction, and visual question answering. While existing deep learning models perform well on English and Chinese documents, they struggle with multilingual, multi-script documents due to script diversity and the limited availability of labeled data. To address this challenge, we introduce MANDALA (Multi-script Annotated Documents for Table Detection), a manually curated dataset of 2,323 table-containing pages spanning 18 languages and 15 scripts across diverse domains. We also propose EMBLEM, a masking-based paradigm for Multi-script Table Detection (MTD). EMBLEM generates masked images that conceal script- and font-specific details, enabling models pre-trained on abundant English documents to focus on script-agnostic page layout. Experiments across three table detection architectures show that EMBLEM consistently outperforms strong baselines on MANDALA while remaining competitive on five standard English-dominant benchmarks. Using only English masked images for fine-tuning, with no multi-script training data, EMBLEM achieves an absolute F1-score gain of 20.8% on MANDALA. We release MANDALA along with the accompanying code and models at https://github.com/IITB-LEAP-OCR/EMBLEM.git.

</details>


### [256] [Distillation as Probability Transport: Routed On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08337)
*Tianle Xia,Lingxiang Hu,Yiding Sun,Linfang Shang,Ming Xu,Lan Xu,Ning Zheng,Wei Xu,Jie Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RouteOPD（Routed On-Policy Distillation），将在线策略蒸馏（OPD）重新定义为教师引导的概率传输问题，通过将局部教师-学生分歧分解为"学生冗余源"与"教师赤字目标"并构建显式传输对，利用有界教师势函数优化成对对数几率，从而更有效地实现知识蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的在线策略蒸馏（OPD）虽然利用学生生成的轨迹进行知识迁移，但高效的采样目标将教师分布降维为单个标记上的标量信用。这种信用只能表明某个标记应增加还是减少概率，却未指明相应的概率重分配方式，导致知识迁移方向不明确。

Method: 作者将OPD重新建模为教师引导的概率传输问题。具体而言，RouteOPD将局部教师-学生分歧分解为学生多余（source）和教师不足（destination）两个部分，并将二者耦合为显式的传输对。该方法通过有界教师势函数优化成对对数几率，使其趋向联合可实现的传输目标，同时根据教师需求的集中程度自适应调整传输预算。

Result: 在四种教师-学生设置和四个数学推理基准上的实验表明，RouteOPD在各项指标上持续优于基于采样反向KL的OPD方法，其性能提升伴随着更高的路由保真度和更低的背景泄漏。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了在在线策略蒸馏中显式建模概率传输的有效性，说明通过将知识迁移细化为源-目标传输对的方式，可以更精准地指导概率更新方向并控制更新幅度。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) transfers teacher knowledge on student-generated trajectories, but efficient sampled objectives reduce the teacher distribution to scalar credit on individual tokens. Such credit indicates whether a token should gain or lose probability, yet leaves the corresponding redistribution unspecified. We recast OPD as teacher-guided probability transport and propose RouteOPD (Routed On-Policy Distillation), which decomposes local teacher--student disagreement into student-excess sources and teacher-deficit destinations and couples them into explicit transport pairs. RouteOPD optimizes pairwise log-odds toward jointly realizable targets obtained from a bounded teacher potential, while adapting the transport budget to the concentration of teacher demand. This formulation directs updates toward teacher-preferred destinations and controls their magnitude within a single transport operator. Experiments across four teacher--student settings and four mathematical-reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that RouteOPD consistently outperforms sampled reverse-KL OPD, with improvements accompanied by higher routing fidelity and lower background leakage. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of explicitly modeling probability transport in on-policy distillation.

</details>


### [257] [TV-Regulated OPD: Direction Matters in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08341)
*Han Xiao,Yifan Niu,Dongyi Liu,Chang Luo,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TV-OPD方法，通过使用Total Variation（全变差）对token级优势函数进行整形，使其变得平滑且有界，从而解决主流在线策略蒸馏（OPD）方法在训练中监督信号高方差、噪声大导致不稳定的问题，实现更稳定的训练动态和更好的后期性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流在线策略蒸馏（OPD）方法在LLM后训练阶段的监督信号存在高方差和高噪声问题，导致训练过程不稳定。作者系统研究了影响性能的关键因素以及训练不稳定的根本机制，发现token级优势的符号信息已足够，且更平滑、有界的优势可以稳定训练。基于这些发现，需要一种新的方法来整形优势函数以提升训练稳定性。

Method: 作者首先系统分析了OPD中究竟什么因素真正影响性能以及训练不稳定的机制，发现仅保留token级优势的符号即可达到与标准OPD相当的性能。基于此，采用Total Variation（TV）对优势函数进行整形，提出TV正则化的在线策略蒸馏方法（TV-OPD），使优势函数平滑且有界，从而减少训练波动。

Result: 通过多组综合实验，在各种设置下TV-OPD在训练后期均一致地取得了更好的性能表现，并且相比标准OPD方法具有更低的方差，证明了其训练过程更稳定。

Conclusion: TV-OPD通过TV正则化对优势函数进行整形，利用有界且递减的优势值有效地稳定了LLM在线策略蒸馏的训练过程，在性能相当甚至更优的同时显著降低了训练波动，为OPD方法提供了一种鲁棒的改进方案。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) facilitates the transfer of knowledge from domain expert to student in the post-training phase of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, the supervision signals in mainstream OPD methods suffer from high variance and noise which is generally instable during training. In this work, we systematically investigated what really matters to the performance and the fundamental mechanisms behind the instability during training. We found that retaining only the sign of token-level advantages is sufficient to achieve the performance comparable to standard OPD. Meanwhile, smoother and bounded advantages can stabilize the training process without sacrificing its performance. These motivated us to shape the advantages using the Total Variation (TV) and propose a robust TV regulated On-Policy Distillation (TV-OPD) method. Benefiting from the bounded and diminished advantages, TV-OPD exhibits stable training dynamics and steady late-stage performance. We conducted comprehensive experiments and found that, across various settings, TV-OPD consistently achieved better performance and lower variance in the late-stage of training.

</details>


### [258] [Geometry-Aware Bayesian Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning on the Stiefel Manifold via Stein Variational Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08354)
*Quang-Duy Tran,Trung Le,Bao Duong,Phuoc Nguyen,Thin Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于奇异值分解因子化适配器，提出一种在Stiefel流形上执行Stein变分梯度下降（SVGD）的几何感知低秩适应方法，用于大型预训练模型的参数高效微调，同时支持不确定性量化并提供更好的校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何感知的低秩适应方法虽在子空间利用效率和去冗余方面表现优异，但其预测置信度可能过高，缺乏不确定性量化能力。

Method: 构建基于SVD分解适配器因子的框架，利用Stein变分梯度下降（SVGD）将低秩矩阵沿Stiefel流形传输以匹配目标分布，保留几何结构并支持推理过程中的多样化解。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在模型校准性能上表现优异，且相比在欧氏空间中制定的SVGD及相关不确定性估计方法，获得了更高的预测精度。

Conclusion: 几何感知的Stiefel流形SVGD方法不仅能有效利用低秩几何结构进行微调，还能通过不确定性量化获得校准良好的适配器，优于在欧氏空间中的同类方法。

Abstract: Several geometry-aware approaches to low-rank adaptation have emerged for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large pre-trained models. These methods aim to take full advantage of the geometric structure of low-rank manifolds for improving the efficiency in subspace utilization and reducing redundancy by enforcing orthogonality constraints during optimization. The strong empirical results of these techniques have motivated further study into whether predictions from such geometry-based adaptation methods could be overconfident. In this paper, we build on the singular value decomposition factorization of adapters to develop a framework based on Stein variational gradient descent (SVGD). In this formulation, the low-rank matrices are transported along the Stiefel manifold to match the targeted distributions while retaining their crucial geometric structure. Since this geometry-aware SVGD approach provides multiple solutions during inference, it supports uncertainty quantification and produces better-calibrated adapters on the Stiefel manifold. Extensive experiments show that our method delivers strong model calibration and attains higher prediction accuracy than SVGD and related uncertainty estimation methods that are formulated in Euclidean space.

</details>


### [259] [Miles v0.1: Production-Level Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08368)
*RadixArk,:,Tom Chen,Mao Cheng,Shi Dong,Kangrui Du,Yanbin Jiang,Jiajun Li,Yiming Li,Tao Lin,Yusheng Su,Andy Ye,Yueming Yuan,Zhichen Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Miles v0.1是一个全栈、生产就绪的前沿后训练系统，基于"组件应可验证、简洁、可定制"的核心原则构建RL训练循环的各个阶段，支持SGLang rollout引擎、两种训练后端（Megatron-LM和PyTorch FSDP）、多种权重同步传输方式，以及LoRA RL、on-policy蒸馏、SFT等能力，并扩展到扩散模型。端到端案例研究展示了在64块NVIDIA GB300 GPU上对GLM-5.2 744B-A40B模型进行完全异步agentic RL训练，中位步时263秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在使前沿规模的强化学习（RL）训练对研究者和企业更具可及性，同时解决现有系统在准确性、效率、可靠性和可扩展性方面的挑战，通过强调组件可验证、简洁和可定制来构建生产级RL训练基础设施。

Method: 基于slime的系统设计，Miles将RL训练循环每个阶段围绕"可验证、简洁、可定制"的原则构建；采用SGLang的rollout引擎；提供两种训练后端选择（NVIDIA Megatron-LM与PyTorch FSDP）；支持三种权重同步传输方式以适应不同部署拓扑；除全参数RL外还支持LoRA RL、on-policy蒸馏、SFT、真on-policy rollout-训练对齐以及扩散模型扩展。

Result: 通过对GLM-5.2 744B-A40B模型在终端编码任务上进行完全异步agentic RL训练的端到端案例研究验证：在64块NVIDIA GB300 GPU上运行，前30步的中位步时间为263秒。

Conclusion: Miles作为一个开源的全栈RL后训练系统，将准确性、效率、可靠性和可扩展性作为一等目标，为前沿规模RL训练提供了可访问的解决方案，并通过开源发布（GitHub与项目网站可获取）促进社区采用与进一步开发。

Abstract: We present Miles v0.1, a full-stack, production-ready system for frontier post-training. Building upon the clean design of slime, Miles designs each stage of the reinforcement-learning (RL) training loop around a single principle: components should be verified, clean, and customizable. With accuracy, efficiency, reliability, and scalability as first-class goals, Miles aims to make frontier-scale RL accessible to researchers and enterprises alike. This report walks through the system end to end: rollout engines built on SGLang, a trainer with a choice of two backends (NVIDIA Megatron-LM and PyTorch FSDP), and three weight-synchronization transports for different deployment topologies. Beyond full-parameter RL, Miles also supports LoRA RL, on-policy distillation, supervised fine-tuning, and true-on-policy rollout-training alignment, and extends the same architecture to diffusion models. We close with an end-to-end case study: fully asynchronous agentic RL on a GLM-5.2 744B-A40B model over terminal-use coding tasks, running on 64 NVIDIA GB300 GPUs with a median step time of 263 seconds over the first 30 measured steps. Miles is open-sourced at https://github.com/radixark/miles, with the project website at https://miles.radixark.com.

</details>


### [260] [IPM-FM: A Foundation Model with Consensus Feature Selection for Industrial Process Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08375)
*Liang Cao,Weide Liu,Yan Qin,Jun Cheng,Weisi Lin,Bhushan Gopaluni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出工业过程监测基础模型（IPM-FM），通过自监督预训练从无标签工业过程数据学习通用表征，再用少量任务标注数据进行适配，并通过不确定性感知预测头产生校准预测。在七年加氢处理装置柴油闪点软测量数据集上，IPM-FM取得了RMSE 2.99、R² 0.50以及95%预测区间97%覆盖率的结果，相比最强的经典基线和从零训练的序列基线分别提升了8.3%和14.6%的RMSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的工业过程监测采取"一个任务一个模型"范式，存在标签效率低下且在运行漂移下容易性能退化的问题。尽管基础模型已重塑语言、视觉和通用时间序列预测领域，但尚未被应用于工业过程监测，因为该领域面临安全关键决策和过程变量与实验室测量之间非对称采样等特殊挑战。

Method: IPM-FM包含四个核心组件：自监督Informer骨干网络用于从无标签数据学习通用表征；多准则共识特征选择器用于筛选关键特征；递归滞后特征回归头用于适应具体监测任务；校准蒙特卡洛dropout不确定性模块用于产生校准的预测。整体流程分为自监督预训练、少量任务标注数据适配、不确定性感知预测三个阶段。

Result: 在七年加氢处理装置柴油闪点软测量数据集上，IPM-FM的RMSE为2.99，R²为0.50，95%预测区间覆盖率达到97%。相比最强的经典基线提升8.3%的RMSE，相比从零训练的序列基线提升14.6%的RMSE。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持了统一"预训练-适配"框架用于工业过程监测的可行性，表明基础模型范式可以成功拓展到工业过程监测领域，为后续该方向的研究提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Industrial process monitoring is fundamental to the safety and economic performance of modern process plants. Current practice remains a one-task-one-model paradigm that is label-inefficient and prone to degradation under operating drift. Foundation models have reshaped language, vision, and generic time-series forecasting, but it has not been adapted to industrial process monitoring. This setting poses domain-specific challenges, including safety-critical decisions and asymmetric sampling between process variables and laboratory measurements. We propose the industrial process monitoring foundation model (IPM-FM). It first learns general-purpose representations from unlabeled industrial process data through self-supervised pretraining, then adapts to specific monitoring tasks using a small amount of task-labeled data, and finally produces calibrated predictions through an uncertainty-aware prediction head. IPM-FM integrates a self-supervised Informer backbone with a multi-criteria consensus feature selector, a recursive lag-feature regression head, and a calibrated Monte Carlo dropout uncertainty module. On a seven-year hydrotreater dataset for diesel flash-point soft sensing, IPM-FM attains an RMSE of 2.99, $R^2$ of 0.50, and 97\% coverage of its 95\% predictive interval, outperforming the strongest classical and from-scratch sequence baselines by 8.3\% and 14.6\% in RMSE respectively, supporting the viability of a unified pretraining--adaptation framework for industrial process monitoring.

</details>


### [261] [Geographically Regularized AUC-Maximizing Personalized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08379)
*Mayu Hiraishi,Kensuke Tanioka,Toshio Shimokawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GrAUC-PFL方法，一种地理正则化的AUC最大化个性化联邦学习方法，在保护患者数据隐私的同时，通过图基正则化利用地理邻近关系，直接优化平滑的成对AUC替代函数来训练个性化模型，以应对跨医疗机构的数据异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传染病暴发期间需要准确的诊断和风险预测模型，但患者级数据因隐私和治理要求无法跨机构共享，且各机构数据分布存在异质性。AUC作为广泛使用的判别性能评估指标，需要直接优化，但在联邦学习框架下直接优化AUC面临挑战。

Method: 提出GrAUC-PFL框架：1）直接优化平滑的成对AUC替代损失函数；2）采用个性化联邦学习以保留各机构的个性化模型；3）通过图基正则化（graph-based regularization）使地理相邻机构获得相似的系数向量，同时保证数据不出本地。

Result: 通过模拟研究和真实数据应用表明，该方法在判别性能上有改进，尤其是在地理相邻机构具有相似数据生成特征时效果更为显著。

Conclusion: GrAUC-PFL能够在保护患者数据隐私的前提下，利用地理邻近机构间的相似性来提升联邦学习模型的判别性能，为传染病暴发期间跨机构的临床决策支持提供了一种有效且有前景的方案。

Abstract: Accurate diagnostic and risk-prediction models are important for supporting clinical decision-making during infectious disease outbreaks. However, privacy and governance requirements may restrict patient-level data sharing across healthcare institutions, and data distributions often vary. Moreover, AUC is widely used to evaluate discriminative performance, motivating its direct optimization in model development. We propose geographically regularized AUC-maximizing personalized federated learning (GrAUC-PFL), which directly optimizes a smooth pairwise AUC surrogate to learn personalized models while keeping patient-level data local and accounting for institutional heterogeneity. Graph-based regularization encourages geographically neighboring institutions to have similar coefficient vectors while retaining a personalized models. Simulations and a real-data application suggest improved discriminative performance, particularly when geographically neighboring institutions have similar data-generating characteristics.

</details>


### [262] [Equivariance Breaks the Learning Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08381)
*Andrei Manolache,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现等变网络中Adam与Muon等矩阵结构优化器的性能差异源于等变线性层内部的逐块（irrep block）学习率失配问题，并提出一种无需新超参数的逐块归一化方法来修复Adam的更新尺度，使其与Muon性能匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变网络通常使用Adam训练，但近期研究发现Muon等矩阵结构优化器在这些架构上表现更优，且缺乏理论解释。作者旨在找出这一差异的根源，并弥合Adam与Muon之间的性能差距。

Method: 识别出等变线性层中每个irrep块的通道混合矩阵W_l共享于2l+1个分量（即W_l⊗I_{2l+1}），其梯度由2l+1个外积贡献求和而成，秩不超过2l+1。Adam逐个缩放权重而未考虑irrep边界，导致同一学习率在不同块间产生不同的谱步长。作者提出对每个块单独归一化更新尺度，保持Adam的动量估计和块内方向不变，仅改变更新的大小，且不引入新的超参数。

Result: 在受控的SO(3)等变模型和e3nn原子间势模型（rMD17和MD22数据集）上进行评估。玩具实验显示失配随宽度增长而扩大，配对稠密对照组无此增长。原子间势模型中，块归一化与调优Adam动量系数各自均能改进性能，但单独使用均不如Muon；两者结合后Adam在所有数据集上与Muon竞争，表明逐块步长控制和动量累积可解释Muon的大部分优势。

Conclusion: Adam在等变网络中的性能劣势主要源于忽略irrep块结构的逐权重缩放策略，通过逐块归一化并配合适当的动量调优，Adam可以达到与Muon相当的性能，这为等变网络优化器的设计提供了清晰的机制性解释。

Abstract: Equivariant networks are commonly trained with Adam, yet recent work reports that matrix-structured optimizers such as Muon can perform better on these architectures without explaining why. We identify one source of this difference inside equivariant linear layers. Each irrep block learns a channel-mixing matrix $W_l$ shared across its $2l+1$ components, giving the expanded map $W_l \otimes I_{2l+1}$. For a single application of the layer, the gradient of $W_l$ sums $2l+1$ outer product contributions and has rank at most $2l+1$. Adam rescales stored weights individually without using the irrep boundaries, so one learning rate can produce different spectral step sizes across blocks within a layer. We address this mismatch by normalizing each block update separately, without introducing a new hyperparameter. This changes only the scale of the update, leaving Adam's moment estimates and its direction within each block unchanged. We evaluate the mechanism in a controlled $\mathrm{SO}(3)$-equivariant model with a matched dense control and in an e3nn interatomic potential model trained on rMD17 and MD22. The toy setup isolates a mismatch that grows with width while the dense control shows no corresponding growth. In the interatomic potential model, block normalization and tuning Adam's momentum coefficients independently improve performance, but neither alone matches Muon. Combined, they make Adam competitive with Muon on all datasets, indicating that blockwise step control and momentum accumulation account for much of Muon's advantage.

</details>


### [263] [MLIP Detective: Active Failure Mode Discovery Beyond Benchmark Scores for Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08399)
*Ryuhei Okuno,Nontawat Charoenphakdee,Kaoru Hisama,Yuta Tsuboi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MLIP Detective，一个基于物理信息搜索的智能体框架，用于主动发现通用机器学习原子间势（u-MLIPs）的隐藏失效模式，弥补基准测试覆盖范围有限的问题。该框架成功识别并表征了MACE-MPA-0模型中的一个系统性异常，并推断出其可能的训练数据来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器学习原子间势（u-MLIPs）旨在跨多种配置泛化，但现有基准测试往往无法暴露超出其预定义范围之外的模型失效，需要一种能够主动发现隐藏失效模式的方法。

Method: 提出MLIP Detective智能体框架，采用物理信息搜索方法：首先基于基准测试证据生成可证伪的、具有物理依据的失效假设，然后通过低成本模拟进行筛选，最后仅将最可疑的案例升级给人类专家，并附带提出的验证协议。

Result: 无需针对特定问题的提示，MLIP Detective即识别出MACE-MPA-0模型的系统性异常：模型预测某些含O或F吸附物的弛豫吸附物-表面体系能量高于其对应的分离碎片（违反物理直觉）。通过跨模型比较，进一步推断该异常可能源于训练数据，与近期研究报告一致。

Conclusion: 物理信息搜索能有效补充基于基准测试的评估方法，为u-MLIPs提供自动化的隐藏失效模式发现与诊断途径，提升模型评估的可靠性。

Abstract: Universal machine-learning interatomic potentials (u-MLIPs) aim to generalize across diverse configurations. Benchmarks enable reproducible evaluation but may not expose failures outside their predefined scope. Here, we show that physics-informed search can complement benchmark-based evaluation by uncovering hidden failure modes. We introduce MLIP Detective, an agentic framework for active failure mode discovery. Starting from benchmark evidence, MLIP Detective generates falsifiable, physics-informed failure hypotheses, screens them with inexpensive simulations, and escalates only the most suspicious cases to human experts together with proposed verification protocols. Without issue-specific prompting, MLIP Detective identified and characterized a systematic anomaly in MACE-MPA-0: the model predicted some relaxed adsorbate-surface systems involving O- or F-containing adsorbates to be higher in energy than their corresponding separated fragments. Using cross-model comparisons, MLIP Detective further inferred a likely training-data origin for the anomaly, consistent with recent reports.

</details>


### [264] [Environments as Scaffold: Enriching Feedback to Bootstrap Self-Evolving Agents in Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08404)
*Hongbang Yuan,Zhuoran Jin,Yixin Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"环境侧自适应"范式，通过构建反馈增强环境（FEEs）来解决LLM作为智能体在RL训练中的奖励稀疏问题，在SciWorld和BFCL基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型虽然在静态推理上表现出色，但通过RL训练为长周期任务的自主智能体时面临严重的奖励稀疏问题。传统的智能体侧预热（SFT）受限于数据稀缺和探索受限，需要新的解决思路。

Method: 提出范式转移：从智能体侧预热转向环境侧适应，构建反馈增强环境（FEEs）。通过试点研究确定反馈设计策略，在后期阶段将环境从行动引导转变为观察增强，覆盖智能体内部探索和智能体间演化两个维度。

Result: 在SciWorld和BFCL基准上，使用多种Qwen3模型规模和RL算法（GRPO、GSPO、DAPO）进行大规模实验，FEEs持续优于标准设置。分析表明FEEs能稳定训练动态、促进主动状态空间探索、确保环境引导内化为策略权重，并发现组内反馈一致性是关键边界。

Conclusion: FEEs是一种有效且通用的环境侧适应方法，能从环境层面缓解RL训练中的奖励稀疏问题，为LLM智能体训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large Language Models demonstrate remarkable proficiency in static reasoning, yet training them as autonomous agents through Reinforcement Learning (RL) for long-horizon tasks is often hindered by severe reward sparsity. While conventional \textit{agent-side warming} up via supervised fine-tuning (SFT) can alleviate this, it is frequently limited by data scarcity and constrained exploration. To address this, we propose a paradigm shift to \textit{environment-side adaptation} by constructing \textbf{F}eedback-\textbf{E}nriched \textbf{E}nvironments (\textbf{FEEs}). Through a pilot study, we establish a feedback design strategy that reformulates environments by transitioning from action guidance to observation enrichment during the later stages of both intra-episode exploration and inter-episode evolution. Large-scale experiments on SciWorld and BFCL benchmarks using various Qwen3 model scales and RL algorithms such as GRPO, GSPO, and DAPO demonstrate that FEEs consistently yield performance improvements over standard settings. Furthermore, our analysis reveals that training with FEEs \textbf{(1)} stabilizes training dynamics by reducing entropy volatility, \textbf{(2)} facilitates proactive state-space exploration in difficult tasks, \textbf{(3) }ensures the internalization of environmental guidance into policy weights rather than acting as a mere inference-time prior, and \textbf{(4) }identifies intra-group feedback consistency as a critical boundary for stable optimization.

</details>


### [265] [Stochastically Perturbed Weights: Ensembles from Deterministic Machine-Learning Weather Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08412)
*Simon Adamov,Oliver Fuhrer,Reto Knutti,Sebastian Schemm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了从现有确定性机器学习天气预测模型中提取不确定性信息的方法，提出随机扰动权重（SPW）方案，即在推理时扰动网络权重张量以生成集合预报。在四个确定性主干模型（Aurora、GraphCast、SFNO、AIFS）上的三阶段消融实验表明，SPW集合在240小时预测时限的CRPSS仅比最佳训练概率模型低0.04-0.13，且无需额外训练成本。但该方法存在局限性：注入噪声的有效位置因模型架构而异，并非即插即用方案；其主要失败模式是产生相干的全场偏移导致域均值过度离散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已部署的机器学习天气模型大多是确定性的，无法提供自身的不确定性估计，而训练概率模型需要专门的训练过程。作者希望探索能否从已存在的确定性检查点中提取不确定性，而无需重新训练模型。

Method: 提出随机扰动权重（SPW）方案：受物理集合预报中随机扰动参数化趋势的启发，在推理时随机扰动网络原始权重张量。通过三阶段消融实验，在四个确定性主干模型（Aurora、GraphCast、SFNO、AIFS）上确定每个模型的生产基线，并与训练概率模型（AIFS-ENS、FourCastNet 3、Atlas）及业务ECMWF集合（IFS-ENS）进行基准对比，覆盖112个初始时间。

Result: 在240小时（10天）预报时效下，SPW集合的CRPSS比最佳训练概率基线仅低0.04到0.13，且边际训练成本为零。但不同模型的适用噪声注入位置各不相同，有效张量组因架构而异；主要失败模式是产生相干的全场偏移导致域均值过度离散，限制噪声至粗尺度或扰动初始条件可部分修复该问题。

Conclusion: SPW展示了从确定性MLWM中提取不确定性信息的可行路径，以零训练成本接近训练概率模型的性能，但当前仍是一种需要逐模型调参的流程而非通用即插即用方案，其全场偏移失败模式提示噪声尺度选择和初始条件扰动是重要的改进方向。

Abstract: Machine-learning weather models (MLWMs) now match or outperform operational numerical weather prediction (NWP) at global medium-range forecasting, at far lower inference cost. Many deployed MLWMs are deterministic, producing a single forecast with no estimate of its own uncertainty, whereas a growing family of trained-probabilistic models generate calibrated ensembles directly, at the price of a dedicated training run. We ask instead how much uncertainty can be extracted from a deterministic checkpoint that already exists, without retraining it. Where physical ensembles represent model uncertainty by stochastically perturbing parametrisation tendencies, we perturb the network's raw weight tensors at inference time, a scheme we call stochastically perturbed weights (SPW). We also ask whether it works, where and on which scales to inject the noise, and where it fails. A three-phase ablation across four deterministic backbones, Aurora, GraphCast, SFNO, and AIFS, selects one production baseline per model, benchmarked against the trained-probabilistic AIFS-ENS, FourCastNet 3 and Atlas as well as the operational ECMWF ensemble (IFS-ENS) over 112 initialisation times. At a 240 h (10-day) lead time the SPW ensembles reach continuous ranked probability skill scores (CRPSS) between 0.04 and 0.13 below the best trained-probabilistic baseline, at zero marginal training cost. No injection site works across models: the productive tensor group is architecture-specific, so SPW is at present a tuning procedure rather than a plug-and-play recipe. Its main failure mode is a coherent whole-field offset that overdisperses the domain mean, and restricting the noise to coarse scales or perturbing the initial conditions each repair part of it.

</details>


### [266] [Topological Fraud Detection in Latent Transaction Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08445)
*Avraham Bourla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于拓扑匿名化嵌入的欺诈检测方法，通过迭代的无监督过滤和有监督精准定位，实现超低延迟且保护隐私的欺诈筛查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在欺诈检测中，机构需要在保护个人身份信息（PII）的同时有效识别可疑活动，现有方法难以在隐私保护和检测效率之间取得平衡。

Method: 在完全拓扑匿名化的嵌入表示上进行操作，采用迭代式两阶段流程：先进行无监督过滤以缩小可疑范围，再进行有监督的精准定位（sniping）来标记欺诈行为。

Result: 实现了超低延迟的隐私保护型欺诈分诊，能够在不对个人身份信息造成泄露的前提下有效标记可疑活动。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法证明了在匿名化拓扑嵌入上进行迭代过滤与监督定位相结合的策略，能够在保护隐私与高效欺诈检测之间取得良好的平衡。

Abstract: Working entirely on topologically anonymized embeddings, we perform fraud detection using iterative rounds of unsupervised filtering followed by supervised sniping. The result is an ultra-low latency privacy--preserving triage that allows institutions to flag suspicious activity without compromising Personally Identifiable Information.

</details>


### [267] [Not All Variables Agree: Reliability-Aware Variable-Wise Gradient Surgery for Multivariate Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08554)
*Jinwoo Park,Hyeongwon Kang,Pilsung Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PV-Surgery优化器侧训练策略，针对多变量时间序列预测中均值损失隐藏变量间梯度冲突的问题，通过变量级梯度代理、可靠性感知层选择和公共方向手术，在五个骨干网络和七个数据集上平均降低MSE 3.61%和MAE 2.93%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列预测通常使用标量损失对所有维度进行平均优化，但优化器只能看到聚合梯度，无法判别各变量梯度贡献是否一致。研究发现30.6%的变量间梯度余弦相似度为负，且35/64的变量在共享训练下表现劣于单目标oracle，梯度冲突频率并不能可靠预测性能受损程度——说明均值损失下的梯度聚合掩盖了可用的变量结构信息。

Method: 提出Per-Variable Surgery（PV-Surgery），一种优化器侧训练策略，适用于具有cache兼容层的骨干网络。核心步骤包括：(1) 一次反向传播从输出端信号构建变量级梯度代理，保持逐点预测损失；(2) 可靠性感知选择，仅针对代理和与其共享梯度切片近似度高的层进行手术；(3) 条件池化构建anchor池和冲突池而不丢弃任何变量；(4) 公共方向手术将变量或池化后的梯度对齐到其归一化均值，并恢复输入范数以避免隐式重新加权。

Result: 在五个骨干网络、七个数据集和四个预测视野的实验中，PV-Surgery平均降低MSE 3.61%和MAE 2.93%。实验表明均值损失训练所隐藏的变量结构可以被提取为有效的优化信号。

Conclusion: 多变量预测中均值损失所掩盖的变量级结构是一个可用的优化信号。梯度冲突与性能损害并非等同，通过设计仅依赖输出端信号、保持原有损失函数的优化器侧干预，可以有效利用这一结构信息提升预测精度。

Abstract: In data-driven training, multivariate time-series forecasting is usually optimized with a scalar loss averaged over samples, variables, and horizons. This averaging is convenient, but the optimizer sees only the aggregated gradient, which does not reveal whether the variable-wise contributions align or oppose one another. To quantify how often this disagreement arises, we measure the variable-wise gradients directly and find that 30.6% of their pairwise cosine similarities are negative on average across seven datasets. However, conflict and harm are not the same thing. Under shared training 35 of the 64 variables do worse than a full-input single-target oracle, and the harmed fraction is not reliably predicted by how often gradients conflict. We propose Per-Variable Surgery (PV-Surgery), an optimizer-side training strategy for backbones with cache-compatible layers. One backward pass builds variable-wise gradient proxies from output-side signals and keeps the pointwise forecasting loss. Reliability-aware selection targets layers whose proxy sums closely approximate their shared-gradient slices. Conditional pooling forms anchor and conflict pools without dropping variables. Common-direction surgery aligns variable or pooled gradients with their normalized mean and restores input norms to avoid reweighting. In experiments across five backbones, seven datasets, and four horizons, PV-Surgery lowers MSE by 3.61% and MAE by 2.93% on average. For multivariate forecasting, this indicates that the variable-wise structure hidden by mean-loss training is a usable optimization signal.

</details>


### [268] [Certified Topological Interaction in Neural Representations: Class Disentanglement Is Mostly Pairwise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08561)
*Sushovan Majhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用相交欧拉特征值轮廓(Intersection Euler Characteristic Profile)来精确测量分类解纠缠，通过对111个训练网络和52,650项认证测量发现：解纠缠具有深度分级性且集中于首个epoch，类别三元组的联合纠缠低于其最强对的纠缠（97%的三层单元和99.5%的深层单元），这一成对支配是规律而非定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的类别解纠缠通常通过描述性曲线粗略观察，缺乏严格的拓扑测量方法和统计检验框架。作者旨在将解纠缠量化为标注点云之间的认证拓扑交互，为这一概念提供精确、可检验的数学基础。

Method: 采用相交欧拉特征值轮廓，即计算标注点云球并集的交叠欧拉特征值随尺度的函数，通过单次Alpha-complex扫描实现，无需边界矩阵约简。该方法支持精确置换检验（双向）、保护分离证书和配对检验。使用交互商对类别对进行可混淆度排名，并通过151个网络的大规模人群实验进行验证。

Result: 研究表明解纠缠具有深度分级性且集中在早期epoch；交互商以Spearman rho=0.83排名类别对的混淆度。在96模型因子设计中，只有数据增强能实现与机会水平相对的类别分离；权重衰减压缩重叠但未分离；深度和宽度无效果。成对支配性在97%的三层单元和99.5%的深层单元中成立。未归一化轮廓质量预测测试准确率R^2=0.94。

Conclusion: 成对支配性是神经表示中的一个结构性规律而非几何必然——它源于重叠的嵌套性但并非强制产生。关键教训是配对检验必须使用无尺度统计量，否则会把特征范数动态误认为解纠缠。

Abstract: Class disentanglement (the separation of a representation's class-conditional point clouds along depth and over training) is usually read off descriptive curves. We measure it as certified topological interaction between labeled point clouds, using the recently introduced Intersection Euler Characteristic Profile: the Euler characteristic of the overlap of the clouds' ball unions as a function of scale, computed by one Alpha-complex sweep with no boundary-matrix reduction. Every number carries a test: exact permutation tests in both directions, a guarded separation certificate, and a paired test for the comparative claims applications make. Across 111 trained networks and 52,650 certified measurements, disentanglement is depth-graded and concentrated in the first epochs, and interaction quotients rank class pairs by confusability (Spearman rho=0.83), on par with cheap separability statistics. In a 96-model factorial population, augmentation is the one training choice that separates classes relative to chance; weight decay compresses the overlap without separating, and depth and width do nothing. The structural finding is one only a k-fold statistic can pose: the joint entanglement of a class triple sits below that of its strongest pair in 97% of triple-layer cells and 99.5% of deep cells, far below a measured null floor, in vision encoders and frozen language models alike. This pairwise dominance is a regularity, not a law: expected from the nesting of overlaps but not forced by geometry, present at initialization and in raw pixels, and manufactured in the last stage alone when a network memorizes random labels. The unnormalized profile mass predicts test accuracy (R^2=0.94), the quotient does not, and neither beats a linear probe. One lesson is reported in full: the paired test must use a scale-free statistic, or it certifies feature-norm dynamics as disentanglement.

</details>


### [269] [AlphaRJM: Reward-Jump Memory for Stochastic Return-Guided Alpha Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08581)
*Sayan Dhan,Selvaraju Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AlphaRJM框架，通过"奖励跳跃记忆"（事件驱动的潜在状态）和"动作条件SDE回报评论家"（随机粒子建模未来折扣发现回报）来解决公式化阿尔法发现中延迟反馈导致的两个耦合难题，在多个股票宇宙和预测周期上取得稳定增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公式化阿尔法发现是依赖池的符号搜索问题，只有完整表达式被评估时才能获得信息性反馈。这种延迟反馈引发两个耦合困难：保留的阿尔法池未保留完整的已实现评估反馈历史，且中间构造动作的价值不确定（因其后果取决于最终完成的公式）。

Method: AlphaRJM包含两个核心组件：(1) Reward-Jump Memory —— 事件驱动的潜在状态，在token构造期间保持不变，仅在终端评估事件发生时使用已实现的池奖励和评估结果更新；(2) 动作条件SDE回报评论家 —— 用随机粒子表示未来折扣发现回报，通过均值与不确定性引导动作选择，并采用结合能量距离匹配、均值校准和跳跃正则化的分布Bellman目标进行学习。

Result: 在多个股票宇宙、预测周期和随机种子下，AlphaRJM取得了强劲且稳定的性能提升；消融实验证实了持久评估历史、随机回报建模和分布监督三者具有互补作用。

Conclusion: AlphaRJM通过引入事件驱动的记忆机制和分布式的随机回报建模，有效缓解了延迟反馈下的两个耦合困难，为公式化阿尔法发现提供了一种稳健且可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Formulaic alpha discovery is a pool-dependent symbolic search problem in which informative feedback is observed primarily when a complete expression is evaluated. This delayed feedback creates two coupled difficulties: the retained alpha pool does not preserve the full history of realized evaluation feedback, and the value of an intermediate construction action is uncertain because its consequence depends on the formula eventually completed. We introduce AlphaRJM, which addresses these difficulties through Reward-Jump Memory, an event-driven latent state that remains fixed during token construction and updates only at terminal evaluation events using the realized pool reward and evaluation outcome, and an action-conditioned SDE return critic that represents future discounted discovery returns with stochastic particles. The particles guide action selection through their mean and uncertainty and are learned using a distributional Bellman objective combining energy-distance matching, mean calibration, and jump regularization. Empirically, AlphaRJM delivers strong and stable gains across multiple equity universes, forecasting horizons, and random seeds, while ablations confirm the complementary roles of persistent evaluation history, stochastic return modeling, and distributional supervision.

</details>


### [270] [Leveraging Cardiac Imaging to Improve ECG-Based Detection of Chagas Disease in Resource-Constrained Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08582)
*Laura Alvarez-Florez,Daniel Uyterlinde,Samuel Ruipérez-Campillo,Lukas P. A. Arts,Folkert W. Asselbergs,Fleur V. Y. Tjong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过对比预训练将心脏磁共振（CMR）的结构知识迁移到心电图上（ECG）。利用UK Biobank中63,193对配对ECG-CMR数据，使用非对称InfoNCE目标对齐ECG编码器与CMR嵌入空间。尽管预训练时未见恰加斯病（Chagas）病例，该表示在多个外部数据集上仍显著提升了ECG检测恰加斯病的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恰加斯病是拉丁美洲心肌病的主要病因，CMR能表征其结构异常但扫描设备和专家读取在流行地区稀缺；ECG廉价普及但只能间接推断结构病变。因此希望通过无创且可及的ECG实现结构性心脏病的检测。

Method: 采用对比预训练策略：使用UK Biobank中63,193对配对的ECG-CMR数据，通过非对称InfoNCE损失函数将ECG编码器与基于临床的CMR嵌入空间对齐，实现跨模态知识迁移。

Result: 在CODE-15%和SaMi-Trop数据集上，冻结线性探测的AUROC达0.851，Top5%-TPR为0.427，优于未对齐ECG-FM基线的0.827和0.377。在PhysioNet/CinC 2025挑战赛测试集上取得SaMi-Trop-3最高AUROC及ELSA-Brasil最佳挑战分数。

Conclusion: 影像监督的ECG表征能够泛化到预训练分布之外的人群和场景，证明了跨模态对比学习在资源受限地区通过ECG检测结构性心脏病的可行性。

Abstract: Chagas disease is a major cause of cardiomyopathy in Latin America. Cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) imaging can characterize its structural abnormalities, but scanners and expert readers remain scarce in endemic regions. Electrocardiography (ECG) is inexpensive and widely available, yet structural disease must be inferred indirectly from electrical signals. We propose to transfer CMR-derived structural knowledge to ECG through contrastive pre-training. Using 63,193 paired ECG-CMR examinations from the UK Biobank, we align an ECG encoder with a clinically grounded CMR embedding space using an asymmetric InfoNCE objective. Despite seeing no Chagas cases during pre-training, the resulting representation improves ECG-based Chagas detection. Across CODE-15% and SaMi-Trop, a frozen linear probe achieves an AUROC of 0.851 and sensitivity at the top 5% of predicted risk (Top5%-TPR) of 0.427 in five-fold cross-validation, compared with 0.827 and 0.377 for an unaligned ECG-FM baseline. On the PhysioNet/CinC 2025 Challenge test set, our model obtains the highest AUROC on SaMi-Trop-3 and the best ELSA-Brasil challenge score among the three top-performing methods, indicating that imaging-supervised ECG representations can generalize to populations and settings beyond the pre-training distribution.

</details>


### [271] [Multi-Level-Set-Based Physics-Driven Neural Network to Solve 3-D Inverse Scattering Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08594)
*Yutong Du,Zicheng Liu,Bo Qi,Yali Zong,Peixian Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于水平集的物理驱动神经网络求解器（LSPDNN），用于三维电磁逆散射成像。通过软并集多材料模型和模型一致的全变分正则化，实现了清晰边界、均匀材料区域和低背景伪影的重建效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的体素级对比度重建方法存在边界模糊和重建伪影问题，无法充分利用实际散射体的分段均匀特性，亟需一种能够更好刻画目标几何结构的多材料表示方法。

Method: 采用多个坐标相关的神经水平集分量表示未知目标，通过软并集多材料模型分别描述物体支撑域和材料分布；全局支撑域由多个水平集分量的并集构成，局部对比度由归一化分量权重和可学习的复介电常数候选决定；对材料区域指示符施加模型一致的全变分正则化以避免过度平滑；采用自适应损失平衡策略减少人工选择正则化权重的依赖。

Result: 数值和实验结果表明，LSPDNN能够重建出具有清晰边界、更均匀材料区域和显著减少背景伪影的散射体，在不规则形状、近距离物体、多材料和测量噪声等挑战性三维逆散射场景中展现出明显优势。

Conclusion: 神经水平集参数化方法在挑战性三维逆散射问题中具有显著优势，可有效提升重建质量并减少人工参数调优负担。

Abstract: This paper proposes a level-set-based physics-driven neural network solver (LSPDNN) for 3-D electromagnetic inverse scattering. To mitigate boundary blurring and reconstruction artifacts in voxel-wise contrast reconstruction, the proposed solver exploits the piecewise homogeneity of practical scatterers by representing unknown targets with multiple coordinate-dependent neural level-set components. Specifically, a soft-union multi-material model is proposed to separately describe the object support and material distribution. The global support is formed by the union of multiple level-set components, while the local contrast is determined by normalized component weights and learnable complex permittivity candidates. In addition, a model-consistent total variation (TV) regularization is imposed on the material-region indicators, rather than directly on the reconstructed contrast, to suppress fragmented material assignments without excessively smoothing material interfaces. An adaptive loss balancing strategy is further introduced to reduce the dependence on manually selected regularization weights. For each measurement instance, the neural level-set parameters and material candidates are optimized by minimizing a physics-consistent objective function. Numerical and experimental results demonstrate that LSPDNN can reconstruct scatterers with clear boundaries, more uniform material regions, and substantially reduced background artifacts. The results highlight the advantage of the neural level-set parameterization in challenging 3-D inverse scattering cases involving irregular shapes, closely spaced objects, multiple materials, and measurement noise.

</details>


### [272] [Why shared attention vectors fail: a case for outcome-indexed tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08615)
*Lenard Dome*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多结果学习条件下，维度注意力向量（全局共享注意力向量）的不稳定性问题，提出了一种以结果索引的注意力矩阵（outcome-indexed attentional matrix）作为解决方案，并通过理论分析和三个合成实验证明了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多结果学习（即模型需要预测多个结果）的情境下，原本广泛使用的全局共享注意力向量会发生坍缩（collapse to its bounds），导致模型无法学习到有意义的注意力调谐，从而阻碍了学习与泛化性能。这一稳定性缺陷缺乏系统的分析与解决方案。

Method: 作者首先对共享注意力向量不稳定的条件进行了理论分析；然后引入一种结果索引的注意力矩阵，将原本全局共享的标量注意力转换为按结果进行索引的矩阵形式，使得每个结果拥有独立且可学习的注意力权重；最后通过三个合成实验对两种注意力机制进行了对比评测。

Result: 理论分析给出了共享向量不稳定的成立条件；三个合成实验表明，结果索引的注意力矩阵能够收敛到有意义的注意力表示，而全局共享的注意力向量在这类任务中失败。

Conclusion: 结果索引的注意力矩阵是对基于梯度下降的注意力过程的一种通用修复方案，能够显著改进模型在多结果条件下的学习表现，有望成为多结果学习任务中注意力机制的标准设计。

Abstract: Dimensional attention in learning is often implemented as a globally shared attention vector, where each stimulus dimension corresponds to a single scalar. These scalars are learned by models through gradient-descent on error, where predictive features acquire more salience. We show that under multi-outcome learning, where models predict more than one outcome, this shared vector becomes unstable; it collapses to its bounds and prevents the models from learning meaningful attentional tunings for learning and generalization. We address this by introducing an outcome-indexed attentional matrix that converts globally shared attentional tuning into an outcome-indexed representation. We present an analysis of the unstable shared vectors and derive the conditions under which it holds. Empirically, three synthetic experiments benchmark the proposed attention matrices and show that they converge to meaningful representations, something shared attention vectors fail to do. These results suggest that outcome-indexed attentional matrices are a general fix for gradient-based attentional processes, which improves models of learning under multi-outcome conditions.

</details>


### [273] [Target-Independent Micro-Interventions for Predicting Training Response Across Language-Model Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08618)
*Zhongxuan Liu,Sicheng Zhou,Hongzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"L-State"框架，通过在检查点执行多个短的标准目标无关微干预，测量当前能力基准无法捕捉的"训练响应状态"，并验证其在多个模型上显著提升了对模型后续训练响应的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准分数只能描述检查点当前的能力水平，无法预测该检查点在下一个训练episode中会如何响应。这种缺失的状态信息阻碍了对模型训练轨迹的准确预测与理解。

Method: 从同一检查点分支出四个短小、标准化、目标无关的微干预，在共享能力空间中记录其效果；结合当前能力构成L-State，提供直接读取算子和保持结构的算子读取两种读出方式；在平滑局部动力学假设下建立跨家族边界。

Result: 在三家族留一交叉验证中，两种脉冲读出使源标准化MSE降低39.4%。在GLM-4-9B上MSE分别降低71.8%和78.3%，符号平衡准确率从0.366提升至0.754。在Granite-3.1-8B上直接读取达到RMSE 0.544。五家族审计发现算子坐标因动作和家族而异。

Conclusion: 目标无关的微干预能够暴露当前能力指标所遗漏的训练响应信息，直接与结构化读出各覆盖互补的迁移场景，为理解模型训练动态提供了新工具。

Abstract: Benchmark scores describe what a checkpoint can do now, but they do not determine how it will respond to the next training episode. We measure this missing state by branching four short, standardized, target-independent micro-interventions from the same checkpoint and recording their effects in a common capability space. Together with current capability, these responses form L-State; its pulse block supports a flexible direct readout and a structure-preserving operator readout. Under smooth local dynamics, the operator construction admits an end-to-end cross-family bound with explicit source- and target-family coordinate heterogeneity. In three-family leave-one-family-out development, both pulse readouts reduce source-standardized MSE by 39.4% relative to capability alone, while separating the best response and direction estimates. On sealed GLM-4-9B, the direct and operator readouts reduce MSE by 71.8% and 78.3%, respectively, and the operator readout raises sign balanced accuracy from 0.366 to 0.754. On sealed Granite-3.1-8B, the direct readout reaches RMSE 0.544 and a development-fitted action-wise selector reaches 0.554, compared with 1.172 for capability alone. A five-family audit finds that the operator coordinate varies by action and family, and that modeling these deviations improves retrospective held-trajectory prediction. Target-independent interventions therefore expose training-response information that current capability misses, with direct and structured readouts covering complementary transfer regimes.

</details>


### [274] [Leveraging contextual events on structure-aware next activity prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08622)
*Alessandro Mele,Claudia Diamantini,Domenico Potena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于实例图（Instance Graphs）的预测性过程监控方法，通过多种编码策略将上下文过程实例信息嵌入图结构中，并利用图神经网络进行下一活动预测。实验表明，融入上下文信息能够有效提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测性过程监控中的下一活动预测虽然研究广泛，但现有方法很少显式编码过程执行的环境上下文信息（上下文过程实例），因此需要探索如何有效利用这些信息来提升预测准确性。

Method: 提出基于Instance Graphs的方法，设计多种编码策略来融入上下文过程实例信息；针对每种编码策略生成前缀实例图（prefix-Instance Graphs），并将其输入到图神经网络（GNN）中执行下一活动分类任务。

Result: 在多个真实世界事件日志上进行了实验评估，结果表明融入上下文过程实例能够显著提升预测性能。

Conclusion: 上下文过程实例的显式编码对预测性过程监控中的下一活动预测有帮助，基于Instance Graphs和GNN的方法是有效的实现途径。

Abstract: Predictive process monitoring aims at forecasting various aspects of running processes. Among the different tasks, next activity prediction represents the most extensively investigated. However, only a limited number of existing approaches explicitly encode contextual information, i.e., the environmental conditions in which the process is executed, typically modeled through event log attributes or aggregated measures. In this paper, an approach based on the concept of Instance Graphs is introduced. To incorporate contextual process instances, several encoding strategies are proposed and evaluated by measuring their impact on prediction performance. For each encoding strategy, a set of prefix-Instance Graphs is generated and subsequently provided as input to a Graph Neural Network for the classification task. The proposed approach is evaluated on multiple real-world event logs, and the experimental results demonstrate that incorporating contextual process instances benefits prediction performance.

</details>


### [275] [Suan: Rectifying Direct Preference Safety Alignment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08634)
*Oleksandr Cherednichenko,Roman Klypa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Suan，一种新型的偏好优化算法，直接在梯度层面构建优化目标，替代标准的变分推导，从而在保持响应实用性的同时实现更强的大语言模型安全对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重模型在安全控制上难以匹敌专有系统，后训练变体常出现过拒答和通用质量下降的问题，需要一种既能保证安全对齐又不损害响应质量的训练方法。

Method: Suan算法直接在梯度层面公式化优化目标，绕过了标准的变分推导过程，从而获得更可解释、更鲁棒的训练动态。

Result: 在一系列竞争性基线和基准的广泛评估中，Suan在安全对齐方面表现优越，同时完全保留了响应实用性。

Conclusion: Suan提供了一种更优的安全对齐方案，能有效克服过拒答和通用质量下降等问题，帮助开放权重模型实现类似专有系统的可靠安全控制。

Abstract: Integrating robust safety guardrails into Large Language Models (LLMs) is essential for delivering helpful yet harmless responses. While proprietary systems exhibit reliable safety controls, their underlying methodologies and trade-offs remain largely undisclosed. Achieving comparable security in open-weight models remains a persistent challenge, as post-trained variants frequently suffer from over-refusal and degraded general quality. To overcome these drawbacks, we introduce Suan, a novel preference optimization algorithm. Unlike existing methods, we formulate the optimization objective directly at the gradient level, bypassing the standard variational derivation. As a result, we obtain more interpretable and robust training dynamics. Extensive evaluations across a diverse suite of competitive baselines and benchmarks demonstrate that Suan achieves superior safety alignment while fully preserving response utility.

</details>


### [276] [SUN: Reaching for Novelty in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08642)
*Wenyan Yang,Arsenii Mustafin,Dominik Baumann,Joni Pajarinen,Simone Parisi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SUN（SUccessor-to-Novelty）框架，通过后继价值函数派生指标来同时评估目标的"新颖性"与"可达性"，可无缝集成到任何离策略RL算法中，并在多个基准环境中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标条件强化学习中，新颖性与可达性这两个关键信号往往被手动权衡、顺序应用或完全忽略其一，缺乏同时显式整合两者的统一框架。

Method: 提出基于后继价值函数导出的SUN指标，结合自适应目标选择策略与轻量级伪计数方法，实现新颖性与可达性的联合评估。

Result: 证明了SUN在极限情况下可恢复基于计数的奖励、能约束短视界命中概率并理论上拒绝不可达目标；在标准及新颖环境（含不可达状态、不可逆转移、障碍、迷宫及无界空间）中一致优于最先进方法。

Conclusion: SUN为离策略RL提供了一种可泛化的、同时兼顾新颖性与可达性的目标选择框架，兼具理论保障与实证优势。

Abstract: Exploration in reinforcement learning (RL) remains a fundamental challenge. Recent goal-conditioned RL strategies (which select goals to encourage broader state coverage) have shown promising results, but none scores a goal by novelty and reachability jointly: the two signals are traded off by hand, applied in sequence, or one is neglected outright. In this paper, we introduce a reachability-aware goal-selection framework that explicitly integrates these two aspects, and that can be seamlessly incorporated into any off-policy RL algorithm. To this aim, we propose SUccessor-to-Novelty (SUN), an indicator derived from successor value functions to identify goals that are both novel and reachable. We prove that SUN recovers count-based bonuses in the limit, bounds short-horizon hitting probabilities, and provably rejects unreachable goals. We further present an adaptive goal-selection strategy that leverages these properties, and an accurate yet lightweight pseudocount to avoid the overhead of classic methods. We back up all our claims with thorough benchmarks: SUN consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in standard and novel environments with unreachable or hard-to-reach states, irreversible transitions, obstacles, mazes, and unbounded spaces.

</details>


### [277] [Difficulty-Adaptive Tree-Structured Policy Optimization for Expanding Reasoning Coverage in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08650)
*Youngjun Yu,Sanghwan Jang,Hwanjo Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DATPO方法，通过难度自适应树搜索和句熵引导的分支策略来扩大训练时rollout的语义多样性，从而提升大语言模型在数学推理中的pass@k指标，进而改善测试时扩展性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽能显著提升单样本准确率，但因训练时探索受限，往往无法扩展模型内在的推理覆盖范围（pass@k），这限制了模型在测试时扩展场景下的表现。

Method: 通过分析确定了三个关键设计原则：(1)难度自适应rollout对扩展pass@k至关重要；(2)基于树的rollout优于并行采样；(3)句熵引导的树分支优于token级分支。在此基础上提出DATPO，整合难度自适应树搜索与同层多样性的优势项，显式促进语义多样性。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的实验表明，DATPO在pass@k指标上优于基线方法，且这直接转化为更优的测试时扩展性能。

Conclusion: 通过优化训练时rollout的结构设计（而非仅优化奖励或策略更新方式），可以有效扩大推理覆盖范围，为RLVR在推理任务中释放更大潜力提供了新路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has been central to the recent success of Large Reasoning Models. However, while RLVR significantly improves single-sample accuracy, it often fails to expand the model's intrinsic reasoning coverage (pass@k) due to limited exploration during training. To address this, we optimize the structural design of train-time rollouts to enhance pass@k. Our analysis identifies three key design principles: (1) difficulty-adaptive rollout can play an important role in expanding pass@k, beyond serving as an efficiency heuristic; (2) tree-based rollout outperforms parallel sampling in discovering correct answers; and (3) sentence-entropy-guided forking overcomes the localization phenomenon of token-level branching to maximize semantic diversity. Building on these insights, we propose DATPO (Difficulty-Adaptive Sentence-entropy-guided Tree-structured Policy Optimization). DATPO integrates difficulty-adaptive tree search with a sibling-diversity advantage term, explicitly promoting semantic diversity to expand reasoning coverage during training. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that DATPO outperforms baselines especially in pass@k, which directly translates to superior test-time scaling performance.

</details>
