### [278] [MoEMB: Scaling Universal Multimodal Embeddings with Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08663)
*Xuanming Cui,Shlok Kumar Mishra,Wentao Bao,Aashu Singh,Zihao Wang,Xiangjun Fan,Jun Xiao,Ser-Nam Lim,Jianpeng Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MOEMB，通过混合专家（MoE）沿专家维度扩展通用多模态嵌入模型，在保持单向量、非自回归编码的同时提升模型容量，仅用3B激活参数即可超越需要4倍以上参数的TTE方法，并在MMEB-V2和MRMR基准上达到最先进水平，同时首次系统研究了MoE嵌入的自适应计算方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UME模型的扩展方法存在诸多问题：增加表示维度或检索开销难以满足延迟约束；将编码器扩展为重型多模态LLM成本过高；直接增加参数会牺牲对比学习所需的大批量训练；而UME任务复杂度多样，统一放大模型会造成大量冗余计算。因此需要一种既能提升模型容量又保持高效推理的扩展方案。

Method: 提出MOEMB方法，沿专家轴（通过混合专家MoE）扩展UME模型，在不依赖长文本推理或自回归生成的前提下增长编码器容量。作者对MoE嵌入模型的设计空间和训练配方进行了系统研究，并首次开展了涵盖训练式和纯推理式多种策略的自适应计算综合研究，以进一步优化可扩展性和效率。

Result: 在MMEB-V2和MRMR基准上，MOEMB在公开MMEB族数据训练的所有模型中达到最先进水平。仅用3B激活参数即可超越需要4倍以上激活参数的TTE方法，且计算开销显著更低。自适应计算策略进一步提升了基于MLLM的嵌入模型在大规模检索和推荐系统中的效率。

Conclusion: 专家扩展是通用多模态嵌入模型有效且高效的发展方向，MOEMB在显著减少计算开销的同时取得更优性能，结合自适应计算可实现面向大规模检索和推荐系统的高效扩展。

Abstract: Universal multimodal embedding (UME) increasingly demands encoder's capacity for handling a broad range of tasks and modalities with increased complexity. Prior scaling methods either increase the representation size, retrieval effort, or scales the encoder into a heavy multimodal LLM. Recent works, such as Think-Then-Embed (TTE), explore scaling via reasoning tokens. However, embedding models are hard to scale up: increasing parameters directly tradeoffs for the large training batch size that contrastive learning needs, and retrieval has to be served under tight latency. Moreover, UME tasks are diverse in complexity, where scaling up embedders can bring significant redundant computation. In this work, we propose MOEMB, which instead scales UME along the expert axis through mixture-of-experts (MoE), growing encoder capacity while preserving single-vector, non-autoregressive encoding. Through a systematic study of the design space and training recipes for MoE-based UME, MoEMB sets a new state of the art on both MMEB-V2 and MRMR among models trained on public MMEB-family data: with only 3B active parameters, MoEMB surpasses TTE-based methods with >4x active parameters, using significantly less computes. To further improve the scalability and efficiency, we conduct the first comprehensive study of adaptive computation for MoE-based embedding, spanning diverse strategies across training-based and inference-only methods. Together, these results support expert scaling as an effective and efficient direction for UME, with adaptive computation further improving efficiency for MLLM-based embedding models towards large-scale retrieval and recommendation systems.

</details>


### [279] [Neither Adversarial Training Nor Purification: Emergent Adversarial Robustness from Oscillatory Predictive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08683)
*Mohammed-Yassine Habibi,Klea Ziu,Martin Takáč,Makoto Yamada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需对抗训练或测试时净化即可实现对抗鲁棒性的两阶段框架OPL，结合人工Kuramoto振荡神经元与预测性自监督预训练，在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上取得了有竞争力的鲁棒准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练和测试时对抗净化虽然有效，但都引入了显著的计算开销。作者希望探索是否可以通过架构设计和表示学习的归纳偏置来获得经验鲁棒性，从而避免这些昂贵的计算负担。

Method: 提出两阶段框架Oscillatory Predictive Learning (OPL)，第一阶段结合人工Kuramoto振荡神经元(AKOrN)，第二阶段使用X-PhiNet进行预测性自监督预训练。默认检查点使用随机初始振荡器状态，与其他随机化对抗防御方法进行比较。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上达到76.63±0.76%的鲁棒准确率，在CIFAR-100上达到50.44%的鲁棒准确率，均在l∞, ε=8/255, AutoAttack-rand (EoT K=20)协议下取得，同时还在CIFAR-10-C上进行了额外的腐败鲁棒性评估。

Conclusion: OPL证明无需显式对抗训练或测试时净化，仅通过架构和表示学习层面的归纳偏置即可获得具有竞争力的对抗鲁棒性，为高效鲁棒学习提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Adversarial robustness in computer vision is still largely achieved through adversarial training or test-time adversarial purification, both of which introduce significant computational overhead by generating adversarial examples during training or performing iterative denoising at test time. We study whether empirical robustness can instead emerge from architectural and representation-learning inductive biases. We introduce Oscillatory Predictive Learning (OPL), a two-stage framework that combines Artificial Kuramoto Oscillatory Neurons (AKOrN) with predictive self-supervised pretraining using X-PhiNet. Because our default checkpoint uses randomized initial oscillator states, we compare it with other randomized adversarial defense methods that provide precise, reproducible, and strong attack protocols. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, with additional corruption evaluation on CIFAR-10-C, demonstrate that our method achieves competitive results under the AutoAttack-rand evaluation protocol. On CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, OPL attains 76.63$\pm$0.76$\%$ and 50.44$\%$ robust accuracy, respectively, under $\ell_\infty$, $ε=8/255$, AutoAttack-rand with EoT $K=20$.

</details>


### [280] [HOPE: Heterophily-Aware Open-Set Node Classification with Pseudo-Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08685)
*Yumeng Dai,Yue Tan,Yixin Liu,Chenxu Wang,Pinghui Wang,Tao Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HOPE方法，解决异配图上的开放集节点分类问题。HOPE通过结构增强的特征初始化层和多跳结构模式捕获、可信邻居聚合机制动态过滤异质邻居，以及异配引导的伪外推策略合成伪未知类代理，最后通过联合分类和logit边界正则化实现可靠的未知类拒绝。实验表明HOPE在多个数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放集节点分类方法依赖同配假设（连接的节点共享标签），但在现实世界的异配图中，跨类连接频繁出现，导致现有方法失效。具体面临两个挑战：一是跨类连接导致已知类和未知类表征在聚合后纠缠，削弱判别能力；二是结构混合使基于阈值的开放集方法和跨类特征插值失效，导致未知类拒绝不可靠。

Method: HOPE包含三个核心组件：(1) 结构增强的特征初始化层，用于捕获多跳结构模式；(2) 可信邻居聚合机制，动态过滤噪声的跨类邻居；(3) 异配引导的伪外推策略，动态维护已知类中心并沿跨类邻域位移方向外推，在结构模糊区域附近合成伪未知类代理。最后通过联合分类和logit边界正则化优化网络，将合成代理路由到专用的拒绝槽中。

Result: 在多个数据集上的大量实验表明，HOPE在开放集节点分类任务上持续优于现有的最先进方法，验证了其有效性、鲁棒性和效率。

Conclusion: HOPE成功地将开放集图神经网络适配到异配场景，通过结构感知的特征初始化、可信的邻居聚合和异配引导的伪外推策略，有效解决了异配图中未知类拒绝的可靠性问题，为异配图上的开放集节点分类提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Standard open-set node classification methods rely on the homophily assumption, where connected nodes share labels. However, real-world graphs are often heterophilic, exposing the limitations of current methods and posing new challenges to open-set node classification. On the one hand, cross-class connectivity causes representations from different known or unknown classes to become intertwined after aggregation, undermining their discriminative capacity. On the other hand, structural mixture invalidates threshold-based open-set methods and cross-class feature interpolation, leading to unreliable unknown-class rejection. To address these challenges, we propose HOPE, a Heterophily-aware Open-set node classification method with Pseudo-Extrapolation. To adapt open-set graph neural networks (GNNs) to heterophilic scenarios, HOPE uses a structure-augmented feature initialization layer to capture multi-hop structural patterns. Meanwhile, we design a trustworthy neighborhood aggregation mechanism for standard GNNs to dynamically filter noisy cross-class neighbors. To enhance unknown-class rejection, we introduce a heterophily-guided pseudo-extrapolation strategy. It dynamically maintains known-class centers and extrapolates along cross-class neighborhood displacement directions, synthesizing pseudo-unknown proxies near structurally ambiguous regions. Finally, we optimize the network with joint classification and logit margin regularization, routing synthetic proxies into a dedicated rejection slot without imposing geometric margin constraints in the representation space. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets show that HOPE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models, validating its effectiveness, robustness, and efficiency.

</details>


### [281] [Hyperparameter Scaling Laws Across MoE Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08690)
*Changxin Tian,Kunlong Chen,Jia Liu,Ziqi Liu,Zhiqiang Zhang,Jun Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过1800次预训练实验（约20万亿token，20万等效H800 GPU小时），发现超稀疏MoE模型的超参数（学习率、batch size）随激活比率变化，揭示了两种缩放机制，并提出统一超参数缩放定律，可跨MoE稀疏度、模型规模和专家粒度进行转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型在增加容量时无需等比增加训练计算，但提高稀疏度使超参数转移变得困难。现有超参数缩放定律在超稀疏MoE上失效，最优学习率和batch size随激活比率变化，无法仅用总参数量或激活参数量解释，需要新的统一缩放规律。

Method: 作者进行了1800次预训练运行，覆盖6个激活参数规模和最高60亿总非嵌入参数的模型（总参数最高120亿），总计约20万亿token。通过实验发现：在固定稀疏度下，最优batch size与训练token数D呈幂律关系，最优学习率与训练计算量C呈幂律关系。跨稀疏度时，激活比率A作为额外的乘性幂律因子进入二者关系，最终构建统一超参数缩放定律。

Result: 统一缩放形式在大型评测中优于其他备选函数形式。在12B总参数、只激活1/64专家的超稀疏MoE上，预测超参数接近实际最优值。实验还验证了跨专家粒度的转移能力，并分离了激活比率与总专家数的影响。

Conclusion: 文章调和了以往研究中的矛盾结论，揭示了两个缩放区域，提出了包含激活比率A的统一超参数缩放定律，支持跨模型规模和稀疏度的联合外推，为训练超稀疏MoE模型提供了可靠的超参数选择方法。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models expand model capacity without a proportional increase in training compute, but increasing sparsity makes reliable hyperparameter transfer challenging. In this work, we show that conventional hyperparameter scaling laws are insufficient for ultra-sparse MoEs: the optimal learning rate and batch size vary with activation ratio, and these shifts cannot be explained by either total or activated parameter count alone. To characterize this dependence, we conduct 1,800 pre-training runs spanning six activated-parameter scales and models with up to 6B total non-embedding parameters, processing approximately 20 trillion tokens at a cost of 200,000 equivalent H800 GPU-hours. Our results reconcile conflicting findings in prior work by revealing two scaling regimes. At fixed sparsity, the optimal batch size follows a power-law relationship with training tokens $D$, whereas the optimal learning rate scales with training compute $C$ and remains robust to the allocation between model size and data. Across sparsity levels, the activation ratio $A$ enters both relationships as an additional multiplicative power-law factor. These observations lead to unified hyperparameter scaling laws that transfer across MoE sparsity levels. Large-scale evaluation shows that the scaling form outperforms alternative functional forms. On a held-out ultra-sparse MoE with 12B total parameters and only 1/64 of its experts activated, the predicted hyperparameters remain close to the observed optima, supporting joint extrapolation across model scale and sparsity. Further experiments demonstrate transfer across expert granularities and isolate the effect of activation ratio from that of total expert count.

</details>


### [282] [Chimaera: A Mixture-of-Graph-Experts Architecture for Cross-Task and Cross-Dataset Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08709)
*Jonathan Frank,David Richerby,Ansgar Scherp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chimaera是一个将混合专家（Mixture-of-Experts）与图基础模型（GFM）相结合的框架，通过整合不同的GFM架构（如图提示和线性GNN模型），利用大语言模型生成嵌入，支持节点、链接和图级别的任务，并在六个文本属性图基准数据集上验证了其跨任务和跨数据集的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计用于图的基础模型具有挑战性，因为图具有不规则的拓扑结构，且嵌入的大小和特征各不相同。现有方法难以统一处理多种图任务和不同数据集，需要一种能够灵活整合多种图模型架构并实现跨任务、跨数据集迁移的通用框架。

Method: Chimaera采用混合专家（MoE）策略，将不同的图基础模型架构（如图提示和线性GNN模型）作为专家进行集成。利用大型语言模型生成嵌入，专家可根据不同策略进行训练和组合。此外，扩展了现有线性GNN以支持除节点级任务之外的链接级和图级任务。

Result: 在六个文本属性图数据集的节点、链接和图分类任务上进行同任务和跨任务实验，结果验证了Chimaera的有效性及其跨任务和跨数据集的迁移能力。同时发现：需要同时使用大型和小型语言模型为专家生成嵌入；简单但有效的线性GNN具有较强的跨任务迁移性；仅需少量样本即可获得强结果。

Conclusion: Chimaera作为一种混合专家驱动的图基础模型框架，成功实现了多种图任务上的有效集成和迁移学习，为图基础模型的设计提供了新的思路和实证依据。

Abstract: Designing foundation models for graphs is challenging due to the irregular structure of graphs and the different sizes and characteristics of embeddings. Chimaera integrates mixture-of-experts with graph foundation models (GFM). It integrates different GFM architectures, such as graph prompts and linear GNN models. Large language models are used to generate embeddings, and experts can be trained and combined following different strategies, GFMs, embeddings, etc. Furthermore, Chimaera extends existing linear GNNs to support link-level and graph-level tasks in addition to node-level tasks. Empirical analyses are performed on same-task and cross-task experiments with node, link, and graph classification tasks using six benchmark text-attributed graph datasets. The experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of Chimaera and its capabilities for transfer across tasks and datasets. Further insights include the need to use both large and small language models to generate embeddings for the experts, a strong cross-task transferability of simple but effective linear GNNs, and using few samples only to provide strong results.

</details>


### [283] [BAFF: Bid-Aware Filter Family for Mitigating Training Data Interference in RTB A/B Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08725)
*Jeonglyul Oh,Ikkyu Choi,Inseop Youn,Youngjae Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对RTB在线A/B测试中对照组与实验组模型共享服务日志导致的数据偏差问题，提出了Bid-Aware Filter Family (BAFF)——一类参数化的硬过滤器族，用于在日志分割（保留数据但偏差大）与日志共享（保留偏差但数据全）之间提供结构化搜索空间，并设计了三阶段在线测量协议来评估数据共享策略与无干扰参考模型的偏差。离线模拟和在线DSP部署均验证了BAFF在保持业务指标方面优于两种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RTB在线A/B测试中，对照组和实验组模型在共享服务日志上训练，由于对方模型选择不同广告或出不同价格，造成训练数据偏差，扭曲测试结果。现有方案日志分割消除偏差但牺牲数据量，日志共享保留数据但偏差未解决，缺乏两者之间的折中方案。

Method: 提出BAFF过滤器族，用(k,l)参数独立控制对广告排序分歧和出价定价分歧的容忍度，形成从日志共享到日志分割之间的结构化搜索空间；同时提出三阶段在线测量协议，通过无干扰参考模型评估各数据共享策略在生产环境中的偏差程度。

Result: 离线模拟中，(k,l)参数扫描找到了比日志共享和日志分割偏差更小的操作点；在线DSP实际部署中，基于过滤器的变体在保留参考模型的业务指标（如CPC、CTR）上优于两个基线；最佳操作点依赖于具体设置。

Conclusion: BAFF提供了一种可调节的中间方案，弥补了日志共享与日志分割之间的空白。由于最佳操作点因场景而异，该搜索空间本身具有重要的实用价值，可帮助实践者在数据量与偏差之间灵活权衡。

Abstract: In online A/B tests for real-time bidding (RTB), control and treatment models are typically trained on a shared serving log that includes data generated by the counterpart model. This shared-log training biases each model's training data through two channels: the counterpart model may have selected a different ad from the ad-candidate pool (ad-ranking disagreement) and may have bid a different price (bid-pricing disagreement), potentially distorting the A/B test outcome. Log-splitting eliminates the bias but sacrifices training data; log-sharing retains all data but leaves the bias unaddressed. We formalize the Bid-Aware Filter Family (BAFF), a class of (k,l)-parameterized hard filters that controls tolerance to each channel independently, providing a structured search space between these two extremes. We further propose a three-stage online measurement protocol that enables evaluating data-sharing strategies by their deviation from an interference-free reference model in production. In offline simulation, a (k,l) sweep surfaces operating points with smaller deviation from the interference-free reference model than both log-sharing and log-splitting. In a live RTB deployment on a demand-side platform (DSP), filter-based variants preserve the reference model's business metrics (e.g., CPC, CTR) more closely than both baselines. The best operating point is setting-dependent, underscoring the practical value of the search space itself.

</details>


### [284] [PAC-Bayesian Bounds for Learning Partially Observed Stochastic Linear Time-Invariant State-Space Systems with Inputs and Sub-Gaussian Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08740)
*Mihaly Petreczky,Mohamad Al Ahdab,John Leth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为部分可观测的线性时不变（LTI）随机动态系统推导了PAC-Bayesian误差界，该界将预测误差的期望与模型在训练数据上产生的预测误差联系起来，并可扩展用于参数估计误差的有限样本界推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习中广泛使用PAC-Bayesian界来刻画从有限数据学习到的模型的预测能力，但此类界在部分可观测的LTI随机动态系统领域尚缺乏系统性的推导，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 针对带输入和次高斯噪声的状态空间形式的LTI系统，推导PAC-Bayesian误差界；利用该界进一步导出参数估计误差的界，从而为一类广泛的系统辨识算法提供有限样本误差界。

Result: 成功推导出部分可观测LTI系统的PAC-Bayesian预测误差界，并展示了该界可用于推导参数估计误差界，为多种系统辨识算法提供有限样本保证。

Conclusion: 该误差界不仅适用于LTI系统，还可作为通向循环神经网络（RNN）PAC-Bayesian界的第一步，因为LTI系统是RNN的一个子类。

Abstract: In this paper we derive a Probably Approximately Correct (PAC)-Bayesian error bound for partially observed linear time-invariant (LTI) stochastic dynamical systems in state-space form with inputs and sub-Gaussian noise. Such bounds are widespread in machine learning, and they are useful for characterizing the predictive power of models learned from finitely many data points. The bound derived in this paper relates the expectation of prediction errors with the prediction error generated by the model on the data used for learning. In addition, we show that it can also be used to derive bounds for the parameter estimation error. In turn, this allows us to provide finite-sample error bounds for the prediction error and parameter estimation error for a wide class of system identification algorithms. Furthermore, as LTI systems are a sub-class of recurrent neural networks (RNNs), these error bounds could be a first step towards PAC-Bayesian bounds for RNNs.

</details>


### [285] [Adaptive Anisotropic Attention for Axis-Structured Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08788)
*Mahir Jain,Parshva Runwal,Aditya Ray Mishra,Arvasu Kulkarni,Sandeep Singh,Siddharth Panwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应各向异性注意力（AAA），将注意力分解为时间轴路径和空间轴路径，并通过门控机制为每个token学习两路径的凸组合权重。该模型AXON在六个EEG下游任务上优于稠密注意力基线，且轴分解的归纳偏置可迁移至音频频谱图任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密自注意力的全token对同概率交互的先验是各向同性的，与结构化信号（如EEG）不匹配。EEG信号具有低信噪比，其依赖关系沿电极轴和时间轴组织，均匀先验使每个token暴露于大量无关交互中，降低学习效率。

Method: 将注意力拆分为两条路径：时间路径（token关注同一电极在不同时间步的token）和空间路径（token关注同一时间步其他电极的token）。一个小型门控网络为每个token预测两路径输出的凸组合（两个非负权重之和为1），实现自适应的各向异性注意力。

Result: 在六个EEG下游任务上，AXON在两种线性探测和全微调设置下均优于稠密基线；消融实验证明时间路径和空间路径均必要，加权和优于硬性选择单一路径；门控在不同网络层学习到不同的时间/空间平衡是该模型主要收益来源；轴分解可迁移至音频频谱图任务。

Conclusion: 将注意力与结构化信号的自然坐标轴对齐能提供有效的归纳偏置，有助于提升低信噪比结构化信号上的模型性能。

Abstract: Dense self-attention treats all token pairs as equally plausible before learning, an interaction-isotropic prior that can be mismatched to structured signals. For structured, low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) signals such as EEG, dependencies are organized along the electrode and time axes, and this uniform prior exposes each token to many irrelevant interactions. We introduce Adaptive Anisotropic Attention (AAA), which splits attention into two paths: a temporal path, where each token attends to the tokens of its own electrode across time, and a spatial path, where it attends to the tokens of the other electrodes at the same time step. A small gate predicts, for every token, a convex combination of the two path outputs: two non-negative weights that sum to one. On six EEG downstream tasks, the resulting model, AXON (AXis-factorized Operator Network), improves mean balanced accuracy over a dense baseline under both linear probing and full fine-tuning. We show that both paths (temporal and spatial) are necessary and that the weighted sum beats a hard choice of one path; most of the benefit comes from the gate learning a different temporal/spatial balance at each layer of the network. Controlled audio spectrogram experiments show that axis factorization transfers beyond EEG. These results suggest that aligning attention with the natural axes of structured signals provides a useful inductive bias.

</details>


### [286] [Eliciting Weak-to-Strong Generalization with On-Policy Reverse Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08798)
*Youngrok Park,Sangmin Bae,Hojung Jung,Jongwoo Ko,Yunseon Choi,Young Jin Kim,Pashmina Cameron,Aaron Courville,Se-Young Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出On-Policy Reverse Distillation (OPRD)方法，通过评估教师策略相对其参考策略在学生轨迹上的偏移，并放大学生验证器驱动的策略梯度沿该方向的分量，在不改变策略优化驻点的情况下加速学习超越教师，在连续模型迁移和多教师蒸馏场景中均优于现有RL和蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱到强泛化问题关注更强模型能否从较弱监督者学习并超越之，这对连续模型代际更迭和多领域整合尤为关键，因为从头重复进行前沿规模的后训练成本过高。然而传统蒸馏将弱教师视为优化目标，可能将教师的能力上限强加给学生。

Method: OPRD的核心方法包括：(1) 在学生轨迹上评估教师策略与其参考策略之间的策略偏移方向；(2) 仅放大学生验证器驱动的策略梯度沿该教师偏移方向的投影分量；(3) 通过仅重缩放验证器支持的更新，保留策略优化的驻点特性，同时加速超越教师的学习进程。

Result: 在连续模型迁移和多教师蒸馏场景中，OPRD以更少的学生更新次数取得了比现有RL和蒸馏方法更高的性能。响应式分析显示OPRD学生更接近仅用验证器RL训练的模型，而非弱教师，表明教师指导起到加速而非改变学生自身优化方向的作用。在强到弱蒸馏中也验证了OPRD能有效结合验证器驱动优化与教师指导，不受能力顺序限制。

Conclusion: OPRD通过重新设计弱到强蒸馏中教师引导的方式，将其从硬性优化目标转变为策略梯度的加速信号，既保留了学生自身优化轨迹，又突破了教师能力天花板，为节省重复前沿训练成本提供了可行的蒸馏范式。

Abstract: Weak-to-strong generalization asks whether stronger models can learn from weaker supervisors and surpass them. This question is particularly important for successive model generations and multi-domain consolidation, where repeating frontier-scale post-training from scratch can be prohibitively expensive. Yet conventional distillation treats the weak teacher as an optimization target, potentially imposing its capacity ceiling on the student. We introduce On-Policy Reverse Distillation (OPRD), which evaluates the teacher's policy shift relative to its reference policy on student rollouts and amplifies the component of the student's verifier-driven policy gradient along that direction. By rescaling only verifier-supported updates, OPRD preserves the stationary points of policy optimization while accelerating learning beyond the teacher. In both successive model transfer and multi-teacher distillation, OPRD achieves higher performance with fewer student updates than existing RL and distillation approaches. Response-style analysis shows that OPRD students remain closer to models trained with verifier-based RL alone than to their weak teachers, suggesting that teacher guidance accelerates rather than redirects the student's own optimization. Results in conventional strong-to-weak distillation further demonstrate that OPRD effectively combines verifier-driven policy optimization with teacher guidance regardless of capacity ordering.

</details>


### [287] [Length Generalization for Transformers via Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08851)
*Georg Zetzsche,Hongjian Jiang,Andy Yang,Pascal Bergsträßer,Marco Sälzer,David Chiang,Anthony W. Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文细化了C-RASP假设，利用C-RASP+和C-RASP1片段，将原本双指数级别的样本复杂度上界指数级收紧，并证明了通过压缩字符串可以多项式级别泛化，且解决了针对C-RASP假设的矛盾实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C-RASP假设在经验上得到验证，但存在理论问题：缺乏可计算的长度泛化边界，且存在看似矛盾的实验结果。C-RASP+和C-RASP1片段虽提供可计算边界，但最坏情况下需要极大的双指数样本量，其紧性为开放问题。

Method: 通过提供指数级更紧的样本复杂度上界来解答开放问题，并利用与幂词（power words）的新颖联系，证明采用压缩字符串时Transformer可实现多项式长度泛化边界。将此应用于C-RASP猜想的细粒度分析。

Result: 成功将样本复杂度界从双指数收紧为单指数，证明了压缩字符串下Transformer的多项式长度泛化界，并通过对C-RASP猜想的细粒度分析解决了与之矛盾的经验证据。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅收紧了Transformer长度泛化的理论边界，还通过细粒度分析调和了理论与实验之间的矛盾，进一步巩固和完善了C-RASP假设的理论基础。

Abstract: Recent advancements in transformer length generalization theory enable us to reliably predict when a transformer can learn to solve a task. In particular, the C-RASP hypothesis (a formalized version of the so-called RASP-l conjecture) posits that transformers length-generalize on a task if and only if a solution is expressible in the C-RASP language. While this hypothesis has strong empirical validation, theoretical problems arise from the fact that no computable length generalization bounds exist for C-RASP, alongside the discovery of seemingly contradictory experiments. To address these problems, we refine the C-RASP hypothesis utilizing the recently-proposed fragments C-RASP+ and C-RASP1. These fragments have computable length generalization bounds, though in the worst case requiring an extremely large (double exponential) sample size. It is an open question whether these sample size bounds are tight. In this paper, we resolve this open question by providing an exponentially tighter bound. In doing so, we show a polynomial length generalization bound for transformers if we adopt compressed strings, via a novel connection to power words. As an application, we show how this yields a fine-grained analysis of the C-RASP conjecture that resolves contradicting experimental evidence against it.

</details>


### [288] [Earth System World Model for What-If Simulations: A Case Study for Terrestrial Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08855)
*Zhihao Wang,Ruichen Wang,Ruohan Li,Lei Ma,George Hurtt,Xiaowei Jia,Gengchen Mai,Shaowen Wang,Yiqun Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向地球系统仿真器的"行动条件世界模型"框架，通过将自然的系统状态变化作为无标签行动监督（transition-action pretraining），并引入掩码响应学习机制，使机器学习仿真器在保持高精度长时程仿真的同时，支持用户指定的干预操作和可控制的系统响应，推动从被动仿真向交互式、可干预的科学替代模型演进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习地球系统仿真器多为"被动预测器"，仅能在给定强迫条件下复现仿真轨迹，缺乏支持用户指定干预的显式交互机制，难以满足交互式科学工作流和地球系统数字孪生的需求。用户需要探索当部分状态变量被改变时系统如何响应的能力。

Method: 提出行动条件世界建模框架：(1) 转换行动预训练——将自然观测到的状态变化视为无标签的行动监督，使模型同时学习预设动力学和行动条件响应，无需人工标注干预；(2) 掩码响应学习——在部分状态编辑下推断未观测变量，学习耦合系统依赖关系。

Result: 在跨越六个全球区域和多个林龄的生态系统动力学测试中，模型在保持竞争性长时程仿真精度的同时，实现了可控的结构性干预和对耦合生态系统-碳循环变量的连贯响应。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，该框架提供了一条从被动地球系统仿真器迈向交互式、具备干预感知能力的科学替代模型的实际可行路径。

Abstract: Machine learning emulators have become essential for accelerating expensive Earth-system simulations, but most existing approaches remain passive forecasters: they reproduce simulator trajectories under prescribed forcings without an explicit interaction mechanism for user-specified interventions. This limits their use in interactive scientific workflows and Earth-system digital twins, where users often need to explore how a system would respond if selected state components were changed. We propose an action-conditioned world-modeling framework for Earth-system emulation that reformulates simulator trajectories as supervision for controllable state-transition learning. The key idea is transition-action pretraining: naturally observed state changes are treated as label-free action supervision, allowing the model to learn both prescribed dynamics and action-conditioned responses without manually annotated interventions. We further introduce masked response learning to infer unobserved variables under partial state edits and learn coupled system dependencies. We test this framework on ecosystem dynamics across six global regions and multiple stand ages. Experiments show that the model preserves competitive long-horizon emulation accuracy while enabling controllable structural interventions and coherent responses in coupled ecosystem-cycle variables. These results suggest a practical route from passive Earth-system emulators toward interactive, intervention-aware scientific surrogates.

</details>


### [289] [The BatchNorm Illusion: Diagnosing Normalization Artifacts in Machine Unlearning Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08901)
*Aaryaman Kalani,Murari Mandal,Dhruv Kumar,Mohan Kankanhalli,Yash Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个此前未被记录的混淆因素：在基于BatchNorm架构的机器学习遗忘评估中，仅对保留数据进行一次前向传播（不修改任何权重）即可确定性重写模型的归一化状态并逆转表面上的遗忘效果。作者将该操作形式化为权重保持的不动点算子，并证明其产生的评估差距完全归因于BN运行统计量而非遗忘方法对权重的修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似机器遗忘旨在不重新训练的情况下移除特定训练数据对模型的影响，但现有评估方法在BatchNorm架构上存在被忽视的评估偏差（confound），可能导致对遗忘方法性能的误判。

Method: 将单次前向传播重写归一化状态的操作形式化为权重保持的不动点算子；通过该框架证明遗忘前后的评估差距可归因于BN运行统计量伪影，并进一步将线性探针的提升分解为BN测量偏差与编码器几何成分两部分。

Result: 该伪影在九个评估方法上使遗忘准确率的头条指标逆转多达78个百分点；仅需10张未标记图像的攻击者即可恢复大部分被掩盖的准确率；采用严格GroupNorm对照组可将所有方法的伪影降至零；重校准后成员推断攻击变化很小。

Conclusion: 评估失败主要定位在遗忘准确率和线性探针上，测量伪影（BN伪影）与编码器失败可被清晰分离，GroupNorm可作为消除该评估偏差的有效手段。

Abstract: Approximate machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific training data from a trained model without retraining from scratch. We identify a previously undocumented confound in how unlearning is evaluated on BatchNorm-based architectures: a single forward pass over retain data, an operation that modifies no weight, can deterministically rewrite the model's normalization state and reverse the apparent surface-metric forgetting. We formalize this operation as a weight-preserving fixed-point operator and prove that any pre-versus-post gap it induces is provably attributable to BN running statistics rather than to any modification the unlearning method made to the weights. This attribution claim cleanly separates measurement failure (BN artifact) from encoder failure (residual weight-encoded information, recently documented in concurrent work), and the same operator framework yields a unique decomposition of linear-probe elevation into BN-measurement-bias and encoder-geometry components. Empirically, the artifact reverses headline forget accuracy by up to 78 pp across nine evaluated methods on standard benchmarks; an attacker with as few as 10 unlabeled images recovers most of the masked accuracy; and a strict GroupNorm control reduces the artifact to zero across all methods. The tested membership-inference attacks change little under recalibration, locating the observed evaluation failure in forget accuracy and linear probing.

</details>


### [290] [Transformers as In-Context Samplers: From Closed-Form Diffusion to Estimation-Free Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08981)
*Arman Adibi,Alireza Jafari,Mohammad Ghavamzadeh,Hadi Daneshmand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明冻结的Transformer不仅能进行上下文学习（如线性回归等监督任务），还能通过上下文样本模拟迭代式生成采样器（如扩散采样器和基于能量的采样器），实现数据生成。作者还通过语义主题采样实验，观察到预训练语言模型隐藏状态呈现U形几何/能量模式，与理论构造一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明大语言模型具备上下文学习能力，但已有理论工作主要聚焦于监督学习任务（如线性回归）。一个悬而未决的问题是：冻结的Transformer是否也能通过上下文样本进行数据生成（而非仅推理/预测）？这需要在理论上证明并实证验证。

Method: 作者首先从理论上证明Transformer可以构造闭式及平滑闭式扩散采样器，其中softmax注意力层计算责任权重和加权经验均值，前馈层实现Euler更新步骤。然后作者证明Transformer可以近似基于能量的采样器。在实证方面，作者研究"语义主题采样"任务：从同一语义类别（如动物、食物、城市）中抽取词作为提示，分析预训练模型各层隐藏状态在归一化后的几何结构和粒子间能量模式。

Result: 理论上证明了Transformer可以模拟迭代式生成采样器（扩散采样器和能量采样器）。实证发现预训练语言模型在语义主题采样中呈现两阶段几何：中间层隐藏状态趋向均匀球面参考分布，输出层附近回归到结构化、依赖主题的表示。粒子间相互作用能量沿层呈现U形模式，与理论构造的基于能量的采样器所产生的U形能量模式一致。

Conclusion: 该工作将上下文学习的理论边界从监督推理扩展到数据生成，证明Transformer可以通过上下文样本进行生成式采样。同时，softmax注意力在生成中承担了具体的生成性角色（计算责任权重与加权经验均值），且预训练模型的实证行为（U形几何/能量模式）与理论构造相吻合，表明理论结果与实际预训练模型之间存在有意义的关联。

Abstract: A growing body of work establishes that large language models are not mere statistical memorizers, but are capable of in-context learning: performing inference at test time using only examples provided in the prompt, without any parameter updates. Prior theoretical work has shown that this capability extends to supervised learning tasks such as linear regression. We prove that in-context learning extends further to \emph{data generation}: frozen transformers can simulate iterative generative samplers from in-context samples. We first show that transformers can realize closed-form and smoothed closed-form diffusion samplers. The construction identifies a concrete generative role for softmax attention: it computes responsibility weights and weighted empirical averages, while feedforward layers implement Euler updates.
  To empirically relate these constructions to pretrained language models, we study \emph{semantic-topic sampling}: prompts consisting of words drawn from a common semantic category, such as animals, foods, or cities. Across transformer layers, the normalized hidden states exhibit a two-stage geometry: they move toward a uniform spherical reference in intermediate layers and then return to structured, topic-dependent representations near the output. We further measure an interacting-particle energy on these hidden-state clouds and observe the same U-shape pattern. We then prove that transformers can approximate an energy-based sampler, constructing the same U-shape energy across the layers.

</details>


### [291] [Physics-Informed Deep Learning for False Ventricular Tachycardia Alarm Reduction in the ICU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08992)
*Athanasios Papastathopoulos-Katsaros,Alexandra Stavrianidi,Zhandong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合1D SE-ResNet、ICU真实数据增强和基于三元件Windkessel血流动力学模型的物理信息辅助重建任务的深度学习框架，用于减少ICU中虚假室性心动过速（VT）报警。该方法在VTaC基准的实时协议下比现有最优方法提升了5分Challenge Score，消融实验证明物理信息目标是性能的主要驱动力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICU中虚假的室性心动过速（VT）报警是导致"报警疲劳"的主要因素之一，亟需提高VT检测的准确性来减少不必要的临床干扰。

Method: 构建深度学习框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 1D SE-ResNet骨干网络；(2) 基于ICU真实场景的数据增强策略；(3) 以三元件Windkessel血流动力学模型为物理约束的可微分前向模拟辅助重建任务，要求网络潜在表征能生成生理上合理的动脉血压波形，从而惩罚由伪影驱动的ECG模式。

Result: 在VTaC基准严格实时协议（10秒报警前窗口）下，该方法比先前最先进方法获得5分Challenge Score提升。消融实验表明物理信息目标是主要性能驱动因素，在准确性上带来增益、实现2倍标签效率提升，并产生更局部化且临床意义更明确的ECG片段。

Conclusion: 物理信息深度学习框架有效减少了ICU虚假VT报警，验证了将血流动力学先验知识融入网络训练的可行性，为临床报警管理提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: False ventricular tachycardia (VT) alarms are a leading contributor to alarm fatigue in intensive care units. We propose a deep learning framework combining a 1D SE-ResNet with ICU-realistic data augmentations and a physics-informed auxiliary reconstruction task based on the three-element Windkessel hemodynamic model, implemented as a differentiable forward simulation. By requiring the network's latent representation to produce physiologically plausible arterial pressure waveforms, artifact-driven ECG patterns are penalized while true VT remains coherent across modalities. Evaluated on the VTaC benchmark under a strict real-time protocol (10-second pre-alarm window), our method achieves a 5-point Challenge Score improvement over prior state-of-the-art. Ablation studies confirm that the physics-informed objective is the primary performance driver, providing gains in accuracy, 2x label efficiency, and more localized and clinically meaningful ECG segments.

</details>


### [292] [Let It Go or Learn to Self-Correct: Continuous Diffusion for Constrained Discrete Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09009)
*Mariia Drozdova,Stéphane Liem Nguyen,François Fleuret*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散模型在全局约束离散任务（如数独、图连通性、拉丁方、N皇后）中的应用，发现标准扩散采样因过度保持当前噪声状态而保留早期错误。直接将采样改为从模型的干净预测中抽样，无需重新训练即可将数独有效性从31%提升至95%。此外提出的自校正训练进一步提升了标准采样器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DDPM在连续域中表现出色，但在全局约束的离散任务中，早期离散错误难以撤销，标准扩散采样可能保留早期错误，即使模型的干净预测具有信息量。因此需要探索更适合离散推理任务的采样与训练策略。

Method: 1) 对比标准扩散采样与直接使用模型干净预测进行采样的效果；2) 提出自校正训练（self-correction training），在训练过程中暴露模型自身的预测，增强其对推理阶段错误的鲁棒性。

Result: 在不重新训练的情况下，仅将采样方式改为从干净预测中抽样，数独有效性从31%提升到95%，其他离散任务上也有一致的性能提升。自校正训练显著改进了标准采样器的表现。

Conclusion: 连续扩散模型能够学习非平凡的全局约束，但离散推理任务需要在训练与推理之间更好地对齐——要么通过减少对早期决策承诺的采样器，要么通过训练让模型学会纠正推理阶段自身的错误。

Abstract: Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Models (DDPMs) generate samples by starting from noise and repeatedly denoising while keeping each update close to the current noisy state. This behavior is effective in many continuous domains, but its role is less clear for globally constrained discrete tasks, such as Sudoku, graph connectivity, Latin squares, and N-queens. In such settings, early discrete errors can be difficult to undo. As a result, standard diffusion sampling may preserve early mistakes, even when the model's clean predictions are informative. We compare standard samplers to sampling directly from the model's clean prediction. Without retraining, this single change improves Sudoku validity from 31% to 95%, with consistent gains across the other discrete tasks. We hypothesize that staying close to the current noisy state is harmful because the reverse trajectory can drift off the forward noising distribution the model was trained on. To reduce this train-test mismatch, we further introduce self-correction training, which exposes the model to its own predictions, improving robustness to errors that arise during inference. This substantially improves the performance of standard samplers. Our results suggest that continuous diffusion models can learn nontrivial global constraints, but discrete reasoning tasks require better alignment between training and inference: either through samplers that reduce commitment to early decisions, or through training that teaches the model to correct its own inference-time errors.

</details>


### [293] [Do Reasoning Representations Help Humans Evaluate LLM Outputs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09038)
*Jaewoo Lim,Sungbok Shin,Sanghyun Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过受控人类研究探讨推理表示作为人类面向接口的效果，发现用户偏好的规划/分解式表示与实际的验证支持能力之间存在错配：用户偏好规划式表示，但简单的思维链反而更能支持验证、信任和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理表示通常以模型中心的标准（如答案准确性和忠实度）来评估，但忽略了一个关键问题——它们是否真正帮助人类评估模型输出。本研究旨在从人类面向接口的角度重新审视推理表示的价值。

Method: 作者开展了受控人类研究，测试六种推理格式在不同复杂度任务上的表现。研究基于网页框架实现，随机化了任务领域、问题实例和表示顺序，并收集了结构理解、错误检测与定位、信任校准等多维度细粒度判断数据。

Result: 研究发现用户的感知偏好与实际评估支持之间存在错配：参与者偏好规划式和分解式表示，但更简单的思维链追踪反而更好地支持验证、信任和可解释性。此外，受欢迎的表示会引入校准风险，表现为更频繁地对正确轨迹产生误报，以及在高信任度下缺乏验证意愿。

Conclusion: 推理表示的设计需要区分"用户喜欢什么"和"什么真正支持人类评估"这两个目标。简单直观的表示（如思维链）在帮助人类验证模型输出方面比复杂但受偏好的表示更有效，这为推理表示作为可解释性界面提供了设计启示。

Abstract: Reasoning representations are increasingly used as explanations for large language model outputs. Yet they are typically evaluated with model-centric criteria, such as answer accuracy and faithfulness, leaving it unclear whether they help people evaluate model responses. In this work, we study reasoning representations as human-facing interfaces rather than proxies for model reasoning ability. We conduct a controlled human study of six reasoning formats across tasks of varying complexity, supported by a web-based framework that randomizes task domains, problem instances, and representation order. The study collects fine-grained judgments of structural understanding, error detection and localization, and trust calibration. Our study shows a mismatch between perceived preference and support for human evaluation. Participants prefer planning- and decomposition-based representations, but simpler chain-of-thought traces better support verification, trust, and interpretability. Preferred representations also introduce calibration risks, with more false alarms on correct traces and high trust despite low willingness to verify.

</details>


### [294] [Training-Free Task Vectors for LLM Behavioral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09054)
*Gabriel J. Perin,Lucas Boscaini,André Araujo,Nina S. T. Hirata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出训练免任务向量（TFTVs），一种无需微调即可生成任务向量方向的方法，通过仅使用前向传播统计将激活引导向量映射为秩一权重空间编辑，能实现行为放大、抑制和组合编辑，并在大语言模型行为控制任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统任务向量的计算依赖微调过程，成本高昂且限制了训练后模型编辑的实际应用。作者希望消除对微调的依赖，使任务向量的发现更加高效和实用。

Method: TFTVs将激活引导向量（activation steering vectors）映射为秩一（rank-one）权重空间编辑，仅需前向传播统计即可完成计算。该方法满足算术性质，支持通过加法实现学习、减法实现遗忘以及多编辑的组合。

Result: 在大语言模型行为控制任务上的实验表明，TFTVs能够一致地放大、抑制和组合目标行为，同时保持通用知识和问题解决能力。与其他编辑和引导基线方法相比，TFTVs在行为控制上表现更强，实用保持方面同等或更优。

Conclusion: 作者希望该工作能为社区在训练后模型编辑和更广泛的训练免模型控制领域开辟新方向，代码已开源。

Abstract: Task vectors enable post-training model editing by identifying semantically meaningful directions in weight space, typically computed as the difference between a fine-tuned model and its pretrained initialization. However, this reliance on fine-tuning makes discovering such directions costly and limits the practicality of post-training model editing. To address this limitation, we introduce Training-Free Task Vectors (TFTVs), a novel method to compute task-vector-like directions without requiring fine-tuning. Our method maps activation steering vectors to rank-one weight-space edits using only forward-pass statistics, while satisfying arithmetic properties that directly support learning via addition, forgetting via subtraction, and the composition of multiple edits. Empirically, we evaluate TFTVs on large language model behavioral control tasks and show that they consistently amplify, suppress, and compose target behaviors while preserving general knowledge and problem-solving skills. We also validate our method against other editing and steering baselines, experimentally demonstrating that TFTVs achieve stronger trait control with better or competitive utility preservation. We hope our work opens new directions for the community in post-training model editing and broader training-free model control. Code is available on the project website: tftv-llm.github.io.

</details>


### [295] [PlayTrain: An Efficient Reinforcement Learning Framework for LLM-Generated Adaptable JavaScript Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09059)
*Ryan Truong,Lance Ying,Samuel J. Gershman,Kazuki Irie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PlayTrain，一个结合大语言模型（LLM）与高效游戏运行管线的强化学习（RL）框架。用户只需提供极简的文字提示，LLM即可生成JavaScript游戏代码，随后通过标准gym环境无缝训练RL智能体。该系统支持像素级端到端训练，在单GPU节点上每秒处理超过100万次智能体决策，并支持对现有游戏进行修改以引入新测试集、程序化生成逻辑或动态变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的视频游戏环境（VGEs）开发或修改过程需要大量手工编码，成本高昂且耗时，严重制约了强化学习研究的发展速度和灵活性。

Method: PlayTrain框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 利用大语言模型从极简人类提示词稳健地生成JavaScript（JS）游戏代码；(2) 一套高效管线，可将任意JS游戏无缝集成到标准gym环境中运行。JS格式既便于用户试玩，又可让RL智能体在同一游戏上进行训练。

Result: 成功克隆了多种知名Atari和ProcGen游戏为简单JS版本，并在单GPU节点上以每秒超过100万次智能体决策的速度端到端训练像素级智能体。此外还演示了对游戏的修改能力，包括新增测试集、程序化生成逻辑和游戏动态变化等。

Conclusion: PlayTrain重塑了RL游戏环境的开发范式——只需一个通过LLM生成和修改的JS文件即可完成整个流程，为RL研究开辟了新的方向。

Abstract: While many video-game environments (VGEs) have played crucial roles in advancing reinforcement learning (RL), developing novel VGEs or modifying existing ones to support new features, has been a laborious process requiring extensive hand-coding. Here we present PlayTrain, an RL framework that combines the abilities of large language models (LLMs) to robustly generate JavaScript (JS) games from a minimal human prompt, and an efficient pipeline that can run any JS game in a standard 'gym' environment. Not only are recent LLMs particularly good at writing JS code, but the JS format also allows users to easily play generated VGEs, while PlayTrain enables us to train RL agents on the exact same games. We demonstrate multiple use cases of PlayTrain, including cloning well-known Atari and ProcGen games in simple JS, where PlayTrain trains pixel-based agents end-to-end at over 1M agent-decisions per second on a single GPU node; and creating modified versions thereof (e.g., that support novel test sets, procedural generation logics, or game dynamics). Through PlayTrain, we reimagine RL VGE development: all we need is a single JS file, generated and modified through an LLM. We discuss promising future RL research directions that PlayTrain unlocks.

</details>


### [296] [Multi-Task Learning for Sparsely-Labeled Time Series: A Case Study on Cold-Hardiness Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09062)
*Aseem Saxena,Paola Pesántez-Cabrera,Jonathan Magby,Markus Keller,Alan Fern*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过真实世界案例研究，探讨了在数据有限且标签稀疏的情况下，利用多任务学习（MTL）进行时间过程建模。研究聚焦于葡萄抗寒性预测这一农业问题，采用RNN处理天气时间序列数据，并通过MTL架构在不同葡萄品种间共享信息，结果显示某些MTL架构优于单任务学习及现有科学模型，且联合学习抗寒性与发芽预测两个任务能进一步提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 葡萄抗寒性预测对种植者决定是否需要采取昂贵的防霜措施至关重要，但抗寒性的实地测量困难且耗费成本。不同品种的抗寒性响应各异，且每个品种的真实标注数据在时间上稀疏且数量有限，这使得传统单任务学习方法难以有效建模。

Method: 利用循环神经网络（RNN）基于天气时间序列数据进行每日抗寒性预测，将不同葡萄品种视为不同任务，开发多种多任务学习（MTL）架构来结合跨品种数据，并在MTL和迁移学习两种设置下进行系统评估。此外还将方法扩展到发芽预测任务，并尝试单一MTL模型同时学习两个相关任务。

Result: 不同MTL架构之间存在显著性能差异，某些架构能够持续超越单任务学习及最先进的科学模型。类似的结果在发芽预测任务中也得到验证。此外，单一MTL模型同时学习抗寒性与发芽预测两个任务时，两个任务的预测精度均获得进一步提升。

Conclusion: 多任务学习是在数据稀缺和标签稀疏条件下进行时间过程建模的有效策略，特别是在农业场景下跨品种和跨相关任务的联合建模能够显著改善预测性能。选择合适的MTL架构至关重要，且同时学习多个相关任务可以带来互相增益。

Abstract: We present a real-world case study of multi-task learning (MTL) for temporal process modeling from limited data with temporally sparse labels. Specifically, we investigate multi-task learning for the important agricultural problem of predicting grape cold hardiness, which is the temperature at which lethal freezing occurs. Cold hardiness changes in response to weather and is difficult to measure directly in the field. Thus, growers rely on predictions to decide when to apply costly frost mitigation measures. We apply recurrent neural networks (RNNs) for daily cold-hardiness prediction from time series weather data. A major challenge is that the cold hardiness response varies across plant cultivars and ground-truth data for each cultivar is temporally sparse and limited. To address this challenge, we investigate multi-task learning (MTL) approaches for combining data, where different tasks correspond to different cultivars. We develop a variety of MTL architectures and evaluate them in both MTL and transfer learning settings. Our results show significant differences between architectures and that certain architectures are able to consistently outperform single-task learning and state-of-the-art scientific models. Additionally, we show similar results for the qualitatively different, but related, task of budbreak prediction. Further, improved accuracy for budbreak and cold hardiness is achieved by a single MTL model that simultaneously learns both tasks.

</details>


### [297] [ThinkPrior: Zero-Rollout Difficulty Priors for Cold-Start Prompt Selection in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09075)
*Tommy Sha,Skylar Zhai,Siqi Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ThinkPrior方法，利用外部验证器的一次离线扫描构建零rollout难度先验，用于GRPO训练中的提示词选择，减少零优势"静默组"带来的rollout浪费，而不改变损失函数和优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GRPO训练中，组内所有rollout全对或全错时优势为零，不产生梯度但浪费计算资源（占run的39%）。现有基于历史的选择方法需要先花费目标策略rollout来估计难度，存在冷启动问题。

Method: ThinkPrior使用外部验证器在一轮离线pass中为每个提示词构建零rollout难度先验。验证器评分的通过率作为Beta后验的外部锚定初始化，按期望可学习性选择提示词，随后用训练结果更新后验，不改变损失函数和优化器。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Math-7B上16个种子的实验中，ThinkPrior使早期静默组减少一半以上，截止第30步的浪费rollout减少近五分之一，最终准确率无差异。在250提示词池中，固定预算结果是重新分配而非净节省。ThinkPrior+DAPO组合将生成rollout减少10.6%。

Conclusion: ThinkPrior通过外部锚定先验实现了零冷启动的提示词选择，有效减少静默组和rollout浪费，且不影响最终性能；在小规模池中体现为rollout的重新分配而非总量节省。

Abstract: In reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) trained with group relative policy optimization (GRPO), the KL-free reward-advantage term studied here depends on within-group reward variation. If all rollouts in a group are correct or all are wrong, their group-relative advantages are identically zero; these zero-advantage silent groups provide no reward-advantage gradient, yet uniform sampling spends 39% of a run's rollouts on them. History-based prompt selection must first spend target-policy rollouts to estimate difficulty, creating a cold start with rollout waste; ThinkPrior instead uses an external anchor in one offline pass to construct a zero-rollout difficulty prior before the first target-policy rollout. The verifier-scored anchor pass rate supplies an external-anchor initialization for a Beta posterior; ThinkPrior selects by expected learnability and then updates from training outcomes, changing neither the loss nor the optimizer. On Qwen2.5-Math-7B across sixteen seeds, ThinkPrior more than halves early silent groups and cuts wasted rollouts through step 30 by nearly a fifth, while we detect no difference in final accuracy. On this 250-prompt pool the fixed-budget result is a reallocation rather than a net saving. The measured ThinkPrior+DAPO composition reduces generated rollouts by 10.6% while both arms retain the same 3840-rollout update budget. The prior requires no target-policy rollout before the first selection, but the posterior thereafter uses target-policy outcomes.

</details>


### [298] [Curriculum Learning as Transport: Understanding Curricula with Wasserstein Geodesics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09099)
*Changho Shin,David Alvarez-Melis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Wasserstein课程路径框架，将课程学习解耦为难度定义、示例排序、各级别曝光量和训练节奏等多个设计因素，并在12个任务、33个难度轴的合成测试套件上进行系统隔离分析，发现课程效果高度依赖上下文，不存在普适最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课程学习的效果由多个耦合的设计选择共同决定（难度定义、排序方式、各级别曝光量、跨级别训练速度），这些因素纠缠在一起，使得难以隔离出真正起作用的因素。

Method: 提出基于传输（transport）的Wasserstein课程路径框架，将课程表示为离散难度级别上训练分布的轨迹，从而解耦排序、匹配曝光、端点平滑性和节奏等因子；在12个任务和33个难度轴的校准合成套件上进行受控实验。

Result: 课程效果高度依赖上下文，没有单一策略在所有任务/难度轴/预算下占优；课程主要改变固定预算在难度谱上的最优分配位置。easy-to-hard排序相比曝光匹配的静态采样能提升困难级别性能，证明收益不能仅由累积曝光解释；端点平滑性和节奏显著影响课程在难度谱上的有效作用范围。

Conclusion: 该传输视角框架可自然扩展至基于几何的学习式节奏控制，以及超越一维排序的结构化难度空间，为课程学习的系统化分析与设计提供了统一工具。

Abstract: Curriculum learning is governed by several coupled design choices---how difficulty is defined, how examples are ordered, how much exposure each level receives, and how quickly training moves across levels---making it hard to isolate what actually helps. We present Wasserstein curriculum paths, a simple transport-based framework that decouples these factors by representing curricula as trajectories of training distributions over discrete difficulty levels. Across a calibrated synthetic suite with 12 tasks and 33 difficulty axes, we use this framework to isolate the effects of ordering, matched exposure, endpoint smoothness, and pacing under fixed training budgets. We find that curriculum effects are strongly context-dependent: no single strategy dominates across tasks, difficulty axes, and budgets, and curricula mainly change where a fixed budget is spent most effectively. Within this framework, easy-to-hard ordering improves hard-level performance relative to exposure-matched static sampling, showing that the benefit is not explained by cumulative exposure alone. We further show that endpoint smoothness and pacing substantially affect where along the difficulty spectrum a curriculum is effective. Finally, we show that the same transport view naturally supports extensions to learned pacing through geometry and to structured difficulty spaces beyond one-dimensional orderings.

</details>


### [299] [When Does Scale-Invariant Optimization Become Unstable? An Exact Schedule Law with Weight Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09116)
*Hasan Amin,Wei-Kai Chang,Rajiv Khanna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了归一化神经网络中的尺度不变性如何通过参数范数与学习率调度、权重衰减相互作用，形成一个精确的离散时间定律：单个标量可捕获所有调度与衰减的驱动力，而范数增长引发几何自淬灭效应，从而在有效学习率区间形成一个清晰的分界。作者通过完全可解的归一化回归模型证明该平衡点内在不稳定，并建立统一同质优化器框架解释为何自适应方法在归一化下稳定化更弱。实验在多种网络和数据集上验证了该定律，且性能在预测边界处达到峰值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 归一化使神经网络的大部分结构呈尺度不变性，但学习率调度与权重衰减通过参数范数相互作用的机制尚未被精确理解。作者希望找到能够统一描述这一隐藏反馈循环的单一控制量，并解释不同优化器在归一化下的行为差异。

Method: 作者首先推导出精确的离散时间定律，用一个标量量捕获所有调度与衰减作用，范数增长产生几何自淬灭效应。随后对完全可解的归一化回归模型进行精确分析，将动态降至二维，并利用离散时间雅可比结构揭示平衡点的内在不稳定性。最后提出统一同质优化器框架，揭示不同优化器在自淬灭强度上的结构性二分法。

Result: 在涵盖MLP、CNN、GPT2及MNIST、CIFAR、wikiText、OpenWebText等数据集的大量实验中，预测定律以高精度成立，训练的最终性能在预测边界处出现尖锐峰值，验证了单一标量对尺度不变优化的控制作用。

Conclusion: 该研究隔离出尺度不变优化的单一主导量，为深度学习中的训练动态、优化器行为和调度设计提供了精确且可操作的框架，并从第一性原理解释了自适应优化器（如Adam类）在归一化下稳定化系统性更弱的原因。

Abstract: Normalization renders large parts of neural networks effectively scale invariant, inducing a hidden feedback loop in which learning-rate schedules and weight decay interact through the parameter norm to control the effective step taken by the optimizer. We show that this interaction is governed by an exact discrete-time law: a single scalar quantity captures all schedule and decay forcing, while norm growth induces an opposing geometric self-quenching effect. This yields a sharp boundary that cleanly separates contraction- and expansion-dominated effective learning rate regimes. To understand the underlying mechanism, we provide exact analysis of a fully solved normalized regression model where the dynamics reduce to two dimensions and show that the balance point is intrinsically unstable, implying that constant learning rate with weight decay cannot stably maintain an interior equilibrium and instead produces recurrent behavior driven by discrete-time Jacobian structure. We further extend this perspective across optimizers through unified homogeneous-optimizer framework that reveals a structural dichotomy in self-quenching strength, providing a first-principles explanation for why adaptive methods exhibit systematically weaker stabilization under normalization.
  Across dynamical systems and neural networks (MLP, CNN, GPT2 / MNIST, CIFAR, wikiText, OpenWebText), the predicted law holds with high precision and enables direct control of training via the identified scalar, with performance peaking sharply at the predicted boundary. Together, these results isolate a single governing quantity for scale-invariant optimization, providing a precise and actionable lens on training dynamics, optimizer behavior, and schedule design in modern deep learning. Code is available in https://github.com/shasanamin/normalized-optimization-dynamics.

</details>


### [300] [Nearly Tight Rademacher Bounds for Sparsely Activated Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09130)
*Xiaoyu Li,Zhizhou Sha,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao,Andi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了单隐层ReLU网络中"输入依赖稀疏性"的统计复杂度。在宽度s、每输入至多k个活跃单元的约束下，建立了Rademacher复杂度上界，消除了先前结果中显式的维度因子（最多对数因子），并通过匹配的下界证明宽度依赖依然存在。还揭示了输入域和偏置界的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然单个输入可能只激活少数隐单元，但不同输入集合起来可能激活整个网络。作者希望理解这种输入依赖稀疏性（而非全局稀疏性）的统计学习复杂度，并修正以往结果在维度上的次优性。

Method: 采用保支持覆盖（support-preserving cover）和单一归一化chaining论证来消除显式维度因子。通过适当的i.i.d.边缘分布建立下界。利用球形帽（spherical-cap）构造来证明零偏置网络在整个球域上的稀疏性限制，以及偏置界恢复最坏情况速率的结果。

Result: 建立了Rademacher复杂度上界为CWR·min{k,√(sk/m)log^{3/2}(2m)} + kB/√m（最多对数因子最优），i.i.d.边缘分布上的下界匹配该对数级别。零偏置网络在整球上稀疏时复杂度为O(kWR/√m)，而偏置界与WR相当时在最坏情况下速率仅在对数维度中恢复。还获得了agnostic minimax excess-risk界min{1,√(s/(km))}（最多对数因子）。

Conclusion: 输入依赖稀疏性的统计复杂度本质上由活跃单元数k、网络宽度s和有效偏置界B共同决定。维度对复杂度的影响可被消除至对数级别；但输入域结构和偏置界对最终速率有本质影响。稀疏性若仅在采样支撑上而非整个域上成立，其带来的复杂度红利可能无法充分利用。

Abstract: An input may activate few hidden units even when different inputs collectively use an entire network. We study the statistical complexity of this input-dependent sparsity in the one-hidden-layer ReLU model of Awasthi et al. (COLT 2024). For width $s$, at most $k$ active units per input, and effective weight and bias bounds $W,B$, every size-$m$ sample in the class's fixed radius-$R$ input domain satisfies $\mathcal{R}(S)\le CWR\min\{k,\sqrt{sk/m}\log^{3/2}(2m)\}+kB/\sqrt m$. A support-preserving cover and a single normalized chaining argument remove the previous explicit dimension factor, up to logarithms. Lower bounds on appropriate i.i.d. marginals match up to those logarithms, showing how changing active units across inputs retains a width dependence. The input domain matters: zero-bias networks sparse on the entire ball have at most $2k$ nonzero units and complexity $O(kWR/\sqrt m)$, whereas bias bounds comparable to $WR$ restore the worst-case rate on that same domain in only logarithmic dimension. A spherical-cap construction proves the latter claim without assuming sparsity merely on the sampling support. For a specified normalized bounded loss and biases comparable to $WR$, we also obtain agnostic minimax excess-risk bounds of order $\min\{1,\sqrt{s/(km)}\}$ up to logarithms.

</details>


### [301] [Entropy-Regularized Rank-Masked Policy Optimization for Test-Time Reinforcement Learning in Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09135)
*Jiacheng Xu,Feng Chen,Xiuneng Xu,Bo An*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出探针驱动的测试时强化学习（Probe-Driven TTRL），通过构造输出无关的探针输入并基于程序行为一致性定义奖励，解决代码生成中测试时强化学习无法使用答案级自投票的问题，并引入熵正则化的排名掩蔽策略优化（ERPO）来防止奖励黑客攻击，在编码基准测试中显著提升pass@1和pass@k指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时强化学习方法依赖答案级自投票获得奖励，但代码生成任务中程序无法通过表面形式进行比较，因此无法直接提供训练信号，需要新的方法使TTRL适用于代码生成。

Method: 1) 提出探针驱动的TTRL：从问题陈述构造无输出的探针输入，执行候选程序并基于行为一致性定义Probe Consensus Reward (PCR)；2) 提出Entropy-Regularized Rank-Masked Policy Optimization (ERPO)：通过排名掩蔽将低PCR转化为保守的负更新，并用熵上限控制策略漂移。

Result: 在编码基准测试中，ERPO在域内适应和零样本迁移两种场景下均显著提升了pass@1和pass@k指标。

Conclusion: 探针驱动的一致行为信号为开放词表程序提供了可行的训练信号，ERPO有效缓解了奖励黑客问题，使测试时强化学习成功应用于代码生成任务。

Abstract: Existing methods for test-time reinforcement learning (TTRL) derive rewards from answer-level self-voting on unlabeled test-time tasks with canonical answers, but this breaks down for code generation because programs cannot be compared by surface form and therefore do not directly provide a usable training signal. To make TTRL applicable to code generation, we propose probe-driven TTRL, which constructs output-free probe inputs from the problem statement, executes candidate programs on these probes, and defines a Probe Consensus Reward (PCR) from the resulting behavioral agreement. PCR provides a behavioral training signal for open-vocabulary programs, but it is not a fully reliable verifier and remains susceptible to reward hacking through spurious consensus. We therefore introduce Entropy-Regularized Rank-Masked Policy Optimization (ERPO), which converts low PCR into conservative negative updates through rank masking and controls policy drift with an entropy ceiling. On coding benchmarks, ERPO substantially improves pass@1 and pass@k in both in-domain adaptation and zero-shot transfer.

</details>


### [302] [NOAH: Learning the Full Patient Journey. A Longitudinal Multimodal Time-Aware Model for Representation and Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09140)
*Tobias Susetzky,Raphael Rehms,Dmitrii Seletkov,Özgün Turgut,Michelle Espranita Liman,Lisa Steinhelfer,Rickmer Braren,Daniel Rueckert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NOAH是一个时间感知、任务无关的生成式Transformer模型，利用超过5.59亿条临床事件数据（涵盖31万患者、43万次住院），能够统一处理医学图像、时间序列、数值信号、分类事件及结构化和非结构化临床文本，实现自回归预测、零样本分类和反事实干预模拟，是医疗领域首个真正全面的生成式临床轨迹建模框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI方法处理纵向患者记录时多为判别式模型、局限于少数模态、受限于封闭的分类词汇表、将时间视为单调归纳偏置，且在预测未来患者状态方面能力有限，难以捕捉真实世界多模态患者数据中的复杂、不规则时间动态和固有随机性。

Method: NOAH采用新型双向时间集成机制和变分潜在空间，以捕获患者状态的连续演变和临床轨迹的随机性，构建基于MIMIC数据集家族的大规模生成式Transformer模型，支持多种模态的原生处理和任意时间条件下的自回归预测。

Result: NOAH生成的临床状态表征在临床结局探测、15个ICD章节、29种合并症的预测及时间-事件预测任务中均表现出强性能，证明了多模态数据和复杂时间动态处理方面的优越性。

Conclusion: NOAH提供了一个多功能、任务无关、可扩展的临床AI基础框架，为个性化临床护理和数字医学中的智能预测系统奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: The digitization of healthcare has generated vast, longitudinal, and multimodal patient records over a lifetime, yet fully exploiting these data to represent and predict patient state trajectories remains a critical challenge. Current AI models often struggle to capture the complex, irregular temporal dynamics and inherent stochasticity of real-world multimodal patient data. Existing AI approaches for modeling longitudinal patient records are predominantly discriminative, limited to a few modalities, constrained by closed categorical vocabularies, treating time as a monotonic inductive bias, or they are limited in forecasting future patient states. We introduce NOAH, a time-aware, task-agnostic, generative transformer model representing and forecasting the full multimodal patient journey. NOAH features a novel bidirectional time integration and a variational latent space to capture the continuous evolution of patient states and the stochasticity of clinical trajectories. Built from over 559 million clinical events from 431,000 hospital visits of 299,000 patients across the MIMIC dataset family, NOAH natively processes medical images, time-series and numeric signals, categorical events, as well as structured and unstructured clinical records. NOAH is the first truly holistic generative model in its field, enabling autoregressive forecasting with optional time control, zero-shot classification, and counterfactual intervention simulation. It generates highly informative and predictive patient state representations that demonstrate strong performance in probing for clinical outcomes, 15 ICD chapters, and 29 comorbidities, as well as in time-to-event prediction. Seamlessly handling diverse modalities and complex temporal dynamics, NOAH provides a versatile, task-agnostic, scalable foundation for intelligent predictive systems in personalized clinical care and digital medicine.

</details>


### [303] [Learning Length-Extrapolatable Recurrent Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09157)
*Hanwen Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"时间信用稳定化"(CST)方法，通过在后向传播中局部重缩放状态信用信号的范数（不旋转被修正分量），来改善循环模型在训练时域之外的长期建模能力，在最高128倍训练长度的外推场景中取得性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环模型是长上下文建模的自然选择，但基于BPTT训练的模型在超出训练时域时常常失效。传统观点归因于梯度消失/爆炸，然而密集逐token损失仍可训练共享循环规则，说明衰减本身并不决定学习是否失败。因此需要从"状态信用"这一新视角理解并解决该问题。

Method: 提出Credit Stabilization through Time (CST)方法：在后向传播过程中，对状态信用信号进行局部重缩放以稳定其范数，同时不旋转被修正的分量；前向计算保持不变。由于合成任务与真实数据具有不同的信用动态特性，CST针对两种情景分别进行专门化设计。

Result: 在受控合成任务和真实数据上，CST均能提升模型在训练时域之外的表现，在高达128倍训练长度的外推场景中观测到增益。

Conclusion: 状态信用信号的衰减而非梯度衰减本身是循环模型长程学习失败的关键因素，CST通过稳定状态信用信号有效扩展了模型的有效训练外推范围。

Abstract: Recurrent models provide a natural path to long-context modeling, yet models trained with backpropagation through time (BPTT) often fail beyond their training horizon. Classical analyses emphasize gradients that vanish or explode along temporal paths. However, dense per-token losses can still train a shared recurrent rule despite severe decay, showing that decay alone does not determine whether learning fails. We instead study state credit: the signal through which future losses reach earlier recurrent states before contributing to parameter updates. Accordingly, we intervene directly on state credit and propose Credit Stabilization through Time (CST). During backward propagation, CST locally rescales the state-credit signal to stabilize its norm without rotating the component being corrected, while leaving the forward computation unchanged. Because controlled synthetic tasks and real data exhibit different credit dynamics, we specialize CST to each regime. In both settings, CST improves performance beyond the training horizon, with gains observed at up to 128x the training length.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [304] [Concord: A Video Relational Algebra for Cross-Modal Query Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05756)
*Sultan Muratbek,Charisse Ivana Yeung,Chanwut Kittivorawong,Alvin Cheung*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Concord是一个用于表达和优化语义视频查询的系统。它引入Video Relational Algebra (VRA)嵌套代数来捕捉常见语义视频操作，并提出近似优化重写规则以减少多模态大语言模型(MLLM)的使用。在足球转播和讲座等真实视频上，Concord能显著降低MLLM处理成本（最高减少87%），并在跨摄像头车辆匹配任务中大幅提升F1分数（从0.364到0.813）且不调用MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义视频查询（如用自然语言提示词查询视频内容）虽然日益流行，但依赖MLLM处理整段视频代价高昂——可能处理数小时媒体只为返回几秒相关输出，导致执行速度慢、成本高且准确性差。

Method: Concord提出三个核心贡献：(1) 引入Video Relational Algebra (VRA)—一种嵌套代数，涵盖视频、转录、帧和对象轨迹等语义视频操作；(2) 推导近似优化规则重写VRA查询以减少MLLM使用并提升结果质量：对带旁白的视频，用转录替代视频处理或用转录挑选视频片段供MLLM处理；对无旁白的跨摄像头查询，用检测和追踪替代全视频MLLM连接，改为轨迹级关系连接；(3) 在真实视频上评估系统性能。

Result: 在4.59小时足球转播和3.92小时讲座数据上，transcript-to-video查询仅分别发送5.32%和2.47%的视频时长给MLLM处理，MLLM成本最高降低87%。在两个五秒钟高速公路片段中（含18辆人工裁决的跨摄像头车辆），Detect-Track-Join查询将F1从0.364提升至0.813，且不产生任何MLLM调用。

Conclusion: Concord通过代数化的查询表示和优化策略，能够大幅减少语义视频查询对MLLM的依赖，在降低成本的同时提升查询准确性，为高效处理大规模视频数据提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Semantic video queries let users embed natural language prompts and use multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to interpret the video. Such queries are increasingly popular for querying video data. However, their expressiveness comes at a steep cost: an MLLM may process hours of media to return only seconds of relevant output, making naive execution slow, expensive, and inaccurate.
  We propose Concord, a system for expressing and optimizing semantic video queries. We makes three contributions. First, we introduce Video Relational Algebra (VRA), a nested algebra over videos, transcripts, frames, and object tracks that captures common semantic video operations. Second, we derive a set of approximate optimizations that rewrite VRA queries to reduce MLLM usage while improving result quality. For narrated video, Concord either processes transcripts instead of video or uses them to identify video clips for MLLM processing. For cross-camera queries without narration, detection and tracking replace a whole-video MLLM join with a track-level relational join.
  Third, we evaluate Concord on real-world videos. Across 4.59 hours of soccer broadcasts and 3.92 hours of lectures, transcript-to-video queries send only 5.32% and 2.47% of source-video duration to the MLLM and reduce MLLM cost by up to 87%. In two five-second highway clips with 18 manually adjudicated cross-camera vehicles, a Detect-Track-Join query improves F1 from .364 to .813 while making no MLLM calls. See our project at https://concord-db.github.io/.

</details>


### [305] [sheval: An RDF data shapes evaluation tool and test-suite for recursive shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05944)
*Shqiponja Ahmetaj,Iovka Boneva,Jan Hidders,Maxime Jakubowski,Jose-Emilio Labra-Gayo,Wim Martens,Filip Murlak,Cem Okulmus,Ognjen Savković,Mantas Šimkus,Dominik Tomaszuk*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了sheval，一个用于评估和比较ShEx和SHACL两种RDF数据形状验证语言中递归形状语义的框架，包含工具和测试套件，可识别不同SHACL引擎在处理递归形状时的行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ShEx对递归形状的语义有明确规范，而SHACL的递归形状语义留给各引擎自行实现，导致不同SHACL引擎在面对递归定义时表现出不一致的行为，缺乏系统性的对比和评估手段。

Method: 提出了sheval评估框架，包含一个评估工具和一个测试套件，通过对比不同的形状引擎在递归定义场景下的行为来揭示实现差异。

Result: 该工具成功评估并展现了ShEx和SHACL在递归形状实现上的差异，识别出各引擎之间的一致性和不一致性，帮助发现潜在的问题。

Conclusion: sheval为形状技术的递归语义对比提供了系统化基础，有助于发现不一致性和潜在问题，并为RDF数据形状验证领域的进一步研究和发展奠定基础。

Abstract: Two different languages have been developed to validate RDF data based on the concept of a shape: ShEx and SHACL. In each language it is possible to define a shape that refers to itself, which is called a recursive shape. While in the case of ShEx, the semantics of recursive shapes is well defined and is part of the specification, in the case of SHACL, the semantics of recursive shapes is left to the implementation of the different SHACL engines. Consequently, the different SHACL engines show different behaviours when confronted with recursive shapes. In this paper we present sheval: an evaluation framework consisting of a tool and a test suite that can be used to compare the behaviour of different shapes technologies when confronted with recursive definitions. The tool has been used to evaluate and understand the differences in the implementation of recursive shapes in ShEx and SHACL. It provides a framework for testing and comparing the behaviour of different shape engines, helping to identify inconsistencies and potential issues, and providing a basis for further research and development in the field of shape-based validation of RDF data.

</details>


### [306] [Compiling Linear Datalog to SQL for Program Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06301)
*Amir Shaikhha,Anna Herlihy,Hung Ngo*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出将 Datalog 程序编译为递归 SQL 的框架，通过中间语言 Midlog 桥接两者鸿沟，并恢复函数依赖作为表键以解锁数据库优化。在 Umbra 后端上相比 Soufflé 最高提升一个数量级，8线程几何平均加速5.46倍，且生成的 SQL 可移植到七个数据库系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Datalog 在静态程序分析中表现优异，但近年来其发展主要来自编译器社区（如 Soufflé），而忽视了现代关系型数据库引擎在递归 SQL 优化上的显著进展。作者希望重新连接 Datalog 与关系数据库，利用成熟的关系引擎能力来加速 Datalog 程序分析。

Method: 提出一个编译框架，将 Datalog 程序（特别是 Linear Datalog 片段）翻译为等价的递归 SQL 查询。所有程序先经过中间语言 Midlog 进行转换，以弥合 Datalog 与 SQL 之间的语义鸿沟；编译器还从程序中恢复函数依赖并将其暴露为数据库模式键，从而触发数据库引擎的标准查询优化。

Result: 在 Umbra 后台上，编译器生成的 SQL 相比 Soufflé 最高快一个数量级，8线程几何平均加速5.46倍；DuckDB 单线程与 Soufflé 竞争力相当，但8线程更慢（几何平均加速0.68倍）。生成的 SQL 无需引擎修改即可在七个数据库系统上运行。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了现代关系数据库引擎能够高效支撑大规模程序分析中的 Datalog 程序，并揭示了关系引擎要完全支持 Datalog 所需的关键能力，为两个领域的交叉融合提供了新路径。

Abstract: Datalog is a declarative query language that has proven highly effective for expressing static program analyses. Although Datalog has deep roots in database theory, most recent advances have largely emerged from the programming languages and compiler communities, with systems such as Soufflé. In contrast, modern relational engines have made significant progress in optimizing recursive SQL.
  This paper revisits the connection between Datalog and relational databases, advocating recursive SQL as a backend for Datalog evaluation. We present a compilation framework that translates Datalog programs, particularly those in the Linear Datalog fragment, into equivalent recursive SQL queries. To bridge the gap between Datalog and SQL, the compiler routes every program through an intermediate language called Midlog. The compiler additionally recovers functional dependencies from the program and exposes them as schema keys, unlocking the engine's standard query optimizations. This approach enables existing database engines to execute a broad class of program analyses, outperforming the Soufflé engine by up to an order of magnitude on the Umbra backend. Umbra achieves a geometric-mean speedup of 5.46$\times$ at 8 threads, whereas DuckDB is competitive with Soufflé single-threaded and is slower at 8 threads (geometric-mean speedup of 0.68$\times$). Furthermore, the generated SQL is portable; it runs on seven database systems without any engine modification. Our results highlight what the relational engines require to fully support Datalog for large-scale program analysis.

</details>


### [307] [X-DigCheck: Co-Evolving Application Profiles and Knowledge Graphs, Demonstrated on the RTI Documentation of Rupe Magna](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07694)
*Celian Ringwald,Huseyin Erdogan,Valentina Presutti,Cristiano Putzolu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: X-DigCheck是一个领域无关的环境，用于在应用档案（application profile）与其所描述的数据共同演化过程中进行构建和维护。它通过将数据提升为RDF、用能力问题和SHACL检查档案，并根据报告联合驱动本体、映射、约束和图表的修订，形成本体-数据共演化循环。在文化遗产领域的RupeMagna-RTI案例中验证了该工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对固定本体开发的应用档案会随着时间推移与它们本应捕获的模式产生漂移（drift），缺乏一种支持档案与数据持续共同演化的环境。

Method: 将档案构建视为连续的"本体-数据共演化循环"：数据按档案提升为RDF，通过能力问题和SHACL检查，产生的双向覆盖报告联合驱动本体、映射、约束和图的修订；该循环对领域和生成图的流水线均不敏感。

Result: 在文化遗产领域构建了RupeMagna-RTI——首个RTI（反射变换成像）特化的CHS-ODP档案，与CIDOC-CRM/CRMdig、ArCo、CHAD-KG和Getty AAT对齐；演示允许访客在Rupe Magna（意大利格罗西奥）RTI调查数据上运行一个完整循环的迭代，并支持使用自定义档案和图，同时提供双向覆盖报告以标记建模差距和过时假设。

Conclusion: X-DigCheck提供了一个可移植的档案工程共演化环境，并附带通过该环境生成的可复用RTI应用档案，展示了其作为领域无关工具的通用性与实用价值。

Abstract: We demonstrate X-DigCheck, a domain-independent environment for building and maintaining application profiles as they co-evolve with the data they describe. Profiles developed against a fixed ontology quickly drift from the schema they were meant to capture. X-DigCheck treats profile construction as a continuous ontology-data co-evolution loop: data are lifted into RDF against the profile, checked through competency questions and SHACL, and the resulting reports jointly drive revisions of the ontology, mappings, constraints, and graph. The loop is agnostic to the domain and to the pipeline that produces the graph. We validate and demonstrate the tool in the cultural heritage domain, on the construction of RupeMagna-RTI, the first Reflectance Transformation Imaging (RTI) specialisation of the Cultural Heritage Survey ODP (CHS-ODP), aligned with CIDOC-CRM/CRMdig, ArCo, CHAD-KG, and Getty AAT, with semRTI as the lifting pipeline of this use case. The demonstration lets visitors run one full turn of the loop -on the shipped Rupe Magna (Grosio, Italy) RTI survey, or on a profile and graph of their own -executing the competency-question and SHACL checks live and reading the bidirectional coverage report that flags modelling gaps and stale assumptions. The result is a portable co-evolution environment for profile engineering, together with a reusable RTI application profile produced through it. A screencast of the demonstration is available at https://zenodo.org/records/22210609.

</details>


### [308] [Preserving contextual information in cultural heritage metadata through multidimensional knowledge graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07695)
*Lyndon Nixon,Valentina Presutti,Celian Ringwald,Andrea Schimmenti*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出多维知识图谱（MKGs）的概念框架，用于在文化遗产对象元数据中建模和查询跨社会、文化、政治等多维度上下文信息，弥补传统知识图谱无法表达陈述情境化有效性的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化遗产对象元数据需要容纳不断演变或相互冲突的观点（如殖民与后殖民视角、科学共识的变化），但标准知识图谱无法捕获陈述依赖上下文的有效性；现有方法（溯源、限定词、再具体化）虽能实现基本情境化，但缺乏统一框架来同时建模和查询跨多个社会、文化、政治维度的数据。

Method: 本文提出多维知识图谱（MKGs）的概念基础，通过同时建模和查询跨越多个社会、文化和政治维度的上下文信息来保存文化遗产对象元数据中复杂、多层的情境。

Result: 论文提出了MKGs的概念框架，阐述了如何在文化遗产对象元数据中保留复杂、多层次的上下文信息。

Conclusion: MKGs为文化遗产元数据的多维情境化提供了统一的理论框架，有望弥补现有知识表示方法在同时处理多维度、多层上下文方面的不足。

Abstract: Using Knowledge Graphs (KGs) to describe Cultural Heritage Objects (CHOs) supports semantic richness and interoperability. However, standard KGs fail to capture the context-dependent validity of statements. This limitation is critical for cultural heritage metadata, which must often accommodate evolving or conflicting viewpoints, such as colonial versus post-colonial perspectives or shifting scientific consensus. While current knowledge representation methods address basic contextualization via provenance, qualifiers or reification, they lack a unified framework to simultaneously model and query data across multiple social, cultural, and political dimensions. To bridge this gap, we introduce the conceptual foundations of Multi-dimensional Knowledge Graphs (MKGs) and discuss how they preserve complex, multi-layered contexts in CHO metadata.

</details>


### [309] [TrajectoryDB: A New Database for Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07782)
*Yunjia Zheng,Juncheng Yang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出将AI代理执行轨迹视为一种独特的数据类型，并构想TrajectoryDB——一个轨迹原生的数据管理系统，协同设计摄取、存储和查询处理以高效管理和分析代理执行轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理生成的执行轨迹对下游任务（如记忆提取、模型微调、运行时优化、安全与成本监控）越来越有价值，但现有数据管理方式无法满足其独特的结构与访问模式需求。

Method: 将轨迹视为独特数据类型，识别其三大核心特性：层级执行结构、大量需要语义推理的文本、事件/中间状态/衍生工件间的丰富依赖与血缘关系。基于这些特性，提出TrajectoryDB系统，在摄取阶段重建并保存执行结构与血缘，在存储阶段有效组织高冗余上下文并维护记录间关系，在查询处理阶段联合推理结构、时序、语义与血缘。

Result: 该论文是愿景/立场论文，提出了TrajectoryDB的概念框架，尚未报告具体实验结果。

Conclusion: 轨迹数据的独特性质在整个数据生命周期（摄取、存储、查询）中引入了新需求，需要为其设计专门的原生数据管理系统（TrajectoryDB），通过协同设计这些环节来解决现有的碎片化数据管理问题。

Abstract: AI agents generate rich execution trajectories that capture their interactions with large language models, tools, and external environments. These trajectories are increasingly valuable for downstream tasks such as memory extraction, model fine-tuning, runtime optimization, and security and cost monitoring. Yet trajectory data today is fragmented across files, databases, and observability systems, with no persistent data management system designed around its unique structure and access patterns.
  We argue that trajectories should be treated as a distinct data type. A trajectory combines hierarchical execution structure, large volumes of text whose analysis often requires semantic reasoning, and rich dependencies and lineage among events, intermediate states, and derived artifacts. These properties introduce new requirements throughout the data lifecycle. Ingestion must reconstruct and preserve execution structure and lineage; storage must efficiently organize large but highly redundant contexts while maintaining relationships among records; and query processing must jointly reason over structure, temporal order, semantics, and lineage.
  We therefore envision TrajectoryDB, a trajectory-native data management system that co-designs ingestion, storage, and query processing to efficiently manage and analyze agent execution trajectories.

</details>


### [310] [Generalized DBLog: A Verified Contract for Interleaving Database Rows with a Change Log](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08160)
*Andreas Andreakis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了"通用化DBLog"理论框架，系统地验证了变更数据捕获（CDC）中copy-to-log交接问题的各种变体设计的正确性条件，并通过Isabelle/HOL、Lean 4和TLA+进行机器验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DBLog算法（由Netflix开发）解决了CDC流水线引导时从数据库复制现有行与活动日志合并的copy-to-log交接问题。Debezium和Flink CDC已改编该设计，但原始的证明仅适用于经典算法。当这些变体被部署时，其正确性是否有保障？此前缺乏一种通用的理论框架来回答"何时同一结果对这些变体依然成立"这一关键问题。

Method: 作者提出了"通用化DBLog"（Generalized DBLog）理论，明确了源数据库与捕获实现必须满足的条件。该理论涵盖经典的watermarking方法、Debezium的信号表和只读模式、Flink CDC的并行chunks、精确绑定日志位置的读写/转储，以及日志位置处于已知边界内的引擎一致性备份等多种变体设计。整个理论在Isabelle/HOL中完成机器校验，核心部分在Lean 4中独立验证，并通过TLA+进行有界模型检查。

Result: 在复制和调和完成之后，证明结果表明：即便不同表和键范围的行的读取时间不同（不要求单一数据库快照），重建状态在所有选定表和键范围上依然保持正确性；后续的日志变更会逐步推进重建状态。所有证明均经过多种形式化工具的机器验证。

Conclusion: 通用化DBLog为CDC设计中copy-to-log交接问题的各种变体提供了统一的正确性证明框架，证明这些算法在满足所陈述条件下都能正确重建源数据库状态，从而为工业界的实现提供了严格的理论保障。

Abstract: Change-data capture (CDC) feeds downstream systems like caches, search indexes, and data warehouses from a database's log of committed row changes. When bootstrapping, adding a table, or repairing downstream data, a pipeline must also copy existing rows. Merging this copy with the active log introduces the copy-to-log handoff problem. Changes must not fall through a gap, and older copied state must not overwrite a newer logged update or resurrect a deleted row. DBLog, developed at Netflix, addressed this problem by reading tables in chunks and interleaving those reads with the live log. Watermarks identify the changes that overlap each read, and the log wins when a copied row is stale. Debezium and Flink CDC have since adapted this design. Earlier work proved that applying the original algorithm's copied rows and logged changes in their emitted order reconstructs the source's rows, including the effect of every logged insert, update, and delete processed. Generalized DBLog asks when the same result holds for variants of that design. We state the conditions the source and capture implementation must satisfy. Once copying and reconciliation are complete, we prove that the result holds across all selected tables and key ranges even when their rows were read at different times. A single database snapshot is not required for the copy. Further logged changes advance the reconstructed state one event at a time. We establish these guarantees for classic watermarking, Debezium's signal-table and read-only modes, Flink CDC's parallel chunks, reads and dumps tied to exact log positions, and engine-consistent backups whose log position lies within known bounds. The complete theory is machine-checked in Isabelle/HOL, its core independently verified in Lean 4, and the protocols are also examined by bounded model checking in TLA+.

</details>


### [311] [UnespDataLens-RM: A Reference Model for Analytical Data Engineering with Governance, Quality, Provenance, and Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08184)
*Ronaldo Celso Messias Correia,Douglas Francisquini Toledo,Camila Tolin Santos da Silva*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出UnespDataLens-RM，一个技术无关的参考模型，将治理、质量、来源追踪、可追溯性和可复现性等横切能力整合到分析数据工程的统一结构中，旨在解决分析数据生命周期中的方法论碎片化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析数据资产的可靠性不仅依赖数据处理能力，还需要贯穿生命周期的治理、质量保证、来源追踪、可追溯性、版本管理和可复现性等机制。然而，这些责任通常由不同的模型、框架和操作实践分别处理，导致分析数据生命周期中的方法论碎片化。

Method: 采用设计科学研究（Design Science Research）方法，提出一个技术无关的参考模型，包含八个技术操作模块、八个横切模块、补充维度和一套形式化的工件、指标与验证标准，从设计阶段集成治理、质量、来源、可追溯性和可复现性。

Result: UnespDataLens-RM提供了一个概念性和方法论框架，为未来的实例化和分析管道的经验性评估奠定基础，使分析管道更加可治理、可文档化、可追溯、可审计和可复现。

Conclusion: 该参考模型填补了分析数据工程中方法论碎片化的空白，为分析管道的规范、组织和演进提供了统一的整合框架，支持后续实例化与实证评估。

Abstract: The growing reliance on data in analytical processes and evidence-based decision-making has reinforced the importance of Data Engineering in building pipelines capable of integrating, transforming, validating, and delivering data from heterogeneous sources. However, the reliability of analytical assets depends not only on data processing capabilities but also on mechanisms for governance, quality assurance, provenance, traceability, versioning, and reproducibility throughout their lifecycle. These responsibilities are commonly addressed by different models, frameworks, and operational practices, resulting in methodological fragmentation across the analytical data lifecycle. To address this gap, this article proposes UnespDataLens-RM, a technology-independent reference model that integrates technical-operational processes and cross-cutting capabilities within a unified structure for Analytical Data Engineering. The model aims to support the specification, organization, and evolution of analytical pipelines by incorporating governance, quality, provenance, traceability, and reproducibility from the design stage. Developed following the Design Science Research approach, UnespDataLens-RM comprises eight technical-operational modules, eight cross-cutting modules, complementary dimensions, and a formalized set of artifacts, metrics, and validation criteria. The resulting specification offers a conceptual and methodological framework for future instantiations and empirical evaluations of analytical pipelines designed to be more governable, documented, traceable, auditable, and reproducible.

</details>


### [312] [SmartANN: Object Causal Modeling Boosts Approximate Nearest Neighbor Diagnosis and Auto-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08240)
*Yutong Zhou,Guoxin Kang,Lei Wang,Xueya Zhang,Qinwei Yang,Jianfeng Zhan*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SmartANN是一个基于对象因果模型（OCM）的框架，用于近似最近邻（ANN）算法的瓶颈归因与自动化重新设计。它通过顺序的"诊断-替换"循环，将ANN工作流表示为八个有序的可替换对象，识别并修复性能瓶颈，从即插即用的动作库中组合优化方案，在IVF-PQ和HNSW两类ANN算法上取得了显著的Recall和QPS提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ANN组件分析主要比较孤立的设计选择，端到端基准测试报告聚合指标，均无法追踪性能损失在相互依赖的索引构建与查询执行阶段之间的传播路径，导致难以进行根因归因和自动化重新设计。

Method: SmartANN将ANN工作流建模为八个有序的可替换对象，采用顺序的诊断与替换循环：每次迭代识别第一个偏离预期行为或输出的对象作为瓶颈；由于上游瓶颈可能掩盖下游问题，发现瓶颈后将其替换为测试预言机（或产生更优结果的实现）再继续下游诊断；最后根据诊断出的瓶颈和失败原因，从可插拔动作库中组合兼容动作，生成优化的端到端ANN设计方案。该方法分别针对IVF-PQ（分区-量化）和HNSW（图基）两类ANN算法族进行了实例化。

Result: 在八个真实数据集上的实验表明，SmartANN在不同场景下将Recall提升了0.24%至74.20%，在可比Recall下将QPS提升了28.8%至256.5%，且诊断和自动设计开销较低。

Conclusion: SmartANN为ANN算法提供了首个跨阶段追踪性能损失传播并自动进行瓶颈归因与重新设计的框架，弥补了组件级分析与端到端基准测试之间的空白，实验验证了其在多种真实数据集和算法族上的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: Approximate Nearest Neighbor (ANN) algorithms achieve high efficiency through interdependent phases across index construction and query execution. This coupling allows upstream performance loss to propagate downstream, affecting execution behavior and measurable outputs. Existing component-level analyses mainly compare isolated design choices, while end-to-end benchmarks report aggregate metrics; neither traces loss propagation across dependent phases, hindering root-cause attribution and automated redesign.
  We present SmartANN, a framework based on the object causal model (OCM) for ANN bottleneck attribution and automated redesign.
